# Jam Board Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Add a **Jam board** page to Apollo: paste a video-essay brief, DeepSeek turns it into a validated board spec, deterministic code lays it out on a white Fabric.js canvas that pans and zooms without a size limit, and the user records themselves scrolling, drawing on it, moving pieces, and (milestone 2) editing it with plain-English commands.

**Architecture:** DeepSeek returns *structure* (an outline, then per-section blocks from a fixed 13-type vocabulary) — never geometry. A pure `layoutBoard(spec, measure)` turns the spec into a scene; a Fabric renderer draws the scene. All board data lives in one immutable `boardState` (`spec`, `overrides`, `annotations`, `connections`, `assets`, `view`) that changes only through validated operations (`applyOperation`), whether the operation came from a mouse gesture, a toolbar button or DeepSeek. Fabric is a renderer of that state, not the source of truth. Boards persist in IndexedDB.

**Tech Stack:** Node 22 + Express (server, `node --test`), React 18 + Vite + Tailwind (client), `fabric@^5.5.2` (client, lazy-loaded with the route), DeepSeek via the existing `AIProvider` abstraction, IndexedDB.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-jam-board-design.md` (read it first). Reference deck: `video/website-is-losing-you-sales.html` — a hand-built Fabric board whose tool code (drawing, snapping, text panel, persistence, recording mode) is the behavioural reference for milestone 1.

## Spec clarifications made while planning

These refine the spec; where the spec and this list differ, this list wins.

1. **A section is *usable*, not perfect.** The spec says a second validation failure yields a degraded section. Implemented as: after the retry, the better of the two attempts is kept if it has at least one block *and* a headline block (`title`/`statement`/`bigtype`); only an unusable section becomes degraded. A section with one dropped block out of six should not be thrown away.
2. **`RESIZE` takes `scale` or `scaleX`+`scaleY`** (multipliers), not absolute `width`/`height` — pieces resize by transform.
3. **Internal operations exist beside the AI vocabulary.** Gestures and chrome need `OVERRIDE_SET`, `ANNOTATION_ADD`, `ANNOTATION_UPDATE`, `ANNOTATION_REMOVE`, `ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR`, `OVERRIDES_RESET`, `SECTION_REPLACE`, `VIEW_SET`. They go through the same `applyOperation` but are never accepted from DeepSeek (`validateBoardOperation` rejects them).
4. **Overrides also carry `w`** (a text box's width) so resizing a text piece survives a re-render.
5. **Items can `follow` a leader** (a browser frame's chrome, a panel's rows) so moving the leader moves them.
6. **Block ids are stable, not positional after generation.** `INSERT_BLOCK` allocates `max+1` so existing overrides never re-attach to the wrong block.
7. **Title/bigtype text is auto-fitted** to ≤ 760 px wide so a centred 9:16 crop still holds it; wide diagrams (hub, compare, panels, cards, timeline) are exempt but symmetric about x = 700.
8. **Relative placement has an `over` position** (centre on the anchor), and a shape placed `over` something with no stated size is drawn around it — that is how "circle LOADING" works. A slim scroll indicator and a limited-storage warning are part of the finished board page.

## How this plan was checked

The code in this plan was not just written: every file was extracted from these steps into a scratch copy of the repository and run. At the time of writing that copy passed **176 client tests and 85 server tests**, built with `vite build` (Fabric only in the lazy board chunk), and passed the four browser scripts in `jam-board-e2e/` in real Chrome with a clean console — including generation with live progress, every drawing tool, images, recording mode, Direct mode, and a rebuild that keeps later annotations attached. Bugs found that way (an autosave timer that threw "Illegal invocation", a saved camera overwritten on first paint, a dialog that never saw Escape, a missing "over" placement) are already fixed in the code below. What the scripts cannot judge — how scrolling *feels*, and what the **real DeepSeek** produces for your brief — is left to the manual steps at the end of Tasks 22 and 24.

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements implicitly include this section. Values are copied from the spec.

- Board world width **1400**; content centred on **x = 700**; text blocks **≤ 700 px** wide (`SAFE_W`); wide diagrams symmetric around x = 700.
- Minimum sizes: normal labels **≥ 22 px**; `scale: 'small'` and `code` **≥ 16 px**.
- Zoom range **0.25–4**; initial zoom fits board width to the window.
- Limits: brief **40–60,000** chars (else HTTP 400); **≤ 20** sections; **≤ 10** blocks per section; per-call `max_tokens` **3,500**; per-call timeout **90 s**; section concurrency **4**; undo/redo **100** snapshots; autosave **800 ms**; images PNG/JPG/WebP downscaled to **≤ 1600 px**.
- Text limits: labels **≤ 60**, statements **≤ 140**, `code` **≤ 120**; over-long text is clipped with `…`, never rejected.
- Tone → colour: `ink` #0a0a0a, `money` #0f7a3c, `problem` #c81e3a, `movement` #1d4ed8, `tech` #6b6b6b, `solution` ink-filled emphasis. The model returns tone *names*, never hex.
- Fonts: Inter, Geist, Space Grotesk, DM Sans, IBM Plex Mono, Source Serif 4 — injected by the board route only; global `index.html` is untouched.
- The canvas is **always white** regardless of Apollo's dark theme; chrome follows the theme.
- IndexedDB database name **`apollo-boards`**; localStorage fallback keys `apollo-boards:*`. Board prefs key `apollo-board-prefs`.
- Only new dependency: **`fabric@^5.5.2`** in `client/`. No new server dependencies.
- Code style: ESM, no TypeScript, 2-space indent, single quotes, semicolons, JSDoc-style block comments explaining *why* (match `server/src/services/ai/DeepSeekProvider.js`). Tailwind tokens from `client/tailwind.config.js` (`bg-void`, `text-ink`, `border-line`, `bg-accent`…); primitives from `client/src/ui/primitives.jsx`.
- Provider names and HTTP statuses never reach the browser; use plain-sentence errors (pattern: `friendlyError` in `server/src/routes/assistant.js`).
- The server/client vocabulary files are **byte-identical mirrors** (`schema.js`, `operations.js`); `server/test/board.parity.test.js` fails if they differ.
- Tests use Node's built-in runner: `import test from 'node:test'; import assert from 'node:assert/strict';`. Run with `npm test` in `server/` and `client/`.
- Line endings: existing repo files are checked out with CRLF (`core.autocrlf=true`); new files you create may be LF. If a multi-line `Edit` to an existing file does not match, retry the anchor one line at a time. Never rewrite whole existing files just to change line endings.
- Acceptance scripts: `docs/superpowers/plans/jam-board-e2e/` holds Playwright scripts that drive the real board in Chrome (see its README). The tasks below name the script that verifies each milestone; they are not part of the product and add no dependency to `package.json`.
- Do not modify existing behaviour of the design editor, Scribble, Apollo AI, or their routes. Additions to shared files (`App.jsx`, `AppHeader.jsx`, `app.js`, `AIProvider.js`, `DeepSeekProvider.js`, `MockProvider.js`, package.json files, `README.md`) are additive.

## Review Focus

Failure modes the spec implies but no obvious task would test. Each has a test in the task named in brackets.

1. **A brief with no usable chapters** (prose only, or almost entirely tool instructions): the outline comes back empty → the user gets a plain-sentence error, nothing is saved. *[Task 5]*
2. **Lax model output:** steps/nodes as bare strings, empty entries, wrong-case types, 40 nodes, 200-character labels, `null`s. The normalizer must coerce or drop, never throw. *[Task 1]*
3. **Long, multi-line or emoji text** (a 60-char label in a 130 px node, `\n` in titles, "💀" as a step): the layout must wrap and grow boxes so nothing overlaps or leaves its block. *[Tasks 9–11]*
4. **Regenerating a section changes its height:** annotations and moved pieces in *later* sections must stay attached to their own section, not stay at their old world y. *[Task 8, Task 21]*
5. **Storage failure and abrupt exit:** IndexedDB unavailable, quota exceeded, or the tab closed inside the 800 ms autosave window must fall back or flush, never lose the whole board or throw. *[Task 14, Task 16]*

## File Structure

**Server** (`server/`)

| Path | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `src/design/board/schema.js` | Block vocabulary, limits, normalizers. **Mirrored** to client. |
| `src/design/board/operations.js` | Board-operation vocabulary + `validateBoardOperation`. **Mirrored** to client. |
| `src/design/board/prompts.js` | Outline, section and edit prompts. |
| `src/services/boardService.js` | `generateBoard`, `generateSection`, `editBoard`, brief checks. |
| `src/routes/board.js` | `createBoardRouter`, `boardRouter`; SSE + plain routes. |
| `src/services/ai/AIProvider.js` (+ DeepSeek, Mock) | `completeJSON`, `live`, `NotConfiguredError`. |
| `test/` | `fixtures/boardSamples.js`, schema, operations, provider, prompts, service, routes, edit, parity tests. |

**Client** (`client/src/components/board/` unless noted)

| Path | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `state/schema.js`, `state/operations.js` | Byte-identical mirrors of the server files. |
| `state/ids.js` | Id helpers (`newAnnotationId`, `isAnnotationId`, `ancestorIds`). |
| `state/styleMap.js` | Semantic style → Fabric property mapping. |
| `state/annotations.js` | Pure builders for text/sticky/shape/arrow annotation records. |
| `state/anchor.js` | Section-relative ↔ world y conversions. |
| `state/boardState.js` | `createBoardState`, `applyOperation`, `replaceSection`. The only writer of board data. |
| `state/history.js` | Snapshot undo/redo with coalescing. |
| `state/boardRecord.js` | State ↔ persisted record, JSON export/import. |
| `engine/theme.js` | World constants, tones, fonts, spacing. |
| `engine/geometry.js` | Rect helpers, connector endpoint maths. |
| `engine/layoutBoard.js` + `engine/blocks/*.js` | Pure layout: spec → scene. |
| `engine/overrides.js` | Effective transform of an item given overrides (follow, block-level). |
| `engine/measure.js`, `engine/fonts.js` | Fabric text measurement; font loading. |
| `engine/viewportMath.js` | Pure pan/zoom maths. |
| `example/website-is-losing-you-sales.json` | The reference deck as a board spec. |
| `editor/LineArrow.js` | Custom Fabric line/arrow class with endpoint controls. |
| `editor/renderer.js` | Scene → Fabric objects, effective-transform patching, live connectors. |
| `editor/annotationSync.js` | Annotation records ↔ Fabric objects. |
| `editor/viewportController.js` | Wheel/pinch/drag DOM glue over `viewportMath`. |
| `editor/createEditor.js` | Assembles canvas, state, history, dispatch, reconcile, tools. |
| `tools/*.js` | One module per tool + manager. |
| `boardStore.js` | IndexedDB persistence with localStorage fallback. |
| `Toolbar.jsx`, `PropertyPanel.jsx`, `SectionRail.jsx`, `RecordingHud.jsx`, `HelpDialog.jsx`, `GenerateProgress.jsx`, `NewBoardForm.jsx`, `DirectBar.jsx`, `BoardCanvas.jsx` | React chrome. |
| `direct/*.js` | Scene outline builder and relative-operation resolver (milestone 2). |
| `client/src/api/board.js`, `client/src/lib/sse.js` | API + SSE reader. |
| `client/src/pages/BoardsPage.jsx`, `BoardPage.jsx` | Routes `/board`, `/board/:id`. |
| `client/test/*.test.js` | Pure-logic tests. |

## Milestone 1 — Generate, canvas, tools, persistence, recording

### Task 1: Server board schema (vocabulary + normalizers)

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/design/board/schema.js`
- Create: `server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js`
- Create: `server/test/board.schema.test.js`
- Modify: `server/package.json` (add `"test": "node --test"` to `scripts`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (all from `schema.js`):
  - `SPEC_VERSION = 1`, `TONES`, `BLOCK_TYPES`, `HEADLINE_TYPES = ['title','statement','bigtype']`, `LIMITS`.
  - `normalizeBlock(raw) → block | null` — a block *without* `id`.
  - `normalizeSection(raw, index) → { section, issues: string[] }` — `section.id = 's' + (index+1)`, block ids `s{n}.b{m}` (m from 1).
  - `normalizeOutline(raw) → { title, audience, sections: [{ title, beat, density }] } | null`.
  - `normalizeBoard(raw) → { version, title, audience, sections } | null` (used for imports/example).
  - `degradedSection(index, entry) → section` (flagged `degraded: true`).
- Produces (fixtures): `SAMPLES` — one valid raw block per `BLOCK_TYPES` entry, keyed by type.

```js
/** One valid raw block per block type — shared by the schema, operations and layout tests. */
export const SAMPLES = {
  title: { type: 'title', text: 'YOUR WEBSITE\nIS LOSING\nYOU SALES', size: 'xl' },
  statement: { type: 'statement', text: 'NOBODY WANTS TO LOOK AT AN HVAC WEBSITE.', tone: 'problem' },
  bigtype: { type: 'bigtype', text: '8:47 PM', caption: 'A customer submits your form' },
  quote: { type: 'quote', text: '"I paid for this click. What happens now?"' },
  flow: { type: 'flow', direction: 'up', tone: 'money', steps: ['GOOGLE', 'WEBSITE', 'CONTACT', 'LEAD', 'CUSTOMER', '$1,500 JOB'] },
  hub: {
    type: 'hub', center: 'BROWSER', links: 'spokes+cross',
```

`server/test/board.schema.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  BLOCK_TYPES, LIMITS, degradedSection, normalizeBlock, normalizeBoard, normalizeOutline, normalizeSection,
} from '../src/design/board/schema.js';
import { SAMPLES } from './fixtures/boardSamples.js';

test('every block type has a valid sample that normalizes', () => {
  for (const type of BLOCK_TYPES) {
    const block = normalizeBlock(SAMPLES[type]);
    assert.ok(block, `${type} sample should normalize`);
    assert.equal(block.type, type);
  }
});

test('flow accepts bare strings and objects, drops empties, defaults direction and scale', () => {
  const b = normalizeBlock({ type: 'flow', steps: ['GOOGLE', { label: 'WEBSITE', note: 'lands here' }, '', null, { text: 'CONTACT' }] });
  assert.deepEqual(b.steps.map((s) => s.label), ['GOOGLE', 'WEBSITE', 'CONTACT']);
  assert.equal(b.steps[1].note, 'lands here');
  assert.equal(b.direction, 'down');
  assert.equal(b.scale, 'normal');
});

test('flow with fewer than two usable steps is dropped', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'flow', steps: ['ONLY ONE', '', null] }), null);
});

test('types are matched case-insensitively and unknown types or non-objects are dropped', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'STATEMENT', text: 'Hi' }).type, 'statement');
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'carousel', text: 'x' }), null);
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock(null), null);
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock('text'), null);
});

test('long labels are clipped with an ellipsis and never rejected', () => {
  const b = normalizeBlock({ type: 'flow', steps: ['A'.repeat(200), 'B'] });
  assert.ok(b.steps[0].label.length <= LIMITS.label);
  assert.ok(b.steps[0].label.endsWith('…'));
});

test('arrays are capped at their maximum', () => {
  const nodes = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => `N${i}`);
  const b = normalizeBlock({ type: 'hub', center: 'X', nodes });
  assert.equal(b.nodes.length, LIMITS.hubNodes[1]);
});

test('hub needs a centre and at least three nodes; nodes may be strings', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'hub', center: 'X', nodes: ['a', 'b'] }), null);
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'hub', nodes: ['a', 'b', 'c'] }), null);
  const ok = normalizeBlock({ type: 'hub', center: 'X', nodes: ['a', 'b', 'c'] });
  assert.deepEqual(ok.nodes, [{ label: 'a' }, { label: 'b' }, { label: 'c' }]);
  assert.equal(ok.links, 'spokes');
});

test('cards clamp columns and keep tags and tones', () => {
  const b = normalizeBlock({ type: 'cards', columns: 9, items: [{ label: 'a', tag: 'LEAK', tone: 'problem' }, { label: 'b' }] });
  assert.equal(b.columns, 4);
  assert.equal(b.items[0].tag, 'LEAK');
  assert.equal(b.items[0].tone, 'problem');
});

test('titles keep their line breaks; other text is collapsed to one line', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'title', text: 'YOUR WEBSITE\nIS LOSING' }).text, 'YOUR WEBSITE\nIS LOSING');
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'statement', text: 'a \n  b' }).text, 'a b');
});

test('unknown tones fall back to ink', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBlock({ type: 'statement', text: 'x', tone: 'neon' }).tone, 'ink');
});

test('emoji and unicode survive', () => {
  const b = normalizeBlock({ type: 'flow', steps: ['EDIT', '💀'] });
  assert.equal(b.steps[1].label, '💀');
});

test('normalizeSection assigns positional ids and reports dropped blocks', () => {
  const { section, issues } = normalizeSection(
    { title: 'THE SALE', beat: 'money', density: 9, blocks: [SAMPLES.title, { type: 'nope' }, SAMPLES.flow] },
    0
  );
  assert.equal(section.id, 's1');
  assert.equal(section.density, 5);
  assert.deepEqual(section.blocks.map((b) => b.id), ['s1.b1', 's1.b2']);
  assert.equal(issues.length, 1);
  assert.match(issues[0], /block 2 dropped/);
});

test('normalizeSection reports a missing headline block', () => {
  const { issues } = normalizeSection({ title: 'x', blocks: [SAMPLES.flow] }, 2);
  assert.ok(issues.some((i) => /headline/.test(i)));
});

test('normalizeSection caps blocks per section', () => {
  const blocks = Array.from({ length: 14 }, () => SAMPLES.statement);
  const { section, issues } = normalizeSection({ title: 'x', blocks }, 0);
  assert.equal(section.blocks.length, LIMITS.blocks);
  assert.equal(issues.length, 4);
});

test('normalizeSection survives garbage', () => {
  const { section } = normalizeSection(null, 3);
  assert.equal(section.id, 's4');
  assert.equal(section.title, 'Section 4');
  assert.deepEqual(section.blocks, []);
});

test('degradedSection is a title plus a quiet statement, flagged', () => {
  const s = degradedSection(1, { title: 'HOW DID THEY FIND YOU?', beat: 'They searched and clicked.' });
  assert.equal(s.id, 's2');
  assert.equal(s.degraded, true);
  assert.deepEqual(s.blocks.map((b) => b.type), ['title', 'statement']);
  assert.equal(s.blocks[1].emphasis, 'quiet');
});

test('normalizeOutline clamps and rejects empties', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeOutline({ sections: [] }), null);
  assert.equal(normalizeOutline({ sections: [{ beat: 'no title' }] }), null);
  assert.equal(normalizeOutline(null), null);
  const o = normalizeOutline({ title: 'T', sections: [{ title: 'One', density: 0 }, { title: 'Two', beat: 'b', density: 4 }] });
  assert.equal(o.sections.length, 2);
  assert.equal(o.sections[0].density, 1);
  assert.equal(o.title, 'T');
});

test('normalizeOutline caps section count', () => {
  const sections = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({ title: `S${i}` }));
  assert.equal(normalizeOutline({ sections }).sections.length, LIMITS.sections);
});

test('normalizeBoard re-indexes after dropping empty sections', () => {
  const board = normalizeBoard({
    title: 'B',
    sections: [
      { title: 'A', blocks: [SAMPLES.title] },
      { title: 'empty', blocks: [{ type: 'nope' }] },
      { title: 'C', blocks: [SAMPLES.statement] },
    ],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(board.sections.map((s) => s.id), ['s1', 's2']);
  assert.deepEqual(board.sections.map((s) => s.title), ['A', 'C']);
  assert.equal(board.version, 1);
  assert.equal(normalizeBoard({ sections: [] }), null);
  assert.equal(normalizeBoard('x'), null);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `server/src/design/board/schema.js`**

```js
/**
 * Jam board — the board spec's vocabulary and its normalizers.
 *
 * MIRRORED byte-for-byte at client/src/components/board/state/schema.js. Keep
 * the two identical; server/test/board.parity.test.js fails if they drift.
 * Pure and dependency-free so the same rules run on both sides.
 *
 * DeepSeek's output is untrusted. Everything here coerces toward something the
 * layout engine can draw and drops what it cannot. It never throws on model
 * output: a board with one lost block is a board, a crashed request is not.
 */

export const SPEC_VERSION = 1;

/** Semantic colour roles. The model names a role; the client owns the hex. */
export const TONES = ['ink', 'money', 'problem', 'movement', 'tech', 'solution'];

export const BLOCK_TYPES = [
  'title', 'statement', 'bigtype', 'quote', 'flow', 'hub', 'cards',
  'compare', 'panels', 'code', 'timeline', 'funnel', 'browser',
];

/** A section without one of these has nothing to say out loud and is sent back for repair. */
export const HEADLINE_TYPES = ['title', 'statement', 'bigtype'];

export const LIMITS = {
  sections: 20,
  blocks: 10,
  label: 60,
  note: 80,
  statement: 140,
  code: 120,
  bigtype: 24,
  title: 80,
  beat: 200,
  flowSteps: [2, 9],
  hubNodes: [3, 12],
  cards: [2, 12],
  compareSteps: [1, 7],
  timeline: [2, 8],
  funnel: [3, 7],
  panels: [1, 3],
  rows: [1, 8],
};

const isObj = (v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);
const list = (v) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
const oneOf = (v, allowed, fallback) => (allowed.includes(v) ? v : fallback);
const tone = (v, fallback) => oneOf(v, TONES, fallback);

const int = (v, lo, hi, fallback) => {
  const n = Math.round(Number(v));
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, n)) : fallback;
};

/** Trim, collapse whitespace (unless multiline), clip with an ellipsis. Non-strings become ''. */
function text(v, max, { multiline = false } = {}) {
  if (typeof v === 'number') v = String(v);
  if (typeof v !== 'string') return '';
  let s = multiline
    ? v.replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim()
    : v.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (s.length > max) s = `${s.slice(0, max - 1).trimEnd()}…`;
  return s;
}

/** Models return steps as strings or as {label|text|name|title|when}; accept all of them. */
function labelOf(item, max) {
  if (isObj(item)) return text(item.label ?? item.text ?? item.name ?? item.title ?? item.when, max);
  return text(item, max);
}

function steps(raw, [min, max]) {
  const out = [];
  for (const item of list(raw)) {
    const label = labelOf(item, LIMITS.label);
    if (!label) continue;
    const step = { label };
    if (isObj(item)) {
      const note = text(item.note ?? item.detail ?? item.caption, LIMITS.note);
      if (note) step.note = note;
      if (TONES.includes(item.tone)) step.tone = item.tone;
    }
    out.push(step);
    if (out.length === max) break;
  }
  return out.length >= min ? out : null;
}

function normalizeSide(raw) {
  if (!isObj(raw)) return null;
  const s = steps(raw.steps ?? raw.items, LIMITS.compareSteps);
  if (!s) return null;
  const side = { heading: text(raw.heading ?? raw.title, LIMITS.label), tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink'), steps: s };
  const footnote = text(raw.footnote, LIMITS.note);
  if (footnote) side.footnote = footnote;
  return side;
}

function normalizePanel(raw) {
  if (!isObj(raw)) return null;
  const rows = [];
  for (const row of list(raw.rows)) {
    const label = labelOf(row, LIMITS.label);
    if (!label) continue;
    rows.push({ label, state: oneOf(isObj(row) ? row.state : undefined, ['open', 'locked'], 'open') });
    if (rows.length === LIMITS.rows[1]) break;
  }
  if (rows.length < LIMITS.rows[0]) return null;
  return { heading: text(raw.heading ?? raw.title, LIMITS.label), tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink'), rows };
}

/** One raw block → a clean block without an id, or null when it cannot be drawn. */
export function normalizeBlock(raw) {
  if (!isObj(raw)) return null;
  const type = typeof raw.type === 'string' ? raw.type.toLowerCase() : '';

  switch (type) {
    case 'title': {
      const t = text(raw.text ?? raw.title, LIMITS.title, { multiline: true });
      if (!t) return null;
      const block = { type, text: t, size: oneOf(raw.size, ['xl', 'l', 'm'], 'l'), tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink') };
      const eyebrow = text(raw.eyebrow, LIMITS.label);
      if (eyebrow) block.eyebrow = eyebrow;
      return block;
    }
    case 'statement': {
      const t = text(raw.text, LIMITS.statement);
      if (!t) return null;
      return { type, text: t, emphasis: oneOf(raw.emphasis, ['loud', 'quiet'], 'loud'), tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink') };
    }
    case 'bigtype': {
      const t = text(raw.text, LIMITS.bigtype);
      if (!t) return null;
      const block = { type, text: t, tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink') };
      const caption = text(raw.caption, LIMITS.note);
      if (caption) block.caption = caption;
      return block;
    }
    case 'quote': {
      const t = text(raw.text, LIMITS.statement);
      if (!t) return null;
      const block = { type, text: t };
      const attribution = text(raw.attribution, LIMITS.label);
      if (attribution) block.attribution = attribution;
      return block;
    }
    case 'flow': {
      const s = steps(raw.steps ?? raw.items, LIMITS.flowSteps);
      if (!s) return null;
      return {
        type,
        direction: oneOf(raw.direction, ['up', 'down', 'right'], 'down'),
        scale: oneOf(raw.scale, ['hero', 'normal', 'small'], 'normal'),
        tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink'),
        steps: s,
      };
    }
    case 'hub': {
      const center = text(raw.center, LIMITS.label);
      const nodes = steps(raw.nodes ?? raw.items, LIMITS.hubNodes);
      if (!center || !nodes) return null;
      return {
        type,
        center,
        nodes: nodes.map(({ label }) => ({ label })),
        links: oneOf(raw.links, ['spokes', 'spokes+cross'], 'spokes'),
        tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink'),
      };
    }
    case 'cards': {
      const items = [];
      for (const item of list(raw.items)) {
        const label = labelOf(item, LIMITS.label);
        if (!label) continue;
        const card = { label };
        if (isObj(item)) {
          const tag = text(item.tag, 24);
          if (tag) card.tag = tag;
          if (TONES.includes(item.tone)) card.tone = item.tone;
        }
        items.push(card);
        if (items.length === LIMITS.cards[1]) break;
      }
      if (items.length < LIMITS.cards[0]) return null;
      return { type, items, columns: int(raw.columns, 2, 4, 3), tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink') };
    }
    case 'compare': {
      const left = normalizeSide(raw.left);
      const right = normalizeSide(raw.right);
      return left && right ? { type, left, right } : null;
    }
    case 'panels': {
      const panels = list(raw.panels).map(normalizePanel).filter(Boolean).slice(0, LIMITS.panels[1]);
      return panels.length >= LIMITS.panels[0] ? { type, panels } : null;
    }
    case 'code': {
      const t = text(raw.text, LIMITS.code);
      if (!t) return null;
      const block = { type, text: t };
      const caption = text(raw.caption, LIMITS.note);
      if (caption) block.caption = caption;
      return block;
    }
    case 'timeline': {
      const events = [];
      for (const ev of list(raw.events ?? raw.items)) {
        const label = isObj(ev) ? text(ev.label ?? ev.text ?? ev.title, LIMITS.label) : text(ev, LIMITS.label);
        if (!label) continue;
        const item = { when: isObj(ev) ? text(ev.when ?? ev.time ?? ev.date, 24) : '', label };
        if (isObj(ev) && TONES.includes(ev.tone)) item.tone = ev.tone;
        events.push(item);
        if (events.length === LIMITS.timeline[1]) break;
      }
      if (events.length < LIMITS.timeline[0]) return null;
      return { type, events, direction: oneOf(raw.direction, ['right', 'down'], 'right'), tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink') };
    }
    case 'funnel': {
      const stages = [];
      for (const st of list(raw.stages ?? raw.items)) {
        const label = labelOf(st, LIMITS.label);
        if (!label) continue;
        const stage = { label };
        const value = isObj(st) ? text(st.value, 24) : '';
        if (value) stage.value = value;
        stages.push(stage);
        if (stages.length === LIMITS.funnel[1]) break;
      }
      if (stages.length < LIMITS.funnel[0]) return null;
      return { type, stages, tone: tone(raw.tone, 'ink') };
    }
    case 'browser': {
      const block = { type, placeholder: oneOf(raw.placeholder, ['screenshot', 'blank'], 'screenshot') };
      const url = text(raw.url, LIMITS.label);
      const title = text(raw.title, LIMITS.label);
      const note = text(raw.note, LIMITS.note);
      if (url) block.url = url;
      if (title) block.title = title;
      if (note) block.note = note;
      return block;
    }
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

function describeDrop(raw) {
  if (!isObj(raw)) return 'not an object';
  const type = String(raw.type).toLowerCase();
  return BLOCK_TYPES.includes(type) ? `invalid ${type} block` : `unknown block type "${raw.type}"`;
}

/**
 * One raw section → a clean section plus a list of problems.
 * `index` is the section's position; it fixes the id (`s1`, `s2`, …).
 */
export function normalizeSection(raw, index) {
  const src = isObj(raw) ? raw : {};
  const issues = [];
  const blocks = [];

  list(src.blocks).forEach((rawBlock, i) => {
    if (blocks.length >= LIMITS.blocks) {
      issues.push(`block ${i + 1} dropped: over the ${LIMITS.blocks}-block limit`);
      return;
    }
    const block = normalizeBlock(rawBlock);
    if (!block) {
      issues.push(`block ${i + 1} dropped: ${describeDrop(rawBlock)}`);
      return;
    }
    blocks.push(block);
  });

  const id = `s${index + 1}`;
  const section = {
    id,
    title: text(src.title, LIMITS.label) || `Section ${index + 1}`,
    beat: text(src.beat, LIMITS.beat),
    density: int(src.density, 1, 5, 3),
    blocks: blocks.map((block, i) => ({ ...block, id: `${id}.b${i + 1}` })),
  };
  if (src.degraded === true) section.degraded = true;
  if (!section.blocks.some((b) => HEADLINE_TYPES.includes(b.type))) {
    issues.push('no headline block (add a title, statement or bigtype)');
  }
  return { section, issues };
}

/** What a section becomes when the model cannot produce a usable one. */
export function degradedSection(index, entry = {}) {
  const id = `s${index + 1}`;
  const title = text(entry.title, LIMITS.label) || `Section ${index + 1}`;
  const beat = text(entry.beat, LIMITS.beat);
  const blocks = [{ type: 'title', text: title, size: 'l', tone: 'ink', id: `${id}.b1` }];
  const line = text(entry.beat, LIMITS.statement);
  if (line) blocks.push({ type: 'statement', text: line, emphasis: 'quiet', tone: 'ink', id: `${id}.b2` });
  return { id, title, beat, density: 1, degraded: true, blocks };
}

/** The outline call's answer: the board's title and the sections it will have. */
export function normalizeOutline(raw) {
  if (!isObj(raw)) return null;
  const sections = [];
  for (const s of list(raw.sections)) {
    if (!isObj(s)) continue;
    const title = text(s.title, LIMITS.label);
    if (!title) continue;
    sections.push({ title, beat: text(s.beat, LIMITS.beat), density: int(s.density, 1, 5, 3) });
    if (sections.length === LIMITS.sections) break;
  }
  if (!sections.length) return null;
  return {
    title: text(raw.title, LIMITS.title) || sections[0].title,
    audience: text(raw.audience, LIMITS.title),
    sections,
  };
}

/** A whole board spec from an untrusted source (import, bundled example). */
export function normalizeBoard(raw) {
  if (!isObj(raw)) return null;
  // Two passes: drop sections with no drawable block first, so ids stay contiguous.
  const kept = list(raw.sections)
    .slice(0, LIMITS.sections)
    .filter((s) => normalizeSection(s, 0).section.blocks.length > 0);
  if (!kept.length) return null;
  return {
    version: SPEC_VERSION,
    title: text(raw.title, LIMITS.title) || 'Untitled board',
    audience: text(raw.audience, LIMITS.title),
    sections: kept.map((s, i) => normalizeSection(s, i).section),
  };
}
```

### Task 2: Board-operation vocabulary and validator

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/design/board/operations.js`
- Create: `server/test/board.operations.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `normalizeBlock`, `TONES` from `./schema.js`.
- Produces:
  - `BOARD_OP_TYPES` (the 16 AI-facing ops), `STYLE_FONTS`.
  - `sanitizeStyle(changes) → style` — only whitelisted keys with clamped values.
  - `validateBoardOperation(op) → { ok: true, op } | { ok: false, error }`. `op` is a cleaned copy; `targets` is always `string[]` when present. Internal ops (`OVERRIDE_SET`, `ANNOTATION_*`, …) are rejected here.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`server/test/board.operations.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { BOARD_OP_TYPES, sanitizeStyle, validateBoardOperation } from '../src/design/board/operations.js';
import { SAMPLES } from './fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const ok = (op) => {
  const r = validateBoardOperation(op);
  assert.ok(r.ok, r.error);
  return r.op;
};
const bad = (op) => assert.equal(validateBoardOperation(op).ok, false, JSON.stringify(op));

test('SET_TEXT needs one target and text', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(ok({ type: 'SET_TEXT', target: 's1.b1.n1', text: 'Hello' }).targets, ['s1.b1.n1']);
  bad({ type: 'SET_TEXT', target: 's1.b1.n1' });
  bad({ type: 'SET_TEXT', text: 'no target' });
});

test('targets are deduplicated, capped and must look like ids', () => {
  const many = Array.from({ length: 80 }, (_, i) => `s1.b${i}`);
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'DELETE', targets: [...many, ...many] }).targets.length, 50);
  bad({ type: 'DELETE', target: '../etc/passwd' });
  bad({ type: 'DELETE', target: 'x'.repeat(200) });
});

test('SET_STYLE keeps only whitelisted keys, clamps values, and needs at least one', () => {
  const op = ok({ type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: ['s3.b1'], changes: { fontSize: 5000, color: 'problem', bogus: 1, fontFamily: 'Comic Sans' } });
  assert.deepEqual(op.changes, { fontSize: 400, color: 'problem' });
  bad({ type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: ['s3.b1'], changes: { bogus: 1 } });
  bad({ type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: ['s3.b1'] });
});

test('sanitizeStyle validates colours and enums', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(sanitizeStyle({ color: '#ff0000', align: 'left', opacity: 2, italic: true }), { color: '#ff0000', align: 'left', opacity: 1, italic: true });
  assert.deepEqual(sanitizeStyle({ color: 'javascript:alert(1)', align: 'justify' }), {});
  assert.deepEqual(sanitizeStyle({ fontWeight: 'bold', dash: true, radius: -4 }), { fontWeight: 'bold', dash: true, radius: 0 });
  assert.deepEqual(sanitizeStyle({ headEnd: true, headStart: false, headMiddle: true }), { headEnd: true, headStart: false });
});

test('MOVE takes dx/dy or a relative placement, never neither', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(ok({ type: 'MOVE', target: 's1.b1', dx: 10, dy: -20 }), { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['s1.b1'], dx: 10, dy: -20 });
  const rel = ok({ type: 'MOVE', target: 's3.b2', relativeTo: 's3.b1', position: 'below' });
  assert.equal(rel.gap, 40);
  assert.equal(rel.align, 'center');
  bad({ type: 'MOVE', target: 's1.b1' });
  bad({ type: 'MOVE', target: 's1.b1', dx: 10 });
  bad({ type: 'MOVE', target: 's3.b2', relativeTo: 's3.b1', position: 'diagonally' });
});

test('RESIZE takes scale or scaleX+scaleY within range', () => {
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'RESIZE', target: 'a_1', scale: 1.5 }).scale, 1.5);
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'RESIZE', target: 'a_1', scale: 99 }).scale, 10);
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'RESIZE', target: 'a_1', scaleX: 2, scaleY: 0.5 }).scaleY, 0.5);
  bad({ type: 'RESIZE', target: 'a_1' });
  bad({ type: 'RESIZE', target: 'a_1', scaleX: 2 });
});

test('ROTATE clamps the angle', () => {
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'ROTATE', target: 'a_1', angle: 720 }).angle, 360);
  bad({ type: 'ROTATE', target: 'a_1', angle: 'up' });
});

test('DELETE and RESTORE need targets', () => {
  ok({ type: 'DELETE', targets: ['s1.b1'] });
  ok({ type: 'RESTORE', target: 's1.b1' });
  bad({ type: 'DELETE' });
});

test('INSERT_BLOCK normalizes the block with the shared schema', () => {
  const op = ok({ type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 's2', index: 1, block: SAMPLES.statement });
  assert.equal(op.block.type, 'statement');
  assert.equal(op.index, 1);
  bad({ type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 's2', block: { type: 'nope' } });
  bad({ type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 'section-two', block: SAMPLES.statement });
});

test('REPLACE_BLOCK targets a block id', () => {
  ok({ type: 'REPLACE_BLOCK', target: 's2.b3', block: SAMPLES.flow });
  bad({ type: 'REPLACE_BLOCK', target: 's2', block: SAMPLES.flow });
  bad({ type: 'REPLACE_BLOCK', target: 's2.b3.n1', block: SAMPLES.flow });
});

test('CONNECT and DISCONNECT need two ids', () => {
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'CONNECT', from: 's1.b1.n1', to: 's1.b1.n2' }).tone, 'ink');
  ok({ type: 'DISCONNECT', from: 's1.b1.n1', to: 's1.b1.n2' });
  bad({ type: 'CONNECT', from: 's1.b1.n1' });
});

test('ADD_TEXT, ADD_SHAPE and ADD_ARROW build annotations', () => {
  const t = ok({ type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'THIS IS THE LEAK', section: 's3', x: 700, y: 400, style: { color: 'problem', fontSize: 120 } });
  assert.equal(t.style.fontSize, 120);
  const s = ok({ type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'circle', relativeTo: 's3.b1.n3', position: 'over' });
  assert.equal(s.shape, 'circle');
  assert.equal(s.position, 'over');
  assert.equal('width' in s, false, 'a shape with no stated size sizes itself to its target');
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'rectangle', width: 300, height: 90 }).width, 300);
  const a = ok({ type: 'ADD_ARROW', from: 's3.b1.n1', to: 's3.b1.n2', dashed: true });
  assert.equal(a.dashed, true);
  bad({ type: 'ADD_TEXT' });
  bad({ type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'hexagon' });
  bad({ type: 'ADD_ARROW' });
});

test('FOCUS and SET_VIEW', () => {
  ok({ type: 'FOCUS', target: 's3.b1' });
  assert.equal(ok({ type: 'SET_VIEW', zoom: 9, x: 0, y: 0 }).zoom, 4);
  bad({ type: 'SET_VIEW', x: 0, y: 0 });
});

test('unknown and internal operation types are rejected', () => {
  bad({ type: 'DROP_TABLE' });
  bad({ type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's1.b1', patch: { dx: 1 } });
  bad({ type: 'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR' });
  bad(null);
  bad('MOVE');
  assert.equal(BOARD_OP_TYPES.length, 16);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `server/src/design/board/operations.js`**

```js
/**
 * Jam board — the operations DeepSeek (and Direct mode) may ask for.
 *
 * MIRRORED byte-for-byte at client/src/components/board/state/operations.js.
 * Keep the two identical; server/test/board.parity.test.js fails if they drift.
 *
 * This validates the *AI-facing* vocabulary only. The editor's own internal
 * operations (OVERRIDE_SET, ANNOTATION_ADD, …) exist in boardState.js and are
 * deliberately rejected here: a model has no business writing raw Fabric JSON
 * into a board, so it cannot.
 */

import { TONES, normalizeBlock } from './schema.js';

export const BOARD_OP_TYPES = [
  'SET_TEXT', 'SET_STYLE', 'MOVE', 'RESIZE', 'ROTATE', 'DELETE', 'RESTORE',
  'INSERT_BLOCK', 'REPLACE_BLOCK', 'CONNECT', 'DISCONNECT',
  'ADD_TEXT', 'ADD_SHAPE', 'ADD_ARROW', 'FOCUS', 'SET_VIEW',
];

export const STYLE_FONTS = ['Inter', 'Geist', 'Space Grotesk', 'DM Sans', 'IBM Plex Mono', 'Source Serif 4'];

const POSITIONS = ['above', 'below', 'left', 'right', 'over'];
const ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$/;
const SECTION_RE = /^s\d{1,2}$/;
const BLOCK_RE = /^s\d{1,2}\.b\d{1,3}$/;
const HEX_RE = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$/;
const RGB_RE = /^rgba?\([\d\s.,%]+\)$/;

const num = (v, lo, hi) => (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v)) : null);
const idOf = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' && ID_RE.test(v) ? v : null);
const clipText = (v, max) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim().slice(0, max) : '');

function targetsOf(op) {
  const raw = Array.isArray(op.targets) ? op.targets : [op.target];
  return [...new Set(raw.map(idOf).filter(Boolean))].slice(0, 50);
}

function colorOf(v) {
  if (TONES.includes(v) || v === 'transparent') return v;
  if (typeof v === 'string' && (HEX_RE.test(v) || RGB_RE.test(v))) return v;
  return null;
}

/** Whitelist and clamp a style patch. Unknown keys and bad values are dropped, not errors. */
export function sanitizeStyle(changes) {
  if (changes === null || typeof changes !== 'object') return {};
  const out = {};
  if (STYLE_FONTS.includes(changes.fontFamily)) out.fontFamily = changes.fontFamily;
  const size = num(changes.fontSize, 8, 400);
  if (size !== null) out.fontSize = Math.round(size);
  if (changes.fontWeight === 'bold' || changes.fontWeight === 'normal') out.fontWeight = changes.fontWeight;
  else {
    const w = num(changes.fontWeight, 100, 900);
    if (w !== null) out.fontWeight = Math.round(w / 100) * 100;
  }
  if (typeof changes.italic === 'boolean') out.italic = changes.italic;
  if (typeof changes.underline === 'boolean') out.underline = changes.underline;
  if (['left', 'center', 'right'].includes(changes.align)) out.align = changes.align;
  const lh = num(changes.lineHeight, 0.6, 3);
  if (lh !== null) out.lineHeight = lh;
  const ls = num(changes.letterSpacing, -100, 800);
  if (ls !== null) out.letterSpacing = Math.round(ls);
  const op = num(changes.opacity, 0, 1);
  if (op !== null) out.opacity = op;
  for (const key of ['color', 'fill', 'stroke']) {
    const c = colorOf(changes[key]);
    if (c) out[key] = c;
  }
  const sw = num(changes.strokeWidth, 0, 40);
  if (sw !== null) out.strokeWidth = sw;
  const radius = num(changes.radius, 0, 200);
  if (radius !== null) out.radius = radius;
  if (typeof changes.dash === 'boolean') out.dash = changes.dash;
  if (typeof changes.headEnd === 'boolean') out.headEnd = changes.headEnd;
  if (typeof changes.headStart === 'boolean') out.headStart = changes.headStart;
  return out;
}

/** Placement shared by the MOVE and ADD_* operations. */
function placement(op) {
  const out = {};
  const rel = idOf(op.relativeTo);
  if (rel && POSITIONS.includes(op.position)) {
    out.relativeTo = rel;
    out.position = op.position;
    out.gap = num(op.gap, 0, 2000) ?? 40;
    out.align = ['center', 'start', 'end'].includes(op.align) ? op.align : 'center';
  }
  if (typeof op.section === 'string' && SECTION_RE.test(op.section)) out.section = op.section;
  const x = num(op.x, -5000, 6000);
  const y = num(op.y, -5000, 60000);
  if (x !== null) out.x = x;
  if (y !== null) out.y = y;
  return out;
}

const fail = (error) => ({ ok: false, error });

export function validateBoardOperation(op) {
  if (op === null || typeof op !== 'object' || !BOARD_OP_TYPES.includes(op.type)) {
    return fail(`unknown operation "${op && op.type}"`);
  }

  switch (op.type) {
    case 'SET_TEXT': {
      const targets = targetsOf(op);
      const text = clipText(op.text, 2000);
      if (!targets.length || !text) return fail('SET_TEXT needs a target and text');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets: targets.slice(0, 1), text } };
    }
    case 'SET_STYLE': {
      const targets = targetsOf(op);
      const changes = sanitizeStyle(op.changes);
      if (!targets.length) return fail('SET_STYLE needs a target');
      if (!Object.keys(changes).length) return fail('SET_STYLE has no valid style changes');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets, changes } };
    }
    case 'MOVE': {
      const targets = targetsOf(op);
      if (!targets.length) return fail('MOVE needs a target');
      const dx = num(op.dx, -20000, 20000);
      const dy = num(op.dy, -20000, 20000);
      if (dx !== null && dy !== null) return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets, dx, dy } };
      const p = placement(op);
      if (!p.relativeTo) return fail('MOVE needs dx and dy, or relativeTo and a valid position');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets, ...p } };
    }
    case 'RESIZE': {
      const targets = targetsOf(op);
      if (!targets.length) return fail('RESIZE needs a target');
      const scale = num(op.scale, 0.1, 10);
      if (scale !== null) return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets, scale } };
      const sx = num(op.scaleX, 0.1, 10);
      const sy = num(op.scaleY, 0.1, 10);
      if (sx !== null && sy !== null) return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets, scaleX: sx, scaleY: sy } };
      return fail('RESIZE needs scale, or scaleX and scaleY');
    }
    case 'ROTATE': {
      const targets = targetsOf(op);
      const angle = num(op.angle, -360, 360);
      if (!targets.length || angle === null) return fail('ROTATE needs a target and an angle');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets, angle } };
    }
    case 'DELETE':
    case 'RESTORE': {
      const targets = targetsOf(op);
      if (!targets.length) return fail(`${op.type} needs a target`);
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets } };
    }
    case 'INSERT_BLOCK': {
      const block = normalizeBlock(op.block);
      if (typeof op.section !== 'string' || !SECTION_RE.test(op.section)) return fail('INSERT_BLOCK needs a section id like "s3"');
      if (!block) return fail('INSERT_BLOCK block is not a valid block');
      const index = num(op.index, 0, 10);
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, section: op.section, index: index === null ? 10 : Math.round(index), block } };
    }
    case 'REPLACE_BLOCK': {
      const target = idOf(op.target);
      const block = normalizeBlock(op.block);
      if (!target || !BLOCK_RE.test(target)) return fail('REPLACE_BLOCK needs a block id like "s3.b2"');
      if (!block) return fail('REPLACE_BLOCK block is not a valid block');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets: [target], block } };
    }
    case 'CONNECT':
    case 'DISCONNECT': {
      const from = idOf(op.from);
      const to = idOf(op.to);
      if (!from || !to || from === to) return fail(`${op.type} needs two different ids`);
      const out = { type: op.type, from, to };
      if (op.type === 'CONNECT') {
        out.tone = TONES.includes(op.tone) ? op.tone : 'ink';
        out.dashed = op.dashed === true;
      }
      return { ok: true, op: out };
    }
    case 'ADD_TEXT': {
      const text = clipText(op.text, 500);
      if (!text) return fail('ADD_TEXT needs text');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, text, style: sanitizeStyle(op.style), ...placement(op) } };
    }
    case 'ADD_SHAPE': {
      if (!['rectangle', 'circle'].includes(op.shape)) return fail('ADD_SHAPE needs shape "rectangle" or "circle"');
      // Size is only set when the model gave one: "circle X" sizes itself to X at placement time.
      const out = { type: op.type, shape: op.shape, style: sanitizeStyle(op.style), ...placement(op) };
      const width = num(op.width, 20, 3000);
      const height = num(op.height, 20, 3000);
      if (width !== null) out.width = width;
      if (height !== null) out.height = height;
      return { ok: true, op: out };
    }
    case 'ADD_ARROW': {
      const from = idOf(op.from);
      const to = idOf(op.to);
      const hasCoords = ['x1', 'y1', 'x2', 'y2'].every((k) => num(op[k], -5000, 60000) !== null);
      if (!(from && to) && !hasCoords) return fail('ADD_ARROW needs from/to ids, or x1 y1 x2 y2');
      const out = { type: op.type, dashed: op.dashed === true, tone: TONES.includes(op.tone) ? op.tone : 'problem' };
      if (from && to) Object.assign(out, { from, to });
      else {
        for (const k of ['x1', 'y1', 'x2', 'y2']) out[k] = num(op[k], -5000, 60000);
        if (typeof op.section === 'string' && SECTION_RE.test(op.section)) out.section = op.section;
      }
      return { ok: true, op: out };
    }
    case 'FOCUS': {
      const targets = targetsOf(op);
      if (!targets.length) return fail('FOCUS needs a target');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, targets: targets.slice(0, 1) } };
    }
    case 'SET_VIEW': {
      const zoom = num(op.zoom, 0.25, 4);
      const x = num(op.x, -1e6, 1e6);
      const y = num(op.y, -1e6, 1e6);
      if (zoom === null || x === null || y === null) return fail('SET_VIEW needs zoom, x and y');
      return { ok: true, op: { type: op.type, zoom, x, y } };
    }
    default:
      return fail(`unknown operation "${op.type}"`);
  }
}
```

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { DeepSeekProvider } from '../src/services/ai/DeepSeekProvider.js';
import { MockProvider } from '../src/services/ai/MockProvider.js';
import { NotConfiguredError } from '../src/services/ai/AIProvider.js';

const provider = () => new DeepSeekProvider({ apiKey: 'k', baseUrl: 'https://x.test/', model: 'm' });
const reply = (content) => new Response(JSON.stringify({ choices: [{ message: { content } }] }), { status: 200 });

async function withFetch(stub, fn) {
  const real = globalThis.fetch;
  globalThis.fetch = stub;
  try {
    return await fn();
  } finally {
    globalThis.fetch = real;
  }
}

test('completeJSON sends a JSON-mode request and returns the parsed object', async () => {
  const seen = [];
  await withFetch(async (url, init) => { seen.push({ url, init }); return reply('{"sections":[1]}'); }, async () => {
    const out = await provider().completeJSON({ system: 'SYS', user: 'USR', maxTokens: 111, temperature: 0.2 });
    assert.deepEqual(out, { sections: [1] });
  });
  const body = JSON.parse(seen[0].init.body);
  assert.equal(seen[0].url, 'https://x.test/chat/completions');
  assert.equal(body.max_tokens, 111);
  assert.equal(body.temperature, 0.2);
  assert.deepEqual(body.response_format, { type: 'json_object' });
  assert.equal(body.messages[0].content, 'SYS');
  assert.equal(body.messages[1].content, 'USR');
});

test('aborting the caller signal cancels the upstream request', async () => {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const stub = (url, init) => new Promise((_, reject) => {
    init.signal.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' })));
  });
  await withFetch(stub, async () => {
    const pending = provider().completeJSON({ system: 's', user: 'u', signal: ctrl.signal });
    setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 5);
    await assert.rejects(pending, { name: 'AbortError' });
  });
});

test('a reply that is valid JSON but not an object becomes {}', async () => {
  await withFetch(async () => reply('[1,2,3]'), async () => {
    assert.deepEqual(await provider().completeJSON({ system: 's', user: 'u' }), {});
  });
});

test('a non-JSON reply throws', async () => {
  await withFetch(async () => reply('not json at all'), async () => {
    await assert.rejects(provider().completeJSON({ system: 's', user: 'u' }), /non-JSON/);
  });
});

test('an HTTP error carries its status', async () => {
  await withFetch(async () => new Response('nope', { status: 401 }), async () => {
    await assert.rejects(provider().completeJSON({ system: 's', user: 'u' }), (err) => err.status === 401);
  });
});

test('only DeepSeek is live; the mock refuses with NotConfiguredError', async () => {

  const mock = new MockProvider();
  assert.equal(mock.live, false);
  await assert.rejects(mock.completeJSON({ system: 's', user: 'u' }), (err) => err instanceof NotConfiguredError && err.status === 503);
});
```

### Task 4: Prompts

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/design/board/prompts.js`
- Test: `server/test/board.prompts.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `BLOCK_TYPES`, `TONES`, `LIMITS` from `./schema.js`.
- Produces:
  - `BLOCK_SHAPES` — `{ [blockType]: string }` (one JSON example per type; the test proves every type has one).
  - `buildOutlinePrompt(brief) → { system, user }`.
  - `buildSectionPrompt({ brief, outline, index, note?, issues? }) → { system, user }` where `outline = { title, audience, sections: [{ title, beat, density }] }`. `user` **starts with** `BRIEF:\n<brief>\n\n---\n\n` for every section so DeepSeek's prefix cache serves the other calls.

`server/test/board.prompts.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { BLOCK_TYPES, TONES } from '../src/design/board/schema.js';
import { BLOCK_SHAPES, buildOutlinePrompt, buildSectionPrompt } from '../src/design/board/prompts.js';

const BRIEF = 'A video about slow websites.\n\n## 1. THE SALE\nGoogle ↓ Website ↓ Lead';
const outline = {
  title: 'Slow sites', audience: 'business owners',
  sections: [{ title: 'THE SALE', beat: 'money', density: 1 }, { title: 'THE LEAK', beat: 'loss', density: 4 }, { title: 'THE FIX', beat: 'relief', density: 2 }],
};

test('every block type has a JSON shape in the vocabulary', () => {
  for (const type of BLOCK_TYPES) assert.ok(BLOCK_SHAPES[type], `missing shape for ${type}`);
});

test('the section system prompt teaches every block type and every tone', () => {
  const { system } = buildSectionPrompt({ brief: BRIEF, outline, index: 0 });
  for (const type of BLOCK_TYPES) assert.ok(system.includes(`"type":"${type}"`), `system prompt lacks ${type}`);
  for (const tone of TONES) assert.ok(system.includes(tone), `system prompt lacks tone ${tone}`);
  assert.match(system, /never write hex/i);
  assert.match(system, /EXACTLY/);
});

test('the brief leads every section prompt and the prefix is identical across sections', () => {
  const a = buildSectionPrompt({ brief: BRIEF, outline, index: 0 });
  const b = buildSectionPrompt({ brief: BRIEF, outline, index: 2, note: 'more visual' });
  const prefix = `BRIEF:\n${BRIEF}\n\n---\n\n`;
  assert.ok(a.user.startsWith(prefix));
  assert.ok(b.user.startsWith(prefix));
  assert.equal(a.system, b.system);
});

test('the section prompt names the section, its neighbours, density and any note', () => {
  const { user } = buildSectionPrompt({ brief: BRIEF, outline, index: 1, note: 'make the graph huge' });
  assert.match(user, /Build section 2 of 3: "THE LEAK"/);
  assert.match(user, /Previous section: "THE SALE"/);
  assert.match(user, /Next section: "THE FIX"/);
  assert.match(user, /Density: 4\/5/);
  assert.match(user, /make the graph huge/);
});

test('first and last sections say so', () => {
  assert.match(buildSectionPrompt({ brief: BRIEF, outline, index: 0 }).user, /first section/);
  assert.match(buildSectionPrompt({ brief: BRIEF, outline, index: 2 }).user, /last section/);
});

test('validator issues from a failed attempt are fed back verbatim', () => {
  const { user } = buildSectionPrompt({ brief: BRIEF, outline, index: 0, issues: ['block 2 dropped: unknown block type "carousel"'] });
  assert.match(user, /previous attempt/i);
  assert.match(user, /unknown block type "carousel"/);
});

test('the outline prompt tells the model to ignore tool and style instructions', () => {
  const { system, user } = buildOutlinePrompt(BRIEF);
  assert.match(system, /IGNORE/);
  assert.match(system, /toolbar/i);
  assert.match(system, /never more than 20/i);
  assert.ok(user.startsWith(`BRIEF:\n${BRIEF}`));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `server/src/design/board/prompts.js`**

```js
/**
 * Jam board — what DeepSeek is told.
 *
 * The model is asked for *structure*, never geometry: which blocks a section
 * needs and what they say. Code places them (client/.../engine/layoutBoard.js).
 * That is the same split the design pipeline uses (planDesign + layout.js), and
 * it is what keeps a generated board aligned, centred and readable.
 *
 * The section prompt is arranged for DeepSeek's prefix cache: an identical
 * system prompt, then the whole brief, and only *then* the part that varies.
 * Twelve section calls therefore pay for the brief once.
 */

import { BLOCK_TYPES, LIMITS, TONES } from './schema.js';

/** One example per block type. A block type added to the schema must appear here (a test checks). */
export const BLOCK_SHAPES = {
  title: '{"type":"title","text":"YOUR WEBSITE\\nIS LOSING\\nYOU SALES","size":"xl"|"l"|"m","eyebrow":"optional small label","tone":"ink"}',
  statement: '{"type":"statement","text":"the claim the video lands on","emphasis":"loud"|"quiet","tone":"problem"}',
  bigtype: '{"type":"bigtype","text":"8:47 PM","caption":"optional line under it","tone":"ink"}',
  quote: '{"type":"quote","text":"\\"Do you service commercial properties?\\"","attribution":"optional, e.g. 10:30 PM"}',
  flow: '{"type":"flow","direction":"down"|"up"|"right","scale":"hero"|"normal"|"small","tone":"money","steps":[{"label":"GOOGLE","note":"optional","tone":"optional"}]}   // 2-9 steps',
  hub: '{"type":"hub","center":"BROWSER","links":"spokes"|"spokes+cross","tone":"tech","nodes":[{"label":"HTML"}]}   // 3-12 nodes',
  cards: '{"type":"cards","columns":2|3|4,"tone":"problem","items":[{"label":"Phone isn\'t clickable","tag":"LEAK","tone":"problem"}]}   // 2-12 items',
  compare: '{"type":"compare","left":{"heading":"GENERIC AI","tone":"tech","steps":[{"label":"QUESTION"}],"footnote":"optional"},"right":{"heading":"BUSINESS AI","tone":"money","steps":[{"label":"QUESTION"}]}}   // 1-7 steps a side',
  panels: '{"type":"panels","panels":[{"heading":"CONTENT","tone":"money","rows":[{"label":"Headline","state":"open"|"locked"}]}]}   // 1-3 panels, 1-8 rows',
  code: '{"type":"code","text":"<a href=\\"tel:+15551234567\\">","caption":"optional"}',
  timeline: '{"type":"timeline","direction":"right"|"down","tone":"problem","events":[{"when":"8:47 PM","label":"Form submitted"}]}   // 2-8 events',
  funnel: '{"type":"funnel","tone":"money","stages":[{"label":"Searches","value":"1,000"}]}   // 3-7 stages, widest first',
  browser: '{"type":"browser","url":"example.com","title":"optional","note":"optional","placeholder":"screenshot"|"blank"}',
};

const VOCABULARY = BLOCK_TYPES.map((type) => BLOCK_SHAPES[type]).join('\n');

const DENSITY_WORDS = {
  1: 'almost empty — one idea, lots of space',
  2: 'sparse',
  3: 'a normal section',
  4: 'busy — several ideas or a big diagram',
  5: 'dense — the visual peak; many parts on screen at once',
};

/* -------------------------------- outline -------------------------------- */

const OUTLINE_SYSTEM = `You are the producer of a video essay. A creator will screen-record themselves talking over a large visual board, scrolling from section to section. You read their brief and decide what the sections of that board are.

The brief mixes several kinds of text. Only ONE kind becomes sections:
- The narrative itself: the chapters, beats, flows, statements and diagrams the video will show. THIS becomes sections, in the brief's own order.
- Instructions about a tool or interface: toolbars, shortcuts, file names, storage, testing, "build me an HTML page", how software should behave. IGNORE these completely.
- Visual style guidance (white theme, typography, restraint, "premium"). IGNORE it: the board's look is built in.

Follow the brief's own chapter structure. If chapters are numbered, keep their numbering and order. If it has none, split the narrative into 6-14 sections at natural beats. Never more than ${LIMITS.sections}.

Return ONLY a JSON object:
{
  "title": "the video's title",
  "audience": "who it is for, a short phrase",
  "sections": [
    { "title": "SHORT SECTION TITLE, ${LIMITS.label} characters at most", "beat": "one sentence: what this section makes the viewer feel or understand", "density": 1 }
  ]
}

density is 1-5 and follows the video's arc: a section that opens or resolves the story is sparse (1-2); the middle builds (3-4); the section the brief describes as chaotic, exploding or the biggest visual moment is 5. If the brief describes a progression such as "simple, then complex, then chaos, then simplified", make the densities follow it.`;

export function buildOutlinePrompt(brief) {
  return {
    system: OUTLINE_SYSTEM,
    user: `BRIEF:\n${brief}\n\n---\n\nReturn the JSON outline now.`,
  };
}

/* -------------------------------- sections -------------------------------- */

const SECTION_SYSTEM = `You build ONE section of a visual board for a video essay. The creator will screen-record themselves scrolling down this board and talking, so every section must work as a big, sparse, readable picture — not a slide of bullet points.

You are given the creator's whole brief, then told which section to build. Build ONLY that section. The brief mixes real content with instructions about a tool, a toolbar, shortcuts, saving, or visual style: ignore all of that; it is not content.

Return ONLY a JSON object: { "blocks": [ ... ] } — between 2 and ${LIMITS.blocks} blocks, in reading order, top to bottom.

BLOCK VOCABULARY (use only these; this is the JSON shape of each):
${VOCABULARY}

TONES are colour roles — never write hex codes. Use: ${TONES.join(', ')}.
- money: revenue, customers, things that work, the good path.
- problem: leaks, failures, losses, the bad path.
- movement: the customer's journey, traffic, steps in motion.
- tech: infrastructure, code, secondary technical detail.
- ink: neutral headlines and plain statements.
- solution: the resolved end state.

CHOOSING A FORM FROM THE BRIEF'S PROSE
- "A ↓ B ↓ C", "A → B → C" or a numbered journey → flow. Direction "up" for a funnel that climbs toward a goal (the FIRST step is drawn at the bottom), "right" for short left-to-right chains of 2-5 steps, "down" otherwise. scale "hero" for the one journey that IS the section; "small" for a technical detail the brief says to keep subordinate.
- A network, dependency graph, architecture, or "everything connected to X" → hub. links "spokes+cross" when the point is tangled chaos, "spokes" otherwise.
- A grid of small labelled things, "leak cards", supporting elements → cards. Use a tag such as "LEAK" when the brief tags them.
- "versus", "generic … versus business …", before and after → compare.
- A panel with editable and locked fields → panels.
- One dominant clock, price or number such as "8:47 PM" or "$1,500" → bigtype.
- A spoken customer line or a quoted question → quote.
- A big claim the video lands on → statement: "loud" for the claim, "quiet" for a follow-up line.
- A code snippet or a metric list such as LCP · TBT → code (always visually subordinate).
- A sequence with times → timeline. A narrowing pipeline with counts → funnel. A picture of a web page or a screenshot the creator will drop in → browser.
- Open the section with a title or statement, unless the brief opens it with a bigtype.

FIDELITY
- Copy quoted strings and explicit flow labels from the brief EXACTLY, including capitalisation, punctuation and emoji. Prefer the brief's own words for labels.
- Never invent statistics, prices, company names, testimonials or claims that are not in the brief.
- If the brief says a product or brand should be introduced subtly, keep it a small title (size "m") or a code block, never a hero.

RULES
- Labels at most ${LIMITS.label} characters, statements at most ${LIMITS.statement}, code at most ${LIMITS.code}.
- Match the density you are given: 1 is one or two blocks with generous space; 3 is a normal section; 5 is dense — several diagrams, many nodes.
- No markdown, no commentary, no code fences. JSON only.`;

export function buildSectionPrompt({ brief, outline, index, note = '', issues = [] }) {
  const entry = outline.sections[index];
  const total = outline.sections.length;
  const previous = outline.sections[index - 1]?.title;
  const next = outline.sections[index + 1]?.title;

  const parts = [
    `BRIEF:\n${brief}`,
    '---',
    `Video: ${outline.title}${outline.audience ? ` — for ${outline.audience}` : ''}`,
    `Build section ${index + 1} of ${total}: "${entry.title}"`,
    entry.beat ? `What this section is for: ${entry.beat}` : '',
    `Density: ${entry.density}/5 (${DENSITY_WORDS[entry.density] || DENSITY_WORDS[3]})`,
    previous ? `Previous section: "${previous}"` : 'This is the first section.',
    next ? `Next section: "${next}"` : 'This is the last section.',
  ];
  if (note) parts.push(`Extra direction from the creator for this section: ${note}`);
  if (issues.length) parts.push(`Your previous attempt had these problems — fix them:\n- ${issues.join('\n- ')}`);
  parts.push('Return the JSON now.');

  return { system: SECTION_SYSTEM, user: parts.filter(Boolean).join('\n\n') };
}
```

### Task 5: Board service (outline → parallel sections → repair → degrade)

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/services/boardService.js`

`server/test/fixtures/stubProvider.js`:

```js
import { SAMPLES } from './boardSamples.js';

/** A stand-in for DeepSeek: records every call, answers with whatever `handler` returns. */
export function makeProvider(handler, { live = true } = {}) {
  const calls = [];
  return {
    live,
    model: 'stub',
    calls,
    async completeJSON(req) {
      calls.push(req);
      return handler(req, calls.length);
    },
  };
}

export const isOutline = (req) => !/Build section \d+ of/.test(req.user);
export const sectionIndex = (req) => Number(/Build section (\d+) of/.exec(req.user)[1]) - 1;
export const goodSection = () => ({ blocks: [SAMPLES.title, SAMPLES.flow] });
export const outlineOf = (n) => ({
  title: 'Board',
  audience: 'business owners',
  sections: Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ title: `Section ${i + 1}`, beat: `beat ${i + 1}`, density: 3 })),
});
export const delay = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
```

`server/test/board.service.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { BRIEF_MAX, BRIEF_MIN, BoardError, checkBrief, generateBoard, generateSection } from '../src/services/boardService.js';
import { delay, goodSection, isOutline, makeProvider, outlineOf, sectionIndex } from './fixtures/stubProvider.js';
import { SAMPLES } from './fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const BRIEF = 'A video essay about why a slow website loses paid clicks. '.repeat(3);

test('checkBrief enforces the length window with plain sentences', () => {
  assert.match(checkBrief('short'), new RegExp(String(BRIEF_MIN)));
  assert.match(checkBrief('x'.repeat(BRIEF_MAX + 1)), /too long/);
  assert.equal(checkBrief(BRIEF), null);
  assert.ok(checkBrief(undefined));
});

test('generateBoard runs the outline, then every section, and emits events', async () => {
  const events = [];
  const provider = makeProvider((req) => (isOutline(req) ? outlineOf(3) : goodSection()));
  const board = await generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider, onEvent: (name, data) => events.push([name, data]) });
  assert.equal(board.version, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(board.sections.map((s) => s.id), ['s1', 's2', 's3']);
  assert.equal(events[0][0], 'outline');
  assert.deepEqual(events[0][1].sections.map((s) => s.id), ['s1', 's2', 's3']);
  assert.equal(events.filter(([name]) => name === 'section').length, 3);
});

test('the brief leads every section prompt', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider((req) => (isOutline(req) ? outlineOf(2) : goodSection()));
  await generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider });
  const sectionCalls = provider.calls.filter((c) => !isOutline(c));
  assert.equal(sectionCalls.length, 2);
  for (const call of sectionCalls) assert.ok(call.user.startsWith(`BRIEF:\n${BRIEF}`));
});

test('sections run in parallel but never more than four at once', async () => {
  let inFlight = 0;
  let max = 0;
  const provider = makeProvider(async (req) => {
    if (isOutline(req)) return outlineOf(10);
    inFlight += 1;
    max = Math.max(max, inFlight);
    await delay(15);
    inFlight -= 1;
    return goodSection();
  });
  const board = await generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider });
  assert.equal(board.sections.length, 10);
  assert.equal(max, 4);
});

test('a section with a bad block is retried once with the validator errors in the prompt', async () => {
  const seen = [];
  const provider = makeProvider((req) => {
    if (isOutline(req)) return outlineOf(1);
    seen.push(req.user);
    return seen.length === 1 ? { blocks: [SAMPLES.title, { type: 'carousel' }] } : goodSection();
  });
  const board = await generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider });
  assert.equal(seen.length, 2);
  assert.match(seen[1], /unknown block type "carousel"/);
  assert.equal(board.sections[0].degraded, undefined);
  assert.equal(board.sections[0].blocks.length, 2);
});

test('a section that stays mildly wrong is kept after the retry, not degraded', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider((req) => (isOutline(req) ? outlineOf(1) : { blocks: [SAMPLES.title, { type: 'carousel' }, SAMPLES.flow] }));
  const board = await generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider });
  assert.equal(board.sections[0].degraded, undefined);
  assert.equal(board.sections[0].blocks.length, 2);
});

test('two failed calls degrade that section but not the board', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider((req) => {
    if (isOutline(req)) return outlineOf(2);
    if (sectionIndex(req) === 1) throw new Error('boom');
    return goodSection();
  });
  const board = await generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider });
  assert.equal(board.sections[0].degraded, undefined);
  assert.equal(board.sections[1].degraded, true);
  assert.deepEqual(board.sections[1].blocks.map((b) => b.type), ['title', 'statement']);
});

test('a model that returns nothing usable degrades the section', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider((req) => (isOutline(req) ? outlineOf(1) : { blocks: [] }));
  const board = await generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider });
  assert.equal(board.sections[0].degraded, true);
});

test('a fatal provider error (bad key) fails the whole board instead of degrading everything', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider((req) => {
    if (isOutline(req)) return outlineOf(3);
    throw Object.assign(new Error('unauthorised'), { status: 401 });
  });
  await assert.rejects(generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider }), (err) => err.status === 401);
});

test('a brief with no usable chapters is a plain-sentence 422', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider(() => ({ sections: [] }));
  await assert.rejects(generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider }), (err) => err instanceof BoardError && err.status === 422 && /couldn't find any sections/.test(err.message));
});

test('a too-short brief is a 400 before any model call', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider(() => outlineOf(1));
  await assert.rejects(generateBoard({ brief: 'tiny', provider }), (err) => err.status === 400);
  assert.equal(provider.calls.length, 0);
});

test('aborting stops further model calls', async () => {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const provider = makeProvider(async (req) => {
    if (isOutline(req)) return outlineOf(8);
    ctrl.abort();
    await delay(5);
    return goodSection();
  });
  await assert.rejects(generateBoard({ brief: BRIEF, provider, signal: ctrl.signal }), { name: 'AbortError' });
  assert.ok(provider.calls.length <= 5, `made ${provider.calls.length} calls after abort`);
});

test('generateSection rebuilds one section and passes the creator note along', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider(() => goodSection());
  const section = await generateSection({ brief: BRIEF, outline: outlineOf(3), index: 1, note: 'make it more visual', provider });
  assert.equal(section.id, 's2');
  assert.equal(section.title, 'Section 2');
  assert.match(provider.calls[0].user, /make it more visual/);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `server/src/services/boardService.js`**

```js
import { getAIProvider } from './ai/index.js';
import {
  HEADLINE_TYPES, SPEC_VERSION, degradedSection, normalizeOutline, normalizeSection,
} from '../design/board/schema.js';
import { buildOutlinePrompt, buildSectionPrompt } from '../design/board/prompts.js';

/**
 * Jam board generation.
 *
 * One outline call decides the sections, then one call per section builds its
 * blocks, four at a time. Splitting the work is what makes a 12-section board
 * possible at all — one call for the lot would sit at DeepSeek's output ceiling
 * and a single stray brace would lose everything — and it lets a failed section
 * be retried, or replaced by a plain one, without touching the others.
 */

export const BRIEF_MIN = 40;
export const BRIEF_MAX = 60_000;
const CONCURRENCY = 4;
const FATAL_STATUSES = new Set([401, 402, 403]);

/** An error whose message is safe to show a person. */
export class BoardError extends Error {
  constructor(message, status = 502) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'BoardError';
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export function checkBrief(brief) {
  if (typeof brief !== 'string' || brief.trim().length < BRIEF_MIN) {
    return `Write at least ${BRIEF_MIN} characters so Apollo has something to build from.`;
  }
  if (brief.length > BRIEF_MAX) {
    return `That brief is too long. Keep it under ${BRIEF_MAX.toLocaleString('en-US')} characters.`;
  }
  return null;
}

/** A bad key or a missing model will fail every section the same way — say so once, loudly. */
const isFatal = (err) => err?.name === 'NotConfiguredError' || FATAL_STATUSES.has(err?.status);

function throwIfAborted(signal) {
  if (signal?.aborted) {
    const err = new Error('aborted');
    err.name = 'AbortError';
    throw err;
  }
}

/** Something a person can talk over: it has at least one block and a headline. */
export const isUsable = (section) =>
  section.blocks.length > 0 && section.blocks.some((b) => HEADLINE_TYPES.includes(b.type));

async function generateOutline({ brief, provider, signal }) {
  throwIfAborted(signal);
  const raw = await provider.completeJSON({ ...buildOutlinePrompt(brief), temperature: 0.3, maxTokens: 1800, signal });
  const outline = normalizeOutline(raw);
  if (!outline) {
    throw new BoardError(
      "Apollo couldn't find any sections in that brief. Describe the video as a sequence of chapters or beats and try again.",
      422
    );
  }
  return outline;
}

/**
 * Build one section. Two attempts: the second is told exactly what the first
 * got wrong. The better usable attempt wins; if neither is usable the section
 * is replaced by a plain title-and-statement one so the board still exists.
 * Aborts and fatal provider errors are the only things that propagate.
 */
export async function generateSection({ brief, outline, index, note = '', provider = getAIProvider(), signal }) {
  const entry = outline.sections[index];
  let best = null;
  let issues = [];

  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt += 1) {
    throwIfAborted(signal);
    try {
      const raw = await provider.completeJSON({
        ...buildSectionPrompt({ brief, outline, index, note, issues }),
        temperature: attempt === 0 ? 0.5 : 0.3,
        maxTokens: 3500,
        signal,
      });
      const { section, issues: found } = normalizeSection({ ...entry, blocks: raw?.blocks }, index);
      if (isUsable(section) && (!best || section.blocks.length > best.blocks.length)) best = section;
      if (!found.length) return section;
      issues = found;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err?.name === 'AbortError' || isFatal(err)) throw err;
      issues = ['the previous attempt failed to produce valid JSON'];
    }
  }
  return best || degradedSection(index, entry);
}

export async function generateBoard({ brief, provider = getAIProvider(), signal, onEvent = () => {}, concurrency = CONCURRENCY }) {
  const problem = checkBrief(brief);
  if (problem) throw new BoardError(problem, 400);

  const outline = await generateOutline({ brief, provider, signal });
  onEvent('outline', {
    title: outline.title,
    audience: outline.audience,
    sections: outline.sections.map((s, i) => ({ id: `s${i + 1}`, ...s })),
  });

  const sections = new Array(outline.sections.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  let failure = null;

  const worker = async () => {
    while (!failure) {
      const index = cursor;
      cursor += 1;
      if (index >= sections.length) return;
      try {
        const section = await generateSection({ brief, outline, index, provider, signal });
        sections[index] = section;
        onEvent('section', { index, section });
      } catch (err) {
        failure = err;
      }
    }
  };

  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, sections.length) }, worker));
  if (failure) throw failure;

  return { version: SPEC_VERSION, title: outline.title, audience: outline.audience, sections };
}
```

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import express from 'express';
import { createBoardRouter } from '../src/routes/board.js';
import { NotConfiguredError } from '../src/services/ai/AIProvider.js';
import { goodSection, isOutline, makeProvider, outlineOf } from './fixtures/stubProvider.js';

const BRIEF = 'A video essay about why a slow website loses paid clicks. '.repeat(3);

async function withServer(provider, fn) {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json());
  app.use('/api/board', createBoardRouter({ provider }));
  const server = await new Promise((resolve) => { const s = app.listen(0, () => resolve(s)); });
  try {
    await fn(`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/api/board`);
  } finally {
    server.closeAllConnections?.();
    await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
  }
}

const post = (base, path, body) =>
  fetch(`${base}${path}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });

function parseSSE(text) {
  return text.split('\n\n').filter(Boolean).map((frame) => ({
    event: /^event: (.+)$/m.exec(frame)?.[1],
    data: JSON.parse(/^data: (.+)$/m.exec(frame)?.[1] || 'null'),
  }));
}

const provider = () => makeProvider((req) => (isOutline(req) ? outlineOf(2) : goodSection()));

test('GET /status reports whether a live model is configured', async () => {
  await withServer(provider(), async (base) => {
    assert.deepEqual(await (await fetch(`${base}/status`)).json(), { ready: true, model: 'stub' });
  });
  await withServer(makeProvider(() => ({}), { live: false }), async (base) => {
    assert.equal((await (await fetch(`${base}/status`)).json()).ready, false);
  });
});

test('POST /generate/stream narrates outline, sections, then the result', async () => {
  await withServer(provider(), async (base) => {
    const res = await post(base, '/generate/stream', { brief: BRIEF });
    assert.equal(res.status, 200);
    assert.match(res.headers.get('content-type'), /text\/event-stream/);
    const frames = parseSSE(await res.text());
    assert.equal(frames[0].event, 'outline');
    assert.equal(frames.filter((f) => f.event === 'section').length, 2);
    const last = frames.at(-1);
    assert.equal(last.event, 'result');
    assert.equal(last.data.board.sections.length, 2);
  });
});

test('POST /generate/stream rejects a short brief with a plain 400 before streaming', async () => {
  await withServer(provider(), async (base) => {
    const res = await post(base, '/generate/stream', { brief: 'tiny' });
    assert.equal(res.status, 400);
    assert.match((await res.json()).error, /at least/);
  });
});

test('POST /generate returns the whole board', async () => {
  await withServer(provider(), async (base) => {
    const res = await post(base, '/generate', { brief: BRIEF });
    assert.equal(res.status, 200);
    assert.equal((await res.json()).board.sections.length, 2);
  });
});

test('a missing model is a friendly 503 that never names a provider or status code', async () => {
  const p = makeProvider(() => { throw new NotConfiguredError(); });
  await withServer(p, async (base) => {
    const res = await post(base, '/generate', { brief: BRIEF });
    assert.equal(res.status, 503);

});

test('a stream that fails after it began ends with a failed event', async () => {
  const p = makeProvider(() => ({ sections: [] }));
  await withServer(p, async (base) => {
    const frames = parseSSE(await (await post(base, '/generate/stream', { brief: BRIEF })).text());
    assert.equal(frames.at(-1).event, 'failed');

test('POST /section rebuilds one section from a client-held outline', async () => {
```
