- [ ] **Step 3: Write `server/src/routes/board.js`**

```js
import { Router } from 'express';
import { getAIProvider } from '../services/ai/index.js';
import { normalizeOutline } from '../design/board/schema.js';
import { BoardError, checkBrief, generateBoard, generateSection } from '../services/boardService.js';

/**
 * Jam board endpoints.
 *
 * Same shape as Apollo AI's: a streamed route that narrates progress, and a
 * plain route that returns the finished result, kept because a proxy that
 * buffers or a browser without streaming bodies should cost a person the live
 * progress and nothing else. Provider names and status codes never reach the
 * browser.
 */

/** Upstream failures, said in a way a person can act on. */
function friendlyError(err) {
  if (err instanceof BoardError) return { status: err.status, error: err.message };
  if (err?.name === 'NotConfiguredError') {
    return { status: 503, error: 'Jam board needs a DeepSeek key to generate. Add DEEPSEEK_API_KEY, or open the example board.' };
  }
  const status = err?.status;
  if (status === 429) return { status: 429, error: 'Apollo is handling a lot of requests right now. Try again in a moment.' };
  if (status === 401 || status === 402 || status === 403) {
    return { status: 503, error: 'Board generation is unavailable right now. This looks like a configuration problem on our side.' };
  }
  if (err?.name === 'TimeoutError') return { status: 504, error: 'The model took too long to answer. Try again.' };
  return { status: 502, error: "Apollo couldn't finish that board. Try again." };
}

export function createBoardRouter({ provider } = {}) {
  const router = Router();
  const getProvider = () => provider || getAIProvider();

  router.get('/status', (req, res) => {
    const p = getProvider();
    res.json({ ready: p.live === true, model: p.live ? p.model || 'deepseek' : 'offline' });
  });

  router.post('/generate/stream', async (req, res) => {
    const { brief } = req.body || {};
    const problem = checkBrief(brief);
    if (problem) return res.status(400).json({ error: problem });

    res.writeHead(200, {
      'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8',
      // `no-transform` stops the compression middleware buffering the stream
      // into one lump at the end; the other two say the same to every proxy.
      'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-transform',
      'X-Accel-Buffering': 'no',
      Connection: 'keep-alive',
    });
    res.flushHeaders?.();

    // Watch the *response*: `req` closes as soon as the body is read. When the
    // person leaves, the abort must reach the model or we keep paying for tokens.
    const abort = new AbortController();
    let open = true;
    res.on('close', () => {
      open = false;
      abort.abort();
    });
    const send = (event, data) => {
      if (!open || res.writableEnded) return;
      res.write(`event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`);
    };

    try {
      const board = await generateBoard({
        brief,
        provider: getProvider(),
        signal: abort.signal,
        onEvent: (event, data) => send(event, data),
      });
      send('result', { board });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err?.name !== 'AbortError' && open) {
        console.error('[board]', err.message);
        send('failed', { error: friendlyError(err).error });
      }
    } finally {
      if (!res.writableEnded) res.end();
    }
  });

  router.post('/generate', async (req, res) => {
    const { brief } = req.body || {};
    const problem = checkBrief(brief);
    if (problem) return res.status(400).json({ error: problem });
    try {
      res.json({ board: await generateBoard({ brief, provider: getProvider() }) });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[board]', err.message);
      const { status, error } = friendlyError(err);
      res.status(status).json({ error });
    }
  });

  router.post('/section', async (req, res) => {
    const { brief, index } = req.body || {};
    const note = typeof req.body?.note === 'string' ? req.body.note.trim().slice(0, 300) : '';
    const problem = checkBrief(brief);
    if (problem) return res.status(400).json({ error: problem });
    const outline = normalizeOutline(req.body?.outline);
    if (!outline) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'outline is required' });
    if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0 || index >= outline.sections.length) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'index is out of range' });
    }
    try {
      res.json({ section: await generateSection({ brief, outline, index, note, provider: getProvider() }) });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[board]', err.message);
      const { status, error } = friendlyError(err);
      res.status(status).json({ error });
    }
  });

  return router;
}

export const boardRouter = createBoardRouter();
```

`client/test/sse.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readEventStream } from '../src/lib/sse.js';

const enc = new TextEncoder();
const streamOf = (chunks) =>
  new Response(new ReadableStream({
    start(controller) {
      for (const c of chunks) controller.enqueue(enc.encode(c));
      controller.close();
    },
  }));

async function collect(chunks) {
  const events = [];
  await readEventStream(streamOf(chunks), (event, data) => events.push([event, data]));
  return events;
}

test('parses events delivered whole', async () => {
  const events = await collect(['event: outline\ndata: {"a":1}\n\nevent: result\ndata: {"b":2}\n\n']);
  assert.deepEqual(events, [['outline', { a: 1 }], ['result', { b: 2 }]]);
});

test('reassembles frames split across chunks', async () => {
  const events = await collect(['event: sec', 'tion\ndata: {"i"', ':3}\n', '\nevent: result\ndata: {}\n\n']);
  assert.deepEqual(events, [['section', { i: 3 }], ['result', {}]]);
});

test('flushes a final frame that has no trailing blank line', async () => {
  const events = await collect(['event: result\ndata: {"ok":true}']);
  assert.deepEqual(events, [['result', { ok: true }]]);
});

test('skips malformed and incomplete frames', async () => {
  const events = await collect(['event: a\ndata: {not json}\n\ndata: {"x":1}\n\nevent: b\n\nevent: c\ndata: {"y":2}\n\n']);
  assert.deepEqual(events, [['c', { y: 2 }]]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `client/src/lib/sse.js`**

```js
/**
 * Read a server-sent-events response body.
 *
 * `EventSource` can only GET, and the board endpoints carry the whole brief in a
 * POST body, so streams arrive over `fetch`. Events are separated by a blank
 * line; whatever trails the last one is a partial frame and waits for the next
 * read (or is flushed when the stream ends).
 */
export async function readEventStream(res, onEvent) {
  const reader = res.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = '';

  const handle = (frame) => {
    const event = /^event:\s*(.+)$/m.exec(frame)?.[1]?.trim();
    const raw = frame
      .split('\n')
      .filter((line) => line.startsWith('data:'))
      .map((line) => line.slice(5).trim())
      .join('\n');
    if (!event || !raw) return;
    let data;
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    onEvent(event, data);
  };

  try {
    for (;;) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
      const frames = buffer.split('\n\n');
      buffer = frames.pop() || '';
      frames.forEach(handle);
    }
    buffer += decoder.decode();
    if (buffer.trim()) handle(buffer);
  } finally {
    reader.cancel().catch(() => {});
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the API tests**

`client/test/boardApi.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { boardApi, generateBoardStream } from '../src/api/board.js';

const enc = new TextEncoder();
const sse = (frames) =>
  new Response(new ReadableStream({ start(c) { c.enqueue(enc.encode(frames)); c.close(); } }), {
    status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream' },
  });
const json = (body, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status });

async function withFetch(stub, fn) {
  const real = globalThis.fetch;
  globalThis.fetch = stub;
  try { return await fn(); } finally { globalThis.fetch = real; }
}

const BOARD = { version: 1, title: 'T', audience: '', sections: [{ id: 's1', title: 'A', blocks: [] }] };

test('the stream reports the outline and each section, then returns the board', async () => {
  const outlines = [];
  const sections = [];
  const frames = 'event: outline\ndata: {"title":"T","sections":[{"id":"s1","title":"A"}]}\n\n'
    + 'event: section\ndata: {"index":0,"section":{"id":"s1"}}\n\n'
    + `event: result\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ board: BOARD })}\n\n`;
  const board = await withFetch(async () => sse(frames), () =>
    generateBoardStream('brief', { onOutline: (o) => outlines.push(o), onSection: (s) => sections.push(s) }));
  assert.deepEqual(board, BOARD);
  assert.equal(outlines[0].title, 'T');
  assert.equal(sections[0].index, 0);
});

test('a failed event becomes a thrown plain-sentence error', async () => {
  await withFetch(async () => sse('event: failed\ndata: {"error":"Apollo couldn\'t finish that board."}\n\n'), async () => {
    await assert.rejects(generateBoardStream('brief'), /couldn't finish/);
  });
});

test('a 4xx from the stream route is a real error, not a fallback', async () => {
  let calls = 0;
  await withFetch(async () => { calls += 1; return json({ error: 'Write at least 40 characters' }, 400); }, async () => {
    await assert.rejects(generateBoardStream('x'), /at least 40/);
  });
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
});

test('a network failure or 5xx falls back to the plain route', async () => {
  const urls = [];
  const stub = async (url) => {
    urls.push(url);
    if (url.includes('/stream')) throw new TypeError('network down');
    return json({ board: BOARD });
  };
  const board = await withFetch(stub, () => generateBoardStream('brief'));
  assert.deepEqual(board, BOARD);
  assert.deepEqual(urls, ['/api/board/generate/stream', '/api/board/generate']);
});

test('a stream that ends without a result falls back to the plain route', async () => {
  const stub = async (url) => (url.includes('/stream') ? sse('event: outline\ndata: {"title":"T","sections":[]}\n\n') : json({ board: BOARD }));
  assert.deepEqual(await withFetch(stub, () => generateBoardStream('brief')), BOARD);
});

test('an abort is not swallowed by the fallback', async () => {
  const abort = Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' });
  await withFetch(async () => { throw abort; }, async () => {
    await assert.rejects(generateBoardStream('brief'), { name: 'AbortError' });
  });
});

test('regenerateSection posts the outline and returns the section', async () => {
  let seen;
  const stub = async (url, init) => { seen = { url, body: JSON.parse(init.body) }; return json({ section: { id: 's2' } }); };
  const section = await withFetch(stub, () => boardApi.regenerateSection({ brief: 'b', outline: { title: 't', sections: [] }, index: 1, note: 'bigger' }));
  assert.equal(section.id, 's2');
  assert.equal(seen.url, '/api/board/section');
  assert.equal(seen.body.note, 'bigger');
});

test('status returns the server answer and errors carry the server sentence', async () => {
  assert.deepEqual(await withFetch(async () => json({ ready: false, model: 'offline' }), () => boardApi.status()), { ready: false, model: 'offline' });
  await withFetch(async () => json({ error: 'Nope, sorry.' }, 503), async () => {
    await assert.rejects(boardApi.status(), /Nope, sorry\./);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `client/src/api/board.js`**

```js
import { readEventStream } from '../lib/sse.js';

/** Jam board API. Thin, like api/client.js — the server owns validation and the wording of errors. */

async function request(path, { method = 'GET', body, signal } = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/board${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {},
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
    signal,
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    let message = `Request failed (${res.status})`;
    try {
      message = (await res.json()).error || message;
    } catch {
      /* keep the generic sentence */
    }
    throw new Error(message);
  }
  return res.json();
}

/**
 * Generate a board, narrating outline and sections as they arrive.
 *
 * If the stream is unavailable for any reason — an old server, a proxy that
 * buffers, a browser without streaming bodies — this falls back to the plain
 * route. A board always gets made; only the live progress is at risk. A 4xx is
 * a real answer ("your brief is too short") and is never retried.
 */
export async function generateBoardStream(brief, { onOutline, onSection, signal } = {}) {
  const plain = () => request('/generate', { method: 'POST', body: { brief }, signal }).then((r) => r.board);

  let res;
  try {
    res = await fetch('/api/board/generate/stream', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'text/event-stream' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ brief }),
      signal,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name === 'AbortError') throw err;
    return plain();
  }

  if (!res.ok) {
    if (res.status !== 404 && res.status >= 400 && res.status < 500) {
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(data.error || `Request failed (${res.status})`);
    }
    return plain();
  }
  if (!res.body) return plain();

  let board = null;
  let failure = null;
  await readEventStream(res, (event, data) => {
    if (event === 'outline') onOutline?.(data);
    else if (event === 'section') onSection?.(data);
    else if (event === 'result') board = data.board;
    else if (event === 'failed') failure = data.error;
  });

  if (failure) throw new Error(failure);
  // A stream that ended without a result was cut short somewhere in transit.
  if (!board) return plain();
  return board;
}

export const boardApi = {
  status: () => request('/status'),
  generate: generateBoardStream,
  regenerateSection: ({ brief, outline, index, note }) =>
    request('/section', { method: 'POST', body: { brief, outline, index, note } }).then((r) => r.section),
};
```

### Task 8: Board state — theme constants, operations, history, records

The heart of the client. Everything that changes a board goes through `applyOperation`; nothing else writes board data.

**Files:**
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/theme.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/state/ids.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/state/styleMap.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/state/annotations.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/state/anchor.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/state/boardState.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/state/history.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js`
- Test: `client/test/state.test.js`, `client/test/history.test.js`, `client/test/boardRecord.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`theme.js`): `WORLD_W=1400`, `CX=700`, `SAFE_W=700`, `PAPER`, `MUTED`, `HAIRLINE`, `STRONG_LINE`, `SOFT`, `TONE_COLORS`, `toneColor(tone)`, `FONT_FAMILIES`, `FONT_URL`, `FONT_PROBES`, `spacingFor(density) → { padTop, padBottom, gap, minHeight }`, `font(overrides) → { family, size, weight, italic, letterSpacing, lineHeight }`.
- Produces (`ids.js`): `newAnnotationId()`, `isAnnotationId(id)`, `blockIdOf(id)`, `sectionIdOf(id)`.
- Produces (`styleMap.js`): `resolveColor(v)`, `styleToFabric(style, kind)` where `kind ∈ 'text'|'sticky'|'line'|'arrow'|'shape'|'path'|'image'`.
- Produces (`annotations.js`): `textRecord`, `stickyRecord`, `shapeRecord`, `arrowRecord`, `arrowGeometry(x1,y1,x2,y2)`. A **record** is `{ id, section, kind, json }`; `json` is Fabric `toObject` JSON whose `top` is **relative to its section's top** and whose `data = { kind: 'annotation', id, ann }`.
- Produces (`anchor.js`): `sectionAt(sections, worldY)`, `toRelative(sections, worldY) → { section, y }`, `toWorldY(sections, sectionId, relY)`. `sections` is `[{ id, y, height }]`.
- Produces (`boardState.js`): `createBoardState({ spec, brief? })`, `applyOperation(state, op) → state`, `INTERNAL_OP_TYPES`, `nextBlockId(section)`, `isViewOp(op)`. State shape: `{ spec, brief, overrides, annotations, connections, assets, view }` where `overrides[id] = { dx, dy, scaleX, scaleY, angle, w, text, style, deleted }`, `connections[i] = { id, from, to, tone, dashed }`, `assets[id] = dataURL`, `view = { zoom, x, y } | null`.
  - Internal ops: `OVERRIDE_SET {id, patch}`, `ANNOTATION_ADD {record, assets?}`, `ANNOTATION_UPDATE {record, assets?}`, `ANNOTATION_REMOVE {ids}`, `ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR`, `OVERRIDES_RESET`, `SECTION_REPLACE {index, section}`, `VIEW_SET {zoom,x,y}`.
  - `ADD_TEXT/ADD_SHAPE/ADD_ARROW` here take **section-relative** `section`, `x`, `y` (the Direct-mode resolver, Task 24, converts `relativeTo`). `ADD_ARROW` without `x1..y2` is a no-op. `MOVE` here requires `dx`/`dy`.
- Produces (`history.js`): `createHistory(limit = 100, now = Date.now) → { record(prev, coalesceKey?), undo(current), redo(current), canUndo, canRedo, clear() }`.
- Produces (`boardRecord.js`): `sanitizeSpec(raw) → spec | null`, `newBoardRecord({ id, spec, brief }) → record`, `recordFromState(state, meta) → record`, `stateFromRecord(record) → state`, `exportBoard(record) → string`, `parseBoardFile(text) → { ok: true, record } | { ok: false, error }`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing state tests**

`client/test/state.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { normalizeBoard } from '../src/components/board/state/schema.js';
import { applyOperation, createBoardState, nextBlockId } from '../src/components/board/state/boardState.js';
import { styleToFabric } from '../src/components/board/state/styleMap.js';
import { sectionAt, toRelative, toWorldY } from '../src/components/board/state/anchor.js';
import { arrowRecord, shapeRecord, stickyRecord, textRecord } from '../src/components/board/state/annotations.js';
import { SAMPLES } from '../../server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const deepFreeze = (o) => {
  if (o && typeof o === 'object' && !Object.isFrozen(o)) {
    Object.freeze(o);
    Object.values(o).forEach(deepFreeze);
  }
  return o;
};

const spec = () => normalizeBoard({
  title: 'T',
  sections: [
    { title: 'One', blocks: [SAMPLES.title, SAMPLES.flow] },
    { title: 'Two', blocks: [SAMPLES.statement, SAMPLES.hub] },
  ],
});
const fresh = () => deepFreeze(createBoardState({ spec: spec(), brief: 'brief' }));

test('createBoardState starts empty and keeps the spec', () => {
  const s = fresh();
  assert.deepEqual(s.overrides, {});
  assert.deepEqual(s.annotations, []);
  assert.deepEqual(s.connections, []);
  assert.deepEqual(s.assets, {});
  assert.equal(s.view, null);
  assert.equal(s.spec.sections.length, 2);
});

test('operations never mutate the state they are given', () => {
  const s = fresh();
  const t = applyOperation(s, { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['s1.b2.n1'], dx: 5, dy: 6 });
  assert.notEqual(t, s);
  assert.deepEqual(s.overrides, {});
});

test('an unknown or malformed operation returns the same state object', () => {
  const s = fresh();
  assert.equal(applyOperation(s, { type: 'NOPE' }), s);
  assert.equal(applyOperation(s, { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['s1.b1'] }), s);
  assert.equal(applyOperation(s, { type: 'FOCUS', targets: ['s1.b1'] }), s);
});

test('SET_TEXT stores an override for a piece and edits an annotation directly', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'SET_TEXT', targets: ['s1.b1.text'], text: 'NEW' });
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b1.text'].text, 'NEW');
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'hi', section: 's1', x: 700, y: 100 });
  const id = s.annotations[0].id;
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'SET_TEXT', targets: [id], text: 'bye' });
  assert.equal(s.annotations[0].json.text, 'bye');
});

test('MOVE accumulates on pieces and shifts annotation coordinates', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['s1.b2'], dx: 10, dy: 20 });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['s1.b2'], dx: 5, dy: -5 });
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b2'].dx, 15);
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b2'].dy, 15);
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'rectangle', section: 's1', x: 300, y: 300 });
  const { id, json } = s.annotations[0];
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'MOVE', targets: [id], dx: 40, dy: 10 });
  assert.equal(s.annotations[0].json.left, json.left + 40);
  assert.equal(s.annotations[0].json.top, json.top + 10);
});

test('RESIZE multiplies scale; arrows ignore it', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'RESIZE', targets: ['s1.b1'], scale: 2 });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'RESIZE', targets: ['s1.b1'], scale: 1.5 });
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b1'].scaleX, 3);
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b1'].scaleY, 3);
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_ARROW', section: 's1', x1: 0, y1: 0, x2: 100, y2: 0 });
  const before = s.annotations[0];
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'RESIZE', targets: [before.id], scale: 3 });
  assert.equal(s.annotations[0], before);
});

test('ROTATE sets the angle', () => {
  const s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'ROTATE', targets: ['s1.b1'], angle: 15 });
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b1'].angle, 15);
});

test('DELETE flags a piece, removes an annotation; RESTORE clears the flag and tidies the override', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'DELETE', targets: ['s1.b1'] });
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b1'].deleted, true);
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'RESTORE', targets: ['s1.b1'] });
  assert.equal('s1.b1' in s.overrides, false);
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'x', section: 's2', x: 1, y: 1 });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'DELETE', targets: [s.annotations[0].id] });
  assert.deepEqual(s.annotations, []);
});

test('SET_STYLE stores semantic style on pieces and Fabric props on annotations', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: ['s1.b1.text'], changes: { fontSize: 110, color: 'problem' } });
  assert.deepEqual(s.overrides['s1.b1.text'].style, { fontSize: 110, color: 'problem' });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: ['s1.b1.text'], changes: { italic: true } });
  assert.deepEqual(s.overrides['s1.b1.text'].style, { fontSize: 110, color: 'problem', italic: true });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'x', section: 's1', x: 1, y: 1 });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: [s.annotations[0].id], changes: { color: 'money', fontSize: 30, italic: true } });
  const j = s.annotations[0].json;
  assert.equal(j.fill, '#0f7a3c');
  assert.equal(j.fontSize, 30);
  assert.equal(j.fontStyle, 'italic');
});

test('INSERT_BLOCK allocates a stable id and never renumbers existing blocks', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's1.b2', patch: { dx: 9 } });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 's1', index: 0, block: SAMPLES.quote });
  const ids = s.spec.sections[0].blocks.map((b) => b.id);
  assert.deepEqual(ids, ['s1.b3', 's1.b1', 's1.b2']);
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b2'].dx, 9);
  assert.equal(nextBlockId(s.spec.sections[0]), 's1.b4');
});

test('INSERT_BLOCK ignores unknown sections and invalid blocks', () => {
  const s = fresh();
  assert.equal(applyOperation(s, { type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 's9', block: SAMPLES.quote }), s);
  assert.equal(applyOperation(s, { type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 's1', block: { type: 'nope' } }), s);
});

test('REPLACE_BLOCK keeps the id and drops only the overrides beneath it', () => {
  let s = fresh();
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's1.b2.n1', patch: { dx: 5 } });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's1.b1.text', patch: { dx: 7 } });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'REPLACE_BLOCK', targets: ['s1.b2'], block: SAMPLES.statement });
  assert.equal(s.spec.sections[0].blocks[1].id, 's1.b2');
  assert.equal(s.spec.sections[0].blocks[1].type, 'statement');
  assert.equal('s1.b2.n1' in s.overrides, false);
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b1.text'].dx, 7);
});

test('SECTION_REPLACE swaps a section, drops its overrides and connections, keeps annotations', () => {
  let s = fresh();
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's2.b1', patch: { dx: 1 } });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's1.b1', patch: { dx: 2 } });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'CONNECT', from: 's2.b1', to: 's1.b1' });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'keep me', section: 's2', x: 1, y: 1 });
  const replacement = { id: 's2', title: 'Two again', beat: '', density: 2, blocks: [{ ...SAMPLES.statement, id: 's2.b1' }] };
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'SECTION_REPLACE', index: 1, section: replacement });
  assert.equal(s.spec.sections[1].title, 'Two again');
  assert.equal('s2.b1' in s.overrides, false);
  assert.equal(s.overrides['s1.b1'].dx, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(s.connections, []);
  assert.equal(s.annotations.length, 1);
});

test('CONNECT deduplicates and DISCONNECT removes', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'CONNECT', from: 'a', to: 'b', tone: 'problem', dashed: true });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'CONNECT', from: 'a', to: 'b' });
  assert.equal(s.connections.length, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(s.connections[0], { id: 'c_a__b', from: 'a', to: 'b', tone: 'problem', dashed: true });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'DISCONNECT', from: 'a', to: 'b' });
  assert.deepEqual(s.connections, []);
});

test('ADD_TEXT, ADD_SHAPE, ADD_ARROW create section-relative annotations', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'THIS IS THE LEAK', section: 's2', x: 700, y: 400, style: { fontSize: 120 } });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'circle', section: 's2', x: 700, y: 900, width: 300, height: 200 });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_ARROW', section: 's2', x1: 100, y1: 100, x2: 400, y2: 100, dashed: true });
  assert.deepEqual(s.annotations.map((a) => a.kind), ['text', 'shape', 'arrow']);
  assert.ok(s.annotations.every((a) => a.section === 's2' && a.id.startsWith('a_')));
  assert.equal(s.annotations[0].json.top, 400);
  assert.equal(s.annotations[0].json.fontSize, 120);
  assert.equal(s.annotations[1].json.type, 'ellipse');
  assert.equal(s.annotations[2].json.dashed, true);
});

test('ADD_ARROW without coordinates is a no-op (Direct mode resolves ids into coordinates first)', () => {
  const s = fresh();
  assert.equal(applyOperation(s, { type: 'ADD_ARROW', from: 'a', to: 'b' }), s);
});

test('internal operations: override set/reset, annotation add/update/remove/clear, view', () => {
  let s = applyOperation(fresh(), { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's1.b1', patch: { dx: 1, w: 500 } });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's1.b1', patch: { dy: 2 } });
  assert.deepEqual(s.overrides['s1.b1'], { dx: 1, w: 500, dy: 2 });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'OVERRIDES_RESET' });
  assert.deepEqual(s.overrides, {});

  const rec = textRecord({ id: 'a_1', section: 's1', x: 10, y: 20, text: 'hi' });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ANNOTATION_ADD', record: rec, assets: { img_1: 'data:image/png;base64,AA' } });
  assert.equal(s.annotations.length, 1);
  assert.equal(s.assets.img_1, 'data:image/png;base64,AA');
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ANNOTATION_UPDATE', record: { ...rec, json: { ...rec.json, left: 99 } } });
  assert.equal(s.annotations[0].json.left, 99);
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ANNOTATION_UPDATE', record: textRecord({ id: 'a_2', section: 's1', x: 0, y: 0, text: 'new' }) });
  assert.equal(s.annotations.length, 2);
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ANNOTATION_REMOVE', ids: ['a_1'] });
  assert.deepEqual(s.annotations.map((a) => a.id), ['a_2']);
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'CONNECT', from: 'x', to: 'y' });
  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR' });
  assert.deepEqual([s.annotations, s.connections, s.assets], [[], [], {}]);

  s = applyOperation(s, { type: 'VIEW_SET', zoom: 1.2, x: -5, y: -100 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.view, { zoom: 1.2, x: -5, y: -100 });
});

test('ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR on an already-empty board returns the same state', () => {
  const s = fresh();
  assert.equal(applyOperation(s, { type: 'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR' }), s);
});

test('styleToFabric maps semantic style by annotation kind', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(styleToFabric({ color: 'problem', italic: true, letterSpacing: 40 }, 'text'), { fill: '#c81e3a', fontStyle: 'italic', charSpacing: 40 });
  assert.deepEqual(styleToFabric({ color: '#123456', strokeWidth: 9, dash: true }, 'arrow'), { color: '#123456', lineWidth: 9, dashed: true });
  assert.deepEqual(styleToFabric({ headEnd: false, headStart: true }, 'arrow'), { headEnd: false, headStart: true });
  assert.deepEqual(styleToFabric({ headEnd: true }, 'shape'), {});
  assert.deepEqual(styleToFabric({ color: 'money', strokeWidth: 3, radius: 12, dash: true }, 'shape'), { stroke: '#0f7a3c', strokeWidth: 3, rx: 12, ry: 12, strokeDashArray: [14, 9] });
  assert.equal(styleToFabric({ fontWeight: 'bold' }).fontWeight, '700');
  assert.deepEqual(styleToFabric({ nonsense: 1 }), {});
});

test('annotation builders produce Fabric-shaped JSON tagged as annotations', () => {
  const t = textRecord({ id: 'a_t', section: 's1', x: 700, y: 50, text: 'T' });
  assert.equal(t.json.type, 'textbox');
  assert.deepEqual(t.json.data, { kind: 'annotation', id: 'a_t', ann: 'text' });
  const st = stickyRecord({ id: 'a_s', section: 's1', x: 10, y: 10 });
  assert.equal(st.json.backgroundColor, '#fff6b7');
  const sh = shapeRecord({ id: 'a_r', section: 's1', shape: 'rectangle', x: 400, y: 300, width: 200, height: 100 });
  assert.equal(sh.json.left, 300);
  assert.equal(sh.json.top, 250);
  const ar = arrowRecord({ id: 'a_a', section: 's1', x1: 500, y1: 100, x2: 100, y2: 300 });
  assert.equal(ar.json.type, 'lineArrow');
  assert.equal(ar.json.left, 100);
  assert.equal(ar.json.top, 100);
  assert.deepEqual([ar.json.sx, ar.json.sy, ar.json.ex, ar.json.ey], [400, 0, 0, 200]);
});

test('anchoring: annotations follow their section when a section changes height', () => {
  const before = [{ id: 's1', y: 0, height: 1000 }, { id: 's2', y: 1000, height: 1000 }, { id: 's3', y: 2000, height: 1000 }];
  const after = [{ id: 's1', y: 0, height: 1600 }, { id: 's2', y: 1600, height: 1000 }, { id: 's3', y: 2600, height: 1000 }];
  const rel = toRelative(before, 2350);
  assert.deepEqual(rel, { section: 's3', y: 350 });
  assert.equal(toWorldY(after, rel.section, rel.y), 2950);
  assert.equal(sectionAt(before, -50).id, 's1');
  assert.equal(sectionAt(before, 99999).id, 's3');
  assert.equal(toWorldY(after, 'missing', 10), 2610);
});
```

`client/test/history.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createHistory } from '../src/components/board/state/history.js';

test('undo and redo walk snapshots in order', () => {
  const h = createHistory();
  h.record('A');
  h.record('B');
  assert.equal(h.canUndo, true);
  assert.equal(h.undo('C'), 'B');
  assert.equal(h.undo('B'), 'A');
  assert.equal(h.undo('A'), null);
  assert.equal(h.canRedo, true);
  assert.equal(h.redo('A'), 'B');
  assert.equal(h.redo('B'), 'C');
  assert.equal(h.redo('C'), null);
});

test('recording a new change clears the redo stack', () => {
  const h = createHistory();
  h.record('A');
  h.undo('B');
  assert.equal(h.canRedo, true);
  h.record('A2');
  assert.equal(h.canRedo, false);
});

test('a burst with the same coalesce key keeps only the first snapshot', () => {
  let t = 0;
  const h = createHistory(100, () => t);
  h.record('S0', 'style:a_1');
  t = 100; h.record('S1', 'style:a_1');
  t = 200; h.record('S2', 'style:a_1');
  assert.equal(h.undo('S3'), 'S0');
  assert.equal(h.canUndo, false);
});

test('coalescing stops after a pause or a different key', () => {
  let t = 0;
  const h = createHistory(100, () => t);
  h.record('S0', 'k');
  t = 2000; h.record('S1', 'k');
  h.record('S2', 'other');
  assert.equal(h.undo('X'), 'S2');
  assert.equal(h.undo('S2'), 'S1');
  assert.equal(h.undo('S1'), 'S0');
});

test('history is capped', () => {
  const h = createHistory(3);
  ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E'].forEach((s) => h.record(s));
  assert.equal(h.undo('F'), 'E');
  assert.equal(h.undo('E'), 'D');
  assert.equal(h.undo('D'), 'C');
  assert.equal(h.undo('C'), null);
});

test('clear empties both stacks', () => {
  const h = createHistory();
  h.record('A');
  h.undo('B');
  h.clear();
  assert.equal(h.canUndo, false);
  assert.equal(h.canRedo, false);
});
```

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { applyOperation, createBoardState } from '../src/components/board/state/boardState.js';
import { exportBoard, newBoardRecord, parseBoardFile, recordFromState, sanitizeSpec, stateFromRecord } from '../src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { SAMPLES } from '../../server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const rawSpec = () => ({
  title: 'T',
  sections: [
    { title: 'One', blocks: [SAMPLES.title, SAMPLES.flow] },
    { title: 'Two', blocks: [SAMPLES.statement] },
  ],
});

test('sanitizeSpec assigns ids, drops invalid blocks and empty sections', () => {
  const spec = sanitizeSpec({ ...rawSpec(), sections: [...rawSpec().sections, { title: 'Bad', blocks: [{ type: 'nope' }] }] });
  assert.deepEqual(spec.sections.map((s) => s.id), ['s1', 's2']);
  assert.deepEqual(spec.sections[0].blocks.map((b) => b.id), ['s1.b1', 's1.b2']);
  assert.equal(sanitizeSpec({ sections: [] }), null);
  assert.equal(sanitizeSpec(null), null);
});

test('sanitizeSpec keeps existing stable ids (a board that had blocks inserted)', () => {
  const spec = sanitizeSpec({
    title: 'T',
    sections: [{ id: 's1', title: 'One', blocks: [{ ...SAMPLES.title, id: 's1.b3' }, { ...SAMPLES.flow, id: 's1.b1' }, { ...SAMPLES.quote }] }],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(spec.sections[0].blocks.map((b) => b.id), ['s1.b3', 's1.b1', 's1.b4']);
});

test('sanitizeSpec repairs duplicate ids', () => {
  const spec = sanitizeSpec({ title: 'T', sections: [{ id: 's1', title: 'One', blocks: [{ ...SAMPLES.title, id: 's1.b1' }, { ...SAMPLES.flow, id: 's1.b1' }] }] });
  assert.deepEqual(spec.sections[0].blocks.map((b) => b.id), ['s1.b1', 's1.b2']);
});

test('a record survives state -> record -> state', () => {
  let state = createBoardState({ spec: sanitizeSpec(rawSpec()), brief: 'the brief' });
  state = applyOperation(state, { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['s1.b1'], dx: 4, dy: 5 });
  state = applyOperation(state, { type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'hi', section: 's2', x: 1, y: 2 });
  state = applyOperation(state, { type: 'VIEW_SET', zoom: 1.5, x: 3, y: 4 });
  const record = recordFromState(state, { id: 'b1', createdAt: 10, updatedAt: 20 });
  assert.equal(record.id, 'b1');
  assert.equal(record.title, 'T');
  const back = stateFromRecord(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(record)));
  assert.deepEqual(back, state);
});

test('exportBoard / parseBoardFile round-trip and validate', () => {
  const rec = newBoardRecord({ id: 'b9', spec: sanitizeSpec(rawSpec()), brief: 'b' });
  const parsed = parseBoardFile(exportBoard(rec));
  assert.equal(parsed.ok, true);
  assert.equal(parsed.record.spec.sections.length, 2);
  assert.equal(parseBoardFile('not json').ok, false);
  assert.equal(parseBoardFile(JSON.stringify({ format: 'something-else' })).ok, false);
  assert.equal(parseBoardFile(JSON.stringify({ format: 'apollo-board', board: { spec: { sections: [] } } })).ok, false);
});

test('parseBoardFile discards malformed overrides, annotations, assets and connections', () => {
  const rec = newBoardRecord({ id: 'b9', spec: sanitizeSpec(rawSpec()), brief: 'b' });
  const dirty = {
    ...rec,
    overrides: { 's1.b1': { dx: 5, evil: '<script>', dy: 'nope' }, junk: 5 },
    annotations: [{ id: 'a_1', section: 's1', kind: 'text', json: { type: 'textbox' } }, { id: 5 }, 'x'],
    assets: { good: 'data:image/png;base64,AAAA', bad: 'http://evil.test/x.png', worse: 7 },
    connections: [{ id: 'c_1', from: 'a', to: 'b', tone: 'ink', dashed: false }, { from: 'a' }],
  };
  const { record } = parseBoardFile(JSON.stringify({ format: 'apollo-board', version: 1, board: dirty }));
  assert.deepEqual(record.overrides, { 's1.b1': { dx: 5 } });
  assert.deepEqual(record.annotations.map((a) => a.id), ['a_1']);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(record.assets), ['good']);
  assert.equal(record.connections.length, 1);
});
```

`client/src/components/board/engine/theme.js`:

```js
/**
 * Jam board — the constants everything else agrees on.
 *
 * The board is a fixed 1400-unit-wide world. Content is centred on x = 700 and
 * text stays inside a ~700-unit safe column so a centred 9:16 crop of a
 * landscape recording still holds the story; wide diagrams are symmetric about
 * the same midline.
 */

export const WORLD_W = 1400;
export const CX = 700;
export const SAFE_W = 700;

export const PAPER = '#ffffff';
export const MUTED = '#6b6b6b';
export const HAIRLINE = '#e5e5e5';
export const STRONG_LINE = '#d4d4d4';
export const SOFT = '#f4f4f2';

/** Tone names come from the model; these are the only colours it ever gets. */
export const TONE_COLORS = {
  ink: '#0a0a0a',
  money: '#0f7a3c',
  problem: '#c81e3a',
  movement: '#1d4ed8',
  tech: '#6b6b6b',
  solution: '#0a0a0a',
};

export const toneColor = (tone) => TONE_COLORS[tone] || TONE_COLORS.ink;

export const FONT_FAMILIES = ['Inter', 'Geist', 'Space Grotesk', 'DM Sans', 'IBM Plex Mono', 'Source Serif 4'];

export const FONT_URL =
  'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:ital,wght@0,400;0,500;0,600;0,700;0,900;1,400;1,600'
  + '&family=Geist:wght@400;600;700;900&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@500;700&family=DM+Sans:wght@400;600;700'
  + '&family=IBM+Plex+Mono:wght@400;600&family=Source+Serif+4:ital,wght@0,600;1,600&display=swap';

/** Canvas text is measured with whatever font is loaded at that instant, so each face is requested explicitly first. */
export const FONT_PROBES = [
  '400 16px Inter', '600 16px Inter', '700 16px Inter', '900 16px Inter', 'italic 600 16px Inter',
  '400 16px Geist', '700 16px Geist', '900 16px Geist',
  '500 16px "Space Grotesk"', '700 16px "Space Grotesk"',
  '400 16px "DM Sans"', '600 16px "DM Sans"', '700 16px "DM Sans"',
  '400 16px "IBM Plex Mono"', '600 16px "IBM Plex Mono"',
  '600 16px "Source Serif 4"', 'italic 600 16px "Source Serif 4"',
];

const PAD_TOP = [280, 240, 200, 160, 120];
const PAD_BOTTOM = [280, 240, 200, 160, 120];
const GAP = [150, 120, 96, 76, 60];

/** Density 1 is airy, 5 is tight — the "simple → chaos → simple" arc lives here and in the block choices. */
export function spacingFor(density) {
  const i = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Math.round(density) || 3)) - 1;
  return { padTop: PAD_TOP[i], padBottom: PAD_BOTTOM[i], gap: GAP[i], minHeight: 1000 };
}

export const font = (overrides = {}) => ({
  family: 'Inter', size: 32, weight: 700, italic: false, letterSpacing: 0, lineHeight: 1.15, ...overrides,
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `state/ids.js`, `state/styleMap.js`, `state/anchor.js`**

`client/src/components/board/state/ids.js`:

```js
/**
 * Ids. Presentation pieces are `s3.b2.n5` (section, block, part) and are
 * deterministic from the spec, so a re-layout never loses a user's change.
 * Annotations are `a_…` and connections `c_…`.
 */

let counter = 0;

export const newAnnotationId = () => `a_${Date.now().toString(36)}${(counter++).toString(36)}`;
export const isAnnotationId = (id) => typeof id === 'string' && id.startsWith('a_');

/** 's3.b2.n5' → 's3.b2'; anything that is not a piece id → null. */
export function blockIdOf(id) {
  const m = /^(s\d+\.b\d+)/.exec(String(id));
  return m ? m[1] : null;
}

export function sectionIdOf(id) {
  const m = /^(s\d+)(?:\.|$)/.exec(String(id));
  return m ? m[1] : null;
}
```

`client/src/components/board/state/styleMap.js`:

```js
import { TONE_COLORS } from '../engine/theme.js';

/**
 * Semantic style (what the property panel and DeepSeek speak: `color: 'problem'`,
 * `italic: true`) → Fabric properties (what the canvas needs: `fill`, `fontStyle`).
 * `kind` decides what "color" means: the fill of text, the stroke of a shape,
 * the ink of an arrow.
 */

export const resolveColor = (v) => TONE_COLORS[v] || v;

export function styleToFabric(style = {}, kind = 'text') {
  const out = {};
  const linear = kind === 'line' || kind === 'arrow';
  const texty = kind === 'text' || kind === 'sticky';

  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(style)) {
    switch (key) {
      case 'fontFamily':
      case 'fontSize':
      case 'lineHeight':
      case 'opacity':
        out[key] = value;
        break;
      case 'fontWeight':
        out.fontWeight = value === 'bold' ? '700' : value === 'normal' ? '400' : String(value);
        break;
      case 'italic':
        out.fontStyle = value ? 'italic' : 'normal';
        break;
      case 'underline':
        out.underline = Boolean(value);
        break;
      case 'align':
        out.textAlign = value;
        break;
      case 'letterSpacing':
        out.charSpacing = value;
        break;
      case 'color': {
        const c = resolveColor(value);
        if (texty) out.fill = c;
        else if (linear) out.color = c;
        else out.stroke = c;
        break;
      }
      case 'fill':
        out.fill = resolveColor(value);
        break;
      case 'stroke':
        if (linear) out.color = resolveColor(value);
        else out.stroke = resolveColor(value);
        break;
      case 'strokeWidth':
        if (linear) out.lineWidth = value;
        else out.strokeWidth = value;
        break;
      case 'radius':
        out.rx = value;
        out.ry = value;
        break;
      case 'dash':
        if (linear) out.dashed = Boolean(value);
        else out.strokeDashArray = value ? [14, 9] : null;
        break;
      case 'headEnd':
      case 'headStart':
        if (linear) out[key] = Boolean(value);
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  }
  return out;
}
```

`client/src/components/board/state/anchor.js`:

```js
/**
 * Annotations and moved pieces are stored relative to the top of the section
 * they belong to, not at a world y. When a section is regenerated and changes
 * height, everything below it must land with its own section, not stay behind
 * at its old absolute position.
 *
 * `sections` is `[{ id, y, height }]`, top to bottom.
 */

/** The section a world y falls in (clamped to the first/last). */
export function sectionAt(sections, y) {
  if (!sections.length) return null;
  for (let i = sections.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
    if (y >= sections[i].y) return sections[i];
  }
  return sections[0];
}

export function toRelative(sections, worldY) {
  const s = sectionAt(sections, worldY);
  return { section: s.id, y: worldY - s.y };
}

/** A missing section (deleted, or a board that shrank) anchors to the last one rather than vanishing. */
export function toWorldY(sections, sectionId, relY) {
  const s = sections.find((x) => x.id === sectionId) || sections[sections.length - 1];
  return s.y + relY;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `client/src/components/board/state/annotations.js`**

```js
import { TONE_COLORS } from '../engine/theme.js';
import { styleToFabric } from './styleMap.js';

/**
 * Pure builders for annotation records — the same objects the drawing tools
 * make by hand, expressed as Fabric `toObject` JSON so Direct mode and the
 * property panel can create them without a canvas.
 *
 * A record is `{ id, section, kind, json }`. `json.top` is relative to the top
 * of its section (see anchor.js). Every json carries
 * `data = { kind: 'annotation', id, ann }` so the canvas can tell it apart from
 * the presentation.
 */

const tag = (id, ann) => ({ kind: 'annotation', id, ann });

export function textRecord({ id, section, x, y, text, style = {}, width = 520 }) {
  return {
    id,
    section,
    kind: 'text',
    json: {
      type: 'textbox', originX: 'center', originY: 'top', left: x, top: y, width, text,
      fontFamily: 'Inter', fontSize: 56, fontWeight: '700', fill: TONE_COLORS.problem, textAlign: 'center', lineHeight: 1.1,
      ...styleToFabric(style, 'text'),
      data: tag(id, 'text'),
    },
  };
}

export function stickyRecord({ id, section, x, y, text = 'Note', style = {} }) {
  return {
    id,
    section,
    kind: 'sticky',
    json: {
      type: 'textbox', originX: 'left', originY: 'top', left: x, top: y, width: 260, text,
      fontFamily: 'DM Sans', fontSize: 26, fontWeight: '400', fill: '#3a3a1a', backgroundColor: '#fff6b7', padding: 16,
      ...styleToFabric(style, 'sticky'),
      data: tag(id, 'sticky'),
    },
  };
}

export function shapeRecord({ id, section, shape, x, y, width = 240, height = 150, style = {} }) {
  const base = {
    originX: 'left', originY: 'top', left: x - width / 2, top: y - height / 2,
    fill: 'rgba(200,30,58,0.06)', stroke: TONE_COLORS.problem, strokeWidth: 5,
    ...styleToFabric(style, 'shape'),
    data: tag(id, 'shape'),
  };
  const json = shape === 'circle'
    ? { type: 'ellipse', rx: width / 2, ry: height / 2, ...base }
    : { type: 'rect', width, height, rx: 10, ry: 10, ...base };
  return { id, section, kind: 'shape', json };
}

/** The box and box-local endpoints of an arrow between two points. Shared with the LineArrow class. */
export function arrowGeometry(x1, y1, x2, y2) {
  const left = Math.min(x1, x2);
  const top = Math.min(y1, y2);
  return {
    left, top,
    width: Math.max(1, Math.abs(x2 - x1)),
    height: Math.max(1, Math.abs(y2 - y1)),
    sx: x1 - left, sy: y1 - top, ex: x2 - left, ey: y2 - top,
  };
}

export function arrowRecord({ id, section, x1, y1, x2, y2, tone = 'problem', dashed = false, head = true, style = {} }) {
  return {
    id,
    section,
    kind: head ? 'arrow' : 'line',
    json: {
      type: 'lineArrow', originX: 'left', originY: 'top', ...arrowGeometry(x1, y1, x2, y2),
      color: TONE_COLORS[tone] || tone, lineWidth: 5, dashed, headEnd: head, headStart: false,
      strokeWidth: 0, fill: null, objectCaching: false, padding: 10,
      ...styleToFabric(style, head ? 'arrow' : 'line'),
      data: tag(id, head ? 'arrow' : 'line'),
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `client/src/components/board/state/boardState.js`**

```js
import { normalizeBlock } from './schema.js';
import { isAnnotationId, newAnnotationId } from './ids.js';
import { styleToFabric } from './styleMap.js';
import { arrowRecord, shapeRecord, textRecord } from './annotations.js';

/**
 * The board's state and the only function that changes it.
 *
 *   spec         what DeepSeek said should exist (sections → blocks)
 *   overrides    what the user changed on the presentation, keyed by piece or
 *                block id: { dx, dy, scaleX, scaleY, angle, w, text, style, deleted }
 *   annotations  what the user drew or added: { id, section, kind, json }
 *   connections  arrows the user (or Direct mode) tied between two pieces
 *   assets       image data by id, so annotations and history snapshots stay small
 *   view         { zoom, x, y } | null
 *
 * State is immutable data. `applyOperation` returns a new object when anything
 * changed and *the same object* when nothing did, so callers can tell.
 * Fabric only ever renders this; a gesture on the canvas becomes an operation
 * here first.
 */

export const INTERNAL_OP_TYPES = [
  'OVERRIDE_SET', 'ANNOTATION_ADD', 'ANNOTATION_UPDATE', 'ANNOTATION_REMOVE',
  'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR', 'OVERRIDES_RESET', 'SECTION_REPLACE', 'VIEW_SET',
];

/** Camera moves are not board edits: they are neither undoable nor worth a history entry. */
export const isViewOp = (op) => op.type === 'VIEW_SET' || op.type === 'SET_VIEW' || op.type === 'FOCUS';

export function createBoardState({ spec, brief = '' }) {
  return { spec, brief, overrides: {}, annotations: [], connections: [], assets: {}, view: null };
}

const targetsOf = (op) => (Array.isArray(op.targets) ? op.targets : op.target ? [op.target] : []);

const setOverride = (state, id, patch) => ({
  ...state,
  overrides: { ...state.overrides, [id]: { ...(state.overrides[id] || {}), ...patch } },
});

function mapAnnotation(state, id, fn) {
  let hit = false;
  const annotations = state.annotations.map((a) => {
    if (a.id !== id) return a;
    hit = true;
    return fn(a);
  });
  return hit ? { ...state, annotations } : state;
}

const withAssets = (state, assets) =>
  assets && Object.keys(assets).length ? { ...state, assets: { ...state.assets, ...assets } } : state;

const sectionIndex = (state, id) => state.spec.sections.findIndex((s) => s.id === id);

const replaceSectionAt = (state, index, section) => ({
  ...state,
  spec: { ...state.spec, sections: state.spec.sections.map((s, i) => (i === index ? section : s)) },
});

/** Block ids are stable once assigned, so a block inserted later takes max + 1, never a reused number. */
export function nextBlockId(section) {
  const max = section.blocks.reduce((m, b) => Math.max(m, Number(b.id.split('.b')[1]) || 0), 0);
  return `${section.id}.b${max + 1}`;
}

/** Forget overrides and connections under an id prefix ('s3.b2' or 's3'): the things they described are gone. */
function dropUnder(state, prefix) {
  const under = (id) => id.startsWith(`${prefix}.`);
  const overrides = {};
  for (const [id, ov] of Object.entries(state.overrides)) if (!under(id)) overrides[id] = ov;
  return {
    ...state,
    overrides,
    connections: state.connections.filter((c) => !under(c.from) && !under(c.to)),
  };
}

function placeOf(state, op) {
  const first = state.spec.sections[0]?.id;
  const section = op.section && sectionIndex(state, op.section) >= 0 ? op.section : first;
  return { section, x: typeof op.x === 'number' ? op.x : 700, y: typeof op.y === 'number' ? op.y : 300 };
}

const addAnnotation = (state, record) => ({ ...state, annotations: [...state.annotations, record] });

export function applyOperation(state, op) {
  switch (op.type) {
    case 'SET_TEXT': {
      const [id] = targetsOf(op);
      if (!id || typeof op.text !== 'string') return state;
      return isAnnotationId(id)
        ? mapAnnotation(state, id, (a) => ({ ...a, json: { ...a.json, text: op.text } }))
        : setOverride(state, id, { text: op.text });
    }

    case 'SET_STYLE':
      return targetsOf(op).reduce((s, id) => (isAnnotationId(id)
        ? mapAnnotation(s, id, (a) => ({ ...a, json: { ...a.json, ...styleToFabric(op.changes, a.kind) } }))
        : setOverride(s, id, { style: { ...(s.overrides[id]?.style || {}), ...op.changes } })), state);

    case 'MOVE': {
      if (typeof op.dx !== 'number' || typeof op.dy !== 'number') return state;
      return targetsOf(op).reduce((s, id) => (isAnnotationId(id)
        ? mapAnnotation(s, id, (a) => ({ ...a, json: { ...a.json, left: a.json.left + op.dx, top: a.json.top + op.dy } }))
        : setOverride(s, id, { dx: (s.overrides[id]?.dx || 0) + op.dx, dy: (s.overrides[id]?.dy || 0) + op.dy })), state);
    }

    case 'RESIZE': {
      const sx = op.scaleX ?? op.scale;
      const sy = op.scaleY ?? op.scale;
      if (typeof sx !== 'number' || typeof sy !== 'number') return state;
      return targetsOf(op).reduce((s, id) => (isAnnotationId(id)
        ? mapAnnotation(s, id, (a) => (a.json.type === 'lineArrow'
          ? a
          : { ...a, json: { ...a.json, scaleX: (a.json.scaleX ?? 1) * sx, scaleY: (a.json.scaleY ?? 1) * sy } }))
        : setOverride(s, id, { scaleX: (s.overrides[id]?.scaleX ?? 1) * sx, scaleY: (s.overrides[id]?.scaleY ?? 1) * sy })), state);
    }

    case 'ROTATE':
      if (typeof op.angle !== 'number') return state;
      return targetsOf(op).reduce((s, id) => (isAnnotationId(id)
        ? mapAnnotation(s, id, (a) => (a.json.type === 'lineArrow' ? a : { ...a, json: { ...a.json, angle: op.angle } }))
        : setOverride(s, id, { angle: op.angle })), state);

    case 'DELETE':
      return targetsOf(op).reduce((s, id) => (isAnnotationId(id)
        ? { ...s, annotations: s.annotations.filter((a) => a.id !== id) }
        : setOverride(s, id, { deleted: true })), state);

    case 'RESTORE':
      return targetsOf(op).reduce((s, id) => {
        const ov = s.overrides[id];
        if (!ov || !ov.deleted) return s;
        const { deleted, ...rest } = ov;
        const overrides = { ...s.overrides };
        if (Object.keys(rest).length) overrides[id] = rest;
        else delete overrides[id];
        return { ...s, overrides };
      }, state);

    case 'INSERT_BLOCK': {
      const si = sectionIndex(state, op.section);
      const block = normalizeBlock(op.block);
      if (si < 0 || !block) return state;
      const section = state.spec.sections[si];
      const blocks = section.blocks.slice();
      const at = Math.min(Math.max(Number.isInteger(op.index) ? op.index : blocks.length, 0), blocks.length);
      blocks.splice(at, 0, { ...block, id: nextBlockId(section) });
      return replaceSectionAt(state, si, { ...section, blocks });
    }

    case 'REPLACE_BLOCK': {
      const [target] = targetsOf(op);
      const block = normalizeBlock(op.block);
      const si = state.spec.sections.findIndex((s) => s.blocks.some((b) => b.id === target));
      if (si < 0 || !block) return state;
      const section = state.spec.sections[si];
      const blocks = section.blocks.map((b) => (b.id === target ? { ...block, id: target } : b));
      return dropUnder(replaceSectionAt(state, si, { ...section, blocks }), target);
    }

    case 'SECTION_REPLACE': {
      const current = state.spec.sections[op.index];
      if (!current || !op.section) return state;
      return dropUnder(replaceSectionAt(state, op.index, { ...op.section, id: current.id }), current.id);
    }

    case 'CONNECT': {
      const id = `c_${op.from}__${op.to}`;
      if (state.connections.some((c) => c.id === id)) return state;
      return {
        ...state,
        connections: [...state.connections, { id, from: op.from, to: op.to, tone: op.tone || 'ink', dashed: Boolean(op.dashed) }],
      };
    }

    case 'DISCONNECT': {
      const connections = state.connections.filter((c) => !(c.from === op.from && c.to === op.to));
      return connections.length === state.connections.length ? state : { ...state, connections };
    }

    case 'ADD_TEXT': {
      if (typeof op.text !== 'string' || !op.text) return state;
      return addAnnotation(state, textRecord({ id: newAnnotationId(), ...placeOf(state, op), text: op.text, style: op.style }));
    }

    case 'ADD_SHAPE':
      if (op.shape !== 'rectangle' && op.shape !== 'circle') return state;
      return addAnnotation(state, shapeRecord({
        id: newAnnotationId(), ...placeOf(state, op), shape: op.shape, width: op.width, height: op.height, style: op.style,
      }));

    case 'ADD_ARROW': {
      if (!['x1', 'y1', 'x2', 'y2'].every((k) => typeof op[k] === 'number')) return state;
      const { section } = placeOf(state, op);
      return addAnnotation(state, arrowRecord({
        id: newAnnotationId(), section, x1: op.x1, y1: op.y1, x2: op.x2, y2: op.y2, tone: op.tone, dashed: op.dashed,
      }));
    }

    case 'OVERRIDE_SET':
      return op.id && op.patch ? setOverride(state, op.id, op.patch) : state;

    case 'OVERRIDES_RESET':
      return Object.keys(state.overrides).length ? { ...state, overrides: {} } : state;

    case 'ANNOTATION_ADD':
      return withAssets({ ...state, annotations: [...state.annotations, op.record] }, op.assets);

    case 'ANNOTATION_UPDATE': {
      const exists = state.annotations.some((a) => a.id === op.record.id);
      const annotations = exists
        ? state.annotations.map((a) => (a.id === op.record.id ? op.record : a))
        : [...state.annotations, op.record];
      return withAssets({ ...state, annotations }, op.assets);
    }

    case 'ANNOTATION_REMOVE':
      return { ...state, annotations: state.annotations.filter((a) => !op.ids.includes(a.id)) };

    case 'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR':
      return state.annotations.length || state.connections.length || Object.keys(state.assets).length
        ? { ...state, annotations: [], connections: [], assets: {} }
        : state;

    case 'VIEW_SET':
    case 'SET_VIEW':
      return { ...state, view: { zoom: op.zoom, x: op.x, y: op.y } };

    default:
      // FOCUS, and anything unknown: not a state change (FOCUS moves the camera in the UI).
      return state;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `client/src/components/board/state/history.js`**

```js
/**
 * Snapshot undo/redo.
 *
 * Board state is immutable, so a snapshot is just a reference — cheap even
 * with a hundred of them, because images live in `assets` and are shared.
 * Bursts (dragging a slider, typing a size) pass the same `coalesceKey` and
 * collapse into the single snapshot taken before the burst began.
 */

const COALESCE_MS = 800;

export function createHistory(limit = 100, now = () => Date.now()) {
  let undoStack = [];
  let redoStack = [];
  let lastKey = null;
  let lastAt = 0;

  return {
    /** Call with the state *before* a change. */
    record(previous, coalesceKey = null) {
      const t = now();
      if (coalesceKey && coalesceKey === lastKey && t - lastAt < COALESCE_MS) {
        lastAt = t;
        return;
      }
      undoStack.push(previous);
      if (undoStack.length > limit) undoStack.shift();
      redoStack = [];
      lastKey = coalesceKey;
      lastAt = t;
    },
    undo(current) {
      const previous = undoStack.pop();
      if (previous === undefined) return null;
      redoStack.push(current);
      lastKey = null;
      return previous;
    },
    redo(current) {
      const next = redoStack.pop();
      if (next === undefined) return null;
      undoStack.push(current);
      lastKey = null;
      return next;
    },
    get canUndo() {
      return undoStack.length > 0;
    },
    get canRedo() {
      return redoStack.length > 0;
    },
    clear() {
      undoStack = [];
      redoStack = [];
      lastKey = null;
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `client/src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js`**

```js
import { LIMITS, normalizeBlock, normalizeSection } from './schema.js';
import { createBoardState, nextBlockId } from './boardState.js';

/**
 * A board as it is stored and as it is exported.
 *
 * Records come from IndexedDB, from a file the user chose, or from the bundled
 * example — the last two are untrusted, so both go through `sanitizeSpec` and
 * the field filters in `parseBoardFile`. Nothing in a record is ever executed:
 * the board draws text onto a canvas, it does not render HTML.
 */

export const BOARD_FILE_FORMAT = 'apollo-board';
const isObj = (v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);
const OVERRIDE_NUMBERS = ['dx', 'dy', 'scaleX', 'scaleY', 'angle', 'w'];

/**
 * A spec from an untrusted source → a clean spec, or null. Unlike
 * `normalizeBoard` this keeps block ids that are already valid and unique, so a
 * board that had blocks inserted keeps its overrides attached to the right ones.
 */
export function sanitizeSpec(raw) {
  if (!isObj(raw) || !Array.isArray(raw.sections)) return null;

  const cleaned = raw.sections.slice(0, LIMITS.sections).map((rs) => {
    const blocks = [];
    for (const rb of Array.isArray(rs?.blocks) ? rs.blocks : []) {
      if (blocks.length >= LIMITS.blocks) break;
      const block = normalizeBlock(rb);
      if (block) blocks.push({ block, wanted: isObj(rb) && typeof rb.id === 'string' ? rb.id : '' });
    }
    return { rs, blocks };
  }).filter((s) => s.blocks.length > 0);
  if (!cleaned.length) return null;

  const givenIds = cleaned.map((s) => s.rs?.id);
  const keepIds = givenIds.every((id) => /^s\d{1,2}$/.test(id)) && new Set(givenIds).size === givenIds.length;

  const sections = cleaned.map(({ rs, blocks }, i) => {
    const meta = normalizeSection({ title: rs.title, beat: rs.beat, density: rs.density, degraded: rs.degraded, blocks: [] }, i).section;
    const id = keepIds ? rs.id : `s${i + 1}`;
    const out = { ...meta, id, blocks: [] };
    const used = new Set();
    const pattern = new RegExp(`^${id}\\.b\\d+$`);
    const slots = blocks.map(({ block, wanted }) => {
      const ok = keepIds && pattern.test(wanted) && !used.has(wanted);
      if (ok) used.add(wanted);
      return { block, id: ok ? wanted : null };
    });
    for (const slot of slots) {
      if (!slot.id) {
        slot.id = nextBlockId({ id, blocks: [...used].map((u) => ({ id: u })) });
        used.add(slot.id);
      }
      out.blocks.push({ ...slot.block, id: slot.id });
    }
    return out;
  });

  return {
    version: 1,
    title: typeof raw.title === 'string' && raw.title.trim() ? raw.title.trim().slice(0, LIMITS.title) : 'Untitled board',
    audience: typeof raw.audience === 'string' ? raw.audience.trim().slice(0, LIMITS.title) : '',
    sections,
  };
}

export function newBoardRecord({ id, spec, brief = '' }) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return { id, title: spec.title, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, brief, spec, overrides: {}, annotations: [], connections: [], assets: {}, view: null };
}

export function recordFromState(state, meta) {
  return {
    id: meta.id,
    title: state.spec.title,
    createdAt: meta.createdAt ?? Date.now(),
    updatedAt: meta.updatedAt ?? Date.now(),
    brief: state.brief,
    spec: state.spec,
    overrides: state.overrides,
    annotations: state.annotations,
    connections: state.connections,
    assets: state.assets,
    view: state.view,
  };
}

export function stateFromRecord(record) {
  return {
    ...createBoardState({ spec: record.spec, brief: record.brief || '' }),
    overrides: record.overrides || {},
    annotations: record.annotations || [],
    connections: record.connections || [],
    assets: record.assets || {},
    view: record.view || null,
  };
}

export function exportBoard(record) {
  return JSON.stringify({ format: BOARD_FILE_FORMAT, version: 1, board: record });
}

function cleanOverrides(raw) {
  const out = {};
  if (!isObj(raw)) return out;
  for (const [id, ov] of Object.entries(raw)) {
    if (!/^s\d+\.b\d+/.test(id) || !isObj(ov)) continue;
    const clean = {};
    for (const key of OVERRIDE_NUMBERS) if (typeof ov[key] === 'number' && Number.isFinite(ov[key])) clean[key] = ov[key];
    if (typeof ov.text === 'string') clean.text = ov.text.slice(0, 2000);
    if (ov.deleted === true) clean.deleted = true;
    if (isObj(ov.style)) clean.style = ov.style;
    if (Object.keys(clean).length) out[id] = clean;
  }
  return out;
}

/** An imported file → a record, or a plain-sentence reason it cannot be used. */
export function parseBoardFile(text) {
  let file;
  try {
    file = JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: "That file isn't a board — it isn't valid JSON." };
  }
  if (!isObj(file) || file.format !== BOARD_FILE_FORMAT || !isObj(file.board)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "That file isn't an Apollo board export." };
  }
  const board = file.board;
  const spec = sanitizeSpec(board.spec);
  if (!spec) return { ok: false, error: 'That board has no sections Apollo can draw.' };

  const assets = {};
  if (isObj(board.assets)) {
    for (const [id, url] of Object.entries(board.assets)) {
      if (typeof url === 'string' && /^data:image\/(png|jpe?g|webp);base64,/.test(url)) assets[id] = url;
    }
  }
  const annotations = (Array.isArray(board.annotations) ? board.annotations : [])
    .filter((a) => isObj(a) && typeof a.id === 'string' && typeof a.section === 'string' && isObj(a.json))
    .slice(0, 2000);
  const connections = (Array.isArray(board.connections) ? board.connections : [])
    .filter((c) => isObj(c) && typeof c.id === 'string' && typeof c.from === 'string' && typeof c.to === 'string')
    .slice(0, 500)
    .map((c) => ({ id: c.id, from: c.from, to: c.to, tone: typeof c.tone === 'string' ? c.tone : 'ink', dashed: c.dashed === true }));

  const now = Date.now();
  return {
    ok: true,
    record: {
      id: typeof board.id === 'string' ? board.id : '',
      title: spec.title,
      createdAt: typeof board.createdAt === 'number' ? board.createdAt : now,
      updatedAt: now,
      brief: typeof board.brief === 'string' ? board.brief.slice(0, 60000) : '',
      spec,
      overrides: cleanOverrides(board.overrides),
      annotations,
      connections,
      assets,
      view: isObj(board.view) && ['zoom', 'x', 'y'].every((k) => typeof board.view[k] === 'number') ? board.view : null,
    },
  };
}
```

### Task 9: Layout engine core — geometry, text blocks, `layoutBoard`

`layoutBoard(spec, measure)` is **pure**: it never imports Fabric. `measure` is injected — the browser passes real Fabric metrics (Task 13), tests pass a stub. That is what makes the layout testable without a browser.

**Files:**
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/geometry.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/common.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/textBlocks.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/index.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/layoutBoard.js`
- Create: `client/test/helpers/stubMeasure.js`
- Create: `client/test/helpers/checkScene.js`
- Test: `client/test/geometry.test.js`, `client/test/layout.invariants.test.js`, `client/test/layout.text.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `theme.js` (Task 8); `SAMPLES` fixture; `sanitizeSpec` (Task 8).
- Produces:
  - **`measure(text, font, maxWidth) → { w, h, lines }`** — `font = { family, size, weight, italic, letterSpacing, lineHeight }`; `w` is the *tight* width of the widest line, `h` the wrapped height, `lines` the line count.
  - **Scene** (returned by `layoutBoard`): `{ width: 1400, height, sections: [{ id, title, index, y, height }], blocks: { [blockId]: { id, section, x, y, w, h } }, items: Item[] }`.
  - **Item** (every item except connectors has `cx, cy, w, h`; all also get `block` and `section` ids from `layoutBoard`):
    - `text`: `{ id, kind:'text', cx, cy, w, h, tight, text, font, color, align, follow? }`
    - `box`: `{ id, kind:'box', cx, cy, w, h, radius, fill, stroke, strokeWidth, dash?, label?, labelFont?, labelColor?, labelAlign, labelWidth, pad, tag?, tagFont?, tagColor?, tagAlign ('bottom-left'|'right'), drop?, follow? }`
    - `poly`: `{ id, kind:'poly', cx, cy, w, h, points:[[x,y]…] (relative to centre), fill, label, labelFont, labelColor, labelWidth }`
    - `rule`: `{ id, kind:'rule', cx, cy, w, h, color, width, dashed? }` — a horizontal (`h = 0`) or vertical (`w = 0`) line centred at `(cx, cy)`.
    - `connector`: `{ id, kind:'connector', from, to, color, width, head, gap, dashed? }` — endpoints are other items' ids, resolved at render time.
  - `geometry.js`: `rectOf(item) → { cx, cy, w, h }`, `edgePoint(rect, tx, ty)`, `connectorPoints(a, b, gap) → { x1, y1, x2, y2, degenerate? }`, `overlaps(a, b)`.
  - `blocks/common.js`: `clamp`, `range(from, to, step)`, `textItem({ id, text, font, color, width, measure, align?, cx? })`, `boxItem(fields)`, `stack(items, gap) → totalHeight`, `fitSize(measure, text, font, width, sizes, maxLines)`.
  - `LAYOUTS` (`blocks/index.js`): `{ [blockType]: (block, { measure, id, density }) => { items, w, h } }` — item `cy` values are **relative to the block's top**; `layoutBoard` shifts them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test helper and the failing tests**

`client/test/helpers/stubMeasure.js`:

```js
/**
 * A deterministic stand-in for Fabric's text metrics: every glyph is a fixed
 * fraction of the font size wide, lines wrap on spaces, an unbreakable word
 * overflows (as it does in a real Textbox). Good enough to prove the layout's
 * geometry; the real thing is checked in the browser.
 */
export function stubMeasure(text, font, maxWidth) {
  const em = font.family === 'IBM Plex Mono' ? 0.6 : 0.58;
  const charW = font.size * em + ((font.letterSpacing || 0) / 1000) * font.size;
  const lines = [];
  for (const paragraph of String(text).split('\n')) {
    let line = '';
    for (const word of paragraph.split(' ')) {
      const trial = line ? `${line} ${word}` : word;
      if (line && trial.length * charW > maxWidth) {
        lines.push(line);
        line = word;
      } else {
        line = trial;
      }
    }
    lines.push(line);
  }
  const widest = Math.max(...lines.map((l) => l.length * charW));
  return { w: widest, h: lines.length * font.size * font.lineHeight, lines: lines.length };
}
```

`client/test/helpers/checkScene.js`:

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

const bounds = (i) => ({ x: i.cx - i.w / 2, y: i.cy - i.h / 2, w: i.w, h: i.h });

/** The layout contract every scene must satisfy — see Task 9. */
export function checkScene(scene) {
  // sections are contiguous and cover the whole height
  let y = 0;
  for (const s of scene.sections) {
    assert.equal(s.y, y, `section ${s.id} should start where the last ended`);
    assert.ok(s.height >= 1000, 'sections have a minimum height');
    y += s.height;
  }
  assert.equal(scene.height, y);

  for (const block of Object.values(scene.blocks)) {
    assert.ok(Math.abs(block.x + block.w / 2 - 700) < 1, `${block.id} is centred on x = 700`);
    const section = scene.sections.find((s) => s.id === block.section);
    assert.ok(block.y >= section.y && block.y + block.h <= section.y + section.height, `${block.id} is inside its section`);
    for (const item of scene.items.filter((i) => i.block === block.id && i.kind !== 'connector')) {
      const b = bounds(item);
      assert.ok(b.x >= block.x - 2 && b.x + b.w <= block.x + block.w + 2, `${item.id} is inside ${block.id} horizontally`);
      assert.ok(b.y >= block.y - 2 && b.y + b.h <= block.y + block.h + 2, `${item.id} is inside ${block.id} vertically`);
    }
  }

  // blocks never overlap each other
  const list = Object.values(scene.blocks);
  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < list.length; j += 1) {
      const a = list[i];
      const b = list[j];
      const apart = a.y + a.h <= b.y + 0.5 || b.y + b.h <= a.y + 0.5;
      assert.ok(apart, `${a.id} and ${b.id} overlap`);
    }
  }

  // legibility: nothing is set below 16
  for (const item of scene.items) {
    if (item.kind === 'text') assert.ok(item.font.size >= 16, `${item.id} font ${item.font.size} is too small`);
    if (item.kind === 'box' && item.label) assert.ok(item.labelFont.size >= 16, `${item.id} label is too small`);
    if (item.kind === 'box' && item.tag) assert.ok(item.tagFont.size >= 16, `${item.id} tag is too small`);
    // every id is unique
  }
  const ids = scene.items.map((i) => i.id);
  assert.equal(new Set(ids).size, ids.length, 'item ids are unique');
}
```

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { connectorPoints, edgePoint, overlaps, rectOf } from '../src/components/board/engine/geometry.js';

const box = (cx, cy, w, h) => ({ cx, cy, w, h });

test('edgePoint leaves a rectangle through the side the target is on', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(edgePoint(box(0, 0, 100, 40), 0, 500), { x: 0, y: 20 });
  assert.deepEqual(edgePoint(box(0, 0, 100, 40), 500, 0), { x: 50, y: 0 });
  assert.deepEqual(edgePoint(box(0, 0, 100, 40), 0, 0), { x: 0, y: 0 });
});

test('connectorPoints joins two stacked boxes edge to edge, pulled back by the gap', () => {
  const p = connectorPoints(box(700, 50, 200, 60), box(700, 250, 200, 60), 10);
  assert.equal(Math.round(p.x1), 700);
  assert.equal(Math.round(p.y1), 90);
  assert.equal(Math.round(p.x2), 700);
  assert.equal(Math.round(p.y2), 210);
});

test('connectorPoints falls back to centre-to-centre when boxes touch', () => {
  const p = connectorPoints(box(0, 0, 100, 100), box(0, 90, 100, 100), 10);
  assert.equal(p.degenerate, true);
});

test('overlaps and rectOf', () => {
  assert.equal(overlaps(box(0, 0, 100, 100), box(60, 0, 100, 100)), true);
  assert.equal(overlaps(box(0, 0, 100, 100), box(101, 0, 100, 100)), false);
  assert.deepEqual(rectOf({ id: 'x', cx: 1, cy: 2, w: 3, h: 4, kind: 'text' }), { cx: 1, cy: 2, w: 3, h: 4 });
});
```
