```js
/**
 * Rectangles and the lines between them. Everything in the scene that takes up
 * space has a centre and a size, so connectors can be worked out from that
 * alone — including after the user has moved, scaled or deleted the ends.
 */

export const rectOf = ({ cx, cy, w, h }) => ({ cx, cy, w, h });

export const overlaps = (a, b) =>
  Math.abs(a.cx - b.cx) * 2 < a.w + b.w && Math.abs(a.cy - b.cy) * 2 < a.h + b.h;

/** Where a ray from the centre of `rect` toward (tx, ty) leaves the rectangle. */
export function edgePoint(rect, tx, ty) {
  const dx = tx - rect.cx;
  const dy = ty - rect.cy;
  if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) return { x: rect.cx, y: rect.cy };
  const sx = dx === 0 ? Infinity : rect.w / 2 / Math.abs(dx);
  const sy = dy === 0 ? Infinity : rect.h / 2 / Math.abs(dy);
  const t = Math.min(sx, sy);
  return { x: rect.cx + dx * t, y: rect.cy + dy * t };
}

/**
 * The two ends of a connector between rectangles `a` and `b`, each pulled back
 * `gap` from its box so an arrowhead never touches the thing it points at.
 * Boxes that touch or overlap get a short centre-to-centre line and
 * `degenerate: true` — better a stub than an arrow that flips backwards.
 */
export function connectorPoints(a, b, gap = 10) {
  const p1 = edgePoint(a, b.cx, b.cy);
  const p2 = edgePoint(b, a.cx, a.cy);
  const dx = p2.x - p1.x;
  const dy = p2.y - p1.y;
  const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  const overlapping = Math.abs(a.cx - b.cx) * 2 < a.w + b.w && Math.abs(a.cy - b.cy) * 2 < a.h + b.h;
  if (overlapping || len <= gap * 2 + 1) return { x1: a.cx, y1: a.cy, x2: b.cx, y2: b.cy, degenerate: true };
  const ux = dx / len;
  const uy = dy / len;
  return { x1: p1.x + ux * gap, y1: p1.y + uy * gap, x2: p2.x - ux * gap, y2: p2.y - uy * gap };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/common.js`**

```js
import { CX, STRONG_LINE } from '../theme.js';

export const clamp = (v, lo, hi) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));

/** range(128, 72, -4) → [128, 124, … 72] */
export function range(from, to, step) {
  const out = [];
  for (let v = from; step < 0 ? v >= to : v <= to; v += step) out.push(v);
  return out;
}

/**
 * A text item. Its width is the width it *wraps at* (the box the renderer's
 * Textbox will have); height comes from measuring that wrap. `cy` is the
 * item's own half-height until `stack` (or the caller) places it.
 */
export function textItem({ id, text, font, color, width, measure, align = 'center', cx = CX }) {
  const m = measure(text, font, width);
  return { id, kind: 'text', cx, cy: m.h / 2, w: width, h: m.h, tight: m.w, text, font, color, align };
}

/** A rounded box, optionally with a label and a tag. `labelWidth` is what the label wraps at. */
export function boxItem(fields) {
  const pad = fields.pad ?? 20;
  return {
    kind: 'box', radius: 14, fill: '#ffffff', stroke: STRONG_LINE, strokeWidth: 2,
    labelAlign: 'center', tagAlign: 'bottom-left', ...fields, pad,
    labelWidth: fields.labelWidth ?? fields.w - 2 * pad,
  };
}

/** Place items top to bottom, `gap` apart. Sets each `cy`; returns the total height. */
export function stack(items, gap = 0) {
  let y = 0;
  items.forEach((item, i) => {
    if (i) y += gap;
    item.cy = y + item.h / 2;
    y += item.h;
  });
  return y;
}

/** The largest size (sizes are descending) whose wrapped text fits in `maxLines` lines. */
export function fitSize(measure, text, font, width, sizes, maxLines) {
  for (const size of sizes) {
    if (measure(text, { ...font, size }, width).lines <= maxLines) return size;
  }
  return sizes[sizes.length - 1];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/textBlocks.js`**

```js
import { CX, HAIRLINE, MUTED, SAFE_W, SOFT, font, toneColor } from '../theme.js';
import { boxItem, clamp, fitSize, range, stack, textItem } from './common.js';

/**
 * title · statement · bigtype · quote · code.
 *
 * Titles and big type are auto-fitted to ≤ 760 wide. The recording is
 * landscape, but a centred 9:16 slice of it becomes the Shorts version, and a
 * headline wider than that slice is a headline cut in half.
 */

const FIT_W = 760;
const TITLE_TARGET = { xl: 128, l: 92, m: 52 };
const TITLE_MIN = { xl: 72, l: 56, m: 36 };

export function layoutTitle(block, { measure, id }) {
  const explicitLines = block.text.split('\n').length;
  const target = TITLE_TARGET[block.size] || 92;
  const min = TITLE_MIN[block.size] || 56;
  const base = font({ weight: 900, lineHeight: 1.02, letterSpacing: -10 });
  const size = fitSize(measure, block.text, base, FIT_W, range(target, min, -4), explicitLines);

  const items = [];
  if (block.eyebrow) {
    items.push(textItem({
      id: `${id}.eyebrow`, text: block.eyebrow.toUpperCase(), width: SAFE_W, measure, color: MUTED,
      font: font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 22, weight: 600, letterSpacing: 300, lineHeight: 1.2 }),
    }));
  }
  items.push(textItem({
    id: `${id}.text`, text: block.text, width: FIT_W, measure, color: toneColor(block.tone), font: { ...base, size },
  }));
  const h = stack(items, 28);
  return { items, w: FIT_W, h };
}

export function layoutStatement(block, { measure, id }) {
  const loud = block.emphasis === 'loud';
  const base = font({ weight: loud ? 900 : 700, lineHeight: 1.12, letterSpacing: loud ? 10 : 0 });
  const sizes = loud ? [56, 52, 48, 44, 40, 36] : [40, 36, 32, 28];
  const size = fitSize(measure, block.text, base, SAFE_W, sizes, loud ? 6 : 5);
  const item = textItem({
    id: `${id}.text`, text: block.text, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: toneColor(block.tone), font: { ...base, size },
  });
  return { items: [item], w: SAFE_W, h: stack([item]) };
}

export function layoutBigtype(block, { measure, id }) {
  const base = font({ family: 'Space Grotesk', weight: 700, lineHeight: 1, letterSpacing: -20 });
  const size = fitSize(measure, block.text, base, FIT_W, range(168, 64, -8), 1);
  const items = [textItem({
    id: `${id}.text`, text: block.text, width: FIT_W, measure, color: toneColor(block.tone), font: { ...base, size },
  })];
  if (block.caption) {
    items.push(textItem({
      id: `${id}.caption`, text: block.caption, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: MUTED,
      font: font({ size: 28, weight: 600, lineHeight: 1.25 }),
    }));
  }
  return { items, w: FIT_W, h: stack(items, 20) };
}

export function layoutQuote(block, { measure, id }) {
  const base = font({ family: 'Source Serif 4', weight: 600, italic: true, lineHeight: 1.25 });
  const size = fitSize(measure, block.text, base, SAFE_W, [42, 38, 34, 30], 4);
  const items = [textItem({ id: `${id}.text`, text: block.text, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: MUTED, font: { ...base, size } })];
  if (block.attribution) {
    items.push(textItem({
      id: `${id}.attribution`, text: block.attribution.toUpperCase(), width: SAFE_W, measure, color: MUTED,
      font: font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 18, weight: 600, letterSpacing: 200, lineHeight: 1.2 }),
    }));
  }
  return { items, w: SAFE_W, h: stack(items, 20) };
}

/** Code is evidence, not the story: small, mono, boxed, and never wider than the safe column. */
export function layoutCode(block, { measure, id }) {
  const f = font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 20, weight: 400, lineHeight: 1.3 });
  const pad = 28;
  const m = measure(block.text, f, SAFE_W - pad * 2);
  const w = clamp(Math.ceil(m.w) + pad * 2, 260, SAFE_W);
  const boxH = Math.ceil(m.h) + 36;
  const items = [boxItem({
    id: `${id}.box`, cx: CX, cy: boxH / 2, w, h: boxH, radius: 10, fill: SOFT, stroke: HAIRLINE,
    label: block.text, labelFont: f, labelColor: MUTED, pad, labelWidth: w - pad * 2,
  })];
  let h = boxH;
  if (block.caption) {
    const caption = textItem({
      id: `${id}.caption`, text: block.caption, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: MUTED,
      font: font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 16, weight: 400, lineHeight: 1.3 }),
    });
    caption.cy = boxH + 14 + caption.h / 2;
    items.push(caption);
    h = boxH + 14 + caption.h;
  }
  return { items, w: block.caption ? SAFE_W : w, h };
}
```

```js
import { layoutBigtype, layoutCode, layoutQuote, layoutStatement, layoutTitle } from './textBlocks.js';

/** One layout function per block type. Each returns { items, w, h } with item cy relative to the block's top. */
export const LAYOUTS = {
  title: layoutTitle,
  statement: layoutStatement,
  bigtype: layoutBigtype,
  quote: layoutQuote,
  code: layoutCode,
};
```

`client/src/components/board/engine/layoutBoard.js`:

```js
import { CX, WORLD_W, spacingFor } from './theme.js';
import { LAYOUTS } from './blocks/index.js';

export { LAYOUTS };

/** Code is a footnote to whatever precedes it, so it sits closer than a full block gap. */
const gapBetween = (previous, block, spacing) => (block.type === 'code' ? Math.round(spacing.gap * 0.55) : spacing.gap);

/**
 * spec → scene. Pure: no Fabric, no DOM, no clock. `measure` supplies text
 * metrics, so the same function runs in the browser with real fonts and in
 * tests with a stub.
 *
 * Sections stack top to bottom. Within a section, blocks stack in the model's
 * order with density-driven whitespace; every block is centred on x = 700.
 */
export function layoutBoard(spec, measure) {
  const items = [];
  const blocks = {};
  const sections = [];
  let y = 0;

  spec.sections.forEach((section, index) => {
    const spacing = spacingFor(section.density);
    const top = y;
    let cursor = top + spacing.padTop;
    let previous = null;

    for (const block of section.blocks) {
      const layout = LAYOUTS[block.type];
      if (!layout) continue;
      const laid = layout(block, { measure, id: block.id, density: section.density });
      if (previous) cursor += gapBetween(previous, block, spacing);

      for (const item of laid.items) {
        if (typeof item.cy === 'number') item.cy += cursor;
        item.block = block.id;
        item.section = section.id;
        items.push(item);
      }
      blocks[block.id] = { id: block.id, section: section.id, x: CX - laid.w / 2, y: cursor, w: laid.w, h: laid.h };
      cursor += laid.h;
      previous = block;
    }

    const height = Math.max(spacing.minHeight, cursor - top + spacing.padBottom);
    sections.push({ id: section.id, title: section.title, index, y: top, height });
    y = top + height;
  });

  return { width: WORLD_W, height: y, sections, blocks, items };
}
```

Item ids: flow steps `${id}.n{k}` (k from 1, the step's index + 1), notes `${id}.n{k}.note` (`follow` = the step), connectors `${id}.c{k}` (from step k to k+1). import test from 'node:test';
```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { layoutBoard } from '../src/components/board/engine/layoutBoard.js';
import { sanitizeSpec } from '../src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { overlaps } from '../src/components/board/engine/geometry.js';
import { stubMeasure } from './helpers/stubMeasure.js';
import { checkScene } from './helpers/checkScene.js';
import { SAMPLES } from '../../server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const sceneOf = (block) =>
  layoutBoard(sanitizeSpec({ title: 'T', sections: [{ title: 'S', density: 3, blocks: [block] }] }), stubMeasure);
const byId = (scene, id) => scene.items.find((i) => i.id === id);
const flow = (over) => ({ type: 'flow', steps: ['A', 'B', 'C'], ...over });

test('a downward flow puts step 1 at the top and connects each step to the next', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ direction: 'down' }));
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.n1').cy < byId(scene, 's1.b1.n2').cy);
  const conns = scene.items.filter((i) => i.kind === 'connector');
  assert.deepEqual(conns.map((c) => [c.from, c.to]), [['s1.b1.n1', 's1.b1.n2'], ['s1.b1.n2', 's1.b1.n3']]);
  assert.ok(conns.every((c) => c.head === true));
});

test('an upward flow puts step 1 at the bottom (a funnel climbing toward the goal)', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ direction: 'up' }));
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.n1').cy > byId(scene, 's1.b1.n3').cy);
  const first = scene.items.find((i) => i.id === 's1.b1.c1');
  assert.deepEqual([first.from, first.to], ['s1.b1.n1', 's1.b1.n2']);
});

test('connectors are drawn behind the steps', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({}));
  const firstStep = scene.items.findIndex((i) => i.kind === 'text');
  const lastConnector = scene.items.map((i) => i.kind).lastIndexOf('connector');
  assert.ok(lastConnector < firstStep);
});

test('a step note sits under its label, follows it, and the connector leaves from the note', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ steps: [{ label: 'A', note: 'the first thing' }, 'B'], direction: 'down' }));
  const label = byId(scene, 's1.b1.n1');
  const note = byId(scene, 's1.b1.n1.note');
  assert.ok(note.cy > label.cy);
  assert.equal(note.follow, label.id);
  assert.equal(scene.items.find((i) => i.kind === 'connector').from, note.id);
});

test('scale changes size; hero > normal > small; normal labels stay at least 22', () => {
  const size = (scale) => byId(sceneOf(flow({ scale })), 's1.b1.n1').font.size;
  assert.ok(size('hero') > size('normal') && size('normal') > size('small'));
  assert.ok(size('normal') >= 22);
  assert.ok(size('small') >= 16);
});

test('per-step tone overrides the block tone', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ tone: 'money', steps: [{ label: 'A', tone: 'problem' }, 'B'] }));
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.n1').color, '#c81e3a');
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.n2').color, '#0f7a3c');
});

test('a rightward flow lays steps in boxes left to right', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ direction: 'right', steps: ['A', 'B', 'C'] }));
  const [a, b, c] = ['n1', 'n2', 'n3'].map((k) => byId(scene, `s1.b1.${k}`));
  assert.ok(a.cx < b.cx && b.cx < c.cx);
  assert.equal(a.cy, b.cy);
  assert.equal(a.kind, 'box');
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('a long rightward flow wraps in a serpentine so each row ends above where the next begins', () => {
  const steps = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E', 'F'];
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ direction: 'right', steps }));
  const n = (k) => byId(scene, `s1.b1.n${k}`);
  assert.ok(n(5).cy > n(4).cy, 'row 2 is below row 1');
  assert.equal(n(5).cx, n(4).cx, 'step 5 sits directly under step 4');
  assert.ok(n(6).cx < n(5).cx, 'row 2 runs right to left');
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('long labels, unbroken words and emoji grow the boxes instead of overflowing them', () => {
  const long = 'A very long label that certainly needs several lines inside a narrow box 💀';
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ direction: 'right', steps: [long, 'B', 'C'] }));
  const a = byId(scene, 's1.b1.n1');
  const textH = stubMeasure(a.label, a.labelFont, a.labelWidth).h;
  assert.ok(a.h >= textH, 'box is at least as tall as its wrapped label');
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.n2').h === a.h, 'boxes in a flow share one height');
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('steps in a column never overlap', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(flow({ steps: ['ONE', 'TWO', 'THREE', 'FOUR', 'FIVE'], scale: 'hero' }));
  const texts = scene.items.filter((i) => i.kind === 'text');
  for (let i = 0; i < texts.length; i += 1) for (let j = i + 1; j < texts.length; j += 1) assert.equal(overlaps(texts[i], texts[j]), false);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('a timeline going right spaces dots evenly and puts when above, label below', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(SAMPLES.timeline);
  const d = (k) => byId(scene, `s1.b1.d${k}`);
  assert.equal(Math.round(d(2).cx - d(1).cx), Math.round(d(3).cx - d(2).cx));
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.w1').cy < d(1).cy);
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.l1').cy > d(1).cy);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('a timeline going down alternates when-left, label-right around the axis', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf({ ...SAMPLES.timeline, direction: 'down' });
  const d1 = byId(scene, 's1.b1.d1');
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.w1').cx < d1.cx);
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.l1').cx > d1.cx);
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.d2').cy > d1.cy);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('an eight-event timeline still fits the board', () => {
  const events = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => ({ when: `${i}:00`, label: `Event number ${i}` }));
  const scene = sceneOf({ type: 'timeline', events });
  checkScene(scene);
  assert.ok(Object.values(scene.blocks)[0].w <= 1300);
});

test('a funnel narrows stage by stage and puts values in the labels', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(SAMPLES.funnel);
  const f = (k) => byId(scene, `s1.b1.f${k}`);
  assert.ok(f(1).w > f(4).w);
  assert.ok(f(1).cy < f(2).cy);
  assert.equal(f(1).kind, 'poly');
  assert.match(f(1).label, /Searches\s+1,000/);
  assert.equal(f(1).points.length, 4);
  checkScene(scene);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/flowBlocks.js`**

```js
import { CX, MUTED, SAFE_W, font, toneColor } from '../theme.js';
import { boxItem, textItem } from './common.js';

/**
 * flow · timeline · funnel — things that happen in order.
 *
 * Connectors are drawn first so they sit behind the steps; they carry the ids
 * of their two ends and are re-routed at render time, so a step the user drags
 * away takes its arrows with it.
 */

const COLUMN = { hero: { size: 64, gap: 100, note: 26 }, normal: { size: 40, gap: 76, note: 22 }, small: { size: 24, gap: 48, note: 16 } };
const NODE = { hero: { size: 34, pad: 24 }, normal: { size: 26, pad: 20 }, small: { size: 18, pad: 14 } };

const connector = (id, from, to, color, over = {}) => ({
  id, kind: 'connector', from, to, color, width: 3, head: true, gap: 12, ...over,
});

function layoutColumn(block, { measure, id }) {
  const cfg = COLUMN[block.scale] || COLUMN.normal;
  const n = block.steps.length;
  // Top-to-bottom drawing order. An upward flow lists its first step first but draws it last.
  const order = block.steps.map((_, i) => i);
  if (block.direction === 'up') order.reverse();

  const items = [];
  const labels = [];
  const lowest = [];
  let y = 0;

  order.forEach((si, pos) => {
    const step = block.steps[si];
    const label = textItem({
      id: `${id}.n${si + 1}`, text: step.label, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: toneColor(step.tone || block.tone),
      font: font({ size: cfg.size, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.1 }),
    });
    label.cy = y + label.h / 2;
    y += label.h;
    items.push(label);
    labels[si] = label;
    lowest[si] = label;

    if (step.note) {
      const note = textItem({
        id: `${id}.n${si + 1}.note`, text: step.note, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: MUTED,
        font: font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: cfg.note, weight: 400, lineHeight: 1.25 }),
      });
      note.follow = label.id;
      note.cy = y + 8 + note.h / 2;
      y += 8 + note.h;
      items.push(note);
      lowest[si] = note;
    }
    if (pos < n - 1) y += cfg.gap;
  });

  const down = block.direction !== 'up';
  const connectors = [];
  for (let si = 0; si < n - 1; si += 1) {
    connectors.push(connector(
      `${id}.c${si + 1}`,
      down ? lowest[si].id : labels[si].id,
      down ? labels[si + 1].id : lowest[si + 1].id,
      toneColor(block.tone)
    ));
  }
  return { items: [...connectors, ...items], w: SAFE_W, h: y };
}

function layoutRow(block, { measure, id }) {
  const cfg = NODE[block.scale] || NODE.normal;
  const n = block.steps.length;
  const perRow = block.scale === 'small' ? 5 : 4;
  const cols = Math.min(perRow, n);
  const gapX = 64;
  const rowGap = 96;
  const nodeW = Math.min(240, Math.floor((1000 - (cols - 1) * gapX) / cols));
  const labelWidth = nodeW - cfg.pad * 2;
  const labelFont = font({ family: 'DM Sans', size: cfg.size, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.15 });
  const nodeH = Math.max(...block.steps.map((s) => Math.ceil(measure(s.label, labelFont, labelWidth).h))) + cfg.pad * 2 + 8;
  const rows = Math.ceil(n / perRow);
  const fullW = cols * nodeW + (cols - 1) * gapX;

  // Serpentine: rows alternate direction, and a new row starts in the slot the
  // previous one ended in, so the last step of a row sits directly above the first of the next.
  let slot = 0;
  let dir = 1;
  const nodes = block.steps.map((step, i) => {
    if (i > 0 && i % perRow === 0) dir = -dir;
    else if (i > 0) slot += dir;
    const color = toneColor(step.tone || block.tone);
    return boxItem({
      id: `${id}.n${i + 1}`, cx: CX - fullW / 2 + nodeW / 2 + slot * (nodeW + gapX),
      cy: Math.floor(i / perRow) * (nodeH + rowGap) + nodeH / 2, w: nodeW, h: nodeH, radius: 18,
      stroke: color, strokeWidth: 3, label: step.label, labelFont, labelColor: color, pad: cfg.pad, labelWidth,
    });
  });
  const connectors = nodes.slice(0, -1).map((node, i) => connector(`${id}.c${i + 1}`, node.id, nodes[i + 1].id, toneColor(block.tone)));
  return { items: [...connectors, ...nodes], w: fullW, h: rows * nodeH + (rows - 1) * rowGap };
}

export function layoutFlow(block, ctx) {
  return block.direction === 'right' ? layoutRow(block, ctx) : layoutColumn(block, ctx);
}

/* -------------------------------- timeline -------------------------------- */

const DOT = 24;

function timelineRight(block, { measure, id }) {
  const n = block.events.length;
  const spacing = Math.min(240, Math.floor(1100 / (n - 1)));
  const span = spacing * (n - 1);
  const colW = Math.max(120, spacing - 24);
  const whenFont = font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 20, weight: 600, letterSpacing: 100, lineHeight: 1.2 });
  const labelFont = font({ family: 'DM Sans', size: 24, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.15 });
  const color = toneColor(block.tone);

  const whens = block.events.map((ev, i) => textItem({
    id: `${id}.w${i + 1}`, text: ev.when || ' ', width: colW, measure, color: MUTED, font: whenFont,
    cx: CX - span / 2 + i * spacing,
  }));
  const labels = block.events.map((ev, i) => textItem({
    id: `${id}.l${i + 1}`, text: ev.label, width: colW, measure, color: toneColor(ev.tone || block.tone), font: labelFont,
    cx: CX - span / 2 + i * spacing,
  }));
  const whenH = Math.max(...whens.map((w) => w.h));
  const dotY = whenH + 28 + DOT / 2;
  const labelTop = dotY + DOT / 2 + 24;

  whens.forEach((w) => { w.cy = whenH - w.h / 2; });
  labels.forEach((l) => { l.cy = labelTop + l.h / 2; });
  const dots = block.events.map((ev, i) => boxItem({
    id: `${id}.d${i + 1}`, cx: CX - span / 2 + i * spacing, cy: dotY, w: DOT, h: DOT, radius: DOT / 2,
    fill: toneColor(ev.tone || block.tone), stroke: toneColor(ev.tone || block.tone), strokeWidth: 0,
  }));
  const connectors = dots.slice(0, -1).map((d, i) => connector(`${id}.c${i + 1}`, d.id, dots[i + 1].id, color, { gap: 6 }));
  const h = labelTop + Math.max(...labels.map((l) => l.h));
  return { items: [...connectors, ...dots, ...whens, ...labels], w: span + colW, h };
}

function timelineDown(block, { measure, id }) {
  const side = 360;
  const offset = 32;
  const rowGap = 56;
  const whenFont = font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 20, weight: 600, letterSpacing: 100, lineHeight: 1.2 });
  const labelFont = font({ family: 'DM Sans', size: 24, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.15 });
  const color = toneColor(block.tone);

  const items = [];
  const dots = [];
  let y = 0;
  block.events.forEach((ev, i) => {
    const when = textItem({
      id: `${id}.w${i + 1}`, text: ev.when || ' ', width: side, measure, color: MUTED, font: whenFont,
      align: 'right', cx: CX - offset - side / 2,
    });
    const label = textItem({
      id: `${id}.l${i + 1}`, text: ev.label, width: side, measure, color: toneColor(ev.tone || block.tone), font: labelFont,
      align: 'left', cx: CX + offset + side / 2,
    });
    const rowH = Math.max(when.h, label.h, DOT);
    when.cy = y + rowH / 2;
    label.cy = y + rowH / 2;
    dots.push(boxItem({
      id: `${id}.d${i + 1}`, cx: CX, cy: y + rowH / 2, w: DOT, h: DOT, radius: DOT / 2,
      fill: toneColor(ev.tone || block.tone), stroke: toneColor(ev.tone || block.tone), strokeWidth: 0,
    }));
    items.push(when, label);
    y += rowH + rowGap;
  });
  const connectors = dots.slice(0, -1).map((d, i) => connector(`${id}.c${i + 1}`, d.id, dots[i + 1].id, color, { gap: 6 }));
  return { items: [...connectors, ...dots, ...items], w: (offset + side) * 2, h: y - rowGap };
}

export function layoutTimeline(block, ctx) {
  return block.direction === 'down' ? timelineDown(block, ctx) : timelineRight(block, ctx);
}

/* --------------------------------- funnel --------------------------------- */

const ALPHAS = ['ff', 'e0', 'c8', 'b0', '98', '84', '70'];

export function layoutFunnel(block, { measure, id }) {
  const n = block.stages.length;
  const topW = 700;
  const bottomW = 260;
  const gap = 10;
  const color = toneColor(block.tone);
  const labelFont = font({ size: 28, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.15 });
  const widthAt = (i) => topW - ((topW - bottomW) * i) / n;

  const items = [];
  let y = 0;
  block.stages.forEach((stage, i) => {
    const wTop = widthAt(i);
    const wBottom = widthAt(i + 1);
    const label = stage.value ? `${stage.label}  ${stage.value}` : stage.label;
    const labelWidth = Math.floor(wBottom - 48);
    const h = Math.max(104, Math.ceil(measure(label, labelFont, labelWidth).h) + 36);
    items.push({
      id: `${id}.f${i + 1}`, kind: 'poly', cx: CX, cy: y + h / 2, w: wTop, h,
      points: [[-wTop / 2, -h / 2], [wTop / 2, -h / 2], [wBottom / 2, h / 2], [-wBottom / 2, h / 2]],
      fill: `${color}${ALPHAS[Math.min(i, ALPHAS.length - 1)]}`, label, labelFont, labelColor: '#ffffff', labelWidth,
    });
    y += h + gap;
  });
  return { items, w: topW, h: y - gap };
}
```

Register the three layouts:

`client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/index.js`:

```js
import { layoutFlow, layoutFunnel, layoutTimeline } from './flowBlocks.js';
import { layoutBigtype, layoutCode, layoutQuote, layoutStatement, layoutTitle } from './textBlocks.js';

/** One layout function per block type. Each returns { items, w, h } with item cy relative to the block's top. */
export const LAYOUTS = {
  title: layoutTitle,
  statement: layoutStatement,
  bigtype: layoutBigtype,
  quote: layoutQuote,
  code: layoutCode,
  flow: layoutFlow,
  timeline: layoutTimeline,
  funnel: layoutFunnel,
};
```

### Task 11: Layout — hub, cards, compare, panels, browser

**Files:**
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/diagramBlocks.js`
- Modify: `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/index.js` (register `hub`, `cards`, `compare`, `panels`, `browser`)
- Test: `client/test/layout.diagrams.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `common.js`, `theme.js`, `flowBlocks.js` conventions.
- Produces: `layoutHub`, `layoutCards`, `layoutCompare`, `layoutPanels`, `layoutBrowser`. Item ids: hub centre `${id}.center`, nodes `${id}.n{k}`, spokes `${id}.s{k}`, cross-links `${id}.x{k}`; cards `${id}.n{k}`; compare `${id}.l.head|n{k}|c{k}|foot`, `${id}.r.…`, divider `${id}.divider`; panels `${id}.p{k}` (box), `${id}.p{k}.head`, `${id}.p{k}.r{j}`; browser `${id}.frame|bar|dot{1..3}|url|body|title|note`. Items that must travel with a leader carry `follow: <leaderId>`. The browser body placeholder has `drop: true`.
- After this task `Object.keys(LAYOUTS)` equals `BLOCK_TYPES` (the invariants suite then covers all 13 types).

`client/test/layout.diagrams.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { BLOCK_TYPES } from '../src/components/board/state/schema.js';
import { LAYOUTS, layoutBoard } from '../src/components/board/engine/layoutBoard.js';
import { sanitizeSpec } from '../src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { overlaps } from '../src/components/board/engine/geometry.js';
import { stubMeasure } from './helpers/stubMeasure.js';
import { checkScene } from './helpers/checkScene.js';
import { SAMPLES } from '../../server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const sceneOf = (block, density = 3) =>
  layoutBoard(sanitizeSpec({ title: 'T', sections: [{ title: 'S', density, blocks: [block] }] }), stubMeasure);
const byId = (scene, id) => scene.items.find((i) => i.id === id);
const kind = (scene, k) => scene.items.filter((i) => i.kind === k);

test('every block type in the vocabulary has a layout', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(LAYOUTS).sort(), [...BLOCK_TYPES].sort());
});

test('hub: a centre, a node per label, a spoke to each, all symmetric about x = 700', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf({ ...SAMPLES.hub, links: 'spokes' });
  assert.equal(kind(scene, 'box').length, 11);
  const spokes = scene.items.filter((i) => i.id.includes('.s'));
  assert.equal(spokes.length, 10);
  assert.ok(spokes.every((s) => s.from === 's1.b1.center' && s.head === false));
  const nodes = scene.items.filter((i) => /\.n\d+$/.test(i.id));
  const mean = nodes.reduce((s, n) => s + n.cx, 0) / nodes.length;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(mean - 700) < 1, 'nodes are balanced around the midline');
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('hub: nodes never overlap each other or the centre, even with twelve long labels', () => {
  const nodes = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => ({ label: `Integration number ${i + 1}` }));
  const scene = sceneOf({ type: 'hub', center: 'BUSINESS', nodes });
  const boxes = kind(scene, 'box');
  for (let i = 0; i < boxes.length; i += 1) for (let j = i + 1; j < boxes.length; j += 1) assert.equal(overlaps(boxes[i], boxes[j]), false, `${boxes[i].id} overlaps ${boxes[j].id}`);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('hub: spokes+cross adds distinct cross-links, more at high density, none twice', () => {
  const plain = sceneOf({ ...SAMPLES.hub, links: 'spokes' });
  const cross = sceneOf(SAMPLES.hub, 3);
  const dense = sceneOf(SAMPLES.hub, 5);
  const x = (s) => s.items.filter((i) => /\.x\d+$/.test(i.id));
  assert.equal(x(plain).length, 0);
  assert.equal(x(cross).length, 10);
  assert.ok(x(dense).length > x(cross).length);
  const pairs = x(dense).map((c) => [c.from, c.to].sort().join('|'));
  assert.equal(new Set(pairs).size, pairs.length);
});

test('hub: cross-links are behind spokes, spokes behind boxes', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(SAMPLES.hub);
  const order = scene.items.map((i) => (i.id.includes('.x') ? 0 : i.id.includes('.s') ? 1 : 2));
  assert.deepEqual(order, [...order].sort((a, b) => a - b));
});

test('cards: a grid of N columns, last row centred, tags and tone carried through', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf({ type: 'cards', columns: 3, tone: 'problem', items: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'].map((label) => ({ label, tag: 'LEAK' })) });
  const c = (k) => byId(scene, `s1.b1.n${k}`);
  assert.equal(c(1).cy, c(2).cy);
  assert.ok(c(4).cy > c(1).cy);
  assert.equal(c(4).cx, 700, 'a lone card on the last row is centred');
  assert.equal(c(1).tag, 'LEAK');
  assert.equal(c(1).tagColor, '#c81e3a');
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('cards: a long unbroken word widens the card rather than spilling out', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf({ type: 'cards', columns: 4, items: [{ label: 'Internationalization' }, { label: 'b' }, { label: 'c' }, { label: 'd' }] });
  const card = byId(scene, 's1.b1.n1');
  assert.ok(stubMeasure('Internationalization', card.labelFont, 9999).w <= card.labelWidth + 1);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('compare: two columns mirrored about the midline with a divider between them', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(SAMPLES.compare);
  const left = byId(scene, 's1.b1.l.head');
  const right = byId(scene, 's1.b1.r.head');
  assert.equal(left.cy, right.cy);
  assert.equal(700 - left.cx, right.cx - 700);
  const divider = byId(scene, 's1.b1.divider');
  assert.equal(divider.kind, 'rule');
  assert.equal(divider.cx, 700);
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.r.foot'));
  assert.equal(left.color, '#6b6b6b');
  assert.equal(right.color, '#0f7a3c');
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('compare: sides of unequal length are top aligned and the divider spans the taller one', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(SAMPLES.compare);
  const block = Object.values(scene.blocks)[0];
  const divider = byId(scene, 's1.b1.divider');
  assert.ok(divider.h >= block.h - 1);
});

test('panels: rows follow their panel; locked rows read locked', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(SAMPLES.panels);
  const p1 = byId(scene, 's1.b1.p1');
  const p2 = byId(scene, 's1.b1.p2');
  assert.equal(p1.h, p2.h, 'panels share a height');
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.p1.r1').follow, p1.id);
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.p1.head').follow, p1.id);
  const locked = byId(scene, 's1.b1.p2.r1');
  assert.equal(locked.tag, 'LOCKED');
  assert.equal(locked.tagAlign, 'right');
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.p1.r1').tag, 'EDITABLE');
  assert.equal(700 - p1.cx, p2.cx - 700);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('panels: three panels still fit and stay symmetric', () => {
  const panel = (heading) => ({ heading, tone: 'ink', rows: [{ label: 'One' }, { label: 'Two' }] });
  const scene = sceneOf({ type: 'panels', panels: [panel('A'), panel('B'), panel('C')] });
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.p2').cx, 700);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('browser: a frame with chrome that follows it, and a drop target for a screenshot', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf(SAMPLES.browser);
  const frame = byId(scene, 's1.b1.frame');
  for (const k of ['bar', 'dot1', 'dot2', 'dot3', 'url', 'body']) assert.equal(byId(scene, `s1.b1.${k}`).follow, frame.id, `${k} follows the frame`);
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.body').drop, true);
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.url').label, 'example.com');
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.title').text, 'Your homepage');
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('browser: a blank placeholder has no drop target, and note text sits below the frame', () => {
  const scene = sceneOf({ type: 'browser', placeholder: 'blank', note: 'iframe · external scripts' });
  assert.equal(byId(scene, 's1.b1.body'), undefined);
  assert.ok(byId(scene, 's1.b1.note').cy > byId(scene, 's1.b1.frame').cy);
  checkScene(scene);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `client/src/components/board/engine/blocks/diagramBlocks.js`**

```js
import { CX, HAIRLINE, MUTED, SAFE_W, SOFT, STRONG_LINE, font, toneColor } from '../theme.js';
import { boxItem, clamp, textItem } from './common.js';

/**
 * hub · cards · compare · panels · browser — things that sit side by side.
 * These are the wide diagrams: they may run past the 700 text column, but
 * every one is built symmetrically about x = 700 so a centred crop still
 * catches its spine.
 */

const connector = (id, from, to, color, over = {}) => ({
  id, kind: 'connector', from, to, color, width: 2, head: false, gap: 0, ...over,
});

/* ---------------------------------- hub ---------------------------------- */

export function layoutHub(block, { measure, id, density }) {
  const n = block.nodes.length;
  const R = clamp(170 + n * 22, 300, 470);
  const rx = Math.min(Math.round(R * 1.3), 540);
  const nodeFont = font({ family: 'DM Sans', size: 22, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.15 });
  const centerFont = font({ family: 'Space Grotesk', size: 30, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.1 });

  const sizes = block.nodes.map((node) => {
    const m = measure(node.label, nodeFont, 190);
    return { w: clamp(Math.ceil(m.w) + 40, 130, 230), h: Math.ceil(m.h) + 30 };
  });
  const maxW = Math.max(...sizes.map((s) => s.w));
  const maxH = Math.max(...sizes.map((s) => s.h));
  const cy0 = R + maxH / 2;

  const cm = measure(block.center, centerFont, 260);
  const cw = clamp(Math.ceil(cm.w) + 64, 200, 324);
  const centerColor = toneColor(block.tone);
  const center = boxItem({
    id: `${id}.center`, cx: CX, cy: cy0, w: cw, h: Math.ceil(cm.h) + 44, radius: 20, fill: centerColor, stroke: centerColor,
    strokeWidth: 0, label: block.center, labelFont: centerFont, labelColor: '#ffffff', pad: 32,
  });

  const nodes = block.nodes.map((node, i) => {
    const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + (2 * Math.PI * i) / n;
    return boxItem({
      id: `${id}.n${i + 1}`, cx: CX + rx * Math.cos(angle), cy: cy0 + R * Math.sin(angle), w: sizes[i].w, h: sizes[i].h,
      stroke: '#0a0a0a', label: node.label, labelFont: nodeFont, labelColor: '#0a0a0a', pad: 20,
    });
  });

  const spokes = nodes.map((node, i) => connector(`${id}.s${i + 1}`, center.id, node.id, '#c7c7c7'));

  const cross = [];
  if (block.links === 'spokes+cross') {
    const hops = density >= 4 && n >= 6 ? [3, Math.floor(n / 2)] : [3];
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const hop of hops) {
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {
        const j = (i + hop) % n;
        const key = [i, j].sort((a, b) => a - b).join('-');
        if (j === i || seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);
        cross.push(connector(`${id}.x${cross.length + 1}`, nodes[i].id, nodes[j].id, '#d9d9d9', { width: 1 }));
      }
    }
  }
  return { items: [...cross, ...spokes, ...nodes, center], w: 2 * rx + maxW, h: 2 * R + maxH };
}

/* --------------------------------- cards --------------------------------- */

export function layoutCards(block, { measure, id }) {
  const count = block.items.length;
  const cols = Math.min(block.columns, count);
  const gap = 24;
  const pad = 20;
  const labelFont = font({ family: 'DM Sans', size: 24, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.2 });
  const tagFont = font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 16, weight: 600, letterSpacing: 120, lineHeight: 1.2 });

  // A card is at least as wide as its longest single word: a Textbox wraps
  // between words, never inside one.
  const longestWord = Math.max(...block.items.flatMap((it) => it.label.split(/s+/).map((w) => measure(w, labelFont, 10000).w)));
  const cardW = Math.max(Math.min(260, Math.floor((900 - (cols - 1) * gap) / cols)), Math.ceil(longestWord) + pad * 2);

  const anyTag = block.items.some((it) => it.tag);
  const labelH = Math.max(...block.items.map((it) => Math.ceil(measure(it.label, labelFont, cardW - pad * 2).h)));
  const cardH = Math.max(anyTag ? 128 : 92, labelH + pad * 2 + (anyTag ? 30 : 0));
  const rows = Math.ceil(count / cols);

  const items = block.items.map((it, i) => {
    const r = Math.floor(i / cols);
    const k = i % cols;
    const inRow = Math.min(cols, count - r * cols);
    const rowW = inRow * cardW + (inRow - 1) * gap;
    return boxItem({
      id: `${id}.n${i + 1}`, cx: CX - rowW / 2 + cardW / 2 + k * (cardW + gap), cy: r * (cardH + gap) + cardH / 2,
      w: cardW, h: cardH, radius: 16, label: it.label, labelFont, labelColor: '#0a0a0a', labelAlign: 'left', pad,
      tag: it.tag || '', tagFont, tagColor: toneColor(it.tone || block.tone), tagAlign: 'bottom-left',
    });
  });
  return { items, w: cols * cardW + (cols - 1) * gap, h: rows * cardH + (rows - 1) * gap };
}

/* -------------------------------- compare -------------------------------- */

export function layoutCompare(block, { measure, id }) {
  const colW = 380;
  const gapMid = 140;
  const offset = gapMid / 2 + colW / 2;
  const headFont = font({ size: 28, weight: 900, letterSpacing: 60, lineHeight: 1.1 });
  const stepFont = font({ size: 28, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.1 });
  const footFont = font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 16, weight: 400, lineHeight: 1.3 });

  const build = (side, key, cx) => {
    const color = toneColor(side.tone);
    const items = [];
    let y = 0;
    const head = textItem({ id: `${id}.${key}.head`, text: side.heading || ' ', width: colW, measure, color, font: headFont, cx });
    head.cy = y + head.h / 2;
    y += head.h + 44;
    items.push(head);

    const steps = side.steps.map((step, i) => {
      const t = textItem({ id: `${id}.${key}.n${i + 1}`, text: step.label, width: colW, measure, color: toneColor(step.tone || side.tone), font: stepFont, cx });
      t.cy = y + t.h / 2;
      y += t.h + (i < side.steps.length - 1 ? 64 : 0);
      return t;
    });
    items.push(...steps);
    const connectors = steps.slice(0, -1).map((t, i) => connector(`${id}.${key}.c${i + 1}`, t.id, steps[i + 1].id, color, { width: 3, head: true, gap: 10 }));

    if (side.footnote) {
      const foot = textItem({ id: `${id}.${key}.foot`, text: side.footnote, width: colW, measure, color: MUTED, font: footFont, cx });
      foot.cy = y + 28 + foot.h / 2;
      y += 28 + foot.h;
      items.push(foot);
    }
    return { items, connectors, h: y };
  };

  const left = build(block.left, 'l', CX - offset);
  const right = build(block.right, 'r', CX + offset);
  const h = Math.max(left.h, right.h);
  const divider = { id: `${id}.divider`, kind: 'rule', cx: CX, cy: h / 2, w: 0, h, color: HAIRLINE, width: 2 };
  return {
    items: [divider, ...left.connectors, ...right.connectors, ...left.items, ...right.items],
    w: colW * 2 + gapMid,
    h,
  };
}

/* --------------------------------- panels --------------------------------- */

export function layoutPanels(block, { measure, id }) {
  const count = block.panels.length;
  const pw = count === 1 ? 460 : count === 2 ? 400 : 300;
  const gap = 40;
  const pad = 26;
  const innerW = pw - pad * 2;
  const headFont = font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 22, weight: 600, letterSpacing: 200, lineHeight: 1.2 });
  const rowFont = font({ family: 'DM Sans', size: 24, weight: 600, lineHeight: 1.2 });
  const tagFont = font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 16, weight: 600, letterSpacing: 100, lineHeight: 1.2 });
  const rowLabelW = innerW - 32 - 120; // room for the tag on the right

  const built = block.panels.map((panel) => {
    const head = measure(panel.heading || ' ', headFont, innerW);
    const rows = panel.rows.map((row) => ({ row, h: Math.max(64, Math.ceil(measure(row.label, rowFont, rowLabelW).h) + 28) }));
    const rowsH = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.h, 0) + (rows.length - 1) * 14;
    return { panel, head, rows, h: pad + Math.ceil(head.h) + 22 + rowsH + pad };
  });
  const panelH = Math.max(...built.map((b) => b.h));
  const total = count * pw + (count - 1) * gap;

  const items = [];
  built.forEach(({ panel, head, rows }, k) => {
    const cx = CX - total / 2 + pw / 2 + k * (pw + gap);
    const color = toneColor(panel.tone);
    const frame = boxItem({ id: `${id}.p${k + 1}`, cx, cy: panelH / 2, w: pw, h: panelH, radius: 22, stroke: color, strokeWidth: 3 });
    const heading = textItem({
      id: `${id}.p${k + 1}.head`, text: panel.heading || ' ', width: innerW, measure, color, font: headFont, align: 'left', cx,
    });
    heading.cy = pad + head.h / 2;
    heading.follow = frame.id;
    items.push(frame, heading);

    let y = pad + Math.ceil(head.h) + 22;
    rows.forEach(({ row, h }, j) => {
      const locked = row.state === 'locked';
      const rowColor = locked ? MUTED : color;
      const box = boxItem({
        id: `${id}.p${k + 1}.r${j + 1}`, cx, cy: y + h / 2, w: innerW, h, radius: 12,
        fill: locked ? '#efefef' : '#ffffff', stroke: locked ? STRONG_LINE : color, strokeWidth: 2,
        label: row.label, labelFont: rowFont, labelColor: locked ? MUTED : '#0a0a0a', labelAlign: 'left', pad: 16,
        labelWidth: rowLabelW, tag: locked ? 'LOCKED' : 'EDITABLE', tagFont, tagColor: rowColor, tagAlign: 'right',
      });
      box.follow = frame.id;
      items.push(box);
      y += h + 14;
    });
  });
  return { items, w: total, h: panelH };
}

/* --------------------------------- browser -------------------------------- */

export function layoutBrowser(block, { measure, id }) {
  const W = 760;
  const barH = 64;
  const bodyH = 420;
  const H = barH + bodyH;
  const items = [];
  let top = 0;

  if (block.title) {
    const title = textItem({ id: `${id}.title`, text: block.title, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: '#0a0a0a', font: font({ size: 30, weight: 700, lineHeight: 1.15 }) });
    title.cy = title.h / 2;
    items.push(title);
    top = title.h + 24;
  }

  const frame = boxItem({ id: `${id}.frame`, cx: CX, cy: top + H / 2, w: W, h: H, radius: 20, stroke: STRONG_LINE, strokeWidth: 3 });
  const follower = (item) => ({ ...item, follow: frame.id });
  items.push(frame);
  items.push(follower({ id: `${id}.bar`, kind: 'rule', cx: CX, cy: top + barH, w: W, h: 0, color: HAIRLINE, width: 2 }));
  for (let d = 0; d < 3; d += 1) {
    items.push(follower(boxItem({
      id: `${id}.dot${d + 1}`, cx: CX - W / 2 + 34 + d * 26, cy: top + barH / 2, w: 14, h: 14, radius: 7,
      fill: STRONG_LINE, stroke: STRONG_LINE, strokeWidth: 0,
    })));
  }
  const pillW = W - 150;
  items.push(follower(boxItem({
    id: `${id}.url`, cx: CX - W / 2 + 120 + pillW / 2 - 6, cy: top + barH / 2, w: pillW - 12, h: 36, radius: 18,
    fill: SOFT, stroke: SOFT, strokeWidth: 0, label: block.url || '', labelFont: font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 18, weight: 400, lineHeight: 1.2 }),
    labelColor: MUTED, pad: 18,
  })));
  if (block.placeholder === 'screenshot') {
    items.push(follower({
      ...boxItem({
        id: `${id}.body`, cx: CX, cy: top + barH + bodyH / 2, w: W - 48, h: bodyH - 48, radius: 12, fill: '#fafafa',
        stroke: STRONG_LINE, strokeWidth: 2, label: 'Drop a screenshot here', labelFont: font({ size: 24, weight: 600, lineHeight: 1.2 }),
        labelColor: MUTED, pad: 24,
      }),
      dash: [10, 8],
      drop: true,
    }));
  }

  let h = top + H;
  if (block.note) {
    const note = textItem({
      id: `${id}.note`, text: block.note, width: SAFE_W, measure, color: MUTED,
      font: font({ family: 'IBM Plex Mono', size: 18, weight: 400, lineHeight: 1.3 }),
    });
    note.cy = h + 20 + note.h / 2;
    h += 20 + note.h;
    items.push(note);
  }
  return { items, w: W, h };
}
```

```js
import { layoutBrowser, layoutCards, layoutCompare, layoutHub, layoutPanels } from './diagramBlocks.js';
import { layoutFlow, layoutFunnel, layoutTimeline } from './flowBlocks.js';
import { layoutBigtype, layoutCode, layoutQuote, layoutStatement, layoutTitle } from './textBlocks.js';

/** One layout function per block type. Each returns { items, w, h } with item cy relative to the block's top. */
export const LAYOUTS = {
  title: layoutTitle,
  statement: layoutStatement,
  bigtype: layoutBigtype,
  quote: layoutQuote,
  code: layoutCode,
  flow: layoutFlow,
  timeline: layoutTimeline,
  funnel: layoutFunnel,
  hub: layoutHub,
  cards: layoutCards,
  compare: layoutCompare,
  panels: layoutPanels,
  browser: layoutBrowser,
};
```

`server/test/board.example.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { HEADLINE_TYPES, normalizeBoard, normalizeSection } from '../src/design/board/schema.js';

const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('../../client/src/components/board/example/website-is-losing-you-sales.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));

test('the example board is twelve sections and loses nothing in normalization', () => {
  assert.equal(raw.sections.length, 12);
  raw.sections.forEach((section, i) => {
    const { section: clean, issues } = normalizeSection(section, i);
    assert.deepEqual(issues, [], `section ${i + 1} (${section.title}) has issues`);
    assert.equal(clean.blocks.length, section.blocks.length, `section ${i + 1} dropped a block`);
    assert.ok(clean.blocks.some((b) => HEADLINE_TYPES.includes(b.type)), `section ${i + 1} has a headline`);
  });
  assert.equal(normalizeBoard(raw).sections.length, 12);
});
```

`client/test/example.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { layoutBoard } from '../src/components/board/engine/layoutBoard.js';
import { sanitizeSpec } from '../src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { stubMeasure } from './helpers/stubMeasure.js';
import { checkScene } from './helpers/checkScene.js';

const spec = sanitizeSpec(JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('../src/components/board/example/website-is-losing-you-sales.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8')));
const scene = layoutBoard(spec, stubMeasure);

test('the example lays out cleanly: contiguous, centred, inside their sections, legible', () => {
  assert.equal(scene.sections.length, 12);
  checkScene(scene);
});

test('it keeps the brief\'s labels verbatim', () => {
  const texts = scene.items.map((i) => i.text || i.label || '').join('|');
  for (const s of ['$1,500 JOB', 'AD / ORGANIC RESULT', 'LOADING...', 'CUSTOMER LEAVES', '8:47 PM', 'FIELD-LOCKED CMS', 'THE RIDICULOUS STACK']) {
    assert.ok(texts.includes(s), `missing "${s}"`);
  }
});

test('the density arc is simple, then complex, then chaos, then simplified', () => {
  const densities = spec.sections.map((s) => s.density);
  assert.equal(densities[0], 1);
  assert.equal(Math.max(...densities), 5);
  assert.equal(densities.indexOf(5), 10, 'the ridiculous stack (section 11) is the visual peak');
  assert.equal(densities[11], 1, 'web operations resets to sparse');
});

test('the ridiculous stack is the busiest section on the board', () => {
  const count = (sectionId) => scene.items.filter((i) => i.section === sectionId).length;
  const peak = count('s11');
  for (const s of scene.sections) if (s.id !== 's11') assert.ok(count(s.id) < peak, `${s.id} is busier than the peak`);
});

test('the final section is quiet and introduces KIT last and small', () => {
  const last = scene.items.filter((i) => i.section === 's12');
  const kit = last.find((i) => i.text === 'KIT');
  assert.ok(kit, 'KIT appears in the last section');
  assert.ok(kit.font.size <= 56, 'KIT is not a hero');
  assert.ok(!scene.items.filter((i) => i.section !== 's12').some((i) => i.text === 'KIT'), 'KIT appears nowhere earlier');
});
```

### Task 13: The Fabric layer — overrides maths, measurement, `LineArrow`, renderer, annotation sync

Everything that touches Fabric. The pure part (`overrides.js`) is unit-tested; the rest is verified in the browser in Task 16, once there is a page to put it on. Nothing here decides *what* a board contains — it draws the scene and the state it is given.

**Files:**
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/overrides.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/measure.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/engine/fonts.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/editor/LineArrow.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/editor/renderer.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/editor/annotationSync.js`
- Test: `client/test/overrides.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: scene/items (Task 9), `styleToFabric`/`toneColor` (Task 8), `connectorPoints` (Task 9), `arrowGeometry` (Task 8), `toRelative`/`toWorldY` (Task 8).
- Produces:
  - `overrides.js`: `buildIndex(scene) → { byId: Map<string, Item>, blocks }`; `effectiveItem(item, ctx) → { cx, cy, w, scaleX, scaleY, angle, deleted, text, style }` where `ctx = { overrides, byId, blocks }`; `effectiveRect(item, ctx) → { cx, cy, w, h }`; `overrideFromGesture(item, target, ctx) → patch` where `target = { cx, cy, scaleX, scaleY, angle, w? }` in world coordinates and `patch` is what to store with `OVERRIDE_SET`.
  - `measure.js`: `createFabricMeasure(fabric) → measure`; `textboxOptions(font, width)`.
  - `fonts.js`: `loadBoardFonts({ timeoutMs? }) → Promise<void>` (never rejects, never waits longer than `timeoutMs`, default 3000).
  - `LineArrow.js`: `registerLineArrow(fabric) → class`; instances have `sx, sy, ex, ey, color, lineWidth, dashed, headEnd, headStart`, `getEndpoints() → { x1, y1, x2, y2 }`, `setEndpoints(x1, y1, x2, y2)`, optional `snap(x, y, obj) → { x, y }`.
  - `renderer.js`: `createRenderer(fabric, canvas, { objectById }) → { rebuild(scene, state), patch(state), updateConnectors(), setConnections(connections), objectOf(id), itemOf(id), idOf(obj), setInteractive(flag), items(), dispose() }`.
  - `annotationSync.js`: `createAnnotationSync(fabric, canvas, { onCreate? }) → { reconcile(state, sections), rebuildAll(state, sections), objectOf(id), adopt(obj, record), raiseAll(), dispose() }`, `recordFromObject(obj, sections) → { record, assets }`, and `applyTraits(obj)` (per-pixel hit-testing for thin strokes).

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { layoutBoard } from '../src/components/board/engine/layoutBoard.js';
import { buildIndex, effectiveItem, effectiveRect, overrideFromGesture } from '../src/components/board/engine/overrides.js';
import { sanitizeSpec } from '../src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { stubMeasure } from './helpers/stubMeasure.js';
import { SAMPLES } from '../../server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const scene = layoutBoard(sanitizeSpec({
  title: 'T',
  sections: [{
    title: 'S', density: 3,
    blocks: [
      { type: 'flow', steps: [{ label: 'A', note: 'a note' }, 'B', 'C'] },
      SAMPLES.browser,
    ],
  }],
}), stubMeasure);
const ctxOf = (overrides = {}) => ({ overrides, ...buildIndex(scene) });
const item = (id) => scene.items.find((i) => i.id === id);
const near = (a, b, msg) => assert.ok(Math.abs(a - b) < 1e-6, `${msg}: ${a} vs ${b}`);

test('with no overrides an item is where the layout put it', () => {
  const a = item('s1.b1.n1');
  const e = effectiveItem(a, ctxOf());
  assert.deepEqual([e.cx, e.cy, e.scaleX, e.scaleY, e.angle, e.deleted], [a.cx, a.cy, 1, 1, 0, false]);
  assert.equal(e.w, a.w);
});

test('an override moves, scales and rotates a single piece', () => {
  const a = item('s1.b1.n2');
  const e = effectiveItem(a, ctxOf({ 's1.b1.n2': { dx: 10, dy: -20, scaleX: 2, scaleY: 3, angle: 15 } }));
  assert.deepEqual([e.cx, e.cy, e.scaleX, e.scaleY, e.angle], [a.cx + 10, a.cy - 20, 2, 3, 15]);
});

test('text, width and style overrides pass through; block style sits under piece style', () => {
  const e = effectiveItem(item('s1.b1.n1'), ctxOf({
    's1.b1': { style: { fontSize: 50, color: 'money' } },
    's1.b1.n1': { text: 'HELLO', w: 400, style: { color: 'problem' } },
  }));
  assert.equal(e.text, 'HELLO');
  assert.equal(e.w, 400);
  assert.deepEqual(e.style, { fontSize: 50, color: 'problem' });
});

test('a follower travels with its leader', () => {
  const label = item('s1.b1.n1');
  const note = item('s1.b1.n1.note');
  const e = effectiveItem(note, ctxOf({ 's1.b1.n1': { dx: 100, dy: 40 } }));
  assert.deepEqual([e.cx, e.cy], [note.cx + 100, note.cy + 40]);
  assert.ok(label.cy < note.cy);
});

test('a leader that grows pushes its follower outward from the leader\'s centre', () => {
  const label = item('s1.b1.n1');
  const note = item('s1.b1.n1.note');
  const e = effectiveItem(note, ctxOf({ 's1.b1.n1': { scaleX: 2, scaleY: 2 } }));
  near(e.cy - label.cy, (note.cy - label.cy) * 2, 'offset doubles');
  assert.equal(e.scaleX, 2);
});

test('a follower can also be nudged on its own, on top of the leader', () => {
  const note = item('s1.b1.n1.note');
  const e = effectiveItem(note, ctxOf({ 's1.b1.n1': { dx: 5 }, 's1.b1.n1.note': { dx: 7 } }));
  assert.equal(e.cx, note.cx + 12);
});

test('deleting a leader, or the whole block, hides everything under it', () => {
  assert.equal(effectiveItem(item('s1.b1.n1.note'), ctxOf({ 's1.b1.n1': { deleted: true } })).deleted, true);
  assert.equal(effectiveItem(item('s1.b1.n2'), ctxOf({ 's1.b1': { deleted: true } })).deleted, true);
  assert.equal(effectiveItem(item('s1.b1.n2'), ctxOf({ 's1.b2': { deleted: true } })).deleted, false);
});

test('the browser chrome follows the frame', () => {
  const frame = item('s1.b2.frame');
  const url = item('s1.b2.url');
  const e = effectiveItem(url, ctxOf({ 's1.b2.frame': { dx: -50, dy: 30 } }));
  assert.deepEqual([e.cx, e.cy], [url.cx - 50, url.cy + 30]);
  assert.ok(frame.cy > 0);
});

test('a block-level move shifts every piece of the block by the same amount', () => {
  const ctx = ctxOf({ 's1.b1': { dx: 30, dy: 60 } });
  for (const id of ['s1.b1.n1', 's1.b1.n2', 's1.b1.n3']) {
    const e = effectiveItem(item(id), ctx);
    assert.deepEqual([e.cx - item(id).cx, e.cy - item(id).cy], [30, 60]);
  }
});

test('a block-level scale grows the block about its own centre', () => {
  const block = scene.blocks['s1.b1'];
  const bcy = block.y + block.h / 2;
  const a = item('s1.b1.n1');
  const e = effectiveItem(a, ctxOf({ 's1.b1': { scaleX: 2, scaleY: 2 } }));
  near(e.cy - bcy, (a.cy - bcy) * 2, 'distance from the block centre doubles');
  assert.equal(e.scaleX, 2);
});

test('a block-level rotation turns pieces about the block centre and adds to their angle', () => {
  const block = scene.blocks['s1.b1'];
  const bcx = block.x + block.w / 2;
  const bcy = block.y + block.h / 2;
  const a = item('s1.b1.n1');
  const e = effectiveItem(a, ctxOf({ 's1.b1': { angle: 90 } }));
  near(Math.hypot(e.cx - bcx, e.cy - bcy), Math.hypot(a.cx - bcx, a.cy - bcy), 'radius preserved');
  assert.equal(e.angle, 90);
});

test('effectiveRect scales the size, for connectors', () => {
  const a = item('s1.b1.n2');
  const r = effectiveRect(a, ctxOf({ 's1.b1.n2': { scaleX: 2, scaleY: 0.5, dx: 4 } }));
  assert.deepEqual(r, { cx: a.cx + 4, cy: a.cy, w: a.w * 2, h: a.h * 0.5 });
});

test('overrideFromGesture produces a patch that reproduces the gesture exactly', () => {
  const targets = [
    { cx: 300, cy: 900, scaleX: 1.5, scaleY: 1.5, angle: 20 },
    { cx: 1000, cy: 400, scaleX: 0.8, scaleY: 1.2, angle: -45 },
  ];
  for (const id of ['s1.b1.n2', 's1.b1.n1.note', 's1.b2.url']) {
    for (const target of targets) {
      const ctx = ctxOf({ 's1.b1.n1': { dx: 12 }, 's1.b2.frame': { dx: 9, dy: -3, scaleX: 1.1, scaleY: 1.1 } });
      const patch = overrideFromGesture(item(id), target, ctx);
      const after = effectiveItem(item(id), { ...ctx, overrides: { ...ctx.overrides, [id]: patch } });
      near(after.cx, target.cx, `${id} cx`);
      near(after.cy, target.cy, `${id} cy`);
      near(after.scaleX, target.scaleX, `${id} scaleX`);
      near(after.scaleY, target.scaleY, `${id} scaleY`);
      near(after.angle, target.angle, `${id} angle`);
    }
  }
});

test('overrideFromGesture records a text width change only when it really changed', () => {
  const a = item('s1.b1.n1');
  const same = overrideFromGesture(a, { cx: a.cx, cy: a.cy, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, angle: 0, w: a.w }, ctxOf());
  assert.equal('w' in same, false);
  const wider = overrideFromGesture(a, { cx: a.cx, cy: a.cy, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, angle: 0, w: a.w + 100 }, ctxOf());
  assert.equal(wider.w, a.w + 100);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `client/src/components/board/engine/overrides.js`**

`client/src/components/board/engine/overrides.js`:

```js
/**
 * What the user has done to a piece, applied to where the layout put it.
 *
 * Three things can move a piece: its own override, the override of the block it
 * belongs to (a block-level move/scale/rotate acts on the block as a whole,
 * about the block's centre — this is how Direct mode says "make this diagram
 * bigger"), and the leader it follows (a browser frame's chrome, a panel's
 * rows, a step's note). Followers ride their leader's *effective* transform,
 * so a leader that was already moved, scaled or deleted takes them with it.
 */

const rad = (deg) => (deg * Math.PI) / 180;

function rotate(x, y, deg) {
  const c = Math.cos(rad(deg));
  const s = Math.sin(rad(deg));
  return { x: x * c - y * s, y: x * s + y * c };
}

const near = (v, ident) => (Math.abs(v - ident) < 1e-9 ? ident : v);

export function buildIndex(scene) {
  return { byId: new Map(scene.items.map((i) => [i.id, i])), blocks: scene.blocks };
}

/** ctx = { overrides, byId, blocks } */
export function effectiveItem(item, ctx) {
  const own = ctx.overrides[item.id] || {};
  const blockOv = ctx.overrides[item.block] || {};
  let x = item.cx;
  let y = item.cy;
  let sx = 1;
  let sy = 1;
  let angle = 0;
  let deleted = Boolean(own.deleted || blockOv.deleted);

  const leader = item.follow ? ctx.byId.get(item.follow) : null;
  if (leader) {
    const lead = effectiveItem(leader, ctx);
    const p = rotate((item.cx - leader.cx) * lead.scaleX, (item.cy - leader.cy) * lead.scaleY, lead.angle);
    x = lead.cx + p.x;
    y = lead.cy + p.y;
    sx = lead.scaleX;
    sy = lead.scaleY;
    angle = lead.angle;
    deleted = deleted || lead.deleted;
  }

  x += own.dx || 0;
  y += own.dy || 0;
  sx *= own.scaleX ?? 1;
  sy *= own.scaleY ?? 1;
  angle += own.angle || 0;

  // Followers already carry their leader's block-level transform.
  const block = ctx.blocks[item.block];
  const blockActs = block && (blockOv.dx || blockOv.dy || blockOv.scaleX != null || blockOv.scaleY != null || blockOv.angle);
  if (!leader && blockActs) {
    const bcx = block.x + block.w / 2;
    const bcy = block.y + block.h / 2;
    const bsx = blockOv.scaleX ?? 1;
    const bsy = blockOv.scaleY ?? 1;
    const p = rotate((x - bcx) * bsx, (y - bcy) * bsy, blockOv.angle || 0);
    x = bcx + p.x + (blockOv.dx || 0);
    y = bcy + p.y + (blockOv.dy || 0);
    sx *= bsx;
    sy *= bsy;
    angle += blockOv.angle || 0;
  }

  return {
    cx: x,
    cy: y,
    w: own.w ?? item.w,
    scaleX: sx,
    scaleY: sy,
    angle,
    deleted,
    text: own.text,
    style: { ...(blockOv.style || {}), ...(own.style || {}) },
  };
}

/** The axis-aligned box a connector should aim at (rotation is ignored: arrows meet the box, not its corners). */
export function effectiveRect(item, ctx) {
  const e = effectiveItem(item, ctx);
  return { cx: e.cx, cy: e.cy, w: e.w * e.scaleX, h: item.h * e.scaleY };
}

/**
 * The override that makes `item` end up exactly at `target` (a world-space
 * centre, scale and angle read off the Fabric object after a gesture).
 * Computed against the item's effective transform *without* its own override,
 * so leaders, followers and block-level overrides are all accounted for.
 */
export function overrideFromGesture(item, target, ctx) {
  const bare = effectiveItem(item, { ...ctx, overrides: { ...ctx.overrides, [item.id]: {} } });
  const patch = {
    dx: near(target.cx - bare.cx, 0),
    dy: near(target.cy - bare.cy, 0),
    scaleX: near(target.scaleX / bare.scaleX, 1),
    scaleY: near(target.scaleY / bare.scaleY, 1),
    angle: near(target.angle - bare.angle, 0),
  };
  if (item.kind === 'text' && typeof target.w === 'number' && Math.abs(target.w - item.w) > 0.5) {
    patch.w = Math.round(target.w);
  }
  return patch;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `client/src/components/board/engine/measure.js` and `engine/fonts.js`**

`client/src/components/board/engine/measure.js`:

```js
/**
 * Text metrics from the real thing: a Fabric Textbox laid out with the same
 * font, size, spacing and wrap width the renderer will use. Layout runs in the
 * browser precisely so it can measure instead of estimate.
 *
 * Call `loadBoardFonts()` first — measuring before a face has loaded measures
 * the fallback face and the wrong wrap is then baked into the whole board.
 */

export function textboxOptions(font, width) {
  return {
    width,
    fontFamily: font.family,
    fontSize: font.size,
    fontWeight: String(font.weight),
    fontStyle: font.italic ? 'italic' : 'normal',
    charSpacing: font.letterSpacing || 0,
    lineHeight: font.lineHeight,
    textAlign: 'center',
    splitByGrapheme: false,
  };
}

export function createFabricMeasure(fabric) {
  const cache = new Map();
  return function measure(text, font, maxWidth) {
    const key = [font.family, font.size, font.weight, font.italic, font.letterSpacing, font.lineHeight, maxWidth, text].join('|');
    const hit = cache.get(key);
    if (hit) return hit;
    const box = new fabric.Textbox(text, textboxOptions(font, maxWidth));
    let widest = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < box._textLines.length; i += 1) widest = Math.max(widest, box.getLineWidth(i));
    const result = { w: widest, h: box.height, lines: box._textLines.length };
    cache.set(key, result);
    return result;
  };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/engine/fonts.js`:

```js
import { FONT_PROBES, FONT_URL } from './theme.js';

let loading = null;

/**
 * Inject the board's typefaces and wait until each face the board uses is
 * actually loaded. Canvas text does not trigger a font fetch by itself, so
 * without the explicit probes Fabric measures and wraps the first render with
 * fallback metrics.
 *
 * Never rejects and never waits past `timeoutMs`: a font CDN that is down must
 * cost the board its typography, not the board.
 */
export function loadBoardFonts({ timeoutMs = 3000 } = {}) {
  if (loading) return loading;
  loading = new Promise((resolve) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs);
    const finish = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      resolve();
    };
    let link = document.querySelector('link[data-board-fonts]');
    if (!link) {
      link = document.createElement('link');
      link.rel = 'stylesheet';
      link.href = FONT_URL;
      link.dataset.boardFonts = '1';
      document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
    const sheetReady = new Promise((done) => {
      if (link.sheet) done();
      link.addEventListener('load', done, { once: true });
      link.addEventListener('error', done, { once: true });
    });
    sheetReady
      .then(() => Promise.all(FONT_PROBES.map((probe) => document.fonts.load(probe))))
      .catch(() => {})
      .then(finish);
  });
  return loading;
}
```
