```js
/**
 * One class for every line and arrow on the board: the Line and Arrow tools,
 * the connectors the layout draws between pieces, and the ones Direct mode adds.
 *
 * Fabric's own `Line` stores absolute endpoint coordinates that drift out of
 * step with `left`/`top` when the object moves (the reference deck's snapping
 * bug), so this is a plain object instead: a bounding box plus two endpoints in
 * box-local coordinates. Moving it changes `left`/`top` and nothing else.
 * It has no scale or rotate handles — only the two endpoint handles — so
 * scale is always 1 and angle 0.
 *
 * `strokeWidth` on the Fabric object stays 0 (the bounding box is exactly the
 * endpoints); the visible width is `lineWidth`.
 */

function drawHead(ctx, tip, angle, size) {
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(tip.x, tip.y);
  ctx.lineTo(tip.x - Math.cos(angle - 0.42) * size, tip.y - Math.sin(angle - 0.42) * size);
  ctx.lineTo(tip.x - Math.cos(angle + 0.42) * size, tip.y - Math.sin(angle + 0.42) * size);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
}

function endpointControl(fabric, which) {
  return new fabric.Control({
    x: 0,
    y: 0,
    cursorStyle: 'crosshair',
    sizeX: 26,
    sizeY: 26,
    actionName: 'endpoint',
    positionHandler(dim, finalMatrix, obj) {
      const local = {
        x: (which === 'start' ? obj.sx : obj.ex) - obj.width / 2,
        y: (which === 'start' ? obj.sy : obj.ey) - obj.height / 2,
      };
      const vpt = obj.canvas ? obj.canvas.viewportTransform : fabric.iMatrix;
      return fabric.util.transformPoint(local, fabric.util.multiplyTransformMatrices(vpt, obj.calcTransformMatrix()));
    },
    // x, y arrive in scene coordinates (the pointer with the viewport undone).
    actionHandler(eventData, transform, x, y) {
      const obj = transform.target;
      const e = obj.getEndpoints();
      const anchor = which === 'start' ? { x: e.x2, y: e.y2 } : { x: e.x1, y: e.y1 };
      let px = x;
      let py = y;
      if (eventData.shiftKey) {
        const dx = px - anchor.x;
        const dy = py - anchor.y;
        const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        const step = Math.PI / 4;
        const a = Math.round(Math.atan2(dy, dx) / step) * step;
        px = anchor.x + Math.cos(a) * len;
        py = anchor.y + Math.sin(a) * len;
      } else if (typeof obj.snap === 'function') {
        ({ x: px, y: py } = obj.snap(px, py, obj));
      }
      if (which === 'start') obj.setEndpoints(px, py, e.x2, e.y2);
      else obj.setEndpoints(e.x1, e.y1, px, py);
      return true;
    },
    render(ctx, left, top) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(left, top, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#1d4ed8';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    },
  });
}

export function registerLineArrow(fabric) {
  if (fabric.LineArrow) return fabric.LineArrow;

  const LineArrow = fabric.util.createClass(fabric.Object, {
    type: 'lineArrow',
    sx: 0,
    sy: 0,
    ex: 100,
    ey: 0,
    color: '#c81e3a',
    lineWidth: 5,
    dashed: false,
    headEnd: true,
    headStart: false,
    fill: null,
    strokeWidth: 0,
    objectCaching: false,
    padding: 10,
    hasBorders: false,

    initialize(options) {
      this.callSuper('initialize', options || {});
      this.controls = { start: endpointControl(fabric, 'start'), end: endpointControl(fabric, 'end') };
    },

    toObject(propertiesToInclude) {
      return fabric.util.object.extend(this.callSuper('toObject', propertiesToInclude), {
        sx: this.sx, sy: this.sy, ex: this.ex, ey: this.ey,
        color: this.color, lineWidth: this.lineWidth, dashed: this.dashed, headEnd: this.headEnd, headStart: this.headStart,
      });
    },

    getEndpoints() {
      return { x1: this.left + this.sx, y1: this.top + this.sy, x2: this.left + this.ex, y2: this.top + this.ey };
    },

    /** Endpoints in scene coordinates. Resets scale and angle: an arrow is only ever its two ends. */
    setEndpoints(x1, y1, x2, y2) {
      const left = Math.min(x1, x2);
      const top = Math.min(y1, y2);
      this.set({
        left, top, angle: 0, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1,
        width: Math.max(1, Math.abs(x2 - x1)), height: Math.max(1, Math.abs(y2 - y1)),
        sx: x1 - left, sy: y1 - top, ex: x2 - left, ey: y2 - top,
      });
      this.setCoords();
      this.dirty = true;
    },

    _render(ctx) {
      const ox = -this.width / 2;
      const oy = -this.height / 2;
      const a = { x: ox + this.sx, y: oy + this.sy };
      const b = { x: ox + this.ex, y: oy + this.ey };
      const w = this.lineWidth;
      const head = Math.max(16, w * 4.2);
      const angle = Math.atan2(b.y - a.y, b.x - a.x);
      const length = Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y);
      const inset = (this.headStart ? 1 : 0) + (this.headEnd ? 1 : 0) > 0 && length > head * 1.4 ? head * 0.7 : 0;
      const start = this.headStart ? { x: a.x + Math.cos(angle) * inset, y: a.y + Math.sin(angle) * inset } : a;
      const end = this.headEnd ? { x: b.x - Math.cos(angle) * inset, y: b.y - Math.sin(angle) * inset } : b;

      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = this.color;
      ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = w;
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
      if (this.dashed) ctx.setLineDash([w * 3, w * 2.2]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(start.x, start.y);
      ctx.lineTo(end.x, end.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      if (this.headEnd && length > 2) drawHead(ctx, b, angle, head);
      if (this.headStart && length > 2) drawHead(ctx, a, angle + Math.PI, head);
      ctx.restore();
    },
  });

  LineArrow.fromObject = (object, callback) => fabric.Object._fromObject('LineArrow', object, callback);
  fabric.LineArrow = LineArrow;
  return LineArrow;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `client/src/components/board/editor/renderer.js`**

```js
import { connectorPoints } from '../engine/geometry.js';
import { buildIndex, effectiveItem } from '../engine/overrides.js';
import { textboxOptions } from '../engine/measure.js';
import { toneColor } from '../engine/theme.js';
import { arrowGeometry } from '../state/annotations.js';
import { styleToFabric } from '../state/styleMap.js';
import { registerLineArrow } from './LineArrow.js';

/**
 * Scene → Fabric, and back into shape when the state changes.
 *
 * Every presentation object is centred on its item's (cx, cy) and carries
 * `data = { kind: 'presentation', id }`. `patch` re-applies the effective
 * transform (layout + overrides + leaders) to the existing objects — cheap, and
 * selection survives it. A change of *style* rebuilds just that piece, because
 * removing a style key must restore the original look, not leave a stale one.
 *
 * Connectors are LineArrows recomputed from the live geometry of their two
 * ends, so an arrow follows a piece while it is being dragged, not after.
 */

const SHAPE_KEYS = ['fill', 'stroke', 'strokeWidth', 'radius', 'dash'];

const pick = (obj, keys) => Object.fromEntries(keys.filter((k) => k in obj).map((k) => [k, obj[k]]));
const omit = (obj, keys) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(obj).filter(([k]) => !keys.includes(k)));

export function createRenderer(fabric, canvas, { objectById = () => null } = {}) {
  const LineArrow = registerLineArrow(fabric);
  let scene = null;
  let index = null;
  let interactive = true;
  let overrides = {};
  const entries = new Map(); // item id → { item, obj, styleKey }
  let layoutConnectors = []; // { item, obj }
  let userConnectors = []; //   { conn, obj }

  const data = (item) => ({ kind: 'presentation', id: item.id, item: item.kind });

  /* ------------------------------ object makers ----------------------------- */

  function makeText(item) {
    return new fabric.Textbox(item.text, {
      ...textboxOptions(item.font, item.w),
      textAlign: item.align,
      fill: item.color,
      left: item.cx,
      top: item.cy,
      originX: 'center',
      originY: 'center',
      lockScalingFlip: true,
      data: data(item),
    });
  }

  function labelObjects(item) {
    const parts = [];
    if (item.label) {
      const hasTag = Boolean(item.tag) && item.tagAlign === 'bottom-left';
      const leftAligned = item.labelAlign === 'left';
      parts.push(new fabric.Textbox(item.label, {
        ...textboxOptions(item.labelFont, item.labelWidth),
        textAlign: item.labelAlign,
        fill: item.labelColor,
        originX: 'center',
        originY: hasTag ? 'top' : 'center',
        left: leftAligned ? -item.w / 2 + item.pad + item.labelWidth / 2 : 0,
        top: hasTag ? -item.h / 2 + item.pad : 0,
        selectable: false,
        evented: false,
      }));
    }
    if (item.tag) {
      const right = item.tagAlign === 'right';
      parts.push(new fabric.Text(item.tag, {
        fontFamily: item.tagFont.family,
        fontSize: item.tagFont.size,
        fontWeight: String(item.tagFont.weight),
        charSpacing: item.tagFont.letterSpacing || 0,
        fill: item.tagColor,
        originX: right ? 'right' : 'left',
        originY: right ? 'center' : 'bottom',
        left: right ? item.w / 2 - 16 : -item.w / 2 + item.pad,
        top: right ? 0 : item.h / 2 - item.pad,
        selectable: false,
        evented: false,
      }));
    }
    return parts;
  }

  function makeBox(item) {
    const rect = new fabric.Rect({
      width: item.w, height: item.h, rx: item.radius, ry: item.radius,
      fill: item.fill, stroke: item.stroke, strokeWidth: item.strokeWidth, strokeUniform: true,
      strokeDashArray: item.dash || null, originX: 'center', originY: 'center', left: 0, top: 0,
    });
    const parts = labelObjects(item);
    if (!parts.length) {
      rect.set({ left: item.cx, top: item.cy, data: data(item) });
      return rect;
    }
    return new fabric.Group([rect, ...parts], {
      left: item.cx, top: item.cy, originX: 'center', originY: 'center', subTargetCheck: false, data: data(item),
    });
  }

  function makePoly(item) {
    const poly = new fabric.Polygon(item.points.map(([x, y]) => ({ x, y })), {
      fill: item.fill, originX: 'center', originY: 'center', left: 0, top: 0,
    });
    const label = new fabric.Textbox(item.label, {
      ...textboxOptions(item.labelFont, item.labelWidth),
      fill: item.labelColor, originX: 'center', originY: 'center', left: 0, top: 0, selectable: false, evented: false,
    });
    return new fabric.Group([poly, label], {
      left: item.cx, top: item.cy, originX: 'center', originY: 'center', subTargetCheck: false, data: data(item),
    });
  }

  function makeRule(item) {
    return new fabric.Line([-item.w / 2, -item.h / 2, item.w / 2, item.h / 2], {
      stroke: item.color, strokeWidth: item.width, strokeDashArray: item.dashed ? [10, 8] : null,
      originX: 'center', originY: 'center', left: item.cx, top: item.cy,
      selectable: false, evented: false, data: data(item),
    });
  }

  function makeConnector(item) {
    const arrow = new LineArrow({
      ...arrowGeometry(0, 0, 1, 0), color: item.color, lineWidth: item.width, dashed: Boolean(item.dashed),
      headEnd: Boolean(item.head), headStart: false, selectable: false, evented: false, hasControls: false,
      data: { kind: 'presentation', id: item.id, item: 'connector' },
    });
    return arrow;
  }

  function makeObject(item) {
    switch (item.kind) {
      case 'text': return makeText(item);
      case 'box': return makeBox(item);
      case 'poly': return makePoly(item);
      case 'rule': return makeRule(item);
      default: return null;
    }
  }

  /* --------------------------------- styling -------------------------------- */

  function applyStyle(item, obj, style) {
    if (!Object.keys(style).length) return;
    if (item.kind === 'text') {
      obj.set(styleToFabric(style, 'text'));
    } else if (item.kind === 'box' || item.kind === 'poly') {
      const parts = obj.getObjects ? obj.getObjects() : [obj];
      const [shape, ...rest] = parts;
      shape.set(styleToFabric(pick(style, SHAPE_KEYS), 'shape'));
      const labelStyle = styleToFabric(omit(style, [...SHAPE_KEYS, 'opacity']), 'text');
      rest.forEach((part) => part.set(labelStyle));
      if (typeof style.opacity === 'number') obj.set('opacity', style.opacity);
      obj.dirty = true;
    } else if (typeof style.opacity === 'number') {
      obj.set('opacity', style.opacity);
    }
  }

  const selectableKind = (item) => item.kind === 'text' || item.kind === 'box' || item.kind === 'poly';

  function place(entry, eff) {
    const { item, obj } = entry;
    obj.set({
      left: eff.cx, top: eff.cy, scaleX: eff.scaleX, scaleY: eff.scaleY, angle: eff.angle,
      visible: !eff.deleted,
      selectable: !eff.deleted && selectableKind(item),
      evented: interactive && !eff.deleted && selectableKind(item),
    });
    if (item.kind === 'text') {
      // Only touch what changed: re-setting text or width re-wraps the whole Textbox.
      const text = eff.text ?? item.text;
      if (obj.text !== text) obj.set('text', text);
      if (obj.width !== eff.w) obj.set('width', eff.w);
    }
    obj.setCoords();
  }

  /* ---------------------------------- public -------------------------------- */

  function replaceObject(entry, next) {
    const list = canvas.getObjects();
    const at = list.indexOf(entry.obj);
    canvas.remove(entry.obj);
    if (at >= 0) canvas.insertAt(next, at, false);
    else canvas.add(next);
    entry.obj = next;
  }

  function rebuild(nextScene, state) {
    canvas.renderOnAddRemove = false;
    for (const { obj } of entries.values()) canvas.remove(obj);
    for (const { obj } of layoutConnectors) canvas.remove(obj);
    entries.clear();
    layoutConnectors = [];

    scene = nextScene;
    index = buildIndex(scene);
    overrides = state.overrides;

    // Connectors first so they sit behind the pieces they join.
    for (const item of scene.items.filter((i) => i.kind === 'connector')) {
      const obj = makeConnector(item);
      canvas.add(obj);
      layoutConnectors.push({ item, obj });
    }
    for (const item of scene.items) {
      if (item.kind === 'connector') continue;
      const obj = makeObject(item);
      if (!obj) continue;
      canvas.add(obj);
      entries.set(item.id, { item, obj, styleKey: '' });
    }
    canvas.renderOnAddRemove = true;
    patch(state);
  }

  function patch(state) {
    if (!scene) return;
    overrides = state.overrides;
    const ctx = { overrides, ...index };
    for (const entry of entries.values()) {
      const eff = effectiveItem(entry.item, ctx);
      const styleKey = JSON.stringify(eff.style);
      if (styleKey !== entry.styleKey) {
        // A style changed (or was undone): start from the original look and re-apply.
        const fresh = makeObject(entry.item);
        if (entry.styleKey !== '' || Object.keys(eff.style).length) replaceObject(entry, fresh);
        applyStyle(entry.item, entry.obj, eff.style);
        entry.styleKey = styleKey;
      }
      place(entry, eff);
    }
    setConnections(state.connections);
    updateConnectors();
    canvas.requestRenderAll();
  }

  /** Where a connector end currently is, read from the live object so arrows track a drag in progress. */
  function liveRect(id) {
    const obj = entries.get(id)?.obj || objectById(id);
    if (!obj || obj.visible === false) return null;
    const d = fabric.util.qrDecompose(obj.calcTransformMatrix());
    return { cx: d.translateX, cy: d.translateY, w: obj.width * d.scaleX, h: obj.height * d.scaleY };
  }

  function route(arrow, fromId, toId, gap) {
    const a = liveRect(fromId);
    const b = liveRect(toId);
    if (!a || !b) {
      arrow.set('visible', false);
      return;
    }
    const p = connectorPoints(a, b, gap);
    arrow.set('visible', true);
    arrow.setEndpoints(p.x1, p.y1, p.x2, p.y2);
  }

  function updateConnectors() {
    layoutConnectors.forEach(({ item, obj }) => route(obj, item.from, item.to, item.gap ?? 10));
    userConnectors.forEach(({ conn, obj }) => route(obj, conn.from, conn.to, 12));
  }

  function setConnections(connections) {
    const wanted = new Map(connections.map((c) => [c.id, c]));
    userConnectors = userConnectors.filter(({ conn, obj }) => {
      const keep = wanted.has(conn.id) && wanted.get(conn.id) === conn;
      if (!keep) canvas.remove(obj);
      return keep;
    });
    const have = new Set(userConnectors.map(({ conn }) => conn.id));
    for (const conn of connections) {
      if (have.has(conn.id)) continue;
      const obj = new LineArrow({
        ...arrowGeometry(0, 0, 1, 0), color: toneColor(conn.tone), lineWidth: 5, dashed: conn.dashed, headEnd: true,
        selectable: false, evented: false, hasControls: false, data: { kind: 'connection', id: conn.id },
      });
      canvas.add(obj);
      userConnectors.push({ conn, obj });
    }
  }

  function setInteractive(flag) {
    interactive = flag;
    for (const { item, obj } of entries.values()) {
      const eff = effectiveItem(item, { overrides, ...index });
      obj.set({ evented: flag && !eff.deleted && selectableKind(item) });
    }
  }

  return {
    rebuild,
    patch,
    updateConnectors,
    setConnections,
    setInteractive,
    objectOf: (id) => entries.get(id)?.obj || null,
    itemOf: (id) => entries.get(id)?.item || null,
    idOf: (obj) => (obj?.data?.kind === 'presentation' ? obj.data.id : null),
    context: () => ({ overrides, ...index }),
    items: () => [...entries.values()],
    dispose() {
      for (const { obj } of entries.values()) canvas.remove(obj);
      layoutConnectors.forEach(({ obj }) => canvas.remove(obj));
      userConnectors.forEach(({ obj }) => canvas.remove(obj));
      entries.clear();
      layoutConnectors = [];
      userConnectors = [];
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `client/src/components/board/editor/annotationSync.js`**

```js
import { toRelative, toWorldY } from '../state/anchor.js';

/**
 * Annotation records ↔ Fabric objects.
 *
 * A record stores its `top` relative to its section (see state/anchor.js) and
 * any image as `asset:<id>`, so this file converts on the way in and out. An
 * object remembers the record it was made from (`__rec`); `reconcile` only
 * recreates objects whose record changed, which is what keeps a selection alive
 * through an edit that originated on the canvas itself.
 */

/** A cheap, stable id for an image's pixels — same image, same asset. */
function hashString(s) {
  let h = 5381;
  const head = s.slice(0, 4096);
  const tail = s.slice(-4096);
  for (const part of [head, tail, String(s.length)]) {
    for (let i = 0; i < part.length; i += 1) h = ((h << 5) + h + part.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  }
  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);
}

export function recordFromObject(obj, sections) {
  const json = obj.toObject(['data']);
  const rel = toRelative(sections, json.top);
  json.top = rel.y;
  const assets = {};
  if (json.type === 'image' && typeof json.src === 'string' && json.src.startsWith('data:')) {
    const id = `img_${hashString(json.src)}`;
    assets[id] = json.src;
    json.src = `asset:${id}`;
  }
  return { record: { id: obj.data.id, section: rel.section, kind: obj.data.ann, json }, assets };
}

/** Make thin strokes and unfilled shapes hit-testable only where they are actually inked. */
export function applyTraits(obj) {
  const transparentFill = typeof obj.fill === 'string' && /rgba\([^)]*,\s*0(\.\d+)?\)|transparent/.test(obj.fill);
  obj.perPixelTargetFind = obj.type === 'path' || obj.type === 'lineArrow' || transparentFill;
}

export function createAnnotationSync(fabric, canvas, { onCreate } = {}) {
  const objects = new Map(); // annotation id → Fabric object
  const tokens = new Map(); //  annotation id → latest creation token (enlivening can be async)
  let counter = 0;

  function prepare(record, state, sections) {
    const json = { ...record.json, top: toWorldY(sections, record.section, record.json.top) };
    if (typeof json.src === 'string' && json.src.startsWith('asset:')) json.src = state.assets[json.src.slice(6)] || '';
    return json;
  }

  function create(record, state, sections) {
    counter += 1;
    const mine = counter;
    tokens.set(record.id, mine);
    fabric.util.enlivenObjects([prepare(record, state, sections)], ([obj]) => {
      if (!obj || tokens.get(record.id) !== mine) return;
      obj.__rec = record;
      obj.data = { ...(record.json.data || {}), kind: 'annotation', id: record.id };
      applyTraits(obj);
      onCreate?.(obj);
      canvas.add(obj);
      objects.set(record.id, obj);
      canvas.requestRenderAll();
    });
  }

  function remove(id) {
    counter += 1;
    tokens.set(id, counter); // cancels an in-flight creation
    const obj = objects.get(id);
    if (obj) canvas.remove(obj);
    objects.delete(id);
  }

  return {
    reconcile(state, sections) {
      const ids = new Set(state.annotations.map((a) => a.id));
      for (const id of [...objects.keys()]) if (!ids.has(id)) remove(id);
      for (const record of state.annotations) {
        const existing = objects.get(record.id);
        if (existing && existing.__rec === record) continue;
        if (existing) remove(record.id);
        create(record, state, sections);
      }
    },
    rebuildAll(state, sections) {
      for (const id of [...objects.keys()]) remove(id);
      for (const record of state.annotations) create(record, state, sections);
    },
    /** A tool made this object by hand; tie it to the record that describes it so reconcile leaves it alone. */
    adopt(obj, record) {
      obj.__rec = record;
      objects.set(record.id, obj);
    },
    objectOf: (id) => objects.get(id) || null,
    raiseAll() {
      objects.forEach((obj) => canvas.bringToFront(obj));
    },
    dispose() {
      for (const id of [...objects.keys()]) remove(id);
    },
  };
}
```

**Files:**

- Create: `client/src/components/board/autosave.js`

- Modify: `client/src/components/AppHeader.jsx` (nav link)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `newBoardRecord`, `parseBoardFile`, `sanitizeSpec` (Task 8); `boardApi` (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `createBoardStore({ idb?, ls? }) → { mode, list(), get(id), put(record), remove(id) }` and the shared instance `boardStore`. `list()` → `[{ id, title, sections, degraded, updatedAt }]` newest first. `get` → record or `null`. `put`/`remove` reject with `StoreError` (message safe to show). `mode` is `'indexeddb' | 'localstorage' | 'none'` after the first call.
  - `createAutosaver({ save, delay = 800, onError, timers? }) → { schedule(getRecord), flush() → Promise, dirty }`.
  - `EXAMPLE_ID = 'example'`, `createExampleRecord()`, `newBoardId()`.
  - Routes `/board` (`BoardsPage`) and `/board/:id` (`BoardPage`).

`client/test/boardStore.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { StoreError, createBoardStore } from '../src/components/board/boardStore.js';
import { newBoardRecord, sanitizeSpec } from '../src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { SAMPLES } from '../../server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const fakeLS = () => {
  const m = new Map();
  return {
    m,
    getItem: (k) => (m.has(k) ? m.get(k) : null),
    setItem: (k, v) => { m.set(k, String(v)); },
    removeItem: (k) => { m.delete(k); },
  };
};
const record = (id, title, updatedAt) => {
  const r = newBoardRecord({ id, spec: sanitizeSpec({ title, sections: [{ title: 'S', blocks: [SAMPLES.title] }] }), brief: 'b' });
  return { ...r, updatedAt };
};

test('the localStorage fallback stores, lists newest first, gets and removes', async () => {
  const store = createBoardStore({ idb: null, ls: fakeLS() });
  await store.put(record('a', 'Older', 100));
  await store.put(record('b', 'Newer', 200));
  assert.deepEqual((await store.list()).map((s) => s.id), ['b', 'a']);
  assert.equal((await store.list())[0].sections, 1);
  assert.equal((await store.get('a')).title, 'Older');
  assert.equal(await store.get('missing'), null);
  await store.remove('a');
  assert.deepEqual((await store.list()).map((s) => s.id), ['b']);
  assert.equal(store.mode, 'localstorage');
});

test('putting the same id again replaces it rather than duplicating it', async () => {
  const store = createBoardStore({ idb: null, ls: fakeLS() });
  await store.put(record('a', 'One', 1));
  await store.put(record('a', 'Two', 2));
  const list = await store.list();
  assert.equal(list.length, 1);
  assert.equal(list[0].title, 'Two');
});

test('a full disk is a plain-sentence StoreError, not a crash', async () => {
  const ls = fakeLS();
  ls.setItem = () => { throw Object.assign(new Error('full'), { name: 'QuotaExceededError' }); };
  const store = createBoardStore({ idb: null, ls });
  await assert.rejects(store.put(record('a', 'x', 1)), (err) => err instanceof StoreError && /storage is full/.test(err.message));
});

test('corrupted entries are skipped, not fatal', async () => {
  const ls = fakeLS();
  const store = createBoardStore({ idb: null, ls });
  await store.put(record('a', 'Good', 1));
  ls.setItem('apollo-boards:item:bad', '{not json');
  ls.setItem('apollo-boards:index', JSON.stringify(['a', 'bad']));
  assert.deepEqual((await store.list()).map((s) => s.id), ['a']);
  assert.equal(await store.get('bad'), null);
});

test('IndexedDB that refuses to open falls back to localStorage', async () => {
  const idb = { open() { const req = {}; queueMicrotask(() => req.onerror?.()); return req; } };
  const store = createBoardStore({ idb, ls: fakeLS() });
  await store.put(record('a', 'x', 1));
  assert.equal(store.mode, 'localstorage');
  assert.equal((await store.get('a')).id, 'a');
});

test('with no storage at all, writes fail with a StoreError and reads are empty', async () => {
  const store = createBoardStore({ idb: null, ls: null });
  assert.deepEqual(await store.list(), []);
  assert.equal(await store.get('a'), null);
  await assert.rejects(store.put(record('a', 'x', 1)), StoreError);
  assert.equal(store.mode, 'none');
});
```

`client/test/autosave.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createAutosaver } from '../src/components/board/autosave.js';

function fakeTimers() {
  let next = 1;
  const pending = new Map();
  return {
    set: (fn) => { const id = next++; pending.set(id, fn); return id; },
    clear: (id) => pending.delete(id),
    fire: () => { const fns = [...pending.values()]; pending.clear(); fns.forEach((f) => f()); },
    get count() { return pending.size; },
  };
}

test('a burst of changes saves once, with the latest record', async () => {
  const timers = fakeTimers();
  const saved = [];
  const saver = createAutosaver({ save: async (r) => { saved.push(r); }, timers });
  saver.schedule(() => 'v1');
  saver.schedule(() => 'v2');
  saver.schedule(() => 'v3');
  assert.equal(timers.count, 1);
  assert.equal(saver.dirty, true);
  timers.fire();
  await saver.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(saved, ['v3']);
  assert.equal(saver.dirty, false);
});

test('flush saves immediately — the closing-the-tab path', async () => {
  const timers = fakeTimers();
  const saved = [];
  const saver = createAutosaver({ save: async (r) => { saved.push(r); }, timers });
  saver.schedule(() => 'now');
  await saver.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(saved, ['now']);
  assert.equal(timers.count, 0, 'the pending timer was cancelled');
});

test('flush with nothing pending does nothing', async () => {
  const saved = [];
  const saver = createAutosaver({ save: async (r) => { saved.push(r); }, timers: fakeTimers() });
  await saver.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(saved, []);
});

test('a failing save is reported, never thrown, and later saves still run', async () => {
  const timers = fakeTimers();
  const errors = [];
  const saved = [];
  let fail = true;
  const saver = createAutosaver({
    save: async (r) => { if (fail) throw new Error('disk full'); saved.push(r); },
    onError: (e) => errors.push(e.message),
    timers,
  });
  saver.schedule(() => 'a');
  await saver.flush();
  fail = false;
  saver.schedule(() => 'b');
  await saver.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(errors, ['disk full']);
  assert.deepEqual(saved, ['b']);
});
```

`client/src/components/board/boardStore.js`:

```js
/**
 * Boards live in the browser: IndexedDB first (room for images), with
 * localStorage as the fallback when IndexedDB is missing or refuses to open —
 * private windows and locked-down profiles do that. If neither works, reads
 * return nothing and writes fail with a sentence the person can act on; the
 * board on screen is unaffected and Save-to-file still works.
 */

const DB_NAME = 'apollo-boards';
const STORE = 'boards';
const LS_INDEX = 'apollo-boards:index';
const LS_ITEM = 'apollo-boards:item:';

export class StoreError extends Error {
  constructor(message) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'StoreError';
  }
}

const isQuota = (err) => err && (err.name === 'QuotaExceededError' || err.code === 22 || err.name === 'NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED');

const writeError = (err) =>
  new StoreError(
    isQuota(err)
      ? "Couldn't save — your browser's storage is full. Save the board to a file to keep it."
      : "Couldn't save this board in your browser. Save it to a file to keep it."
  );

export function summarize(record) {
  return {
    id: record.id,
    title: record.title || record.spec?.title || 'Untitled board',
    sections: record.spec?.sections?.length || 0,
    degraded: (record.spec?.sections || []).filter((s) => s.degraded).length,
    updatedAt: record.updatedAt || 0,
  };
}

export function createBoardStore({
  idb = typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined' ? indexedDB : null,
  ls = typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' ? localStorage : null,
} = {}) {
  let dbPromise = null;
  let idbFailed = false;
  let mode = 'none';

  const openDb = () => {
    if (!dbPromise) {
      dbPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const req = idb.open(DB_NAME, 1);
        req.onupgradeneeded = () => req.result.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: 'id' });
        req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
        req.onerror = () => reject(req.error || new Error('indexedDB open failed'));
        req.onblocked = () => reject(new Error('indexedDB blocked'));
      });
    }
    return dbPromise;
  };

  async function usingIdb() {
    if (!idb || idbFailed) return false;
    try {
      await openDb();
      mode = 'indexeddb';
      return true;
    } catch {
      idbFailed = true;
      return false;
    }
  }

  const usingLs = () => {
    if (!ls) return false;
    mode = 'localstorage';
    return true;
  };

  async function run(kind, fn) {
    const db = await openDb();
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = db.transaction(STORE, kind);
      const request = fn(tx.objectStore(STORE));
      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve(request?.result);
      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
      tx.onabort = () => reject(tx.error);
    });
  }

  const readIndex = () => {
    try {
      const ids = JSON.parse(ls.getItem(LS_INDEX) || '[]');
      return Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  };

  const readItem = (id) => {
    try {
      const raw = ls.getItem(LS_ITEM + id);
      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  };

  return {
    get mode() {
      return mode;
    },

    async list() {
      let records = [];
      if (await usingIdb()) records = (await run('readonly', (s) => s.getAll())) || [];
      else if (usingLs()) records = readIndex().map(readItem).filter(Boolean);
      return records.map(summarize).sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);
    },

    async get(id) {
      if (await usingIdb()) return (await run('readonly', (s) => s.get(id))) || null;
      if (usingLs()) return readItem(id);
      return null;
    },

    async put(record) {
      try {
        if (await usingIdb()) {
          await run('readwrite', (s) => s.put(record));
          return;
        }
        if (!usingLs()) throw new Error('no storage');
        ls.setItem(LS_ITEM + record.id, JSON.stringify(record));
        const ids = readIndex().filter((x) => x !== record.id);
        ids.push(record.id);
        ls.setItem(LS_INDEX, JSON.stringify(ids));
      } catch (err) {
        throw writeError(err);
      }
    },

    async remove(id) {
      try {
        if (await usingIdb()) {
          await run('readwrite', (s) => s.delete(id));
          return;
        }
        if (!usingLs()) return;
        ls.removeItem(LS_ITEM + id);
        ls.setItem(LS_INDEX, JSON.stringify(readIndex().filter((x) => x !== id)));
      } catch (err) {
        throw writeError(err);
      }
    },
  };
}

export const boardStore = createBoardStore();
```

`client/src/components/board/autosave.js`:

```js
/**
 * Debounced saving that cannot lose the last edit.
 *
 * `schedule` takes a *thunk*, evaluated when the save actually runs, so the
 * board written is the one that exists then, not the one that existed when the
 * timer started. `flush` runs it now — wired to pagehide/visibilitychange so
 * closing the tab inside the debounce window still writes. A failed save is
 * reported through `onError` and never thrown, and never blocks later saves.
 */
// Wrapped, not passed as `{ set: setTimeout }`: called as a method, the browser's setTimeout throws "Illegal invocation".
const realTimers = { set: (fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms), clear: (handle) => clearTimeout(handle) };

export function createAutosaver({ save, delay = 800, onError, timers = realTimers }) {
  let pending = null;
  let handle = null;
  let inflight = Promise.resolve();

  const run = () => {
    handle = null;
    if (!pending) return inflight;
    const getRecord = pending;
    pending = null;
    inflight = inflight.then(() => save(getRecord())).catch((err) => onError?.(err));
    return inflight;
  };

  return {
    schedule(getRecord) {
      pending = getRecord;
      if (handle) timers.clear(handle);
      handle = timers.set(run, delay);
    },
    flush() {
      if (handle) {
        timers.clear(handle);
        handle = null;
      }
      return run();
    },
    get dirty() {
      return pending !== null;
    },
  };
}
```

```js
import exampleSpec from './example/website-is-losing-you-sales.json';
import { newBoardRecord, sanitizeSpec } from './state/boardRecord.js';

/** The bundled example lives under a fixed id so it is created once and then behaves like any other board. */
export const EXAMPLE_ID = 'example';

export function createExampleRecord() {
  return newBoardRecord({ id: EXAMPLE_ID, spec: sanitizeSpec(exampleSpec), brief: '' });
}

export const newBoardId = () => `b_${Date.now().toString(36)}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`;
```

`client/src/components/board/GenerateProgress.jsx`:

```jsx
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button, Spinner } from '../../ui/primitives.jsx';

/**
 * What Apollo is doing while a board is generated: the sections the outline
 * found, ticked off as each one arrives. Sections finish out of order (four run
 * at once), so this shows a set of finished ids, not a counter.
 */
export default function GenerateProgress({ outline, done, onCancel }) {
  const total = outline?.sections.length || 0;
  return (
    <div className="mt-8 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-6">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="font-mono text-2xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-ink-3">
            {outline ? `Drafting section ${Math.min(done.size + 1, total)} of ${total}` : 'Reading your brief'}
          </p>
          <h2 className="mt-1 truncate font-display text-xl text-ink">{outline?.title || 'Finding the chapters…'}</h2>
        </div>
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</Button>
      </div>
      {outline && (
        <ol className="mt-5 grid gap-x-8 gap-y-1.5 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {outline.sections.map((section, i) => (
            <li key={section.id} className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]">
              {done.has(section.id) ? <Check size={14} className="shrink-0 text-accent-text" /> : <Spinner size={12} className="shrink-0" />}
              <span className={done.has(section.id) ? 'truncate text-ink' : 'truncate text-ink-3'}>
                {i + 1}. {section.title}
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      )}
      <p className="mt-5 text-xs text-ink-3">A full board can take a couple of minutes. Nothing is lost if a section needs a second try.</p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`client/src/components/board/NewBoardForm.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../../ui/primitives.jsx';

const MIN = 40;
const MAX = 60000;

/**
 * The brief box. Paste the story of the video — chapters, flows, the lines you
 * want on screen. Anything in it about tools, toolbars or file formats is
 * ignored by the outline pass, so a brief written for a different tool still works.
 */
export default function NewBoardForm({ ready, busy, onGenerate }) {
  const [brief, setBrief] = useState('');
  const length = brief.trim().length;
  const tooShort = length < MIN;
  const tooLong = brief.length > MAX;

  return (
    <form
      className="mt-8"
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!tooShort && !tooLong && ready && !busy) onGenerate(brief);
      }}
    >
      <label htmlFor="brief" className="text-[13px] font-medium text-ink">Your video, in your own words</label>
      <textarea
        id="brief"
        value={brief}
        onChange={(e) => setBrief(e.target.value)}
        disabled={busy}
        rows={10}
        placeholder={'Paste the outline of your video: the chapters, the flows you want to show (A ↓ B ↓ C), the lines you will say on screen, and how the story should build.'}
        className="mt-2 w-full resize-y rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3.5 py-3 text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3 focus:border-line-strong focus:outline-none"
      />
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="lg" disabled={!ready || busy || tooShort || tooLong}>
          <Sparkles size={15} />
          Generate board
        </Button>
        <span className={`font-mono text-2xs ${tooLong ? 'text-danger' : 'text-ink-3'}`}>
          {length.toLocaleString('en-US')} / {MAX.toLocaleString('en-US')}
        </span>
        {!ready && (
          <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">Generating needs a DeepSeek key on the server. The example board works without one.</span>
        )}
        {ready && tooShort && length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">Write at least {MIN} characters.</span>}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `client/src/pages/BoardsPage.jsx`**

```jsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Download, LayoutTemplate, Trash2, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react';
import { boardApi } from '../api/board.js';
import { useToast } from '../lib/toast.jsx';
import { Button, IconButton } from '../ui/primitives.jsx';
import AppHeader from '../components/AppHeader.jsx';
import GenerateProgress from '../components/board/GenerateProgress.jsx';
import NewBoardForm from '../components/board/NewBoardForm.jsx';
import { boardStore } from '../components/board/boardStore.js';
import { EXAMPLE_ID, newBoardId } from '../components/board/exampleBoard.js';
import { newBoardRecord, parseBoardFile, sanitizeSpec } from '../components/board/state/boardRecord.js';

const when = (t) => (t ? new Date(t).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }) : '');

/**
 * Jam board — the front door. Paste a brief to generate a board, open one you
 * already made, or open the bundled example (which needs no key).
 */
export default function BoardsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState(null);
  const [boards, setBoards] = useState([]);
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState('idle'); // idle | generating
  const [outline, setOutline] = useState(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(() => new Set());
  const abortRef = useRef(null);
  const fileRef = useRef(null);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => setBoards(await boardStore.list()), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    boardApi.status().then(setStatus).catch(() => setStatus({ ready: false }));
    refresh();
    return () => abortRef.current?.abort();
  }, [refresh]);

  const generate = async (brief) => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortRef.current = controller;
    setPhase('generating');
    setOutline(null);
    setDone(new Set());
    try {
      const board = await boardApi.generate(brief, {
        signal: controller.signal,
        onOutline: setOutline,
        onSection: ({ section }) => setDone((prev) => new Set(prev).add(section.id)),
      });
      const spec = sanitizeSpec(board);
      if (!spec) throw new Error('Apollo could not build any sections from that brief.');
      const record = newBoardRecord({ id: newBoardId(), spec, brief });
      await boardStore.put(record);
      navigate(`/board/${record.id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.name !== 'AbortError') toast.error("Couldn't build the board", err.message);
      setPhase('idle');
    }
  };

  const cancel = () => {
    abortRef.current?.abort();
    setPhase('idle');
  };

  const importFile = async (file) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const parsed = parseBoardFile(await file.text());
    if (!parsed.ok) return toast.error("Couldn't open that file", parsed.error);
    try {
      const record = { ...parsed.record, id: newBoardId() };
      await boardStore.put(record);
      navigate(`/board/${record.id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error("Couldn't save the board", err.message);
    }
  };

  const remove = async (id) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this board? This cannot be undone.')) return;
    try {
      await boardStore.remove(id);
      refresh();
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error("Couldn't delete the board", err.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-void text-ink">
      <AppHeader />
      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1180px] px-4 pb-24 pt-10 sm:px-8">
        <h1 className="font-display text-3xl tracking-tight">Jam board</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 max-w-xl text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-ink-2">
          Paste the story of your video. Apollo turns it into one big, quiet board you can scroll, draw on and talk over while you record.
        </p>

        {phase === 'generating' ? (
          <GenerateProgress outline={outline} done={done} onCancel={cancel} />
        ) : (
          <NewBoardForm ready={Boolean(status?.ready)} busy={false} onGenerate={generate} />
        )}

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => navigate(`/board/${EXAMPLE_ID}`)}>
            <LayoutTemplate size={14} />
            Open the example board
          </Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}>
            <Download size={14} />
            Import a board file
          </Button>
          <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="application/json,.json" hidden onChange={(e) => { importFile(e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ''; }} />
        </div>

        {boards.length > 0 && (
          <section className="mt-12">
            <h2 className="font-mono text-2xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-ink-3">Your boards</h2>
            <ul className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {boards.map((b) => (
                <li key={b.id} className="group relative rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface transition-colors hover:border-line-strong">
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate(`/board/${b.id}`)} className="block w-full p-4 text-left">
                    <p className="truncate font-display text-lg text-ink">{b.title}</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-3">
                      {b.sections} sections · {when(b.updatedAt)}
                    </p>
                    {b.degraded > 0 && (
                      <p className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-2">
                        <TriangleAlert size={12} /> {b.degraded} section{b.degraded > 1 ? 's' : ''} need a second try
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </button>
                  <IconButton aria-label="Delete board" onClick={() => remove(b.id)} className="absolute right-2 top-2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">
                    <Trash2 size={14} />
                  </IconButton>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </section>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

The wheel pans the canvas; Ctrl/Cmd+wheel and pinch zoom about the cursor. The maths is pure and tested; the controller is DOM glue.

- Produces (`viewportMath.js`): `MIN_ZOOM = 0.25`, `MAX_ZOOM = 4`, `MARGIN = 160`, `clampZoom(z)`, `fitZoom(viewW, worldW = 1400)`, `clampView(view, { viewW, viewH, worldW, worldH })`, `zoomAbout(view, factor, sx, sy)`, `panBy(view, dx, dy)`, `focusRect(rect, { viewW, viewH, pad?, maxZoom? })`, `worldYAtCenter(view, viewH)`, `scrollThumb(view, viewH, worldH) → { top, size }`, `viewForSection(section, view)`. A **view** is `{ zoom, x, y }` where `(x, y)` is the screen position of world (0, 0).

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  MARGIN, MAX_ZOOM, MIN_ZOOM, clampView, clampZoom, fitZoom, focusRect, panBy, scrollThumb, viewForSection, worldYAtCenter, zoomAbout,
} from '../src/components/board/engine/viewportMath.js';

const box = { viewW: 1000, viewH: 800, worldW: 1400, worldH: 20000 };

test('zoom is clamped to 0.25–4', () => {
  assert.equal(clampZoom(100), MAX_ZOOM);
  assert.equal(clampZoom(0.001), MIN_ZOOM);
  assert.equal(clampZoom(1.7), 1.7);
});

test('fitZoom fits the board width to the window', () => {
  assert.equal(fitZoom(1400), 1);
  assert.equal(fitZoom(700), 0.5);
  assert.equal(fitZoom(100), MIN_ZOOM);
});

test('zoomAbout keeps the world point under the cursor fixed', () => {
  const view = { zoom: 1, x: -100, y: -2000 };
  const sx = 300;
  const sy = 400;
  const before = { wx: (sx - view.x) / view.zoom, wy: (sy - view.y) / view.zoom };
  const next = zoomAbout(view, 2, sx, sy);
  assert.equal(next.zoom, 2);
  assert.ok(Math.abs((sx - next.x) / next.zoom - before.wx) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs((sy - next.y) / next.zoom - before.wy) < 1e-9);
});

test('zoomAbout respects the zoom limits and does not drift at them', () => {
  const view = { zoom: MAX_ZOOM, x: 0, y: 0 };
  assert.deepEqual(zoomAbout(view, 2, 500, 500), { zoom: MAX_ZOOM, x: 0, y: 0 });
});

test('a board narrower than the window is centred and cannot be panned sideways', () => {
  const v = clampView({ zoom: 0.5, x: 999, y: 0 }, box);
  assert.equal(v.x, (1000 - 700) / 2);
});

test('a board wider than the window can be panned only to its edges, plus a margin', () => {
  const left = clampView({ zoom: 1, x: 9999, y: 0 }, box);
  const right = clampView({ zoom: 1, x: -9999, y: 0 }, box);
  assert.equal(left.x, MARGIN);
  assert.equal(right.x, 1000 - 1400 - MARGIN);
});

test('vertical pan stops at the top and the bottom of the board', () => {
  assert.equal(clampView({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 5000 }, box).y, MARGIN);
  assert.equal(clampView({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: -999999 }, box).y, 800 - 20000 - MARGIN);
});

test('a board shorter than the window sits at the top margin', () => {
  assert.equal(clampView({ zoom: 0.25, x: 0, y: -500 }, { ...box, worldH: 1000 }).y, MARGIN);
});

test('panBy translates', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(panBy({ zoom: 2, x: 10, y: 20 }, 5, -5), { zoom: 2, x: 15, y: 15 });
});

test('focusRect centres a rectangle and never zooms past maxZoom', () => {
  const v = focusRect({ x: 600, y: 3000, w: 200, h: 100 }, { viewW: 1000, viewH: 800, pad: 100, maxZoom: 2.5 });
  assert.equal(v.zoom, 2.5);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(700 * v.zoom + v.x - 500) < 1e-9, 'rect centre x is at the window centre');
  assert.ok(Math.abs(3050 * v.zoom + v.y - 400) < 1e-9, 'rect centre y is at the window centre');
});

test('focusRect zooms out to fit a large rectangle', () => {
  const v = focusRect({ x: 0, y: 0, w: 1400, h: 2000 }, { viewW: 1000, viewH: 800, pad: 50 });
  assert.ok(v.zoom < 0.4);
});

test('scrollThumb tracks position through the board and never vanishes', () => {
  const top = scrollThumb({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 }, 800, 8000);
  assert.equal(top.top, 0);
  assert.equal(top.size, 0.1);
  const bottom = scrollThumb({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: -(8000 - 800) }, 800, 8000);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(bottom.top - 0.9) < 1e-9);
  const middle = scrollThumb({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: -3600 }, 800, 8000);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(middle.top - 0.45) < 1e-9);
  assert.equal(scrollThumb({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 }, 800, 500).size, 1, 'a board shorter than the window fills the track');
  assert.equal(scrollThumb({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 }, 800, 1e7).size, 0.04, 'a huge board still has a visible thumb');
});

test('worldYAtCenter and viewForSection', () => {
  assert.equal(worldYAtCenter({ zoom: 2, x: 0, y: -1000 }, 800), 700);
  const v = viewForSection({ y: 5000 }, { zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 });
  assert.equal(v.zoom, 1);
  assert.equal(v.y, -5000 + 24);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `client/src/components/board/engine/viewportMath.js`**

```js
import { WORLD_W } from './theme.js';

/**
 * Pan/zoom as plain maths. A view is { zoom, x, y }: (x, y) is where world
 * (0, 0) sits on screen, in pixels, so screen = world * zoom + (x, y).
 * Keeping it pure means the camera behaves the same on a 13" laptop and in a
 * test, and the controller is only wiring.
 */

export const MIN_ZOOM = 0.25;
export const MAX_ZOOM = 4;
export const MARGIN = 160;

const clamp = (v, lo, hi) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));

export const clampZoom = (z) => clamp(z, MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM);

export const fitZoom = (viewW, worldW = WORLD_W) => clampZoom(viewW / worldW);

export function clampView(view, { viewW, viewH, worldW = WORLD_W, worldH }) {
  const zoom = clampZoom(view.zoom);
  const w = worldW * zoom;
  const h = worldH * zoom;
  const x = w <= viewW ? (viewW - w) / 2 : clamp(view.x, viewW - w - MARGIN, MARGIN);
  const minY = viewH - h - MARGIN;
  const y = minY > MARGIN ? MARGIN : clamp(view.y, minY, MARGIN);
  return { zoom, x, y };
}

/** Zoom by `factor` keeping the world point under screen (sx, sy) where it is. */
export function zoomAbout(view, factor, sx, sy) {
  const zoom = clampZoom(view.zoom * factor);
  const k = zoom / view.zoom;
  return { zoom, x: sx - (sx - view.x) * k, y: sy - (sy - view.y) * k };
}

export const panBy = (view, dx, dy) => ({ zoom: view.zoom, x: view.x + dx, y: view.y + dy });

/** The view that centres a world rectangle, zoomed as far as fits (never past maxZoom). */
export function focusRect(rect, { viewW, viewH, pad = 120, maxZoom = 2.5 }) {
  const zoom = clamp(Math.min((viewW - pad * 2) / rect.w, (viewH - pad * 2) / rect.h), MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(maxZoom, MAX_ZOOM));
  return {
    zoom,
    x: viewW / 2 - (rect.x + rect.w / 2) * zoom,
    y: viewH / 2 - (rect.y + rect.h / 2) * zoom,
  };
}

export const worldYAtCenter = (view, viewH) => (viewH / 2 - view.y) / view.zoom;

/** Where a slim position indicator's thumb sits, as fractions (0–1) of its track. */
export function scrollThumb(view, viewH, worldH) {
  const total = worldH * view.zoom;
  const size = clamp(viewH / Math.max(total, 1), 0.04, 1);
  const range = Math.max(total - viewH, 1);
  const top = clamp((-view.y / range) * (1 - size), 0, 1 - size);
  return { top, size };
}

/** Scroll so a section's top sits just under the top of the window, keeping the current zoom. */
export function viewForSection(section, view) {
  return { zoom: view.zoom, x: view.x, y: -section.y * view.zoom + 24 };
}
```


- [ ] **Step 4: Write `client/src/components/board/editor/viewportController.js`**

```js
import { WORLD_W } from '../engine/theme.js';
import { clampView, fitZoom, focusRect, panBy, viewForSection, zoomAbout } from '../engine/viewportMath.js';

/**
 * The camera. Wheel pans, Ctrl/Cmd+wheel (and a trackpad pinch, which browsers
 * report as ctrl+wheel) zooms about the cursor, Shift+wheel pans sideways.
 * A mouse-wheel notch eases into place over a few frames; a trackpad glide is
 * applied as it arrives, because it is already smooth.
 *
 * The board is not a scrolled element, so there is no browser scroll height and
 * no canvas-size ceiling: the Fabric canvas is only ever as big as the window.
 */
export function createViewportController({ canvas, container, getWorld, onChange }) {
  let view = { zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 };
  let target = null;
  let raf = 0;
  let drag = null;

  const size = () => ({ viewW: container.clientWidth, viewH: container.clientHeight });
  const limits = () => ({ ...size(), worldW: WORLD_W, worldH: getWorld().height });

  function apply(next, { notify = true } = {}) {
    view = clampView(next, limits());
    canvas.setViewportTransform([view.zoom, 0, 0, view.zoom, view.x, view.y]);
    canvas.requestRenderAll();
    if (notify) onChange?.(view);
  }

  const stopEase = () => {
    cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    raf = 0;
    target = null;
  };

  function ease() {
    if (!target) return;
    const dx = target.x - view.x;
    const dy = target.y - view.y;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 0.5 && Math.abs(dy) < 0.5) {
      apply(target);
      stopEase();
      return;
    }
    apply({ zoom: view.zoom, x: view.x + dx * 0.28, y: view.y + dy * 0.28 });
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(ease);
  }

  function easeBy(dx, dy) {
    target = clampView(panBy(target || view, dx, dy), limits());
    if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(ease);
  }

  const lineHeight = (e, v) => (e.deltaMode === 1 ? v * 32 : e.deltaMode === 2 ? v * container.clientHeight : v);

  function onWheel(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
      stopEase();
      const factor = Math.exp(-lineHeight(e, e.deltaY) * 0.002);
      apply(zoomAbout(view, factor, e.clientX - rect.left, e.clientY - rect.top));
      return;
    }
    const dx = lineHeight(e, e.deltaX);
    const dy = lineHeight(e, e.deltaY);
    const sideways = e.shiftKey && dx === 0;
    const px = sideways ? dy : dx;
    const py = sideways ? 0 : dy;
    const notch = e.deltaMode === 0 && dx === 0 && Number.isInteger(dy) && Math.abs(dy) >= 50 && !sideways;
    if (notch) easeBy(-px, -py);
    else {
      stopEase();
      apply(panBy(view, -px, -py));
    }
  }

  container.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });

  const centre = () => {
    const { viewW, viewH } = size();
    return { sx: viewW / 2, sy: viewH / 2 };
  };

  return {
    get view() {
      return view;
    },
    set(next, opts) {
      stopEase();
      apply(next, opts);
    },
    zoomBy(factor, sx, sy) {
      stopEase();
      const c = centre();
      apply(zoomAbout(view, factor, sx ?? c.sx, sy ?? c.sy));
    },
    /** Fit the board's width to the window, keeping whatever is at the centre in view. */
    fit() {
      stopEase();
      const { viewW, viewH } = size();
      const worldY = (viewH / 2 - view.y) / view.zoom;
      const zoom = fitZoom(viewW);
      apply({ zoom, x: 0, y: viewH / 2 - worldY * zoom });
    },
    reset() {
      stopEase();
      const { viewW } = size();
      apply({ zoom: fitZoom(viewW), x: 0, y: 0 });
    },
    focusRect(rect) {
      stopEase();
      apply(focusRect(rect, size()));
    },
    goToSection(section) {
      stopEase();
      apply(viewForSection(section, view));
    },
    panBy(dx, dy) {
      stopEase();
      apply(panBy(view, dx, dy));
    },
    beginDrag(clientX, clientY) {
      stopEase();
      drag = { x: clientX, y: clientY };
    },
    moveDrag(clientX, clientY) {
      if (!drag) return;
      apply(panBy(view, clientX - drag.x, clientY - drag.y));
      drag = { x: clientX, y: clientY };
    },
    endDrag() {
      drag = null;
    },
    isDragging: () => drag !== null,
    resize() {
      const { viewW, viewH } = size();
      canvas.setDimensions({ width: viewW, height: viewH });
      apply(view);
    },
    dispose() {
      stopEase();
      container.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);
    },
  };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/util.js`:

```js
/** Small pure helpers the tools share — kept apart from Fabric so they can be tested. */

export function tint(color, alpha) {
  const m = /^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(color);
  if (!m) return color;
  const hex = m[1].length === 3 ? m[1].split('').map((c) => c + c).join('') : m[1];
  const n = parseInt(hex, 16);
  return `rgba(${(n >> 16) & 255},${(n >> 8) & 255},${n & 255},${alpha})`;
}

export function nearestPoint(points, x, y, maxDist) {
  let best = null;
  let bestDist = maxDist;
  for (const p of points) {
    const d = Math.hypot(p.x - x, p.y - y);
    if (d <= bestDist) {
      best = p;
      bestDist = d;
    }
  }
  return best ? { x: best.x, y: best.y } : null;
}

/** Where an arrow end may snap on an object: its centre and the midpoint of each edge. */
export function rectSnapPoints({ left, top, width, height }) {
  const cx = left + width / 2;
  const cy = top + height / 2;
  return [
    { x: cx, y: cy },
    { x: cx, y: top },
    { x: left + width, y: cy },
    { x: cx, y: top + height },
    { x: left, y: cy },
  ];
}

export function constrain45(anchor, p) {
  const dx = p.x - anchor.x;
  const dy = p.y - anchor.y;
  const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  const step = Math.PI / 4;
  const a = Math.round(Math.atan2(dy, dx) / step) * step;
  return { x: anchor.x + Math.cos(a) * len, y: anchor.y + Math.sin(a) * len };
}

/** Downscale so the longer side is at most `max`; never enlarge. */
export function scaledSize(w, h, max) {
  const longest = Math.max(w, h);
  if (longest <= max) return { w, h };
  const k = max / longest;
  return { w: Math.round(w * k), h: Math.round(h * k) };
}

/** Scale and position that centre a w×h image inside `box`, leaving `pad` all round. */
export function fitInto(w, h, box, pad = 0) {
  const scale = Math.min((box.w - pad * 2) / w, (box.h - pad * 2) / h);
  return { scale, left: box.x + (box.w - w * scale) / 2, top: box.y + (box.h - h * scale) / 2 };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/defaults.js`:

```js
const KEY = 'apollo-board-prefs';

/** What a new stroke, shape or text looks like. Remembered across boards and sessions. */
export const DEFAULT_PREFS = { color: 'problem', strokeWidth: 5, fontFamily: 'Inter', fontSize: 56 };

function load() {
  try {
    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFS, ...JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(KEY) || '{}') };
  } catch {
    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFS };
  }
}

export function createDefaults(onChange) {
  let prefs = load();
  return {
    get: () => prefs,
    set(patch) {
      prefs = { ...prefs, ...patch };
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(prefs));
      } catch {
        /* preferences are a convenience; never fail an edit over them */
      }
      onChange?.(prefs);
    },
  };
}
```

```js
/**
 * Owns which tool is active and routes the canvas's mouse events to it.
 *
 * A tool is a plain object of optional callbacks. `set` resets the canvas to
 * the neutral "select" baseline, then lets the tool bend it (a drawing mode, a
 * crosshair, hit-testing off). One-shot tools — shapes, arrows, text — hand
 * control back to Select when they finish, unless locked with a double-click.
 * `hold` is the temporary Hand while Space is down.
 */
export function createToolManager(editor, tools, { onTool } = {}) {
  const { canvas } = editor;
  let name = 'select';
  let tool = tools.select;
  let locked = false;
  let held = null;

  function baseline() {
    canvas.isDrawingMode = false;
    canvas.selection = true;
    canvas.skipTargetFind = false;
    canvas.defaultCursor = 'default';
    canvas.hoverCursor = 'move';
    canvas.freeDrawingCursor = 'crosshair';
  }

  function set(next, { lock = false } = {}) {
    if (!tools[next]) return;
    tool.deactivate?.();
    editor.renderer.setInteractive(true);
    baseline();
    name = next;
    tool = tools[next];
    locked = lock;
    if (next !== 'select' && next !== 'text' && next !== 'sticky') canvas.discardActiveObject();
    tool.activate?.();
    canvas.requestRenderAll();
    onTool?.(name, locked);
  }

  function finishOneShot() {
    if (!locked && tool.oneShot) set('select');
  }

  canvas.on('mouse:down', (opt) => tool.onDown?.(opt));
  canvas.on('mouse:move', (opt) => tool.onMove?.(opt));
  canvas.on('mouse:up', (opt) => tool.onUp?.(opt));
  canvas.on('path:created', (e) => tool.onPathCreated?.(e.path));
  canvas.on('text:editing:exited', () => {
    if (name === 'text' || name === 'sticky') finishOneShot();
  });

  return {
    set,
    finishOneShot,
    hold(next) {
      if (held) return;
      held = { name, locked };
      set(next);
    },
    release() {
      if (!held) return;
      const back = held;
      held = null;
      set(back.name, { lock: back.locked });
    },
    /** Tell the active tool the defaults (colour, width…) changed. */
    refresh() {
      tool.onDefaultsChanged?.();
    },
    get name() {
      return name;
    },
    get locked() {
      return locked;
    },
  };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/basicTools.js`:

```js
/** Select is the baseline; Hand drags the camera instead of anything on the board. */

export function selectTool() {
  return { name: 'select' };
}

export function handTool(editor) {
  const { canvas, viewport } = editor;
  return {
    name: 'hand',
    activate() {
      canvas.selection = false;
      canvas.skipTargetFind = true;
      canvas.defaultCursor = 'grab';
      canvas.hoverCursor = 'grab';
    },
    onDown(opt) {
      canvas.defaultCursor = 'grabbing';
      viewport.beginDrag(opt.e.clientX, opt.e.clientY);
    },
    onMove(opt) {
      viewport.moveDrag(opt.e.clientX, opt.e.clientY);
    },
    onUp() {
      canvas.defaultCursor = 'grab';
      viewport.endDrag();
    },
  };
}
```

```js
import { handTool, selectTool } from './basicTools.js';

/** The tool registry. Each later task adds its tools here. */
export function createTools(editor) {
  return {
    select: selectTool(editor),
    hand: handTool(editor),
  };
}
```
