- [ ] **Step 5: Write `client/src/components/board/editor/createEditor.js`**

```js
import { sectionAt } from '../state/anchor.js';
import { applyOperation, isViewOp } from '../state/boardState.js';
import { createHistory } from '../state/history.js';
import { isAnnotationId, newAnnotationId } from '../state/ids.js';
import { styleToFabric } from '../state/styleMap.js';
import { layoutBoard } from '../engine/layoutBoard.js';
import { createFabricMeasure } from '../engine/measure.js';
import { overrideFromGesture } from '../engine/overrides.js';
import { PAPER } from '../engine/theme.js';
import { worldYAtCenter } from '../engine/viewportMath.js';
import { createDefaults } from '../tools/defaults.js';
import { createTools } from '../tools/index.js';
import { createToolManager } from '../tools/toolManager.js';
import { nearestPoint, rectSnapPoints } from '../tools/util.js';
import { applyTraits, createAnnotationSync, recordFromObject } from './annotationSync.js';
import { registerLineArrow } from './LineArrow.js';
import { createRenderer } from './renderer.js';
import { createViewportController } from './viewportController.js';

const SNAP_PX = 15;
const OVERRIDE_DEFAULTS = { dx: 0, dy: 0, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, angle: 0 };
const same = (a, b) => (typeof a === 'number' && typeof b === 'number' ? Math.abs(a - b) < 1e-6 : a === b);

/** Selection handles that read as part of the recording tool rather than as browser chrome. */
function styleControls(fabric) {
  fabric.Object.prototype.set({
    transparentCorners: false, cornerColor: '#ffffff', cornerStrokeColor: '#1d4ed8', borderColor: '#1d4ed8',
    cornerStyle: 'circle', cornerSize: 11, borderScaleFactor: 2, padding: 4,
  });
}

/**
 * The board's editor: one Fabric canvas, the immutable board state, and the
 * only place the two are reconciled.
 *
 *   gesture / toolbar / Direct mode ──► dispatch(op) ──► state ──► reconcile ──► canvas
 *
 * A gesture on the canvas is committed *after* it finishes (`object:modified`),
 * turned into an operation, and the resulting state is reconciled back. Objects
 * remember the record they were made from (`__rec`) so reconciling a change
 * that started on the canvas does not tear down the object being edited.
 */
export function createEditor({ fabric, canvasEl, container, state, hooks = {} }) {
  registerLineArrow(fabric);
  styleControls(fabric);

  const measure = createFabricMeasure(fabric);
  const canvas = new fabric.Canvas(canvasEl, {
    width: container.clientWidth || 800,
    height: container.clientHeight || 600,
    backgroundColor: PAPER,
    selection: true,
    preserveObjectStacking: true,
    stopContextMenu: true,
    targetFindTolerance: 6,
  });

  let current = state;
  let scene = layoutBoard(current.spec, measure);
  const history = createHistory(100);
  const editor = {};

  /* ------------------------------ snapping ------------------------------ */

  function snapPoint(x, y, ignore) {
    const points = [];
    for (const obj of canvas.getObjects()) {
      if (obj === ignore || obj.visible === false || !obj.data || obj.type === 'lineArrow' || obj.type === 'line') continue;
      const r = obj.getBoundingRect(true, true);
      points.push(...rectSnapPoints(r));
    }
    return nearestPoint(points, x, y, SNAP_PX / viewport.view.zoom) || { x, y };
  }

  /* ------------------------------- parts ------------------------------- */

  const sync = createAnnotationSync(fabric, canvas, {
    onCreate: (obj) => {
      if (obj.type === 'lineArrow') obj.snap = snapPoint;
    },
  });
  const renderer = createRenderer(fabric, canvas, { objectById: (id) => sync.objectOf(id) });

  const sectionIndexAtCenter = () =>
    sectionAt(scene.sections, worldYAtCenter(viewport.view, container.clientHeight)).index;

  const viewport = createViewportController({
    canvas,
    container,
    getWorld: () => scene,
    onChange: (view) => {
      // The camera is remembered with the board but is not an edit: no history, no reconcile.
      current = { ...current, view: { zoom: view.zoom, x: view.x, y: view.y } };
      hooks.onView?.(view, sectionIndexAtCenter());
    },
  });

  /* ------------------------------ selection ----------------------------- */

  const objectForId = (id) => (isAnnotationId(id) ? sync.objectOf(id) : renderer.objectOf(id));
  const selectionIds = () => canvas.getActiveObjects().map((o) => o.data?.id).filter(Boolean);

  function getSelection() {
    return canvas.getActiveObjects()
      .filter((o) => o.data)
      .map((obj) => ({ obj, id: obj.data.id, kind: obj.data.kind, ann: obj.data.ann, type: obj.type }));
  }

  function restoreSelection(ids) {
    if (!ids.length) return;
    const objs = ids.map(objectForId).filter((o) => o && o.visible !== false);
    const active = canvas.getActiveObjects();
    if (objs.length === active.length && objs.every((o) => active.includes(o))) return;
    canvas.discardActiveObject();
    if (objs.length === 1) canvas.setActiveObject(objs[0]);
    else if (objs.length > 1) canvas.setActiveObject(new fabric.ActiveSelection(objs, { canvas }));
  }

  function select(ids) {
    const objs = ids.map(objectForId).filter(Boolean);
    canvas.discardActiveObject();
    if (objs.length === 1) canvas.setActiveObject(objs[0]);
    else if (objs.length > 1) canvas.setActiveObject(new fabric.ActiveSelection(objs, { canvas }));
    canvas.requestRenderAll();
  }

  /* -------------------------------- state ------------------------------- */

  function relayout() {
    scene = layoutBoard(current.spec, measure);
    canvas.discardActiveObject();
    renderer.rebuild(scene, current);
    sync.rebuildAll(current, scene.sections);
    viewport.resize(); // the world's height may have changed
  }

  function reconcile(prev, next) {
    if (prev.spec !== next.spec) {
      relayout();
      return;
    }
    if (prev.overrides !== next.overrides || prev.connections !== next.connections) renderer.patch(next);
    if (prev.annotations !== next.annotations) sync.reconcile(next, scene.sections);
    canvas.requestRenderAll();
  }

  function notify(fromCanvas = false, viewOnly = false) {
    hooks.onState?.(current, { canUndo: history.canUndo, canRedo: history.canRedo, viewOnly, fromCanvas });
  }

  function dispatch(input, { record = true, coalesce = null, fromCanvas = false } = {}) {
    const ops = Array.isArray(input) ? input : [input];
    let next = current;
    for (const op of ops) next = applyOperation(next, op);
    if (next === current) return false;

    const viewOnly = ops.every(isViewOp);
    if (record && !viewOnly) history.record(current, coalesce);
    const prev = current;
    current = next;
    const keep = fromCanvas ? [] : selectionIds();
    reconcile(prev, next);
    restoreSelection(keep);
    if (prev.view !== next.view && next.view && !fromCanvas) viewport.set(next.view, { notify: false });
    notify(fromCanvas, viewOnly);
    return true;
  }

  function swapTo(next) {
    const before = current;
    current = { ...next, view: before.view };
    canvas.discardActiveObject();
    reconcile(before, current);
    notify();
  }

  function undo() {
    const previous = history.undo(current);
    if (!previous) return false;
    swapTo(previous);
    return true;
  }

  function redo() {
    const next = history.redo(current);
    if (!next) return false;
    swapTo(next);
    return true;
  }

  /* ------------------------ committing canvas gestures ------------------ */

  function overrideOp(obj) {
    const item = renderer.itemOf(obj.data.id);
    if (!item) return null;
    const d = fabric.util.qrDecompose(obj.calcTransformMatrix());
    const patch = overrideFromGesture(
      item,
      { cx: d.translateX, cy: d.translateY, scaleX: d.scaleX, scaleY: d.scaleY, angle: d.angle, w: item.kind === 'text' ? obj.width : undefined },
      renderer.context()
    );
    if (item.kind === 'text' && typeof obj.text === 'string' && obj.text !== item.text) patch.text = obj.text;
    const existing = current.overrides[item.id] || {};
    const changed = Object.keys(patch).some((k) => !same(existing[k] ?? OVERRIDE_DEFAULTS[k] ?? (k === 'w' ? item.w : undefined), patch[k]));
    return changed ? { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: item.id, patch } : null;
  }

  /** Turn finished canvas objects into operations. Idempotent: an unchanged object records nothing. */
  function commitObjects(objs, { coalesce = null } = {}) {
    const ops = [];
    for (const obj of objs) {
      const kind = obj?.data?.kind;
      if (kind === 'annotation') {
        const { record, assets } = recordFromObject(obj, scene.sections);
        const before = obj.__rec;
        if (before && before.section === record.section && JSON.stringify(before.json) === JSON.stringify(record.json)) continue;
        obj.__rec = record;
        if (!before) sync.adopt(obj, record);
        ops.push({ type: before ? 'ANNOTATION_UPDATE' : 'ANNOTATION_ADD', record, assets });
      } else if (kind === 'presentation') {
        const op = overrideOp(obj);
        if (op) ops.push(op);
      }
    }
    if (ops.length) dispatch(ops, { fromCanvas: true, coalesce });
  }

  /** A multi-selection reports members in the group's coordinate space; ungroup first, commit, then regroup. */
  function commitTarget(target, opts) {
    if (target.type === 'activeSelection') {
      const members = target.getObjects().slice();
      canvas.discardActiveObject();
      commitObjects(members, opts);
      const live = members.filter((o) => o.canvas && o.visible !== false);
      if (live.length) canvas.setActiveObject(new fabric.ActiveSelection(live, { canvas }));
    } else {
      commitObjects([target], opts);
    }
  }

  function addObject(obj, ann) {
    obj.data = { kind: 'annotation', id: newAnnotationId(), ann };
    applyTraits(obj);
    if (obj.type === 'lineArrow') obj.snap = snapPoint;
    if (!obj.canvas) canvas.add(obj);
    commitObjects([obj]);
    return obj;
  }

  function deleteSelection() {
    const objs = canvas.getActiveObjects();
    if (!objs.length) return;
    canvas.discardActiveObject();
    const annotationIds = objs.filter((o) => o.data?.kind === 'annotation').map((o) => o.data.id);
    const pieceIds = objs.filter((o) => o.data?.kind === 'presentation').map((o) => o.data.id);
    const ops = [];
    if (annotationIds.length) ops.push({ type: 'ANNOTATION_REMOVE', ids: annotationIds });
    if (pieceIds.length) ops.push({ type: 'DELETE', targets: pieceIds });
    dispatch(ops);
  }

  function nudge(dx, dy) {
    const target = canvas.getActiveObject();
    if (!target || target.isEditing) return false;
    target.set({ left: target.left + dx, top: target.top + dy });
    target.setCoords();
    renderer.updateConnectors();
    commitTarget(target, { coalesce: 'nudge' });
    canvas.requestRenderAll();
    return true;
  }

  function applyStyle(changes) {
    const objs = canvas.getActiveObjects();
    const annotations = objs.filter((o) => o.data?.kind === 'annotation');
    const pieceIds = objs.filter((o) => o.data?.kind === 'presentation').map((o) => o.data.id);
    for (const obj of annotations) {
      obj.set(styleToFabric(changes, obj.data.ann));
      obj.dirty = true;
    }
    canvas.requestRenderAll();
    if (annotations.length) commitObjects(annotations, { coalesce: `style:${annotations.map((o) => o.data.id).join(',')}` });
    if (pieceIds.length) dispatch({ type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: pieceIds, changes }, { coalesce: `style:${pieceIds.join(',')}` });
  }

  /* ----------------------------- canvas events ---------------------------- */

  canvas.on('object:modified', (e) => e.target && commitTarget(e.target));
  const live = () => renderer.updateConnectors();
  canvas.on('object:moving', live);
  canvas.on('object:scaling', live);
  canvas.on('object:rotating', live);
  canvas.on('object:resizing', live);
  canvas.on('selection:created', () => hooks.onSelection?.(getSelection()));
  canvas.on('selection:updated', () => hooks.onSelection?.(getSelection()));
  canvas.on('selection:cleared', () => hooks.onSelection?.([]));

  // A text box a tool just made is not in the state until editing ends.
  canvas.on('text:editing:exited', (e) => {
    const obj = e.target;
    if (obj?.data?.kind !== 'annotation') return;
    if (!String(obj.text || '').trim()) {
      canvas.remove(obj);
      if (obj.__rec) dispatch({ type: 'ANNOTATION_REMOVE', ids: [obj.data.id] });
      return;
    }
    commitObjects([obj]);
  });

  /* ------------------------------- navigation ---------------------------- */

  function boundsOf(id) {
    const rectOf = (obj) => {
      const r = obj.getBoundingRect(true, true);
      return { x: r.left, y: r.top, w: r.width, h: r.height };
    };
    const single = objectForId(id);
    if (single) return rectOf(single);
    const parts = renderer.items().filter((e) => e.item.block === id).map((e) => rectOf(e.obj));
    if (!parts.length) return null;
    const x1 = Math.min(...parts.map((r) => r.x));
    const y1 = Math.min(...parts.map((r) => r.y));
    const x2 = Math.max(...parts.map((r) => r.x + r.w));
    const y2 = Math.max(...parts.map((r) => r.y + r.h));
    return { x: x1, y: y1, w: x2 - x1, h: y2 - y1 };
  }

  function stepSection(delta) {
    const v = viewport.view;
    const topY = -v.y / v.zoom;
    const at = sectionAt(scene.sections, topY + 60 / v.zoom);
    const startedInside = topY > at.y + 120 / v.zoom;
    const index = delta < 0 && startedInside ? at.index : at.index + delta;
    viewport.goToSection(scene.sections[Math.min(Math.max(index, 0), scene.sections.length - 1)]);
  }

  /* ------------------------------- lifecycle ------------------------------ */

  Object.assign(editor, {
    canvas,
    fabric,
    viewport,
    renderer,
    sync,
    defaults: createDefaults((d) => {
      editor.tools?.refresh();
      hooks.onDefaults?.(d);
    }),
    getState: () => current,
    getScene: () => scene,
    dispatch,
    undo,
    redo,
    /** Close a batch that was applied with `record: false` as one undo step (Direct mode). */
    recordUndoPoint(previous) {
      history.record(previous);
      notify();
    },
    canUndo: () => history.canUndo,
    canRedo: () => history.canRedo,
    commitObjects,
    commitTarget,
    addObject,
    deleteSelection,
    nudge,
    applyStyle,
    getSelection,
    select,
    deselect: () => canvas.discardActiveObject() && canvas.requestRenderAll(),
    boundsOf,
    focusObject(id) {
      const b = boundsOf(id);
      if (b) viewport.focusRect(b);
    },
    goToSection(i) {
      viewport.goToSection(scene.sections[Math.min(Math.max(i, 0), scene.sections.length - 1)]);
    },
    stepSection,
    currentSectionIndex: sectionIndexAtCenter,
    snapPoint,
    /** Replace the whole board (Load from file, reopen). Clears undo history. */
    loadState(next) {
      history.clear();
      current = { ...next };
      relayout();
      if (next.view) viewport.set(next.view, { notify: false });
      else viewport.reset();
      notify();
    },
    /** A regenerated section: swap it in and start undo history afresh (its old pieces no longer exist). */
    replaceSection(index, section) {
      dispatch({ type: 'SECTION_REPLACE', index, section }, { record: false });
      history.clear();
      notify();
    },
    dispose() {
      canvas.dispose();
      viewport.dispose();
      sync.dispose();
      renderer.dispose();
    },
  });

  editor.tools = createToolManager(editor, createTools(editor), { onTool: hooks.onTool });

  // First paint. The saved camera is read *before* `resize()`, which reports the default view through
  // onChange and would otherwise overwrite it in `current`.
  const savedView = current.view;
  renderer.rebuild(scene, current);
  sync.rebuildAll(current, scene.sections);
  viewport.resize();
  if (savedView) viewport.set(savedView, { notify: false });
  else viewport.reset();
  notify();

  return editor;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `client/src/components/board/BoardCanvas.jsx`**

`client/src/components/board/BoardCanvas.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { fabric } from 'fabric';
import { createEditor } from './editor/createEditor.js';
import { loadBoardFonts } from './engine/fonts.js';

/**
 * Mounts the editor. Fonts load first, because layout measures text and a
 * measurement taken before a face arrives is wrong for the life of the board.
 * The initial state is read once; after that the editor owns it.
 */
export default function BoardCanvas({ initialState, hooks, onEditor }) {
  const hostRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let disposed = false;
    let editor = null;
    let observer = null;

    loadBoardFonts().then(() => {
      if (disposed || !hostRef.current) return;
      editor = createEditor({ fabric, canvasEl: canvasRef.current, container: hostRef.current, state: initialState, hooks });
      observer = new ResizeObserver(() => editor.viewport.resize());
      observer.observe(hostRef.current);
      // A console handle for poking at the board while developing: window.__boardEditor.getState(), .dispatch(op), …
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) window.__boardEditor = editor;
      onEditor(editor);
    });

    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      observer?.disconnect();
      editor?.dispose();
      onEditor(null);
    };
    // The editor is created once per mounted board; `initialState` and `hooks` are deliberately read only then.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  return (
    <div ref={hostRef} className="absolute inset-0 touch-none overflow-hidden bg-white">
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

Replace the whole file (this version is extended in Tasks 17, 19 and 21):

`client/src/pages/BoardPage.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Maximize, Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useToast } from '../lib/toast.jsx';
import { IconButton, Spinner } from '../ui/primitives.jsx';
import BoardCanvas from '../components/board/BoardCanvas.jsx';
import { createAutosaver } from '../components/board/autosave.js';
import { boardStore } from '../components/board/boardStore.js';
import { EXAMPLE_ID, createExampleRecord } from '../components/board/exampleBoard.js';
import { recordFromState, stateFromRecord } from '../components/board/state/boardRecord.js';

/**
 * A board. Loads it from the browser's storage (the bundled example is created
 * on first open), mounts the editor, and saves every change 800 ms after it
 * settles — and immediately when the tab is hidden or closed.
 */
export default function BoardPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(null); // { record } | { missing: true }
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const editorRef = useRef(null);
  const metaRef = useRef(null);
  const saverRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    setLoaded(null);
    (async () => {
      let record = null;
      try {
        record = await boardStore.get(id);
        if (!record && id === EXAMPLE_ID) {
          record = createExampleRecord();
          await boardStore.put(record).catch(() => {});
        }
      } catch {
        record = null;
      }
      if (live) setLoaded(record ? { record } : { missing: true });
    })();
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [id]);

  // Autosave, with a guaranteed flush on the way out.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loaded?.record) return undefined;
    metaRef.current = { id: loaded.record.id, createdAt: loaded.record.createdAt };
    const saver = createAutosaver({
      save: (record) => boardStore.put(record),
      onError: (err) => toast.error("Couldn't save this board", err.message),
    });
    saverRef.current = saver;
    const flush = () => saver.flush();
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') flush();
    };
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', flush);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    return () => {
      flush();
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', flush);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    };
  }, [loaded, toast]);

  const hooks = useMemo(() => {
    const schedule = () => {
      const editor = editorRef.current;
      const saver = saverRef.current;
      if (!editor || !saver) return;
      saver.schedule(() => recordFromState(editor.getState(), metaRef.current));
    };
    return {
      onState: schedule,
      onView: (view) => {
        setZoom(view.zoom);
        schedule();
      },
    };
  }, []);

  const initialState = useMemo(() => (loaded?.record ? stateFromRecord(loaded.record) : null), [loaded]);

  if (!loaded) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center bg-void">
        <Spinner />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (loaded.missing) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-void text-ink">
        <p>That board isn't in this browser.</p>
        <Link to="/board" className="text-accent-text underline">Back to Jam board</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white">
      <BoardCanvas
        key={loaded.record.id}
        initialState={initialState}
        hooks={hooks}
        onEditor={(editor) => {
          editorRef.current = editor;
        }}
      />

      <Link
        to="/board"
        className="absolute left-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink shadow-pop"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={14} />

      <div className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-pop">
        <IconButton aria-label="Zoom out" onClick={() => editorRef.current?.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2)}><Minus size={14} /></IconButton>
        <span className="w-10 text-center font-mono text-2xs text-ink-2">{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
        <IconButton aria-label="Zoom in" onClick={() => editorRef.current?.viewport.zoomBy(1.2)}><Plus size={14} /></IconButton>
        <IconButton aria-label="Reset view" onClick={() => editorRef.current?.viewport.reset()}><Maximize size={14} /></IconButton>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/freehand.js`:

```js
import { resolveColor } from '../state/styleMap.js';

/**
 * Pen and highlighter. Fabric's PencilBrush draws the stroke; when it finishes
 * (`path:created`) the finished Path becomes an annotation like anything else.
 * The highlighter multiplies onto the page so it stays legible over text and
 * never hides what it marks.
 */
export function freehandTool(editor, kind) {
  const { canvas, fabric } = editor;
  const highlighter = kind === 'highlighter';

  function styleBrush() {
    const d = editor.defaults.get();
    const brush = canvas.freeDrawingBrush;
    if (!brush) return;
    brush.color = highlighter ? 'rgba(255, 221, 51, 0.5)' : resolveColor(d.color);
    brush.width = highlighter ? Math.max(24, d.strokeWidth * 5) : d.strokeWidth;
    brush.strokeLineCap = highlighter ? 'butt' : 'round';
  }

  return {
    name: kind,
    activate() {
      canvas.isDrawingMode = true;
      canvas.selection = false;
      canvas.freeDrawingBrush = new fabric.PencilBrush(canvas);
      styleBrush();
    },
    deactivate() {
      canvas.isDrawingMode = false;
    },
    onDefaultsChanged: styleBrush,
    onPathCreated(path) {
      if (highlighter) path.set('globalCompositeOperation', 'multiply');
      editor.addObject(path, highlighter ? 'highlight' : 'path');
    },
  };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/eraser.js`:

```js
/**
 * Object-level eraser: dragging across an annotation removes the whole
 * annotation. Presentation pieces are never erased — they are deleted with
 * Select + Delete, which is undoable and deliberate — so the tool turns their
 * hit-testing off while it is active and only ever sees what the person drew.
 * One drag is one undo step.
 */
export function eraserTool(editor) {
  const { canvas, renderer } = editor;
  let erasing = false;

  function hit(opt) {
    const target = canvas.findTarget(opt.e, false);
    if (target?.data?.kind === 'annotation') {
      editor.dispatch({ type: 'ANNOTATION_REMOVE', ids: [target.data.id] }, { coalesce: 'erase' });
    }
  }

  return {
    name: 'eraser',
    activate() {
      canvas.selection = false;
      canvas.defaultCursor = 'crosshair';
      canvas.hoverCursor = 'crosshair';
      renderer.setInteractive(false);
    },
    deactivate() {
      renderer.setInteractive(true);
    },
    onDown(opt) {
      erasing = true;
      hit(opt);
    },
    onMove(opt) {
      if (erasing) hit(opt);
    },
    onUp() {
      erasing = false;
    },
  };
}
```

Register them:

`client/src/components/board/tools/index.js`:

```js
import { eraserTool } from './eraser.js';
import { freehandTool } from './freehand.js';
import { handTool, selectTool } from './basicTools.js';

/** The tool registry. Each later task adds its tools here. */
export function createTools(editor) {
  return {
    select: selectTool(editor),
    hand: handTool(editor),
    pen: freehandTool(editor, 'pen'),
    highlighter: freehandTool(editor, 'highlighter'),
    eraser: eraserTool(editor),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `client/src/components/board/tools/keys.js`**

```js
/**
 * Keyboard shortcuts. Bare-key shortcuts are ignored whenever a person is
 * typing — in a text input, or inside a text box on the canvas — because "r"
 * and "e" must type letters there, not switch tools. They are also ignored
 * while a dialog is open: the dialog owns the keyboard, and reacting to its
 * Escape here would re-render the page between listeners and make the dialog's
 * own Escape handler miss the event. Ctrl/Cmd+key is left to the browser except
 * undo/redo and the Direct bar.
 */

const TOOL_KEYS = {
  v: 'select', h: 'hand', p: 'pen', m: 'highlighter', e: 'eraser', a: 'arrow', l: 'line',
  r: 'rectangle', c: 'circle', t: 'text', n: 'sticky',
};

export function attachKeys(editor, { onEscape, onHelp, onDirect } = {}) {
  const typing = () => {
    const el = document.activeElement;
    if (el && (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(el.tagName) || el.isContentEditable)) return true;
    return Boolean(editor.canvas.getActiveObject()?.isEditing);
  };

  const dialogOpen = () => Boolean(document.querySelector('[role="dialog"][aria-modal="true"]'));

  function onKeyDown(e) {
    if (typing() || dialogOpen()) return;
    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
    const mod = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;

    if (mod && key === 'z') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (e.shiftKey) editor.redo();
      else editor.undo();
      return;
    }
    if (mod && key === 'y') {
      e.preventDefault();
      editor.redo();
      return;
    }
    if (mod && key === 'k') {
      e.preventDefault();
      onDirect?.();
      return;
    }
    if (mod || e.altKey) return;

    if (e.code === 'Space') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (!e.repeat) editor.tools.hold('hand');
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      onEscape?.();
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
      if (editor.getSelection().length) {
        e.preventDefault();
        editor.deleteSelection();
      }
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === '?') {
      onHelp?.();
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === '/') {
      e.preventDefault();
      onDirect?.();
      return;
    }
    if (TOOL_KEYS[key]) {
      editor.tools.set(TOOL_KEYS[key]);
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'i') {
      editor.requestImage?.();
      return;
    }
    if (key === '+' || key === '=') return editor.viewport.zoomBy(1.2);
    if (key === '-' || key === '_') return editor.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2);
    if (key === '0') return editor.viewport.reset();
    if (key === 'f') {
      const first = editor.getSelection()[0];
      if (first) editor.focusObject(first.id);
      return;
    }
    if (key === '[') return editor.stepSection(-1);
    if (key === ']') return editor.stepSection(1);
    if (e.key === 'PageDown') return editor.stepSection(1);
    if (e.key === 'PageUp') return editor.stepSection(-1);
    if (e.key === 'Home') return editor.goToSection(0);
    if (e.key === 'End') return editor.goToSection(Infinity);

    const arrows = { ArrowLeft: [-1, 0], ArrowRight: [1, 0], ArrowUp: [0, -1], ArrowDown: [0, 1] };
    if (arrows[e.key]) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const [x, y] = arrows[e.key];
      const step = e.shiftKey ? 10 : 1;
      // With something selected the arrows nudge it; otherwise they scroll the board.
      if (!editor.nudge(x * step, y * step)) editor.viewport.panBy(-x * 60, -y * 60);
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  function onKeyUp(e) {
    if (e.code === 'Space') editor.tools.release();
  }

  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `client/src/components/board/Toolbar.jsx`**

```jsx
import {
  ArrowUpRight, Circle, Eraser, Hand, Highlighter, ImagePlus, Maximize, Minus, MousePointer2, Pen, Redo2,
  Square, StickyNote, Trash2, Type, Undo2, ZoomIn, ZoomOut,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { cx } from '../../lib/cx.js';
import { Tooltip } from '../../ui/primitives.jsx';

const TOOLS = [
  ['select', MousePointer2, 'Select', 'V'],
  ['hand', Hand, 'Hand', 'H'],
  ['pen', Pen, 'Pen', 'P'],
  ['highlighter', Highlighter, 'Highlighter', 'M'],
  ['eraser', Eraser, 'Eraser', 'E'],
  ['arrow', ArrowUpRight, 'Arrow', 'A'],
  ['line', Minus, 'Line', 'L'],
  ['rectangle', Square, 'Rectangle', 'R'],
  ['circle', Circle, 'Circle', 'C'],
  ['text', Type, 'Text', 'T'],
  ['sticky', StickyNote, 'Sticky note', 'N'],
];

function Btn({ label, hint, active, locked, onClick, onDoubleClick, disabled, children }) {
  return (
    <Tooltip label={label} hint={hint} side="right">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={label}
        aria-pressed={active}
        disabled={disabled}
        onClick={onClick}
        onDoubleClick={onDoubleClick}
        className={cx(
          'relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-md transition-colors duration-150',
          'disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-30',
          active ? 'bg-ink text-void' : 'text-ink-2 hover:bg-raised hover:text-ink'
        )}
      >
        {children}
        {locked && <span className="absolute bottom-1 right-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent" />}
      </button>
    </Tooltip>
  );
}

const Rule = () => <div className="mx-1 my-1 h-px bg-line" />;

/**
 * The floating tool bar. One click picks a tool; a double click locks it so
 * you can keep drawing arrows without going back to it each time. Tool names,
 * order and shortcuts match the keyboard map in tools/keys.js and the help sheet.
 */
export default function Toolbar({ ui, actions, compact = false }) {
  const { tool, locked, canUndo, canRedo } = ui;
  return (
    <div className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 z-10 flex -translate-y-1/2 flex-col rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-1.5 shadow-pop">
      {TOOLS.map(([name, Icon, label, key]) => (
        <Btn
          key={name}
          label={label}
          hint={key}
          active={tool === name}
          locked={tool === name && locked}
          onClick={() => actions.onTool(name, false)}
          onDoubleClick={() => actions.onTool(name, true)}
        >
          <Icon size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </Btn>
      ))}
      <Btn label="Add image" hint="I" onClick={actions.onImage}>
        <ImagePlus size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </Btn>
      <Rule />
      <Btn label="Undo" hint="Ctrl Z" disabled={!canUndo} onClick={actions.onUndo}><Undo2 size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} /></Btn>
      <Btn label="Redo" hint="Ctrl Shift Z" disabled={!canRedo} onClick={actions.onRedo}><Redo2 size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} /></Btn>
      {!compact && (
        <Btn label="Clear my annotations" onClick={actions.onClear}><Trash2 size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} /></Btn>
      )}
      <Rule />
      <Btn label="Zoom in" hint="+" onClick={actions.onZoomIn}><ZoomIn size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} /></Btn>
      <Btn label="Zoom out" hint="-" onClick={actions.onZoomOut}><ZoomOut size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} /></Btn>
      <Btn label="Reset view" hint="0" onClick={actions.onReset}><Maximize size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} /></Btn>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`client/src/pages/BoardPage.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Maximize, Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useToast } from '../lib/toast.jsx';
import { IconButton, Spinner } from '../ui/primitives.jsx';
import BoardCanvas from '../components/board/BoardCanvas.jsx';
import Toolbar from '../components/board/Toolbar.jsx';
import { createAutosaver } from '../components/board/autosave.js';
import { boardStore } from '../components/board/boardStore.js';
import { EXAMPLE_ID, createExampleRecord } from '../components/board/exampleBoard.js';
import { recordFromState, stateFromRecord } from '../components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { attachKeys } from '../components/board/tools/keys.js';

/**
 * A board. Loads it from the browser's storage (the bundled example is created
 * on first open), mounts the editor, and saves every change 800 ms after it
 * settles — and immediately when the tab is hidden or closed.
 */
export default function BoardPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(null); // { record } | { missing: true }
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [ui, setUi] = useState({ tool: 'select', locked: false, canUndo: false, canRedo: false });
  const [editor, setEditor] = useState(null);
  const editorRef = useRef(null);
  const metaRef = useRef(null);
  const saverRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    setLoaded(null);
    (async () => {
      let record = null;
      try {
        record = await boardStore.get(id);
        if (!record && id === EXAMPLE_ID) {
          record = createExampleRecord();
          await boardStore.put(record).catch(() => {});
        }
      } catch {
        record = null;
      }
      if (live) setLoaded(record ? { record } : { missing: true });
    })();
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [id]);

  // Autosave, with a guaranteed flush on the way out.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loaded?.record) return undefined;
    metaRef.current = { id: loaded.record.id, createdAt: loaded.record.createdAt };
    const saver = createAutosaver({
      save: (record) => boardStore.put(record),
      onError: (err) => toast.error("Couldn't save this board", err.message),
    });
    saverRef.current = saver;
    const flush = () => saver.flush();
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') flush();
    };
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', flush);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    return () => {
      flush();
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', flush);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    };
  }, [loaded, toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editor) return undefined;
    return attachKeys(editor, {
      onEscape: () => {
        editor.tools.set('select');
        editor.deselect();
      },
    });
  }, [editor]);

  const hooks = useMemo(() => {
    const schedule = () => {
      const current = editorRef.current;
      const saver = saverRef.current;
      if (!current || !saver) return;
      saver.schedule(() => recordFromState(current.getState(), metaRef.current));
    };
    return {
      onState: (state, meta) => {
        setUi((u) => ({ ...u, canUndo: meta.canUndo, canRedo: meta.canRedo }));
        schedule();
      },
      onTool: (tool, locked) => setUi((u) => ({ ...u, tool, locked })),
      onView: (view) => {
        setZoom(view.zoom);
        schedule();
      },
    };
  }, []);

  const initialState = useMemo(() => (loaded?.record ? stateFromRecord(loaded.record) : null), [loaded]);

  if (!loaded) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center bg-void">
        <Spinner />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (loaded.missing) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-void text-ink">
        <p>That board isn't in this browser.</p>
        <Link to="/board" className="text-accent-text underline">Back to Jam board</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white">
      <BoardCanvas
        key={loaded.record.id}
        initialState={initialState}
        hooks={hooks}
        onEditor={(e) => {
          editorRef.current = e;
          setEditor(e);
        }}
      />

      <Link
        to="/board"
        className="absolute left-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink shadow-pop"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={14} />
        <span className="max-w-[40vw] truncate">{initialState.spec.title}</span>
      </Link>

      {editor && (
        <Toolbar
          ui={ui}
          actions={{
            onTool: (name, lock) => editor.tools.set(name, { lock }),
            onUndo: () => editor.undo(),
            onRedo: () => editor.redo(),
            onClear: () => {
              if (window.confirm('Clear everything you drew or added? The board itself is not affected.')) {
                editor.dispatch({ type: 'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR' });
              }
            },
            onZoomIn: () => editor.viewport.zoomBy(1.2),
            onZoomOut: () => editor.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2),
            onReset: () => editor.viewport.reset(),
            onImage: () => editor.requestImage?.(),
          }}
        />
      )}

      <div className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-pop">
        <IconButton aria-label="Zoom out" onClick={() => editor?.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2)}><Minus size={14} /></IconButton>
        <span className="w-10 text-center font-mono text-2xs text-ink-2">{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
        <IconButton aria-label="Zoom in" onClick={() => editor?.viewport.zoomBy(1.2)}><Plus size={14} /></IconButton>
        <IconButton aria-label="Reset view" onClick={() => editor?.viewport.reset()}><Maximize size={14} /></IconButton>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tools**

`client/src/components/board/tools/shapes.js`:

```js
import { resolveColor } from '../state/styleMap.js';
import { tint } from './util.js';

/**
 * Rectangle and circle: drag to size (Shift keeps it square/round). A plain
 * click, with no drag, drops a default-sized shape at the click. The shape is
 * drawn straight onto the canvas while dragging and recorded once, on release.
 */
export function shapeTool(editor, shape) {
  const { canvas, fabric } = editor;
  let start = null;
  let obj = null;

  return {
    name: shape,
    oneShot: true,
    activate() {
      canvas.selection = false;
      canvas.skipTargetFind = true;
      canvas.defaultCursor = 'crosshair';
    },
    onDown(opt) {
      start = canvas.getPointer(opt.e);
      const d = editor.defaults.get();
      const color = resolveColor(d.color);
      const common = {
        originX: 'left', originY: 'top', left: start.x, top: start.y,
        fill: tint(color, 0.06), stroke: color, strokeWidth: d.strokeWidth, strokeUniform: true,
      };
      obj = shape === 'rectangle'
        ? new fabric.Rect({ ...common, width: 1, height: 1, rx: 10, ry: 10 })
        : new fabric.Ellipse({ ...common, rx: 1, ry: 1 });
      canvas.add(obj);
    },
    onMove(opt) {
      if (!obj) return;
      const p = canvas.getPointer(opt.e);
      let w = p.x - start.x;
      let h = p.y - start.y;
      if (opt.e.shiftKey) {
        const s = Math.max(Math.abs(w), Math.abs(h));
        w = Math.sign(w || 1) * s;
        h = Math.sign(h || 1) * s;
      }
      const left = Math.min(start.x, start.x + w);
      const top = Math.min(start.y, start.y + h);
      if (shape === 'rectangle') obj.set({ left, top, width: Math.abs(w), height: Math.abs(h) });
      else obj.set({ left, top, rx: Math.abs(w) / 2, ry: Math.abs(h) / 2 });
      obj.setCoords();
      canvas.requestRenderAll();
    },
    onUp() {
      if (!obj) return;
      if (obj.getScaledWidth() < 12 && obj.getScaledHeight() < 12) {
        const left = start.x - 120;
        const top = start.y - 75;
        if (shape === 'rectangle') obj.set({ left, top, width: 240, height: 150 });
        else obj.set({ left, top, rx: 120, ry: 75 });
        obj.setCoords();
      }
      const made = editor.addObject(obj, 'shape');
      obj = null;
      editor.tools.finishOneShot();
      editor.select([made.data.id]);
    },
  };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/lines.js`:

```js
import { arrowGeometry } from '../state/annotations.js';
import { resolveColor } from '../state/styleMap.js';
import { constrain45 } from './util.js';

/**
 * Line and arrow: press where it starts, drag to where it ends. Ends snap to
 * the centre or an edge midpoint of any nearby object (within 15 screen px),
 * Shift constrains to 45°, and a plain click drops a 180-unit arrow. Both are
 * the same LineArrow object; the arrow just has a head.
 */
export function lineTool(editor, kind) {
  const { canvas, fabric } = editor;
  const LineArrow = fabric.LineArrow;
  let start = null;
  let obj = null;

  return {
    name: kind,
    oneShot: true,
    activate() {
      canvas.selection = false;
      canvas.skipTargetFind = true;
      canvas.defaultCursor = 'crosshair';
    },
    onDown(opt) {
      const p = canvas.getPointer(opt.e);
      start = editor.snapPoint(p.x, p.y);
      const d = editor.defaults.get();
      obj = new LineArrow({
        ...arrowGeometry(start.x, start.y, start.x + 1, start.y),
        color: resolveColor(d.color), lineWidth: d.strokeWidth, dashed: false, headEnd: kind === 'arrow', headStart: false,
      });
      canvas.add(obj);
    },
    onMove(opt) {
      if (!obj) return;
      const p = canvas.getPointer(opt.e);
      const end = opt.e.shiftKey ? constrain45(start, p) : editor.snapPoint(p.x, p.y, obj);
      obj.setEndpoints(start.x, start.y, end.x, end.y);
      canvas.requestRenderAll();
    },
    onUp() {
      if (!obj) return;
      const e = obj.getEndpoints();
      if (Math.hypot(e.x2 - e.x1, e.y2 - e.y1) < 8) obj.setEndpoints(start.x, start.y, start.x + 180, start.y);
      const made = editor.addObject(obj, kind);
      obj = null;
      editor.tools.finishOneShot();
      editor.select([made.data.id]);
    },
  };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/textTools.js`:

```js
import { newAnnotationId } from '../state/ids.js';
import { stickyRecord, textRecord } from '../state/annotations.js';

/**
 * Text and sticky note: click to place, type immediately. The box is not in
 * the board state until editing ends (an empty one is simply discarded), and
 * the tool hands back to Select at that moment — see toolManager and the
 * `text:editing:exited` handler in createEditor. Clicking away while typing
 * only finishes the edit; it never starts a second box.
 */
export function textTool(editor, kind) {
  const { canvas, fabric } = editor;
  return {
    name: kind,
    oneShot: true,
    activate() {
      canvas.selection = false;
      canvas.skipTargetFind = true;
      canvas.defaultCursor = 'text';
    },
    onDown(opt) {
      const active = canvas.getActiveObject();
      if (active?.isEditing) {
        canvas.discardActiveObject();
        canvas.requestRenderAll();
        return;
      }
      const p = canvas.getPointer(opt.e);
      const d = editor.defaults.get();
      const id = newAnnotationId();
      // The record builders take a section-relative y; here it is a world y, which is fine:
      // the box is converted to a real record (with its true section) when editing ends.
      const rec = kind === 'text'
        ? textRecord({ id, section: '', x: p.x, y: p.y, text: 'Text', style: { fontFamily: d.fontFamily, fontSize: d.fontSize, color: d.color } })
        : stickyRecord({ id, section: '', x: p.x, y: p.y });
      const box = new fabric.Textbox(rec.json.text, rec.json);
      box.set('data', rec.json.data);
      canvas.add(box);
      canvas.setActiveObject(box);
      box.enterEditing();
      box.selectAll();
      canvas.requestRenderAll();
    },
  };
}
```

Register:

```js
import { eraserTool } from './eraser.js';
import { freehandTool } from './freehand.js';
import { handTool, selectTool } from './basicTools.js';
import { lineTool } from './lines.js';
import { shapeTool } from './shapes.js';
import { textTool } from './textTools.js';

/** The tool registry. Every tool the toolbar offers is one entry here. */
export function createTools(editor) {
  return {
    select: selectTool(editor),
    hand: handTool(editor),
    pen: freehandTool(editor, 'pen'),
    highlighter: freehandTool(editor, 'highlighter'),
    eraser: eraserTool(editor),
    rectangle: shapeTool(editor, 'rectangle'),
    circle: shapeTool(editor, 'circle'),
    line: lineTool(editor, 'line'),
    arrow: lineTool(editor, 'arrow'),
    text: textTool(editor, 'text'),
    sticky: textTool(editor, 'sticky'),
  };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/tools/images.js`:

```js
import { fitInto, scaledSize } from './util.js';

/**
 * Screenshots are how this board gets used: a Google result, a DevTools panel,
 * a PageSpeed report. So images arrive three ways — the toolbar button, a file
 * dragged onto the board, and Ctrl+V straight from the clipboard — and behave
 * like any other object once they land (move, scale, rotate, delete, undo).
 */

export const IMAGE_TYPES = ['image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/webp'];
export const MAX_SIDE = 1600;
export const isImageType = (type) => IMAGE_TYPES.includes(type);

/** Read a file as a data URL, downscaled so the long side is at most MAX_SIDE (a saved board must stay small). */
export function readImageFile(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (!isImageType(file.type)) {
      reject(new Error('Please add a PNG, JPG or WebP image.'));
      return;
    }
    const unreadable = () => reject(new Error("Couldn't read that image file."));
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = unreadable;
    reader.onload = () => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = unreadable;
      img.onload = () => {
        const { w, h } = scaledSize(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight, MAX_SIDE);
        if (w === img.naturalWidth && h === img.naturalHeight) {
          resolve({ dataUrl: reader.result, w, h });
          return;
        }
        const off = document.createElement('canvas');
        off.width = w;
        off.height = h;
        off.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h);
        resolve({ dataUrl: off.toDataURL(file.type, 0.92), w, h });
      };
      img.src = reader.result;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

/** The body of a browser frame under a world point, if there is one: that is where a screenshot belongs. */
function dropTargetAt(editor, point) {
  for (const { item, obj } of editor.renderer.items()) {
    if (!item.drop || obj.visible === false) continue;
    const r = obj.getBoundingRect(true, true);
    if (point.x >= r.left && point.x <= r.left + r.width && point.y >= r.top && point.y <= r.top + r.height) {
      return { x: r.left, y: r.top, w: r.width, h: r.height };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/** Place an image at `at` (a world point), or at the middle of the window. */
export function addImage(editor, { dataUrl, w, h }, at) {
  const { fabric, canvas, viewport } = editor;
  const v = viewport.view;
  const point = at || { x: (canvas.getWidth() / 2 - v.x) / v.zoom, y: (canvas.getHeight() / 2 - v.y) / v.zoom };
  const target = dropTargetAt(editor, point);

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    fabric.Image.fromURL(dataUrl, (img) => {
      let scale;
      if (target) {
        const fit = fitInto(w, h, target, 12);
        scale = fit.scale;
        img.set({ left: fit.left, top: fit.top });
      } else {
        scale = Math.min(1, 640 / w, 480 / h);
        img.set({ left: point.x - (w * scale) / 2, top: point.y - (h * scale) / 2 });
      }
      img.set({ originX: 'left', originY: 'top', scaleX: scale, scaleY: scale });
      const made = editor.addObject(img, 'image');
      editor.select([made.data.id]);
      resolve(made);
    });
  });
}

const typing = (editor) => {
  const el = document.activeElement;
  return Boolean((el && ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(el.tagName)) || editor.canvas.getActiveObject()?.isEditing);
};

export function attachImageInputs(editor, { host, input, onError }) {
  const handle = async (files, at) => {
    for (const file of files) {
      try {
        await addImage(editor, await readImageFile(file), at);
      } catch (err) {
        onError?.(err.message);
      }
    }
  };

  const onChange = () => {
    handle([...input.files]);
    input.value = '';
  };
  const onDragOver = (e) => {
    if ([...(e.dataTransfer?.types || [])].includes('Files')) e.preventDefault();
  };
  const onDrop = (e) => {
    const files = [...(e.dataTransfer?.files || [])].filter((f) => f.type.startsWith('image/'));
    if (!files.length) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    handle(files, editor.canvas.getPointer(e));
  };
  const onPaste = (e) => {
    if (typing(editor)) return;
    const files = [...(e.clipboardData?.files || [])].filter((f) => f.type.startsWith('image/'));
    if (!files.length) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    handle(files);
  };

  editor.requestImage = () => input.click();
  input.addEventListener('change', onChange);
  host.addEventListener('dragover', onDragOver);
  host.addEventListener('drop', onDrop);
  window.addEventListener('paste', onPaste);
  return () => {
    input.removeEventListener('change', onChange);
    host.removeEventListener('dragover', onDragOver);
    host.removeEventListener('drop', onDrop);
    window.removeEventListener('paste', onPaste);
    delete editor.requestImage;
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Wire it into `BoardCanvas.jsx`**

Replace the whole file:

`client/src/components/board/BoardCanvas.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { fabric } from 'fabric';
import { createEditor } from './editor/createEditor.js';
import { loadBoardFonts } from './engine/fonts.js';
import { attachImageInputs } from './tools/images.js';

/**
 * Mounts the editor. Fonts load first, because layout measures text and a
 * measurement taken before a face arrives is wrong for the life of the board.
 * The initial state is read once; after that the editor owns it.
 */
export default function BoardCanvas({ initialState, hooks, onEditor }) {
  const hostRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const inputRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let disposed = false;
    let editor = null;
    let observer = null;
    let detachImages = null;

    loadBoardFonts().then(() => {
      if (disposed || !hostRef.current) return;
      editor = createEditor({ fabric, canvasEl: canvasRef.current, container: hostRef.current, state: initialState, hooks });
      observer = new ResizeObserver(() => editor.viewport.resize());
      observer.observe(hostRef.current);
      detachImages = attachImageInputs(editor, {
        host: hostRef.current,
        input: inputRef.current,
        onError: (message) => hooks.onError?.(message),
      });
      // A console handle for poking at the board while developing: window.__boardEditor.getState(), .dispatch(op), …
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) window.__boardEditor = editor;
      onEditor(editor);
    });

    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      detachImages?.();
      observer?.disconnect();
      editor?.dispose();
      onEditor(null);
    };
    // The editor is created once per mounted board; `initialState` and `hooks` are deliberately read only then.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  return (
    <div ref={hostRef} className="absolute inset-0 touch-none overflow-hidden bg-white">
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
      <input ref={inputRef} type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" multiple hidden />
    </div>
  );
}
```

  - `<PropertyPanel selection tool defaults onApply onDefaults compact? />`. `onApply(changes)` takes **semantic** style keys (the vocabulary of `sanitizeStyle`); `onDefaults(patch)` takes `{ color, strokeWidth, fontFamily, fontSize }`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `propertyModel.js`**

`client/src/components/board/propertyModel.js`:

```js
/**
 * What the property panel should show for a selection, read off the live
 * Fabric objects (the canvas is the truth while a person is editing). Pure
 * apart from reading properties, so it is tested with plain objects.
 */

const ANNOTATION_MODE = {
  text: 'text', sticky: 'text', shape: 'shape', arrow: 'line', line: 'line', path: 'stroke', highlight: 'stroke', image: 'image',
};

export function modeOf(entry) {
  if (entry.kind === 'annotation') return ANNOTATION_MODE[entry.ann] || 'other';
  if (entry.type === 'textbox') return 'text';
  if (entry.type === 'group') return 'piece';
  return 'other';
}

const isBold = (w) => w === 'bold' || Number(w) >= 600;

export function describeSelection(selection) {
  if (!selection.length) return { mode: 'none', values: {} };
  const modes = new Set(selection.map(modeOf));
  if (modes.size > 1) return { mode: 'mixed', values: {} };
  const mode = [...modes][0];
  const o = selection[0].obj;

  switch (mode) {
    case 'text':
      return {
        mode,
        values: {
          fontFamily: o.fontFamily, fontSize: o.fontSize, bold: isBold(o.fontWeight), italic: o.fontStyle === 'italic',
          underline: Boolean(o.underline), color: o.fill, align: o.textAlign, lineHeight: o.lineHeight, letterSpacing: o.charSpacing,
        },
      };
    case 'shape':
      return {
        mode,
        values: {
          fill: o.fill, stroke: o.stroke, strokeWidth: o.strokeWidth, opacity: o.opacity ?? 1, radius: o.rx, isRect: o.type === 'rect',
        },
      };
    case 'line':
      return {
        mode,
        values: { color: o.color, strokeWidth: o.lineWidth, dashed: Boolean(o.dashed), headEnd: Boolean(o.headEnd), headStart: Boolean(o.headStart) },
      };
    case 'stroke':
      return { mode, values: { color: o.stroke, strokeWidth: o.strokeWidth, opacity: o.opacity ?? 1 } };
    case 'image':
      return { mode, values: { opacity: o.opacity ?? 1 } };
    case 'piece': {
      const [shape = {}, label = {}] = o.getObjects ? o.getObjects() : [];
      return {
        mode,
        values: { fill: shape.fill, stroke: shape.stroke, strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth, opacity: o.opacity ?? 1, color: label.fill, fontSize: label.fontSize },
      };
    }
    default:
      return { mode: 'other', values: {} };
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `PropertyPanel.jsx`**

`client/src/components/board/PropertyPanel.jsx`:

```jsx
import { AlignCenter, AlignLeft, AlignRight, Bold, ChevronsRight, Italic, Underline } from 'lucide-react';
import { cx } from '../../lib/cx.js';
import { ColorField, IconToggle, NumberField, PropRow, Segmented, SelectField } from '../../ui/fields.jsx';
import { FONT_FAMILIES, TONE_COLORS } from './engine/theme.js';
import { describeSelection } from './propertyModel.js';
import { resolveColor } from './state/styleMap.js';

const DRAWING_TOOLS = ['pen', 'highlighter', 'arrow', 'line', 'rectangle', 'circle', 'text'];
const TONES = ['problem', 'ink', 'money', 'movement', 'tech'];

/** The six board colours as one-click swatches, then a free picker. `value` may be a tone name or CSS colour. */
function Swatches({ value, onPick }) {
  const current = String(resolveColor(value) || '').toLowerCase();
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
      {TONES.map((tone) => {
        const hex = TONE_COLORS[tone];
        const active = current === hex.toLowerCase();
        return (
          <button
            key={tone}
            type="button"
            aria-label={tone}
            title={tone}
            onClick={() => onPick(tone)}
            className={cx('h-5 w-5 rounded-full border transition-transform hover:scale-110', active ? 'border-ink ring-2 ring-accent/60' : 'border-line-strong')}
            style={{ background: hex }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

const Group = ({ children }) => <div className="space-y-2">{children}</div>;

function TextControls({ v, apply }) {
  return (
    <Group>
      <SelectField name="Font" value={v.fontFamily} options={FONT_FAMILIES} onChange={(fontFamily) => apply({ fontFamily })} />
      <PropRow label="Size">
        <NumberField name="Font size" label="Aa" value={v.fontSize} min={8} max={400} onChange={(fontSize) => apply({ fontSize })} className="flex-1" />
        <IconToggle icon={Bold} label="Bold" active={v.bold} onClick={() => apply({ fontWeight: v.bold ? 'normal' : 'bold' })} />
        <IconToggle icon={Italic} label="Italic" active={v.italic} onClick={() => apply({ italic: !v.italic })} />
        <IconToggle icon={Underline} label="Underline" active={v.underline} onClick={() => apply({ underline: !v.underline })} />
      </PropRow>
      <PropRow label="Colour"><Swatches value={v.color} onPick={(color) => apply({ color })} /></PropRow>
      <ColorField label="Custom" value={v.color} onChange={(color) => apply({ color })} />
      <PropRow label="Align">
        <Segmented
          value={v.align}
          onChange={(align) => apply({ align })}
          options={[
            { value: 'left', icon: AlignLeft, title: 'Left' },
            { value: 'center', icon: AlignCenter, title: 'Centre' },
            { value: 'right', icon: AlignRight, title: 'Right' },
          ]}
        />
      </PropRow>
      <PropRow label="Spacing">
        <NumberField name="Line height percent" label="LH" value={Math.round((v.lineHeight || 1.1) * 100)} min={60} max={300} onChange={(n) => apply({ lineHeight: n / 100 })} className="flex-1" />
        <NumberField name="Letter spacing" label="LS" value={v.letterSpacing || 0} min={-100} max={800} onChange={(letterSpacing) => apply({ letterSpacing })} className="flex-1" />
      </PropRow>
    </Group>
  );
}

function ShapeControls({ v, apply }) {
  return (
    <Group>
      <PropRow label="Border"><Swatches value={v.stroke} onPick={(stroke) => apply({ stroke })} /></PropRow>
      <ColorField label="Fill" value={v.fill} onChange={(fill) => apply({ fill })} />
      <ColorField label="Border" value={v.stroke} onChange={(stroke) => apply({ stroke })} />
      <PropRow label="Width">
        <NumberField name="Border width" label="W" value={v.strokeWidth} min={0} max={40} onChange={(strokeWidth) => apply({ strokeWidth })} className="flex-1" />
        {v.isRect && <NumberField name="Corner radius" label="R" value={v.radius || 0} min={0} max={200} onChange={(radius) => apply({ radius })} className="flex-1" />}
      </PropRow>
      <PropRow label="Opacity">
        <NumberField name="Opacity percent" label="%" value={Math.round(v.opacity * 100)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(n) => apply({ opacity: n / 100 })} className="flex-1" />
      </PropRow>
    </Group>
  );
}

function LineControls({ v, apply }) {
  return (
    <Group>
      <PropRow label="Colour"><Swatches value={v.color} onPick={(color) => apply({ color })} /></PropRow>
      <ColorField label="Custom" value={v.color} onChange={(color) => apply({ color })} />
      <PropRow label="Width">
        <NumberField name="Line width" label="W" value={v.strokeWidth} min={1} max={40} onChange={(strokeWidth) => apply({ strokeWidth })} className="flex-1" />
        <IconToggle icon={ChevronsRight} label="Dashed" active={v.dashed} onClick={() => apply({ dash: !v.dashed })} />
      </PropRow>
      <PropRow label="Heads">
        <Segmented
          value={`${v.headStart ? 1 : 0}${v.headEnd ? 1 : 0}`}
          onChange={(k) => apply({ headStart: k[0] === '1', headEnd: k[1] === '1' })}
          options={[{ value: '00', label: 'None' }, { value: '01', label: 'End' }, { value: '10', label: 'Start' }, { value: '11', label: 'Both' }]}
        />
      </PropRow>
    </Group>
  );
}

function StrokeControls({ v, apply }) {
  return (
    <Group>
      <PropRow label="Colour"><Swatches value={v.color} onPick={(color) => apply({ color })} /></PropRow>
      <ColorField label="Custom" value={v.color} onChange={(color) => apply({ color })} />
      <PropRow label="Width">
        <NumberField name="Stroke width" label="W" value={v.strokeWidth} min={1} max={80} onChange={(strokeWidth) => apply({ strokeWidth })} className="flex-1" />
        <NumberField name="Opacity percent" label="%" value={Math.round(v.opacity * 100)} min={5} max={100} onChange={(n) => apply({ opacity: n / 100 })} className="flex-1" />
      </PropRow>
    </Group>
  );
}

function PieceControls({ v, apply }) {
  return (
    <Group>
      <ColorField label="Fill" value={v.fill} onChange={(fill) => apply({ fill })} />
      <ColorField label="Border" value={v.stroke} onChange={(stroke) => apply({ stroke })} />
      <ColorField label="Text" value={v.color} onChange={(color) => apply({ color })} />
      <PropRow label="Size">
        <NumberField name="Label size" label="Aa" value={v.fontSize || 22} min={8} max={200} onChange={(fontSize) => apply({ fontSize })} className="flex-1" />
        <NumberField name="Opacity percent" label="%" value={Math.round(v.opacity * 100)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(n) => apply({ opacity: n / 100 })} className="flex-1" />
      </PropRow>
    </Group>
  );
}

/** With nothing selected, a drawing tool's panel edits what the *next* mark will look like. */
function DefaultsControls({ tool, defaults, onDefaults }) {
  const text = tool === 'text';
  return (
    <Group>
      <PropRow label="Colour"><Swatches value={defaults.color} onPick={(color) => onDefaults({ color })} /></PropRow>
      {text ? (
        <>
          <SelectField name="Font" value={defaults.fontFamily} options={FONT_FAMILIES} onChange={(fontFamily) => onDefaults({ fontFamily })} />
          <PropRow label="Size"><NumberField name="Font size" label="Aa" value={defaults.fontSize} min={8} max={400} onChange={(fontSize) => onDefaults({ fontSize })} className="flex-1" /></PropRow>
        </>
      ) : (
        <PropRow label="Width"><NumberField name="Stroke width" label="W" value={defaults.strokeWidth} min={1} max={40} onChange={(strokeWidth) => onDefaults({ strokeWidth })} className="flex-1" /></PropRow>
      )}
    </Group>
  );
}

const TITLES = { text: 'Text', shape: 'Shape', line: 'Line', stroke: 'Stroke', piece: 'Piece', image: 'Image' };

export default function PropertyPanel({ selection, tool, defaults, onApply, onDefaults, compact = false }) {
  const { mode, values } = describeSelection(selection);
  const drawing = DRAWING_TOOLS.includes(tool);
  const showDefaults = mode === 'none' && drawing;
  if (!showDefaults && !TITLES[mode]) return null;

  return (
    <div className={cx('w-[248px] rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-3 shadow-pop', compact && 'scale-90')}>
      <p className="label mb-2">{showDefaults ? `New ${tool}` : TITLES[mode]}</p>
      {showDefaults && <DefaultsControls tool={tool} defaults={defaults} onDefaults={onDefaults} />}
      {mode === 'text' && <TextControls v={values} apply={onApply} />}
      {mode === 'shape' && <ShapeControls v={values} apply={onApply} />}
      {mode === 'line' && <LineControls v={values} apply={onApply} />}
      {mode === 'stroke' && <StrokeControls v={values} apply={onApply} />}
      {mode === 'piece' && <PieceControls v={values} apply={onApply} />}
      {mode === 'image' && (
        <PropRow label="Opacity">
          <NumberField name="Opacity percent" label="%" value={Math.round(values.opacity * 100)} min={5} max={100} onChange={(n) => onApply({ opacity: n / 100 })} className="flex-1" />
        </PropRow>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Run `npm run dev`, open `/board/example`. Verify (no console errors):

6. Select a **piece of the deck** (a step label, a node): the panel titled Text/Piece changes it, and a refresh keeps the change. `Ctrl+Z` undoes the whole drag of a slider as **one** step.
7. Select two different kinds of object with a marquee: no panel appears.
8. With the Pen tool active and nothing selected, "New pen" offers colour and width; the next stroke uses them. Reload: the choice is remembered.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `boardActions.js`**

`client/src/components/board/boardActions.js`:

```js
/** Small, pure helpers behind the page's file and section actions. */

export function slugify(title) {
  return String(title).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '') || 'board';
}

/** Has the person moved, edited or deleted anything in this section? `s1` must not match `s13`. */
export function sectionHasWork(state, sectionId) {
  return Object.keys(state.overrides).some((id) => id.startsWith(`${sectionId}.`));
}

/** What the server needs to rebuild one section: the outline, without the blocks. */
export function outlineFromSpec(spec) {
  return {
    title: spec.title,
    audience: spec.audience || '',
    sections: spec.sections.map(({ title, beat, density }) => ({ title, beat, density })),
  };
}

export function downloadText(filename, text, mime = 'application/json') {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: mime }));
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}
```

```jsx
import { Maximize, Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { IconButton } from '../../ui/primitives.jsx';
import { scrollThumb } from './engine/viewportMath.js';

export function ZoomControls({ zoom, onIn, onOut, onReset }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-pop">
      <IconButton aria-label="Zoom out" onClick={onOut}><Minus size={14} /></IconButton>
      <span className="w-10 text-center font-mono text-2xs text-ink-2">{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
      <IconButton aria-label="Zoom in" onClick={onIn}><Plus size={14} /></IconButton>
      <IconButton aria-label="Reset view" onClick={onReset}><Maximize size={14} /></IconButton>
    </div>
  );
}

/** A hairline down the right edge: how far through the board you are. Display only. */
export function ScrollIndicator({ view, worldH }) {
  const { top, size } = scrollThumb(view, window.innerHeight, worldH);
  return (
    <div aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-16 right-1.5 top-16 z-10 w-1 rounded-full bg-ink/10">
      <div className="absolute inset-x-0 rounded-full bg-ink/35" style={{ top: `${top * 100}%`, height: `${size * 100}%` }} />
    </div>
  );
}

/** "4 / 12 — YOU PAID FOR THE CLICK": where you are, at a glance, on the recording. */
export function SectionPill({ index, total, title }) {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-[46vw] truncate rounded-full bg-ink px-3.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-void shadow-pop">
      {index + 1} / {total} — {title}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`client/src/components/board/SectionRail.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { RefreshCw, TriangleAlert, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { cx } from '../../lib/cx.js';
import { Button, IconButton, Spinner, Tooltip } from '../../ui/primitives.jsx';

/**
 * Every section, one click away — and a way to ask for a better one. A section
 * DeepSeek could not build shows a warning; "Rebuild" rebuilds just that section
 * (optionally with a note like "make the graph much bigger") and leaves the
 * rest of the board, and everything you drew, alone.
 */
export default function SectionRail({ sections, current, busyIndex, canRegenerate, onGo, onRegenerate, onClose }) {
  const [openNote, setOpenNote] = useState(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');

  return (
    <aside className="absolute bottom-16 right-4 top-16 z-10 flex w-72 flex-col rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface shadow-pop">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line px-3 py-2">
        <p className="label">Sections</p>
        <IconButton aria-label="Close sections" onClick={onClose}><X size={14} /></IconButton>
      </div>
      <ol className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-1.5">
        {sections.map((section, i) => (
          <li key={section.id} className={cx('rounded-lg', i === current && 'bg-raised')}>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => onGo(i)} className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[13px] text-ink">
                <span className="w-5 shrink-0 font-mono text-2xs text-ink-3">{i + 1}</span>
                <span className="truncate">{section.title}</span>
                {section.degraded && (
                  <Tooltip label="Apollo couldn't build this one properly" side="left">
                    <TriangleAlert size={12} className="shrink-0 text-danger" />
                  </Tooltip>
                )}
              </button>
              {busyIndex === i ? (
                <Spinner size={12} className="mr-2" />
              ) : (
                <Tooltip label={canRegenerate ? 'Rebuild this section' : 'This board has no brief to rebuild from'} side="left">
                  <IconButton
                    aria-label={`Rebuild section ${i + 1}`}
                    disabled={!canRegenerate || busyIndex !== null}
                    onClick={() => { setOpenNote(openNote === i ? null : i); setNote(''); }}
                  >
                    <RefreshCw size={13} />
                  </IconButton>
                </Tooltip>
              )}
            </div>
            {openNote === i && (
              <form
                className="flex gap-1.5 px-2 pb-2"
                onSubmit={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  setOpenNote(null);
                  onRegenerate(i, note.trim());
                }}
              >
                <input
                  autoFocus
                  value={note}
                  onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Optional note, e.g. make the graph bigger"
                  maxLength={300}
                  className="field min-w-0 flex-1"
                />
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="sm">Rebuild</Button>
              </form>
            )}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </aside>
  );
}
```

`client/src/components/board/HelpDialog.jsx`:

```jsx
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import { IconButton, Kbd } from '../../ui/primitives.jsx';
import { Modal } from '../../ui/overlay.jsx';

const TOOLS = [
  ['V', 'Select'], ['H', 'Hand (or hold Space)'], ['P', 'Pen'], ['M', 'Highlighter'], ['E', 'Eraser'], ['A', 'Arrow'],
  ['L', 'Line'], ['R', 'Rectangle'], ['C', 'Circle'], ['T', 'Text'], ['N', 'Sticky note'], ['I', 'Add image'],
];
const VIEW = [
  ['Wheel', 'Scroll the board'], ['Ctrl/⌘ + wheel', 'Zoom at the cursor (pinch works too)'], ['+  −', 'Zoom in / out'], ['0', 'Fit the board to the window'],
  ['[  ]', 'Previous / next section'], ['F', 'Focus the selected object'], ['Arrow keys', 'Nudge the selection, or scroll'],
];
const EDIT = [
  ['Ctrl/⌘ Z', 'Undo'], ['Ctrl/⌘ Shift Z', 'Redo'], ['Delete', 'Delete the selection'], ['Double-click a tool', 'Keep it selected'],
  ['Drop or paste', 'Add an image (onto a browser frame to fill it)'], ['Esc', 'Back to Select / leave recording mode'],
];

function Table({ title, rows }) {
  return (
    <section>
      <h3 className="label mb-2">{title}</h3>
      <dl className="space-y-1.5">
        {rows.map(([key, text]) => (
          <div key={key} className="flex items-baseline gap-3 text-[13px]">
            <dt className="w-32 shrink-0"><Kbd>{key}</Kbd></dt>
            <dd className="text-ink-2">{text}</dd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </dl>
    </section>
  );
}

export default function HelpDialog({ open, onClose }) {
  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} className="max-w-2xl" labelledBy="board-help-title">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line px-5 py-3">
        <h2 id="board-help-title" className="font-display text-lg">Controls</h2>
        <IconButton aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose}><X size={14} /></IconButton>
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-6 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <Table title="Tools" rows={TOOLS} />
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <Table title="Moving around" rows={VIEW} />
          <Table title="Editing" rows={EDIT} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="border-t border-line px-5 py-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-3">
        Everything you draw or move saves itself in this browser. <b className="text-ink-2">Save</b> writes the board to a file;{' '}
        <b className="text-ink-2">Load</b> restores from one. <b className="text-ink-2">Clear</b> removes only what you added — the board
        itself is never touched. The eraser removes whole marks; delete a piece of the board with Select + Delete (undoable).
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```
