```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Download, Eraser, FileUp, HelpCircle, LayoutList, Maximize2, RotateCcw, Video, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { boardApi } from '../api/board.js';
import { useToast } from '../lib/toast.jsx';
import { Button, IconButton, Spinner, Tooltip } from '../ui/primitives.jsx';
import BoardCanvas from '../components/board/BoardCanvas.jsx';
import HelpDialog from '../components/board/HelpDialog.jsx';
import PropertyPanel from '../components/board/PropertyPanel.jsx';
import { ScrollIndicator, SectionPill, ZoomControls } from '../components/board/RecordingHud.jsx';
import SectionRail from '../components/board/SectionRail.jsx';
import Toolbar from '../components/board/Toolbar.jsx';
import { createAutosaver } from '../components/board/autosave.js';
import { boardStore } from '../components/board/boardStore.js';
import { downloadText, outlineFromSpec, sectionHasWork, slugify } from '../components/board/boardActions.js';
import { EXAMPLE_ID, createExampleRecord, newBoardId } from '../components/board/exampleBoard.js';
import { exportBoard, parseBoardFile, recordFromState, stateFromRecord } from '../components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { attachKeys } from '../components/board/tools/keys.js';

/**
 * A board. Loads it from the browser's storage (the bundled example is created
 * on first open), mounts the editor, and saves every change 800 ms after it
 * settles — and immediately when the tab is hidden or closed.
 *
 * Recording mode strips the page to the canvas, the slim toolbar, zoom, and a
 * "4 / 12 — TITLE" pill, so what you screen-record is only the board.
 */
export default function BoardPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const toast = useToast();
  const fileRef = useRef(null);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(null); // { record } | { missing: true }
  const [view, setView] = useState({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [section, setSection] = useState(0);
  const [ui, setUi] = useState({ tool: 'select', locked: false, canUndo: false, canRedo: false });
  const [editor, setEditor] = useState(null);
  const [selection, setSelection] = useState([]);
  const [defaults, setDefaults] = useState(null);
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [railOpen, setRailOpen] = useState(false);
  const [helpOpen, setHelpOpen] = useState(false);
  const [busySection, setBusySection] = useState(null);
  const [sections, setSections] = useState([]);
  const editorRef = useRef(null);
  const metaRef = useRef(null);
  const saverRef = useRef(null);
  const recordingRef = useRef(false);
  recordingRef.current = recording;

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    setLoaded(null);
    (async () => {
      let record = null;
      try {
        record = await boardStore.get(id);
        if (!record && id === EXAMPLE_ID) {
          record = createExampleRecord();
          await boardStore.put(record).catch(() => {});
        }
      } catch {
        record = null;
      }
      if (live && boardStore.mode === 'localstorage') {
        toast.show('This browser is using limited storage', {
          detail: 'Boards with many images may not fit. Use Save to keep a copy.',
          duration: 8000,
        });
      }
      if (live) setLoaded(record ? { record } : { missing: true });
    })();
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [id, toast]);

  // Autosave, with a guaranteed flush on the way out.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loaded?.record) return undefined;
    metaRef.current = { id: loaded.record.id, createdAt: loaded.record.createdAt };
    const saver = createAutosaver({
      save: (record) => boardStore.put(record),
      onError: (err) => toast.error("Couldn't save this board", err.message),
    });
    saverRef.current = saver;
    const flush = () => saver.flush();
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') flush();
    };
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', flush);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    return () => {
      flush();
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', flush);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    };
  }, [loaded, toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editor) return undefined;
    setDefaults(editor.defaults.get());
    setSections(editor.getState().spec.sections);
    return attachKeys(editor, {
      onEscape: () => {
        if (recordingRef.current) setRecording(false);
        editor.tools.set('select');
        editor.deselect();
      },
      onHelp: () => setHelpOpen(true),
    });
  }, [editor]);

  const hooks = useMemo(() => {
    const schedule = () => {
      const current = editorRef.current;
      const saver = saverRef.current;
      if (!current || !saver) return;
      saver.schedule(() => recordFromState(current.getState(), metaRef.current));
    };
    return {
      onState: (state, meta) => {
        setUi((u) => ({ ...u, canUndo: meta.canUndo, canRedo: meta.canRedo }));
        setSections(state.spec.sections);
        schedule();
      },
      onTool: (tool, locked) => setUi((u) => ({ ...u, tool, locked })),
      onSelection: setSelection,
      onDefaults: setDefaults,
      onError: (message) => toast.error(message),
      onView: (next, sectionIndex) => {
        setView({ zoom: next.zoom, x: next.x, y: next.y });
        setSection(sectionIndex);
        schedule();
      },
    };
  }, [toast]);

  const initialState = useMemo(() => (loaded?.record ? stateFromRecord(loaded.record) : null), [loaded]);

  /* --------------------------------- actions -------------------------------- */

  const saveFile = () => {
    const record = recordFromState(editor.getState(), metaRef.current);
    downloadText(`${slugify(record.title)}.apollo-board.json`, exportBoard(record));
    toast.success('Board saved to a file');
  };

  const loadFile = async (file) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const parsed = parseBoardFile(await file.text());
    if (!parsed.ok) {
      toast.error("Couldn't open that file", parsed.error);
      return;
    }
    if (parsed.record.id === metaRef.current.id) {
      if (!window.confirm('Replace the drawings and edits on this board with the ones in the file?')) return;
      editor.loadState(stateFromRecord(parsed.record));
      toast.success('Board restored from the file');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const record = { ...parsed.record, id: newBoardId() };
      await boardStore.put(record);
      navigate(`/board/${record.id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error("Couldn't save the board", err.message);
    }
  };

  const clearAnnotations = () => {
    if (window.confirm('Clear everything you drew or added? The board itself is not affected.')) {
      editor.dispatch({ type: 'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR' });
    }
  };

  const resetLayout = () => {
    if (window.confirm('Put every piece back where the layout placed it? Your drawings stay.')) {
      editor.dispatch({ type: 'OVERRIDES_RESET' });
    }
  };

  const regenerate = async (index, note) => {
    const state = editor.getState();
    const target = state.spec.sections[index];
    if (!state.brief) {
      toast.error('This board has no brief to rebuild from.');
      return;
    }
    if (sectionHasWork(state, target.id)
      && !window.confirm('Rebuilding replaces this section and drops the pieces you moved in it. Your drawings stay. Continue?')) {
      return;
    }
    setBusySection(index);
    try {
      const built = await boardApi.regenerateSection({ brief: state.brief, outline: outlineFromSpec(state.spec), index, note });
      editor.replaceSection(index, built);
      toast.success(`Rebuilt "${target.title}"`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error("Couldn't rebuild that section", err.message);
    } finally {
      setBusySection(null);
    }
  };

  const toggleFullscreen = () => {
    if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen();
    else document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.().catch(() => {});
  };

  /* ---------------------------------- render -------------------------------- */

  if (!loaded) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center bg-void">
        <Spinner />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (loaded.missing) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-void text-ink">
        <p>That board isn't in this browser.</p>
        <Link to="/board" className="text-accent-text underline">Back to Jam board</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const zoomControls = (
    <ZoomControls
      zoom={view.zoom}
      onIn={() => editor?.viewport.zoomBy(1.2)}
      onOut={() => editor?.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2)}
      onReset={() => editor?.viewport.reset()}
    />
  );

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white">
      <BoardCanvas
        key={loaded.record.id}
        initialState={initialState}
        hooks={hooks}
        onEditor={(e) => {
          editorRef.current = e;
          setEditor(e);
        }}
      />

      {!recording && (
        <>
          <Link
            to="/board"
            className="absolute left-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink shadow-pop"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={14} />
            <span className="max-w-[40vw] truncate">{initialState.spec.title}</span>
          </Link>

          <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-pop">
            <Tooltip label="Sections" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Sections" active={railOpen} onClick={() => setRailOpen((o) => !o)}><LayoutList size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Save to a file" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Save to a file" onClick={saveFile} disabled={!editor}><Download size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Load from a file" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Load from a file" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={!editor}><FileUp size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Clear my annotations" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Clear my annotations" onClick={clearAnnotations} disabled={!editor}><Eraser size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Reset layout" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Reset layout" onClick={resetLayout} disabled={!editor}><RotateCcw size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Controls" hint="?" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Controls" onClick={() => setHelpOpen(true)}><HelpCircle size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" disabled={!editor} onClick={() => { setRecording(true); setRailOpen(false); }}>
              <Video size={13} />
              Start recording mode
            </Button>
          </div>
          <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="application/json,.json" hidden onChange={(e) => { loadFile(e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ''; }} />
        </>
      )}

      {recording && (
        <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface/90 p-1 shadow-pop">
          <Tooltip label="Fullscreen" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Fullscreen" onClick={toggleFullscreen}><Maximize2 size={14} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
          <Tooltip label="Leave recording mode" hint="Esc" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Leave recording mode" onClick={() => setRecording(false)}><X size={14} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
        </div>
      )}

      {editor && (
        <Toolbar
          compact={recording}
          ui={ui}
          actions={{
            onTool: (name, lock) => editor.tools.set(name, { lock }),
            onUndo: () => editor.undo(),
            onRedo: () => editor.redo(),
            onClear: clearAnnotations,
            onZoomIn: () => editor.viewport.zoomBy(1.2),
            onZoomOut: () => editor.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2),
            onReset: () => editor.viewport.reset(),
            onImage: () => editor.requestImage?.(),
          }}
        />
      )}

      {editor && defaults && (
        <div className={recording ? 'absolute bottom-16 left-1/2 z-10 -translate-x-1/2' : 'absolute left-[76px] top-1/2 z-10 -translate-y-1/2'}>
          <PropertyPanel
            compact={recording}
            selection={selection}
            tool={ui.tool}
            defaults={defaults}
            onApply={(changes) => editor.applyStyle(changes)}
            onDefaults={(patch) => editor.defaults.set(patch)}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {railOpen && !recording && (
        <SectionRail
          sections={sections}
          current={section}
          busyIndex={busySection}
          canRegenerate={Boolean(editor?.getState().brief)}
          onGo={(i) => editor.goToSection(i)}
          onRegenerate={regenerate}
          onClose={() => setRailOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      {editor && <ScrollIndicator view={view} worldH={editor.getScene().height} />}

      <div className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-2">
        {recording && sections.length > 0 && <SectionPill index={section} total={sections.length} title={sections[section]?.title || ''} />}
        {zoomControls}
      </div>

      <HelpDialog open={helpOpen} onClose={() => setHelpOpen(false)} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Run tests and verify in the browser**

Run: `cd client && npm test` — Expected: PASS.

Run `npm run dev`, open `/board/example`. Verify (no console errors):

1. **Start recording mode**: the back link, the top-right file/section cluster and the "Clear" toolbar button disappear; the toolbar, zoom controls and a `1 / 12 — THE SALE` pill remain. Scroll: the pill updates to `4 / 12 — …` as sections cross the middle of the window. The **fullscreen** button works. `Esc` (or the ✕) leaves recording mode. Selecting an object in recording mode shows the property panel as a small popover at the bottom centre.
2. **Sections** (list icon): a rail lists all 12; clicking one jumps there; the current one is highlighted. Rebuild buttons are disabled with a tooltip for the example (it has no brief).
3. **Save** downloads `your-website-is-losing-you-sales.apollo-board.json`. Draw something, **Clear my annotations**: it disappears, the board is untouched, `Ctrl+Z` brings it back. Move some pieces, **Reset layout**: they snap home, `Ctrl+Z` undoes.
4. **Load** the saved file: after the confirm, your earlier drawings and moves are restored. Load a file from a *different* board: it opens as a new board.
5. Press `?`: the controls dialog lists every tool and shortcut, and `Esc` closes it.
6. Generate a real board (needs `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`), open it, and use **Rebuild** on one section with the note "make it much sparser": only that section changes, a toast confirms, and drawings **in the sections below it** are still attached to the same content (they moved with their section if it changed height).
7. Reload during a slow rebuild or while offline: nothing is lost.
8. A hairline down the right edge shows how far through the board you are, in both normal and recording mode; it moves as you scroll and never blocks a click.
9. With IndexedDB unavailable (Firefox private windows, or `indexedDB` deleted in the console before load), the board still opens from localStorage and a toast warns that storage is limited.

### Task 22: Milestone 1 documentation and full verification

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: Document the feature**

In `README.md`, add a section after **Apollo AI** (before **Stack**), with this content (adjust the surrounding heading levels to match):

````markdown
## Jam board

A different way to use Apollo: a big, quiet, scrollable board for **recording a video essay**. `/board`.

Paste the story of your video — chapters, flows like `GOOGLE ↓ WEBSITE ↓ LEAD`, the lines you will say on screen, how it should build — and Apollo turns it into a board: titles, flows, dependency graphs, cards, comparisons, timelines and screenshots frames, laid out on a white canvas that scrolls smoothly and never runs out. Then you record yourself talking over it: scroll, zoom, draw, circle the leak, add a note, drop in a screenshot, move a piece if the composition is wrong.

```
brief → outline → one call per section (four at a time) → validate → repair → board spec
board spec → layout (measured, centred, deterministic) → Fabric canvas
```

As in the design pipeline, **the model never chooses coordinates.** DeepSeek returns *structure* — which blocks a section needs and what they say — from a fixed vocabulary of thirteen (`title`, `statement`, `bigtype`, `quote`, `flow`, `hub`, `cards`, `compare`, `panels`, `code`, `timeline`, `funnel`, `browser`). `client/.../engine/layoutBoard.js` places them, centred on the board's midline with text inside a ~700-unit column, so a centred 9:16 crop of a landscape recording still holds the story.

**One state, changed only by operations.** A board is `{ spec, overrides, annotations, connections, assets }`. Every change — a drag, a toolbar button, a colour swatch — is a validated operation applied by `applyOperation`; Fabric only *renders* that state. That is what makes undo exact, lets connectors follow the pieces they join, and is the seam Direct mode uses.

**It survives a bad section.** The outline and sections are separate calls. A section that fails validation is retried once with the errors fed back; one that still fails becomes a plain title-and-statement section marked *needs a second try*, and can be rebuilt on its own from the section rail. The rest of the board, and everything you drew, is untouched.

**Boards live in your browser** (IndexedDB, with a localStorage fallback) and save themselves 800 ms after you stop. *Save* writes a board to a file and *Load* restores from one. *Clear* removes only what you added.

- **Runs without a key:** the example board (this project's "Your Website Is Losing You Sales" deck) opens with no `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`; generating a new board needs one and says so.
- **Tools:** select, hand, pen, highlighter, eraser, arrow, line, rectangle, circle, text, sticky note, image (upload, drop or paste), undo/redo, zoom, reset. `?` lists every shortcut.
- **Recording mode** strips everything but the board, a slim toolbar, zoom, and a `4 / 12 — TITLE` pill.
- **Limits, honestly:** the eraser removes whole marks, not pixels; text inside a card or node is edited by rebuilding the section, not in place; there is no image crop; a board lives in one browser (use Save/Load to move it); the model chooses *which* diagram fits a chapter, and can pick differently than you would — rebuild the section with a note.
````

and add the routes to the **API** list:

```
GET    /api/board/status               # → { ready, model } — is a live model configured?
POST   /api/board/generate/stream      # { brief } → SSE: outline · section · result · failed
POST   /api/board/generate             # { brief } → { board } — the stream's fallback
POST   /api/board/section              # { brief, outline, index, note? } → { section }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Full milestone 1 verification, in a real browser**

First run the acceptance scripts (they need no API key): with `npm run dev` running, `cd docs/superpowers/plans/jam-board-e2e`, install `playwright-core` as its README says, then `node 01-render-and-tools.mjs` and `node 02-panel-images-chrome.mjs`. Both must end with `ALL PASSED`.

Then run `npm run dev` with a `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` set if you have one, and work through the spec's test list by hand for what a script cannot judge — how scrolling *feels*, whether the layout looks right for your real brief, and what the real model produces. Every item must pass with a clean console:

1. **Generate:** paste the reference brief (the "Your Website Is Losing You Sales" prompt) into `/board`. Progress lists the sections and ticks them off. The result has ~12 sections, your quoted lines are verbatim, and the "ridiculous stack" is the densest section. (Without a key, confirm the form is disabled with a plain explanation and the example still opens.)
2. **Scroll & zoom:** wheel, trackpad, `Ctrl+wheel`, pinch, `+`/`−`/`0`, `[`/`]`, Home/End across the whole board; a board of 20 sections behaves like one of 3 (generate one by pasting a long brief, or paste the reference brief twice).
3. **Every tool** creates the right object; **text styling** (all controls) applies; **shapes** (fill, border, width, opacity, radius, resize, rotate); **arrows** (solid/dashed, heads, snapping); **image** (upload, drop, paste, screenshot frame).
4. **Move, resize, rotate, delete** pieces of the deck; arrows follow.
5. **Undo/redo** across drawings, erasures, moved pieces, deleted pieces, and property changes.
6. **Persistence:** refresh in the middle of drawing; close the tab within a second of an edit and reopen — the edit is there.
7. **Save / Load / Clear / Reset layout** as in Task 21.
8. **Recording mode** as in Task 21.
9. **Rebuild a section** and confirm annotations in later sections stay with their content.
10. **Failure cases:** stop the server mid-generation (the page shows a plain error and nothing is saved); revoke storage (`Application → Storage → Clear site data` while open) and edit — a toast says it couldn't save, the board on screen keeps working, *Save to file* still works.

## Milestone 2 — Direct mode

Milestone 1 is a complete, usable board. Milestone 2 adds the command bar: type *"make the $5 click huge and put the browser diagram under it"* and Apollo edits the board through the same validated operations the toolbar uses — nothing regenerates, and the whole instruction is one undo step.

### Task 23: The edit endpoint — outline in, validated operations out

**Files:**
- Modify: `server/src/design/board/prompts.js` (add `buildEditPrompt`)
- Modify: `server/src/services/boardService.js` (add `editBoard`, `sanitizeEditContext`)
- Modify: `server/src/routes/board.js` (add `POST /edit`)
- Test: `server/test/board.edit.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `validateBoardOperation` (Task 2), `BLOCK_SHAPES` and `VOCABULARY` (Task 4), `BoardError` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `buildEditPrompt({ message, outline, selection }) → { system, user }`.
  - `EDIT_MAX_CHARS = 600`.
  - `sanitizeEditContext(outline, selection) → { outline, selection }` — clips labels to 80 chars, replaces any `data:` string, caps arrays (20 sections · 12 blocks · 30 parts · 60 annotations · 30 selected ids); throws `BoardError(400)` if `outline` is not an object with a `sections` array, exceeds 500,000 characters as received, or exceeds 90,000 once cleaned.
  - `editBoard({ message, outline, selection, provider?, signal? }) → Promise<{ operations, message, dropped }>` — every operation is validated with `validateBoardOperation` **and** must refer only to ids present in the outline; the rest are counted in `dropped`.
  - `POST /api/board/edit` `{ message, outline, selection }` → `{ operations, message }`.
  - The outline shape: `{ title, sections: [{ id, title, blocks: [{ id, type, hidden?, parts: [{ id, label, hidden? }] }] }], annotations: [{ id, kind, section, label? }] }`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`server/test/board.edit.test.js`:

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import express from 'express';
import { buildEditPrompt } from '../src/design/board/prompts.js';
import { createBoardRouter } from '../src/routes/board.js';
import { BoardError, editBoard, sanitizeEditContext } from '../src/services/boardService.js';
import { NotConfiguredError } from '../src/services/ai/AIProvider.js';
import { makeProvider } from './fixtures/stubProvider.js';
import { SAMPLES } from './fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const outline = {
  title: 'T',
  sections: [{
    id: 's3', title: 'YOU PAID FOR THE CLICK',
    blocks: [
      { id: 's3.b2', type: 'flow', parts: [{ id: 's3.b2.n1', label: '$5 CLICK' }, { id: 's3.b2.n2', label: 'WEBSITE' }] },
      { id: 's3.b3', type: 'hub', parts: [{ id: 's3.b3.center', label: 'BROWSER' }] },
    ],
  }],
  annotations: [{ id: 'a_1', kind: 'text', section: 's3', label: 'LEAK' }],
};
const run = (ops, extra = {}) =>
  editBoard({ message: 'do a thing', outline, selection: [], provider: makeProvider(() => ({ operations: ops, message: 'Done.' })), ...extra });

test('valid operations on known ids are kept, with the model\'s sentence', async () => {
  const r = await run([
    { type: 'SET_STYLE', target: 's3.b2.n1', changes: { fontSize: 120 } },
    { type: 'MOVE', target: 's3.b3', relativeTo: 's3.b2', position: 'below' },
    { type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'circle', relativeTo: 's3.b2.n2', position: 'below', style: { color: 'problem' } },
  ]);
  assert.equal(r.operations.length, 3);
  assert.equal(r.operations[0].targets[0], 's3.b2.n1');
  assert.equal(r.message, 'Done.');
  assert.equal(r.dropped, 0);
});

test('an operation naming an id that is not in the outline is dropped, not applied', async () => {
  const r = await run([
    { type: 'DELETE', target: 's3.b2.n1' },
    { type: 'DELETE', target: 's9.b1.n7' },
    { type: 'MOVE', target: 's3.b2', relativeTo: 's3.zzz', position: 'below' },
    { type: 'CONNECT', from: 's3.b2.n1', to: 'a_1' },
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(r.operations.map((o) => o.type), ['DELETE', 'CONNECT']);
  assert.equal(r.dropped, 2);
});

test('malformed and internal operations are dropped, and a silent model gets a plain sentence', async () => {
  const silent = makeProvider(() => ({ operations: [{ type: 'NOPE' }, { type: 'OVERRIDE_SET', id: 's3.b2', patch: { dx: 1 } }, 'text', null, { type: 'SET_STYLE', target: 's3.b2', changes: { junk: 1 } }] }));
  const r = await editBoard({ message: 'do a thing', outline, selection: [], provider: silent });
  assert.deepEqual(r.operations, []);
  assert.equal(r.dropped, 5);
  assert.match(r.message, /couldn't turn that into a change/i);
});

test('INSERT_BLOCK needs a known section', async () => {
  const good = await run([{ type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 's3', index: 1, block: SAMPLES.statement }]);
  assert.equal(good.operations.length, 1);
  const bad = await run([{ type: 'INSERT_BLOCK', section: 's8', block: SAMPLES.statement }]);
  assert.equal(bad.operations.length, 0);
});

test('at most forty operations are considered', async () => {
  const many = Array.from({ length: 60 }, () => ({ type: 'ROTATE', target: 's3.b2', angle: 5 }));
  assert.equal((await run(many)).operations.length, 40);
});

test('a model that answers with nonsense yields no operations and a sentence, not an error', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider(() => ({ operations: 'lots', message: 12 }));
  const r = await editBoard({ message: 'do a thing', outline, selection: [], provider });
  assert.deepEqual(r.operations, []);
  assert.ok(r.message.length > 0);
});

test('the instruction must be between 3 and 600 characters', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider(() => ({ operations: [], message: '' }));
  await assert.rejects(editBoard({ message: 'hi', outline, selection: [], provider }), (e) => e instanceof BoardError && e.status === 400);
  await assert.rejects(editBoard({ message: 'x'.repeat(601), outline, selection: [], provider }), (e) => e.status === 400);
  assert.equal(provider.calls.length, 0);
});

test('a fatal provider error propagates', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider(() => { throw Object.assign(new Error('nope'), { status: 401 }); });
  await assert.rejects(editBoard({ message: 'make it bigger', outline, selection: [], provider }), (e) => e.status === 401);
});

test('sanitizeEditContext never lets pixel data or huge lists reach the model', () => {
  const dirty = {
    title: 'T',
    sections: Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => ({
      id: `s${i + 1}`, title: 'x'.repeat(300),
      blocks: [{ id: `s${i + 1}.b1`, type: 'flow', parts: Array.from({ length: 80 }, (__, j) => ({ id: `s${i + 1}.b1.n${j}`, label: 'data:image/png;base64,AAAA' })) }],
    })),
    annotations: Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, i) => ({ id: `a_${i}`, kind: 'text', section: 's1', label: 'data:image/png;base64,BBBB' })),
  };
  const { outline: clean, selection } = sanitizeEditContext(dirty, Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => `a_${i}`));
  assert.equal(clean.sections.length, 20);
  assert.equal(clean.sections[0].blocks[0].parts.length, 30);
  assert.equal(clean.annotations.length, 60);
  assert.equal(selection.length, 30);
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(clean), /data:image/);
  assert.ok(clean.sections[0].title.length <= 80);
});

test('sanitizeEditContext rejects a context that is not an outline or is enormous', () => {
  assert.throws(() => sanitizeEditContext('nope', []), (e) => e.status === 400);
  assert.throws(() => sanitizeEditContext({ sections: 'x' }, []), (e) => e.status === 400);
  const enormous = { sections: [{ id: 's1', title: 't', blocks: [] }], annotations: [], padding: 'x'.repeat(600000) };
  assert.throws(() => sanitizeEditContext(enormous, []), (e) => e.status === 400);
  const wide = {
    title: 'T',
    sections: Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => ({
      id: `s${i + 1}`, title: 't',
      blocks: Array.from({ length: 12 }, (__, j) => ({
        id: `s${i + 1}.b${j + 1}`, type: 'flow',
        parts: Array.from({ length: 30 }, (___, k) => ({ id: `s${i + 1}.b${j + 1}.n${k + 1}`, label: 'L'.repeat(80) })),
      })),
    })),
    annotations: [],
  };
  assert.throws(() => sanitizeEditContext(wide, []), (e) => e.status === 400, 'a board too big to fit a prompt is refused, not silently truncated');
});

test('the edit prompt carries the instruction, the selection and the outline', () => {
  const { system, user } = buildEditPrompt({ message: 'make this bigger', outline, selection: ['s3.b2.n1'] });
  assert.match(user, /make this bigger/);
  assert.match(user, /s3\.b2\.n1/);
  assert.match(user, /\$5 CLICK/);
  for (const type of ['SET_TEXT', 'SET_STYLE', 'MOVE', 'RESIZE', 'INSERT_BLOCK', 'CONNECT', 'ADD_ARROW', 'FOCUS']) assert.ok(system.includes(type), `system prompt lacks ${type}`);
  assert.match(system, /Never invent ids/);
});

async function withServer(provider, fn) {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json());
  app.use('/api/board', createBoardRouter({ provider }));
  const server = await new Promise((resolve) => { const s = app.listen(0, () => resolve(s)); });
  try {
    await fn(`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/api/board`);
  } finally {
    server.closeAllConnections?.();
    await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
  }
}
const post = (base, body) => fetch(`${base}/edit`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });

test('POST /edit returns validated operations', async () => {
  const provider = makeProvider(() => ({ operations: [{ type: 'ROTATE', target: 's3.b2', angle: 10 }, { type: 'DELETE', target: 'ghost' }], message: 'Tilted it.' }));
  await withServer(provider, async (base) => {
    const res = await post(base, { message: 'tilt the flow', outline, selection: [] });
    assert.equal(res.status, 200);
    const body = await res.json();
    assert.equal(body.operations.length, 1);
    assert.equal(body.message, 'Tilted it.');
  });
});

test('POST /edit answers a bad instruction with a plain 400 and a missing model with a friendly 503', async () => {
  await withServer(makeProvider(() => ({})), async (base) => {
    const res = await post(base, { message: 'a', outline, selection: [] });
    assert.equal(res.status, 400);
    assert.match((await res.json()).error, /instruction/i);
  });
  await withServer(makeProvider(() => { throw new NotConfiguredError(); }), async (base) => {
    const res = await post(base, { message: 'make it bigger', outline, selection: [] });
    assert.equal(res.status, 503);
    assert.match((await res.json()).error, /needs a DeepSeek key/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add `buildEditPrompt` to `prompts.js`**

Append to `server/src/design/board/prompts.js`:

```js
/* ---------------------------------- edits ---------------------------------- */

const EDIT_SYSTEM = `You operate a visual board for a video essay. The creator gives you a plain instruction; you answer with a small list of OPERATIONS that carry it out. You never regenerate the board, and you do not write coordinates unless the instruction asks for an exact nudge.

You are given an OUTLINE of the board — its sections, their blocks, every named part with its id and current label, and the creator's own annotations — and the ids of what is currently SELECTED. Refer to things ONLY by those ids.

Return ONLY a JSON object: { "operations": [ ... ], "message": "one short sentence saying what you did" }

OPERATIONS (use only these):
{ "type":"SET_TEXT", "target":"<id>", "text":"..." }
{ "type":"SET_STYLE", "targets":["<id>"], "changes": { "fontFamily":"Inter|Geist|Space Grotesk|DM Sans|IBM Plex Mono|Source Serif 4", "fontSize":n, "fontWeight":"bold|normal|100-900", "italic":bool, "underline":bool, "color":"<tone or #hex>", "fill":"<tone|#hex|transparent>", "stroke":"<tone|#hex>", "strokeWidth":n, "opacity":0-1, "align":"left|center|right", "lineHeight":n, "letterSpacing":n, "radius":n, "dash":bool, "headEnd":bool, "headStart":bool } }
{ "type":"MOVE", "targets":["<id>"], "dx":n, "dy":n }   or   { "type":"MOVE", "targets":["<id>"], "relativeTo":"<id>", "position":"above|below|left|right|over", "gap":n, "align":"center|start|end" }
{ "type":"RESIZE", "targets":["<id>"], "scale":0.1-10 }
{ "type":"ROTATE", "targets":["<id>"], "angle":degrees }
{ "type":"DELETE", "targets":["<id>"] }   { "type":"RESTORE", "targets":["<id>"] }
{ "type":"INSERT_BLOCK", "section":"s3", "index":n, "block":{ ...a block } }
{ "type":"REPLACE_BLOCK", "target":"s3.b2", "block":{ ...a block } }
{ "type":"CONNECT", "from":"<id>", "to":"<id>", "tone":"problem", "dashed":bool }
{ "type":"ADD_TEXT", "text":"...", "style":{ ... }, "relativeTo":"<id>", "position":"below" }
{ "type":"ADD_SHAPE", "shape":"rectangle|circle", "width":n, "height":n, "style":{ ... }, "relativeTo":"<id>", "position":"over" }
{ "type":"ADD_ARROW", "from":"<id>", "to":"<id>", "dashed":bool, "tone":"problem" }
{ "type":"FOCUS", "target":"<id>" }

Block shapes for INSERT_BLOCK and REPLACE_BLOCK:
${VOCABULARY}

TONES: ${TONES.join(', ')}.

RULES
- Make the SMALLEST change that satisfies the instruction. "Make X bigger" is SET_STYLE fontSize or RESIZE, never a rebuild.
- Sizes are in board units: a body label is about 40, a headline about 90. "Huge" is 2-3 times the current size; "bigger" is 1.3-1.5 times.
- Use a whole block id ("s3.b2") to move, scale or delete a diagram as one; use a part id to change one label.
- "This", "it" and "these" mean the SELECTED ids. If nothing is selected and the instruction is ambiguous, pick the most likely id from the outline.
- To circle or box something: ADD_SHAPE relative to its id with position "over" and no width or height (it sizes itself around the target), in tone "problem" unless told otherwise. To point at something: ADD_ARROW.
- Never invent ids. If the instruction cannot be done with these operations, return { "operations": [], "message": "<why, in a few words>" }.
- No markdown, no commentary, no code fences. JSON only.`;

export function buildEditPrompt({ message, outline, selection = [] }) {
  return {
    system: EDIT_SYSTEM,
    user: [
      `OUTLINE:\n${JSON.stringify(outline)}`,
      `SELECTED: ${JSON.stringify(selection)}`,
      `INSTRUCTION: ${message}`,
      'Return the JSON now.',
    ].join('\n\n'),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add `editBoard` to `boardService.js`**

In `server/src/services/boardService.js` add the import `import { validateBoardOperation } from '../design/board/operations.js';`, change the prompts import to `import { buildEditPrompt, buildOutlinePrompt, buildSectionPrompt } from '../design/board/prompts.js';`, and append:

```js
/* ---------------------------------- edits ---------------------------------- */

export const EDIT_MIN_CHARS = 3;
export const EDIT_MAX_CHARS = 600;
const MAX_EDIT_OPS = 40;
const RAW_CONTEXT_CHARS = 500_000; //  refuse to even walk something absurd
const MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS = 90_000; //   what may actually reach the model (roughly 25k tokens)
const CAPS = { sections: 20, blocks: 12, parts: 30, annotations: 60, selection: 30, label: 80 };

const isObj = (v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);

/** A label the model may read: short, one line, and never pixel data. */
function safeLabel(v) {
  if (typeof v !== 'string') return '';
  if (v.startsWith('data:')) return '[image]';
  const one = v.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  return one.length > CAPS.label ? `${one.slice(0, CAPS.label - 1)}…` : one;
}

const safeId = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' && /^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$/.test(v) ? v : null);

/**
 * The client sends a compact outline of the board. It is untrusted input to a
 * model prompt: bound its size, and make sure no image data — the lesson of
 * `sanitizeForPrompt` in DeepSeekProvider — can ride along in a label.
 */
export function sanitizeEditContext(outline, selection = []) {
  if (!isObj(outline) || !Array.isArray(outline.sections)) throw new BoardError('The board outline is missing.', 400);
  if (JSON.stringify(outline).length > RAW_CONTEXT_CHARS) throw new BoardError('That board is too large to edit with a command.', 400);

  const sections = outline.sections.slice(0, CAPS.sections).map((s) => ({
    id: safeId(s?.id),
    title: safeLabel(s?.title),
    blocks: (Array.isArray(s?.blocks) ? s.blocks : []).slice(0, CAPS.blocks).map((b) => ({
      id: safeId(b?.id),
      type: safeLabel(b?.type),
      ...(b?.hidden ? { hidden: true } : {}),
      parts: (Array.isArray(b?.parts) ? b.parts : []).slice(0, CAPS.parts).map((p) => ({
        id: safeId(p?.id), label: safeLabel(p?.label), ...(p?.hidden ? { hidden: true } : {}),
      })).filter((p) => p.id),
    })).filter((b) => b.id),
  })).filter((s) => s.id);

  const annotations = (Array.isArray(outline.annotations) ? outline.annotations : []).slice(0, CAPS.annotations).map((a) => ({
    id: safeId(a?.id), kind: safeLabel(a?.kind), section: safeId(a?.section), ...(a?.label ? { label: safeLabel(a.label) } : {}),
  })).filter((a) => a.id);

  const clean = { title: safeLabel(outline.title), sections, annotations };
  if (JSON.stringify(clean).length > MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS) throw new BoardError('That board is too large to edit with a command.', 400);
  return {
    outline: clean,
    selection: (Array.isArray(selection) ? selection : []).map(safeId).filter(Boolean).slice(0, CAPS.selection),
  };
}

function knownIds(outline) {
  const ids = new Set();
  const sections = new Set();
  for (const s of outline.sections) {
    ids.add(s.id);
    sections.add(s.id);
    for (const b of s.blocks) {
      ids.add(b.id);
      for (const p of b.parts) ids.add(p.id);
    }
  }
  for (const a of outline.annotations) ids.add(a.id);
  return { ids, sections };
}

/**
 * Direct mode: an instruction in, validated operations out. The model can only
 * name things that exist — an operation that mentions an id the outline did not
 * contain is a hallucination and is dropped, exactly like one that fails the
 * shape check. Nothing here changes a board; the client applies the result.
 */
export async function editBoard({ message, outline, selection = [], provider = getAIProvider(), signal }) {
  const text = typeof message === 'string' ? message.trim() : '';
  if (text.length < EDIT_MIN_CHARS || text.length > EDIT_MAX_CHARS) {
    throw new BoardError(`Give Apollo an instruction between ${EDIT_MIN_CHARS} and ${EDIT_MAX_CHARS} characters.`, 400);
  }
  const context = sanitizeEditContext(outline, selection);
  const { ids, sections } = knownIds(context.outline);

  throwIfAborted(signal);
  const raw = await provider.completeJSON({
    ...buildEditPrompt({ message: text, outline: context.outline, selection: context.selection }),
    temperature: 0.2,
    maxTokens: 2500,
    signal,
  });

  const operations = [];
  let dropped = 0;
  for (const candidate of (Array.isArray(raw?.operations) ? raw.operations : []).slice(0, MAX_EDIT_OPS)) {
    const checked = validateBoardOperation(candidate);
    const refs = checked.ok
      ? [...(checked.op.targets || []), checked.op.relativeTo, checked.op.from, checked.op.to].filter(Boolean)
      : [];
    const known = checked.ok && refs.every((id) => ids.has(id)) && (!checked.op.section || sections.has(checked.op.section));
    if (known) operations.push(checked.op);
    else dropped += 1;
  }
  // Operations beyond the cap are ignored, not counted as errors.
  const sentence = typeof raw?.message === 'string' ? raw.message.trim().slice(0, 200) : '';
  return {
    operations,
    message: sentence || (operations.length ? 'Done.' : "I couldn't turn that into a change to the board."),
    dropped,
  };
}
```

When the model returns a sentence, its sentence wins (with no operations it is usually the reason). Only a silent reply gets the fallback text.

### Task 24: Direct mode on the client — outline, resolver, command bar

**Files:**
- Create: `client/src/components/board/direct/sceneOutline.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/direct/resolveOps.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/direct/runDirect.js`
- Create: `client/src/components/board/direct/DirectBar.jsx`
- Modify: `client/src/api/board.js` (add `edit`)
- Modify: `client/src/pages/BoardPage.jsx` (replace: the finished page with the command bar)
- Test: `client/test/direct.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `editor.getScene()`, `getState()`, `getSelection()`, `boundsOf(id)`, `dispatch(op, { record: false })`, `recordUndoPoint(previous)`, `focusObject(id)` (Task 16); `toRelative` (Task 8); `boardApi` (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `buildSceneOutline({ scene, state }) → { title, sections, annotations }` (matches the server's outline shape; never includes image data).
  - `resolveOperation(op, { boundsOf, sections }) → op[]` — turns `MOVE`/`ADD_TEXT`/`ADD_SHAPE` with `relativeTo` into numeric operations, turns `ADD_ARROW` between ids into a live `CONNECT`, drops what it cannot place. `FOCUS` passes through.
  - `applyAIOperations(editor, { operations, message }) → { message, applied, changed }` — applies the batch one operation at a time (so each sees the previous one's result), records **one** undo step, then honours a `FOCUS`.
  - `runDirect(editor, api, message) → Promise<{ message, applied, changed }>`.
  - `boardApi.edit({ message, outline, selection }) → Promise<{ operations, message }>`.
  - `<DirectBar open ready onClose onRun />`.

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { applyAIOperations } from '../src/components/board/direct/runDirect.js';
import { resolveOperation } from '../src/components/board/direct/resolveOps.js';
import { buildSceneOutline } from '../src/components/board/direct/sceneOutline.js';
import { layoutBoard } from '../src/components/board/engine/layoutBoard.js';
import { applyOperation, createBoardState } from '../src/components/board/state/boardState.js';
import { sanitizeSpec } from '../src/components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { stubMeasure } from './helpers/stubMeasure.js';
import { SAMPLES } from '../../server/test/fixtures/boardSamples.js';

const sections = [{ id: 's1', y: 0, height: 1000 }, { id: 's2', y: 1000, height: 1000 }];
const boxes = { mover: { x: 100, y: 100, w: 200, h: 50 }, rel: { x: 500, y: 300, w: 100, h: 100 } };
const ctx = { sections, boundsOf: (id) => boxes[id] || null };

/* ------------------------------ resolveOperation --------------------------- */

test('MOVE below another object: centred under it, a gap apart', () => {
  const [op] = resolveOperation({ type: 'MOVE', targets: ['mover'], relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'below', gap: 40, align: 'center' }, ctx);
  assert.deepEqual(op, { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['mover'], dx: 350, dy: 340 });
});

test('MOVE honours above, left, right and start/end alignment', () => {
  const at = (extra) => resolveOperation({ type: 'MOVE', targets: ['mover'], relativeTo: 'rel', gap: 20, ...extra }, ctx)[0];
  assert.deepEqual([at({ position: 'over' }).dx, at({ position: 'over' }).dy], [550 - 100 - 100, 350 - 25 - 100], 'over centres the mover on the anchor');
  assert.deepEqual([at({ position: 'above' }).dx, at({ position: 'above' }).dy], [350, 300 - 20 - 50 - 100]);
  assert.deepEqual([at({ position: 'right' }).dx, at({ position: 'right' }).dy], [500 + 100 + 20 - 100, 300 + 100 / 2 - 50 / 2 - 100]);
  assert.equal(at({ position: 'below', align: 'start' }).dx, 400);
  assert.equal(at({ position: 'below', align: 'end' }).dx, 500 + 100 - 200 - 100);
  assert.equal(at({ position: 'left' }).dx, 500 - 20 - 200 - 100);
});

test('moving several targets moves the group as one, by the union of their bounds', () => {
  const both = { ...ctx, boundsOf: (id) => ({ a: { x: 0, y: 0, w: 100, h: 100 }, b: { x: 200, y: 0, w: 100, h: 100 }, rel: boxes.rel })[id] };
  const [op] = resolveOperation({ type: 'MOVE', targets: ['a', 'b'], relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'below', gap: 0, align: 'start' }, both);
  assert.equal(op.dx, 500 - 0);
  assert.equal(op.dy, 400 - 0);
});

test('a relative move whose target or anchor cannot be found is dropped', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(resolveOperation({ type: 'MOVE', targets: ['ghost'], relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'below' }, ctx), []);
  assert.deepEqual(resolveOperation({ type: 'MOVE', targets: ['mover'], relativeTo: 'ghost', position: 'below' }, ctx), []);
});

test('MOVE with dx/dy, and other operations, pass through unchanged', () => {
  const move = { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['mover'], dx: 5, dy: 6 };
  assert.deepEqual(resolveOperation(move, ctx), [move]);
  const style = { type: 'SET_STYLE', targets: ['mover'], changes: { fontSize: 90 } };
  assert.deepEqual(resolveOperation(style, ctx), [style]);
  const focus = { type: 'FOCUS', targets: ['mover'] };
  assert.deepEqual(resolveOperation(focus, ctx), [focus]);
});

test('ADD_SHAPE relative to an object becomes a section-relative placement', () => {
  const [op] = resolveOperation({ type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'circle', width: 200, height: 100, relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'below', gap: 20 }, {
    sections, boundsOf: () => ({ x: 500, y: 1900, w: 100, h: 50 }),
  });
  // rel is at world y 1900–1950 (section s2, which starts at 1000); the shape's top goes at 1970 and its centre at 2020
  assert.equal(op.section, 's2');
  assert.equal(op.x, 550);
  assert.equal(op.y, 2020 - 1000);
  assert.equal('relativeTo' in op, false);
});

test('ADD_SHAPE over an object sizes itself around it and is centred on it', () => {
  const [op] = resolveOperation({ type: 'ADD_SHAPE', shape: 'circle', relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'over' }, ctx);
  assert.equal(op.width, 100 + 80);
  assert.equal(op.height, 100 + 50);
  assert.equal(op.x, 550);
  assert.equal(op.y, 350);
  assert.equal(op.section, 's1');
});

test('ADD_TEXT relative to an object is centred on it, top-anchored', () => {
  const [op] = resolveOperation({ type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'LEAK', style: { fontSize: 100 }, relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'above', gap: 10 }, ctx);
  assert.equal(op.section, 's1');
  assert.equal(op.x, 550);
  assert.equal(op.y, 300 - 10 - 100 * 1.1);
});

test('ADD_ARROW between two ids becomes a live connection', () => {
  const [op] = resolveOperation({ type: 'ADD_ARROW', from: 'mover', to: 'rel', dashed: true, tone: 'problem' }, ctx);
  assert.deepEqual(op, { type: 'CONNECT', from: 'mover', to: 'rel', tone: 'problem', dashed: true });
  const free = { type: 'ADD_ARROW', section: 's1', x1: 0, y1: 0, x2: 10, y2: 10 };
  assert.deepEqual(resolveOperation(free, ctx), [free]);
});

/* ------------------------------ buildSceneOutline -------------------------- */

const spec = sanitizeSpec({ title: 'T', sections: [{ title: 'S', blocks: [SAMPLES.title, { type: 'flow', steps: ['A', 'B', 'C'] }] }] });
const scene = layoutBoard(spec, stubMeasure);

test('the outline names every block and its labelled parts', () => {
  const o = buildSceneOutline({ scene, state: createBoardState({ spec }) });
  assert.equal(o.title, 'T');
  const flow = o.sections[0].blocks.find((b) => b.type === 'flow');
  assert.deepEqual(flow.parts.map((p) => [p.id, p.label]), [['s1.b2.n1', 'A'], ['s1.b2.n2', 'B'], ['s1.b2.n3', 'C']]);
  assert.ok(o.sections[0].blocks.every((b) => b.parts.every((p) => !p.id.includes('.c'))), 'connectors are not parts');
});

test('the outline reflects edits: changed text, hidden pieces, annotations', () => {
  let state = createBoardState({ spec });
  state = applyOperation(state, { type: 'SET_TEXT', targets: ['s1.b2.n1'], text: 'RENAMED' });
  state = applyOperation(state, { type: 'DELETE', targets: ['s1.b2.n2'] });
  state = applyOperation(state, { type: 'ADD_TEXT', text: 'THIS IS THE LEAK', section: 's1', x: 700, y: 100 });
  const o = buildSceneOutline({ scene, state });
  const flow = o.sections[0].blocks[1];
  assert.equal(flow.parts[0].label, 'RENAMED');
  assert.equal(flow.parts[1].hidden, true);
  assert.deepEqual(o.annotations.map((a) => [a.kind, a.label]), [['text', 'THIS IS THE LEAK']]);
});

test('the outline never carries image data and clips long labels', () => {
  let state = createBoardState({ spec });
  state = { ...state, assets: { img_1: `data:image/png;base64,${'A'.repeat(5000)}` }, annotations: [{ id: 'a_1', section: 's1', kind: 'image', json: { type: 'image', src: 'asset:img_1' } }] };
  state = applyOperation(state, { type: 'SET_TEXT', targets: ['s1.b2.n1'], text: 'x'.repeat(500) });
  const text = JSON.stringify(buildSceneOutline({ scene, state }));
  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /data:image/);
  assert.ok(text.length < 3000);
});

/* ------------------------------ applyAIOperations -------------------------- */

function fakeEditor() {
  const calls = [];
  const editor = {
    state: { v: 0 },
    getState() { return this.state; },
    getScene: () => ({ sections }),
    boundsOf: (id) => boxes[id] || null,
    dispatch(op, opts) { calls.push(['dispatch', op.type, opts]); this.state = { v: this.state.v + 1 }; return true; },
    recordUndoPoint(prev) { calls.push(['undo-point', prev]); },
    focusObject(id) { calls.push(['focus', id]); },
  };
  return { editor, calls };
}

test('a batch is applied op by op with one undo step, then focus', () => {
  const { editor, calls } = fakeEditor();
  const before = editor.getState();
  const r = applyAIOperations(editor, {
    message: 'Done.',
    operations: [
      { type: 'RESIZE', targets: ['mover'], scale: 2 },
      { type: 'MOVE', targets: ['mover'], relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'below' },
      { type: 'FOCUS', targets: ['mover'] },
    ],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(calls.map((c) => c[0]), ['dispatch', 'dispatch', 'undo-point', 'focus']);
  assert.ok(calls.filter((c) => c[0] === 'dispatch').every((c) => c[2].record === false));
  assert.equal(calls.find((c) => c[0] === 'undo-point')[1], before);
  assert.equal(r.applied, 2);
  assert.equal(r.changed, true);
});

test('a batch that changes nothing records no undo step', () => {
  const { editor, calls } = fakeEditor();
  editor.dispatch = () => false;
  const r = applyAIOperations(editor, { message: 'Nothing to do.', operations: [{ type: 'MOVE', targets: ['ghost'], relativeTo: 'rel', position: 'below' }] });
  assert.deepEqual(calls, []);
  assert.equal(r.changed, false);
  assert.equal(r.message, 'Nothing to do.');
});
```

`client/src/components/board/direct/sceneOutline.js`:

```js
/**
 * The board, as DeepSeek is allowed to see it in Direct mode: every section,
 * block and *named* part with its id and current label, plus the creator's own
 * annotations. Enough to say "move the browser diagram under the $5 click";
 * nothing else. Labels are short and single-line, and image data never appears
 * (the same lesson as sanitizeForPrompt in the server's DeepSeekProvider).
 */

const CAP = { sections: 20, blocks: 12, parts: 30, annotations: 60, label: 40 };

const clip = (value) => {
  const one = String(value ?? '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  return one.length > CAP.label ? `${one.slice(0, CAP.label - 1)}…` : one;
};

const labelOf = (item) => (item.kind === 'text' ? item.text : item.kind === 'box' || item.kind === 'poly' ? item.label : '');

export function buildSceneOutline({ scene, state }) {
  const partsByBlock = new Map();
  for (const item of scene.items) {
    if (!labelOf(item)) continue;
    if (!partsByBlock.has(item.block)) partsByBlock.set(item.block, []);
    partsByBlock.get(item.block).push(item);
  }

  const sections = state.spec.sections.slice(0, CAP.sections).map((section) => ({
    id: section.id,
    title: clip(section.title),
    blocks: section.blocks.slice(0, CAP.blocks).map((block) => ({
      id: block.id,
      type: block.type,
      ...(state.overrides[block.id]?.deleted ? { hidden: true } : {}),
      parts: (partsByBlock.get(block.id) || []).slice(0, CAP.parts).map((item) => ({
        id: item.id,
        label: clip(state.overrides[item.id]?.text ?? labelOf(item)),
        ...(state.overrides[item.id]?.deleted ? { hidden: true } : {}),
      })),
    })),
  }));

  const annotations = state.annotations.slice(0, CAP.annotations).map((a) => ({
    id: a.id,
    kind: a.kind,
    section: a.section,
    ...(typeof a.json?.text === 'string' && a.json.text ? { label: clip(a.json.text) } : {}),
  }));

  return { title: clip(state.spec.title), sections, annotations };
}
```

`client/src/components/board/direct/resolveOps.js`:

```js
import { toRelative } from '../state/anchor.js';

/**
 * DeepSeek speaks in relationships ("under the $5 click"); the board stores
 * numbers. This turns one into the other at the moment of applying, from the
 * objects' current bounds — so stored state stays numeric and replayable, and
 * an instruction that mentions something that has since moved still lands right.
 *
 * `ctx.boundsOf(id) → { x, y, w, h } | null` is in world coordinates;
 * `ctx.sections` is `[{ id, y, height }]`.
 */

function union(rects) {
  if (!rects.length) return null;
  const x1 = Math.min(...rects.map((r) => r.x));
  const y1 = Math.min(...rects.map((r) => r.y));
  const x2 = Math.max(...rects.map((r) => r.x + r.w));
  const y2 = Math.max(...rects.map((r) => r.y + r.h));
  return { x: x1, y: y1, w: x2 - x1, h: y2 - y1 };
}

/** Top-left of a `size`-sized thing placed next to `rel` (or, for "over", centred on it). */
function place(size, rel, op) {
  const gap = typeof op.gap === 'number' ? op.gap : 40;
  const align = op.align || 'center';
  if (op.position === 'over') return { x: rel.x + rel.w / 2 - size.w / 2, y: rel.y + rel.h / 2 - size.h / 2 };
  if (op.position === 'above' || op.position === 'below') {
    const y = op.position === 'below' ? rel.y + rel.h + gap : rel.y - gap - size.h;
    const x = align === 'start' ? rel.x : align === 'end' ? rel.x + rel.w - size.w : rel.x + rel.w / 2 - size.w / 2;
    return { x, y };
  }
  const x = op.position === 'right' ? rel.x + rel.w + gap : rel.x - gap - size.w;
  const y = align === 'start' ? rel.y : align === 'end' ? rel.y + rel.h - size.h : rel.y + rel.h / 2 - size.h / 2;
  return { x, y };
}

const stripPlacement = ({ relativeTo, position, gap, align, ...rest }) => rest;

export function resolveOperation(op, ctx) {
  switch (op.type) {
    case 'MOVE': {
      if (!op.relativeTo) return [op];
      const rel = ctx.boundsOf(op.relativeTo);
      const mover = union(op.targets.map(ctx.boundsOf).filter(Boolean));
      if (!rel || !mover) return [];
      const p = place(mover, rel, op);
      return [{ type: 'MOVE', targets: op.targets, dx: p.x - mover.x, dy: p.y - mover.y }];
    }

    case 'ADD_TEXT':
    case 'ADD_SHAPE': {
      if (!op.relativeTo) return [op];
      const rel = ctx.boundsOf(op.relativeTo);
      if (!rel) return [];
      const text = op.type === 'ADD_TEXT';
      // Text is placed by its top edge and centre; a shape by its centre.
      // A shape placed "over" something with no stated size is drawn around it.
      const around = op.position === 'over';
      const size = text
        ? { w: 520, h: (op.style?.fontSize || 56) * 1.1 }
        : { w: op.width || (around ? rel.w + 80 : 220), h: op.height || (around ? rel.h + 50 : 140) };
      const p = place(size, rel, op);
      const worldY = text ? p.y : p.y + size.h / 2;
      const at = toRelative(ctx.sections, worldY);
      const sized = text ? {} : { width: size.w, height: size.h };
      return [{ ...stripPlacement(op), ...sized, section: at.section, x: p.x + size.w / 2, y: at.y }];
    }

    case 'ADD_ARROW':
      // An arrow between two named things should stay tied to them.
      if (op.from && op.to) return [{ type: 'CONNECT', from: op.from, to: op.to, tone: op.tone || 'problem', dashed: Boolean(op.dashed) }];
      return [op];

    default:
      return [op];
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `runDirect.js`, the API method, and `DirectBar.jsx`**

`client/src/components/board/direct/runDirect.js`:

```js
import { buildSceneOutline } from './sceneOutline.js';
import { resolveOperation } from './resolveOps.js';

/**
 * Apply what the model asked for, one operation at a time so each sees the
 * result of the one before (a resize, then a move relative to the resized
 * thing), and record the whole batch as a single undo step.
 */
export function applyAIOperations(editor, { operations, message }) {
  const before = editor.getState();
  let focus = null;
  let applied = 0;

  for (const op of operations) {
    const ctx = { boundsOf: editor.boundsOf, sections: editor.getScene().sections };
    for (const resolved of resolveOperation(op, ctx)) {
      if (resolved.type === 'FOCUS') {
        focus = resolved.targets?.[0] || null;
        continue;
      }
      if (editor.dispatch(resolved, { record: false })) applied += 1;
    }
  }

  const changed = editor.getState() !== before;
  if (changed) editor.recordUndoPoint(before);
  if (focus) editor.focusObject(focus);
  return { message, applied, changed };
}

export async function runDirect(editor, api, message) {
  const selection = editor.getSelection().map((s) => s.id);
  const outline = buildSceneOutline({ scene: editor.getScene(), state: editor.getState() });
  const reply = await api.edit({ message, outline, selection });
  return applyAIOperations(editor, reply);
}
```

`client/src/components/board/direct/DirectBar.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { cx } from '../../../lib/cx.js';
import { Button, IconButton, Spinner } from '../../../ui/primitives.jsx';

/**
 * The command bar. Plain English in, edits out — no regenerating, and the whole
 * instruction undoes with one Ctrl+Z. Opens with `/` or Ctrl/Cmd+K; Escape closes it.
 */
export default function DirectBar({ open, ready, onClose, onRun }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [reply, setReply] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const inputRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return undefined;
    setReply('');
    setError('');
    const t = setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const submit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const message = text.trim();
    if (!message || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const result = await onRun(message);
      setReply(result.message);
      setText('');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="absolute bottom-6 left-1/2 z-20 w-[min(640px,calc(100vw-2rem))] -translate-x-1/2">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface p-2 shadow-pop">
        <Sparkles size={16} className="ml-2 shrink-0 text-accent-text" />
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
              e.stopPropagation();
              onClose();
            }
          }}
          disabled={!ready || busy}
          maxLength={600}
          aria-label="Direct mode instruction"
          placeholder={ready ? 'Tell Apollo what to change — e.g. make “$5 CLICK” huge and put the graph under it' : 'Direct mode needs a DeepSeek key'}
          className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent text-[14px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-3"
        />
        {busy ? <Spinner size={14} className="mr-2" /> : <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="sm" disabled={!ready || !text.trim()}>Do it</Button>}
        <IconButton aria-label="Close Direct mode" onClick={onClose}><X size={14} /></IconButton>
      </form>
      {(reply || error) && (
        <p className={cx('mt-2 rounded-lg border bg-surface px-3 py-2 text-[13px]', error ? 'border-danger/40 text-danger' : 'border-line text-ink-2')}>
          {error || reply}
          {!error && <span className="ml-2 text-ink-3">Ctrl+Z undoes it.</span>}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`client/src/pages/BoardPage.jsx`:

```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Download, Eraser, FileUp, HelpCircle, LayoutList, Maximize2, RotateCcw, Sparkles, Video, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { boardApi } from '../api/board.js';
import { useToast } from '../lib/toast.jsx';
import { Button, IconButton, Spinner, Tooltip } from '../ui/primitives.jsx';
import BoardCanvas from '../components/board/BoardCanvas.jsx';
import DirectBar from '../components/board/direct/DirectBar.jsx';
import HelpDialog from '../components/board/HelpDialog.jsx';
import PropertyPanel from '../components/board/PropertyPanel.jsx';
import { ScrollIndicator, SectionPill, ZoomControls } from '../components/board/RecordingHud.jsx';
import SectionRail from '../components/board/SectionRail.jsx';
import Toolbar from '../components/board/Toolbar.jsx';
import { createAutosaver } from '../components/board/autosave.js';
import { boardStore } from '../components/board/boardStore.js';
import { downloadText, outlineFromSpec, sectionHasWork, slugify } from '../components/board/boardActions.js';
import { EXAMPLE_ID, createExampleRecord, newBoardId } from '../components/board/exampleBoard.js';
import { exportBoard, parseBoardFile, recordFromState, stateFromRecord } from '../components/board/state/boardRecord.js';
import { runDirect } from '../components/board/direct/runDirect.js';
import { attachKeys } from '../components/board/tools/keys.js';

/**
 * A board. Loads it from the browser's storage (the bundled example is created
 * on first open), mounts the editor, and saves every change 800 ms after it
 * settles — and immediately when the tab is hidden or closed.
 *
 * Recording mode strips the page to the canvas, the slim toolbar, zoom, and a
 * "4 / 12 — TITLE" pill, so what you screen-record is only the board.
 */
export default function BoardPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const toast = useToast();
  const fileRef = useRef(null);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(null); // { record } | { missing: true }
  const [view, setView] = useState({ zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [section, setSection] = useState(0);
  const [ui, setUi] = useState({ tool: 'select', locked: false, canUndo: false, canRedo: false });
  const [editor, setEditor] = useState(null);
  const [selection, setSelection] = useState([]);
  const [defaults, setDefaults] = useState(null);
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [railOpen, setRailOpen] = useState(false);
  const [helpOpen, setHelpOpen] = useState(false);
  const [busySection, setBusySection] = useState(null);
  const [directOpen, setDirectOpen] = useState(false);
  const [aiReady, setAiReady] = useState(false);
  const [sections, setSections] = useState([]);
  const editorRef = useRef(null);
  const metaRef = useRef(null);
  const saverRef = useRef(null);
  const recordingRef = useRef(false);
  recordingRef.current = recording;

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    setLoaded(null);
    (async () => {
      let record = null;
      try {
        record = await boardStore.get(id);
        if (!record && id === EXAMPLE_ID) {
          record = createExampleRecord();
          await boardStore.put(record).catch(() => {});
        }
      } catch {
        record = null;
      }
      if (live && boardStore.mode === 'localstorage') {
        toast.show('This browser is using limited storage', {
          detail: 'Boards with many images may not fit. Use Save to keep a copy.',
          duration: 8000,
        });
      }
      if (live) setLoaded(record ? { record } : { missing: true });
    })();
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [id, toast]);

  // Autosave, with a guaranteed flush on the way out.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loaded?.record) return undefined;
    metaRef.current = { id: loaded.record.id, createdAt: loaded.record.createdAt };
    const saver = createAutosaver({
      save: (record) => boardStore.put(record),
      onError: (err) => toast.error("Couldn't save this board", err.message),
    });
    saverRef.current = saver;
    const flush = () => saver.flush();
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') flush();
    };
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', flush);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    return () => {
      flush();
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', flush);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    };
  }, [loaded, toast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    boardApi.status().then((s) => live && setAiReady(Boolean(s.ready))).catch(() => live && setAiReady(false));
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editor) return undefined;
    setDefaults(editor.defaults.get());
    setSections(editor.getState().spec.sections);
    return attachKeys(editor, {
      onEscape: () => {
        if (recordingRef.current) setRecording(false);
        editor.tools.set('select');
        editor.deselect();
      },
      onHelp: () => setHelpOpen(true),
      onDirect: () => {
        if (!recordingRef.current) setDirectOpen(true);
      },
    });
  }, [editor]);

  const hooks = useMemo(() => {
    const schedule = () => {
      const current = editorRef.current;
      const saver = saverRef.current;
      if (!current || !saver) return;
      saver.schedule(() => recordFromState(current.getState(), metaRef.current));
    };
    return {
      onState: (state, meta) => {
        setUi((u) => ({ ...u, canUndo: meta.canUndo, canRedo: meta.canRedo }));
        setSections(state.spec.sections);
        schedule();
      },
      onTool: (tool, locked) => setUi((u) => ({ ...u, tool, locked })),
      onSelection: setSelection,
      onDefaults: setDefaults,
      onError: (message) => toast.error(message),
      onView: (next, sectionIndex) => {
        setView({ zoom: next.zoom, x: next.x, y: next.y });
        setSection(sectionIndex);
        schedule();
      },
    };
  }, [toast]);

  const initialState = useMemo(() => (loaded?.record ? stateFromRecord(loaded.record) : null), [loaded]);

  /* --------------------------------- actions -------------------------------- */

  const saveFile = () => {
    const record = recordFromState(editor.getState(), metaRef.current);
    downloadText(`${slugify(record.title)}.apollo-board.json`, exportBoard(record));
    toast.success('Board saved to a file');
  };

  const loadFile = async (file) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const parsed = parseBoardFile(await file.text());
    if (!parsed.ok) {
      toast.error("Couldn't open that file", parsed.error);
      return;
    }
    if (parsed.record.id === metaRef.current.id) {
      if (!window.confirm('Replace the drawings and edits on this board with the ones in the file?')) return;
      editor.loadState(stateFromRecord(parsed.record));
      toast.success('Board restored from the file');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const record = { ...parsed.record, id: newBoardId() };
      await boardStore.put(record);
      navigate(`/board/${record.id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error("Couldn't save the board", err.message);
    }
  };

  const clearAnnotations = () => {
    if (window.confirm('Clear everything you drew or added? The board itself is not affected.')) {
      editor.dispatch({ type: 'ANNOTATIONS_CLEAR' });
    }
  };

  const resetLayout = () => {
    if (window.confirm('Put every piece back where the layout placed it? Your drawings stay.')) {
      editor.dispatch({ type: 'OVERRIDES_RESET' });
    }
  };

  const regenerate = async (index, note) => {
    const state = editor.getState();
    const target = state.spec.sections[index];
    if (!state.brief) {
      toast.error('This board has no brief to rebuild from.');
      return;
    }
    if (sectionHasWork(state, target.id)
      && !window.confirm('Rebuilding replaces this section and drops the pieces you moved in it. Your drawings stay. Continue?')) {
      return;
    }
    setBusySection(index);
    try {
      const built = await boardApi.regenerateSection({ brief: state.brief, outline: outlineFromSpec(state.spec), index, note });
      editor.replaceSection(index, built);
      toast.success(`Rebuilt "${target.title}"`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error("Couldn't rebuild that section", err.message);
    } finally {
      setBusySection(null);
    }
  };

  const toggleFullscreen = () => {
    if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen();
    else document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.().catch(() => {});
  };

  /* ---------------------------------- render -------------------------------- */

  if (!loaded) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center bg-void">
        <Spinner />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (loaded.missing) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-void text-ink">
        <p>That board isn't in this browser.</p>
        <Link to="/board" className="text-accent-text underline">Back to Jam board</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const zoomControls = (
    <ZoomControls
      zoom={view.zoom}
      onIn={() => editor?.viewport.zoomBy(1.2)}
      onOut={() => editor?.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2)}
      onReset={() => editor?.viewport.reset()}
    />
  );

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white">
      <BoardCanvas
        key={loaded.record.id}
        initialState={initialState}
        hooks={hooks}
        onEditor={(e) => {
          editorRef.current = e;
          setEditor(e);
        }}
      />

      {!recording && (
        <>
          <Link
            to="/board"
            className="absolute left-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink shadow-pop"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={14} />
            <span className="max-w-[40vw] truncate">{initialState.spec.title}</span>
          </Link>

          <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-1 shadow-pop">
            <Tooltip label="Sections" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Sections" active={railOpen} onClick={() => setRailOpen((o) => !o)}><LayoutList size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Save to a file" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Save to a file" onClick={saveFile} disabled={!editor}><Download size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Load from a file" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Load from a file" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={!editor}><FileUp size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Clear my annotations" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Clear my annotations" onClick={clearAnnotations} disabled={!editor}><Eraser size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Reset layout" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Reset layout" onClick={resetLayout} disabled={!editor}><RotateCcw size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Direct mode" hint="/" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Direct mode" active={directOpen} disabled={!editor} onClick={() => setDirectOpen((o) => !o)}><Sparkles size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Tooltip label="Controls" hint="?" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Controls" onClick={() => setHelpOpen(true)}><HelpCircle size={15} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" disabled={!editor} onClick={() => { setRecording(true); setRailOpen(false); setDirectOpen(false); }}>
              <Video size={13} />
              Start recording mode
            </Button>
          </div>
          <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="application/json,.json" hidden onChange={(e) => { loadFile(e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ''; }} />
        </>
      )}

      {recording && (
        <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface/90 p-1 shadow-pop">
          <Tooltip label="Fullscreen" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Fullscreen" onClick={toggleFullscreen}><Maximize2 size={14} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
          <Tooltip label="Leave recording mode" hint="Esc" side="bottom"><IconButton aria-label="Leave recording mode" onClick={() => setRecording(false)}><X size={14} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
        </div>
      )}

      {editor && (
        <Toolbar
          compact={recording}
          ui={ui}
          actions={{
            onTool: (name, lock) => editor.tools.set(name, { lock }),
            onUndo: () => editor.undo(),
            onRedo: () => editor.redo(),
            onClear: clearAnnotations,
            onZoomIn: () => editor.viewport.zoomBy(1.2),
            onZoomOut: () => editor.viewport.zoomBy(1 / 1.2),
            onReset: () => editor.viewport.reset(),
            onImage: () => editor.requestImage?.(),
          }}
        />
      )}

      {editor && defaults && (
        <div className={recording ? 'absolute bottom-16 left-1/2 z-10 -translate-x-1/2' : 'absolute left-[76px] top-1/2 z-10 -translate-y-1/2'}>
          <PropertyPanel
            compact={recording}
            selection={selection}
            tool={ui.tool}
            defaults={defaults}
            onApply={(changes) => editor.applyStyle(changes)}
            onDefaults={(patch) => editor.defaults.set(patch)}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {railOpen && !recording && (
        <SectionRail
          sections={sections}
          current={section}
          busyIndex={busySection}
          canRegenerate={Boolean(editor?.getState().brief)}
          onGo={(i) => editor.goToSection(i)}
          onRegenerate={regenerate}
          onClose={() => setRailOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      {editor && <ScrollIndicator view={view} worldH={editor.getScene().height} />}

      <div className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-2">
        {recording && sections.length > 0 && <SectionPill index={section} total={sections.length} title={sections[section]?.title || ''} />}
        {zoomControls}
      </div>

      <DirectBar
        open={directOpen && !recording}
        ready={aiReady}
        onClose={() => setDirectOpen(false)}
        onRun={(message) => runDirect(editor, boardApi, message)}
      />

      <HelpDialog open={helpOpen} onClose={() => setHelpOpen(false)} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Verify in the browser**

Automated first, with no key: start the scripted model and the client as `docs/superpowers/plans/jam-board-e2e/README.md` describes, then `node 03-generate-and-direct.mjs`. It must end with `ALL PASSED`.

Then, with a real `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, check what a script cannot — that the *real* model's answers are good. Run `npm run dev`. Generate or open a board with a `brief` (not the example — Direct mode itself works on the example, only per-section rebuild needs a brief). Press `/`:

1. The bar opens, focused. `Esc` closes it (and does not leave recording mode or deselect anything).
2. With a flow step selected: **"make this huge"** → the label grows (a `SET_STYLE fontSize` or `RESIZE`), a sentence appears, and one `Ctrl+Z` reverts it entirely.
3. **"move the browser diagram under the $5 click"** → the hub moves to sit centred under that step, a gap below it; arrows into the hub follow.
4. **"circle LOADING in red"** → a red circle appears around that step, in its section.
5. **"draw a dashed arrow from WEBSITE to CUSTOMER LEAVES"** → a live connector that follows if you then drag either end.
6. **"change WEBSITE to YOUR SITE"** → the label changes (piece text override).
7. **"delete the pixels node"**, then **"bring it back"** → deleted, then restored.
8. **"add a statement under the hub saying THE BROWSER IS DOING ALL THIS"** → a new block appears in the section, the layout re-flows, and annotations already in later sections stay attached to their sections.
9. An instruction it cannot do (**"translate everything into French"**) → a sentence explaining, and nothing changes; no undo step is added.
10. Without a key: the bar shows "Direct mode needs a DeepSeek key" and is disabled. In recording mode, `/` does nothing.
11. Reload: every edit persisted.

- [ ] **Step 8: Commit**

```bash
git add client/src client/test
git commit -m "feat(board): Direct mode - command bar with relative placement and one-step undo"
```

### Task 25: Direct mode docs, performance, and the final pass

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: Document Direct mode**

In the README's *Jam board* section add a paragraph after "It survives a bad section":

```markdown
**Direct mode** (`/` or `Ctrl/⌘ K`) edits the board you have, in plain English — *"make the $5 click huge and put the browser diagram under it"*. The model is sent a compact outline (section, block and part ids with their labels — never image data) and answers with the same validated operations the toolbar uses. Anything that names an id the outline did not contain is dropped, relative placement ("under X") is resolved to numbers from the objects' real bounds at that moment, and the whole instruction is one undo step. It edits; it never regenerates.
```

and add to the **API** list:

```
POST   /api/board/edit                 # { message, outline, selection } → { operations, message }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Performance and scale check**

Run `node 04-perf.mjs` from `docs/superpowers/plans/jam-board-e2e` (see its README). It builds a 20-section board and prints render, drag-step, dispatch and scroll timings; it must end with `ALL PASSED`. Then, in DevTools, on a board built from the example with all twelve sections (≈270 objects) and again on a 20-section board (paste the reference brief twice):

1. Open the Performance panel, record a two-second wheel scroll: frames stay smooth (no long tasks over ~50 ms during scroll).
2. Drag a diagram piece with several connectors attached: the arrows track without lag.
3. `Ctrl+Z` after a Direct-mode batch of ten operations returns in well under a second.
4. Memory does not climb across repeated undo/redo (History holds references, not copies of images).

If scrolling is not smooth, the first thing to check is `canvas.renderOnAddRemove` and any per-frame work in `object:moving` handlers; the renderer's `updateConnectors` is the only per-drag work and touches only connector objects.

Then run the *Real-browser verification* list under **Testing** in the spec. Anything that fails is a bug in the implementation, not a reason to edit the spec.
